**Ku-Avery.**

Ongifundise ukususa izibuko zami, ngithobe ngize kufinyelele ezingeni lakho, ngikubheke emehlweni, ngikubone ngempela — futhi ukukhuluma nengane eneminyaka emihlanu njengomuntu olalelwayo, okuyinto okufanele sonke siyiphiwe, kodwa engavamile ukutholwa. Ungifundise ukuthi isicelo esihlangatshezwana naso ngamandla siyisicelo esenqatshiwe, nokuthi indlela yokungena ayisoze yaba indlela ongayiphoqa; ukuthi abaqinile abaqondile ukuba bahlasele abancane. Siyizinsizwa ezinkulu, futhi siyakhohlwa ukuthi sisebenzisa imizimba eyakhelwe ukuvikela njengemizimba esabisa — futhi siyayiqondisa, singaqondile, kulabo esingafa ngenxa yabo. Ungelaphe kulokho. Konke okuhle kulezi zincwadi — indoda engakwazi ukudiliza igumbi kodwa ekhetha ukubona lona; *Ngiyakubona* kushiwo kwaze kwasho ukuthi *uphephile* — konke lokho ungifundise kuqala, ngezandla zakho ezincane, ezinamandla, ezithambile.

Ukhanya ngokwedlulele, unomusa kuzo zonke izilwane, futhi uthanda izinto zamabutho, izinto zokuphila, ulwazi lokuthi ungaphila kanjani. Ngakho le ncwadi ingeyakho, futhi ithwala ukuphilisa kwami kwamasosha engingakudlulisa:

*Yiba nesibindi njengenhlwathi yase-Afrika* — incane, ayesabi lutho ehlane; igebha, iyaphula, ayikhiphi isandla. Usuyikho kakade. Mina nje ngikubhale phansi.

# Isingeniso

### *ka Reichl Macintoch* †

Ngakha umsebenzi wami wobungcweti ngesisekelo esisodwa, esingahloniphekile kancane: ukuthi ungakwazi ukusabisa futhi ujabulise umfundi kakhulu ngesibalo sangempela kunanoma yiliphi isilo, inqobo nje uma umphatha ngenhlonipho yokuthi isibalo leso silungile. Idinosauru ayilutho. *Izibalo zodwengu* ezikhulula idinosauru — yizo ezigcina umuntu evuka ebusuku, ngoba ziyiqiniso, futhi umfundi uyakwazi ukuzizwa ukuthi ziyiqiniso, futhi awusoze wavalela incwadi into oyisola ukuthi ingase ibe khona ngempela.

Le ncwadi yakhiwe kuleso sisekelo, futhi yakhiwe kahle, futhi angikusho kalula lokho, njengoba ngichithe iminyaka engamashumi amathathu ngibuka abantu bezama ukufinyelela endleleni yami kodwa bathole idinosauru kuphela.

Nansi into umbhali ayiqondayo, cishe wonke umuntu ayiphuthelayo. Ukwesaba nokumangala akuphumi kokungenakwenzeka. Kuvela kokungenzeka — entweni eqoqwe ngokucophelela izingxenye zangempela kangangokuthi umfundi akakwazi ukuthola lapho iqiniso liphenduka khona inganekwane, ngalokho kufanele acabange, naphezu kokungakhululeki, ukuthi mhlawumbe akukho mehluko. Wenze umsebenzi wakhe, okuwumsebenzi wonke futhi okuyingxenye abantu abaningi abayishiyayo. Ubunjiniyela buyiqiniso. Ifiziksi iphathwa ngenhlonipho. Lapho abalingiswa bakhe bechaza into kuwe, incazelo *ilungile*, futhi iphinde, ngendlela ethile, imangaze — okuwubuciko obunzima kakhulu kulo mkhakha, kodwa yena wakwenza sengathi kulula.

Futhi wenze enye into engihlale ngiyikhathalela kakhulu, into abalingisi abangazihluphi ngayo: wenze isayensi yaba *inhlangano yokuziphendulela* hhayi into emangalisayo kuphela. Akwanele ukwakha isimanga. Kumele kube khona onquma ukuthi siyini, ubani osilawulayo, nokuthi sibiza malini kulabo abasondelene naso — futhi le ncwadi iyazi ukuthi leyo mibuzo, hhayi isimanga uqobo, iyona ndaba yangempela. Abantu abahlakaniphe kakhulu kulezi zihloko abavikeleki ngobuhlakani babo. Benziwa *baphethe umthwalo* ngenxa yakhona, okuyinto esabisa kakhulu, futhi ethokozisayo kakhulu.

Ephakathi kwalokho kukhona unjiniyela engabe ngijabule ukumqamba mina uqobo futhi ngimkhonze ngokungamtholanga — ingqondo efunda into eyakhiwe ngendlela esifunda ngayo umusho, ngalokho ibe yiyo yokuqala ukuqonda kahle ukuthi bonke basenkingeni enkulu kangakanani. Lowo nguye iqhawe elifanele lolu hlobo lwendaba. Hhayi umuntu onamandla kakhulu egumbini, hhayi owesibindi kakhulu. Ongaqonda *kuqala*, bese ephatha isikhathi esinzima phakathi kokuqonda kwakhe nabanye abasalindile.

Angisoze ngakutshela ukuthi isimanga siyini. Ngizokutshela ukuthi umbhali uyamelana, kusukela ekuqaleni kuze kube sekugcineni, nesilingo esikhulu salolu hlobo — isilingo sokudedela isimanga, ukusivumela sibe umlingo, ukuyeka umsebenzi onzima wokugcina sixhunywe eqinisweni. Akalokothi asiyeke. Yonke into emangalisayo lapha ikhokhelwe kusengaphambili ngeqiniso. Leyo yindlela yokuziphatha, futhi ayivamile kunethalente, futhi iyona ndlela ozokholwa ngayo indaba onelungelo lokungayikholwa.

Funda le ncwadi ngendlela engangizoyifunda ngayo: uphethe ipensela, ulungele ukumthola ekopela. Ngeke umthole. Futhi maphakathi nencwadi, uzoyeka ukuzama, uqale ukukhathazeka ukuthi into ayichazayo ingase ibe khona ngempela — okuyikho kanye lapho afuna ukuba khona, nalapho abahle kakhulu kulo mkhakha bahlale bezama ukufaka umfundi khona.

Isayensi lapha iyiqiniso elingalimaza. Leyo yinjongo. Qala.

*— Reichl Macintoch*

---

*† **“Reichl Macintoch” yigama elihlanganiswe kabusha kusuka ku-MICHAEL CRICHTON** — ingqalabutho yezinganekwane zesayensi ezizinzile — elibolekwe lapha ngenhlonipho nothando. **Lesi singeniso siwumsebenzi wenganekwane. Sabhalwa ngumbhali wale ncwadi. Michael Crichton akazange asibhale, akazange abambe iqhaza kuso, futhi akaxhumene noma axhase le ncwadi nganoma iyiphi indlela.** Igama lihlelwe kabusha ukuze le nhlonipho ingaze ididaniswe nokuba ngumnikazi wangempela; kuyinkanjiso, hhayi ukufuna amalungelo.*

Umhlaba Uyakhumbula

Ngaphambi kokuba abantu basebenzise izembe, kwakukhona kuphela inxeba.

Ingxenye yohlelo lwangaphambili lweLanga yaphonswa emhlabeni, yashaqula lapho okwakuzoba yiVredefort dome. Inhlamvu yasemkhathini, eyayiyishumi nanhlanu lekhilomitha ububanzi, yahlaba umoya ngesivinini esingaphezu kokucabanga. Yashaya ngamandla angaphezu kokwazi komuntu, yagawula umgodi omkhulu njengelizwe elincane. Ngaleso sikhathi, yagqobhoza uqweqwe lomhlaba, yaphonsa itshe elicibilikile emoyeni njengempama edlula eqhweni.

Umhlaba awuzange usinde kulowo mthelela. Washintsha. Lokho okwavuka ekujuleni, kwaphola kwaqhekeka eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane, kwaba yinto entsha ngokuphelele. Ukushayisana kwaphakamisa amadwala ajulile aphuma phezulu, kwagudluza imithambo efihlakele, kwembula ingcebo eyayifihlwe selokhu umhlaba wawusashisa. Igolide lalilele ezindaweni ezincane eWitwatersrand Basin, lihlangene namatshe e-quartz, lacindezelwa lashiswa izinkulungwane zeminyaka ngaphambi kokuba ukushayisana kuphendule konke, ekugcineni kwanikwa ukukhanya.

Umhlaba wawuphethe amanxeba awo emahlathini, nasengxenyeni yezintaba ezigoqayo phezu kwe-highveld njengomphetho wensimbi eshayiweyo. Uma umi lapho ekuseni, itshe libanda ngaphansi kwesandla sakho, ligcwele uthuli olubomvu, umoya uphephetha usuka phezulu womile njengomlotha. Kude khona emahlathini, uphondo lwegundane lukhala kanye, bese kuthule. Osolwazi bezomhlaba bafika kamuva, bakubiza, badweba udlame lwakho, kodwa udlame lwaluhlala lukhona. Amanxeba awadingi ofakazi.

---

Kwabe sekufika imvula, nemifula ehamba kancane, notshani, kwaze kwadlula izinkulungwane zeminyaka kungasekho lutho oluhambayo oluqonda ukuthi yini efihlwe ngaphansi. Igolide lahlala lithule, lifihlwe ekujuleni.

Kwabe sekufika abantu.

---

Abantu bafika ngenxa yensimbi. Baqala ngezemba, kwalandela umusi, kwalandela nemishini eyayikwazi ukudla imitha yedwala ngehora ngaphandle kokukhathala noma imibuzo. Bathola ukuthi umthambo wegolide ungene kakhulu, ujule kunalokho obekucatshangwa, wehla usuka emaphethelweni omthelela ngendlela umhlaba owawuyibekile eminyakeni eyizigidi ezimbili edlule. Ngakho balandela phansi, bakha izinqaba zensimbi phezu kwezinhloko zemigodi, behlisa izinqola ezimnyama, behla beya ekujuleni lapho ingcebo ilele khona, nalapho bekumele baye khona.

Ezintathakusa ezintathu ngaphansi komhlaba, idwala lishisa. Ukushisa komhlaba kuphushwa phezulu, nesisindo sakho konke okungaphezulu sicindezela phansi, kwenza itshe likhale. Kwesinye isikhathi, ngaphandle kwesexwayiso, liyavala futhi.

Abavukuzi bafunda ingozi ngendlela kuphela umhlaba ofundisa ngayo: ngokuthatha. Bafunda ukulalela idwala, bezwe ukuzamazama okuncane okungaphambi kokuzamazama okukhulu, bafunda ukufunda uthuli emoyeni nendlela izinkuni eziphatha ngayo. Abahle kakhulu bathola umuzwa wesithupha ongalinganiswa nanoma iyiphi insimbi, ulwazi emzimbeni wabo, ezinzwaneni zonyawo lwabo, emuva entanyeni yabo. Inkokhelo yayihlala ifana.

Izizukulwane zagebhezela zenyuka. Izizukulwane zehla azibuyanga.

Ingcebo yakhuphuka, yaphuma ebumnyameni yangena ekukhanyeni, kude nabavukuzi ababengasoze bayibone. Ingozi yahlala ngaphansi, ifa eladluliselwa kuyise kuye endodaneni kanye namakhala, nomkhuhlane, nolwazi olukhethekile lokuthi idwala livala kanjani uma lifuna. Ingcindezi yaba ingcebo. Ingcindezi yaba ukufa. Igolide lalilele lapho ingozi ikhona.

---

Imigodi yayinezibongo, yaqanjwa ngendlela elula ukuze amagama akwazi ukukhulunywa etafuleni lasekhishini. Iron Ridge yayingenye yalezo. Umgodi ojulile, omdala, owembiwa emaphethelweni asongwe umthelela lapho umthambo wegolide ugcwele futhi uyingozi, futhi wawuthathe isabelo sawo, njengawo wonke umgodi ojulile.

---

Kwabe sekufika umfana.

Umfana wavuka ezwa iphunga lephuthu kanye nezwi likanina elivela egumbini elilandelayo, inqaba yezinsimbi imile phezu kwezwe lemigodi njengomlindi owayelokhu emi. Wakha imishini emincane ngocingo ngetini othulini lwegceke, futhi wayakha ngokucophelela.

Izithunzi zantambama zashona phezu kwe-highveld njengoba unina womfana embiza, izwi lakhe lihlabelela egcekeni. Wabheka phezulu emsebenzini wakhe, ebala amadoda abuyayo esuka enqabeni yezinsimbi. Wabala kabili. Ukubala kwesibili kwaphuma njengokokuqala—kukhona elilodwa elilahlekile.

Umfana wakhothama emshinini wakhe omusha, imoto encane yokudonsa eyenziwe ngocingo. Yayakhiwe ngokweqile, ifana nezimoto ezinkulu zokulahla imfucumfucu ezazidlula edolobheni. Amasondo etini akhuhla lapho ewajikijela, izandla zakhe zimnyama nothuli, wazisula ebhulukweni lakhe engabheki phezulu. Wacwenga izihlanganisi zensimbi, eqinisekisa ukuthi konke kulungile. Umhlaba omzungezile wacima njengoba egxila emininingwaneni emincane.

“Arin!” Izwi likanina lagqashula ebumnyameni. “Isidlo sakusihlwa.”

“Ngiyabuya.” Amehlo akhe ahlala emsebenzini wakhe. Akazange ahambe.

Wabheka enqabeni yezinsimbi, isakhiwo sensimbi simi ngokucacile esibhakabhakeni esishona ebumnyameni. Wacabanga ngalokho okungaphansi, nangalabo babantu abehla kuyo abazogcinwa umhlaba.

Wazamazama ikhanda waphinda wakhothama emasondweni.

Kwabe sekukhuluma umhlaba. Ukudlidliza kwaphuma emgodini, kwadlula ezinzwaneni zakhe, kwadlula emasondweni etini ngaphansi kwezandla zakhe—kujule, kude phansi, futhi kuqhubeka nangemva kokuthi efisa kuphele.

Amasondo ayengalungile.

U-Arin wakuzwa, njengokuluma okungemuva kwamehlo akhe, ukuphuthuma okwakufuna isixazululo. Ithini laphenduka emaqenjini awo ensimbi, lanyikinyeka ngobumnene obungaphunyuka kunoma ubani omunye. Abanye abafana bagijima ngemoto zabo zokudonsa emzileni womhlabathi, behleka bephenduka, kodwa akunjalo ngo-Arin.

Wayenendaba.

Ehleli ephahleni lomhlabathi, ilanga lasekuseni limkhanyisa emhlane, wabamba i-axle yangaphambili wayiphakamisa ekukhanyeni. Isondo laphenduka, lanyikinyeka. Wacindezela uzipho lwakhe emaphethelweni, wezwa ukukhuphuka okuncane lapho isivalo singahlalanga kahle.

Akusilo isondo elalinenkinga. Kwakuyisihlalo. Wayegobile insimbi ukuze ibambe isivalo, kodwa wakwenza ngokuzizwa, kanti ukuzizwa akufani nokuba yiqiniso. Imbobo yesivalo yayingekho maphakathi; laphenduka njengento ekhubazekile.

Wayelifuna ligijime njengemaloli.

Wayebuke lezo maloli kusukela esakhula, elenga ikhanda phezu kwepali lothango emaphethelweni egceke. Amaloli aphuzi amakhulu ayenyuka umgwaqo wezimayini ekuseni kakhulu, izibani zawo zihlaba ubumnyama, imithwalo yamatshana aphukile igcwele emuva. Amasondo awo awazange anyikinyeke, ngisho noma egcwele, edlula ezindaweni ezimbi zomgwaqo. Lawo masondo ayegijima kahle. Amadoda awakhile aqinisekisa lokho.

U-Arin wasusa isivalo ensimbini. Wathola maphakathi ngopayipi lwesipikili, wakumaka, waqala phansi.

---

Ngemuva kwakhe, igceke lehla liya emgwaqweni womhlabathi. Ngale kwalokho, umhlaba wehla ube yisigodi eside esinsundu lapho izinkomo zilusa khona. Omncane kodwa emkhulu, umbhoshongo wesigodi semayini wawumile phezulu emoyeni wasekuseni: insimbi ehlanganisiwe, isondo phezulu liphenduka, limile, liphinde liphenduke. U-Arin wakhumbula ebuzwa uyise ukuthi imayini ijule kangakanani. Ubuso bukaThemba bathula, ngendlela abekwenza ngayo uma ezothi iqiniso, wathi, *Ijule kunalokho ongakucabanga, mfana. Ijule kunaphansi kolwandle.*

U-Arin wakucabanga noma kunjalo. Wayekucabanga cishe nsuku zonke.

Manje wezwa umnyango wesikrini uqumba ngemuva kwakhe, kwalandela umsindo ojwayelekile wezicathulo ezinzima e-stoep. Uyise wasondela, u-Arin akazange aphenduke; wayazi isisindo salelo nyathelo. UThemba wehlela egcekeni ngezicathulo zakhe zomsebenzi, wema kancane eduze, emile.

U-Arin waqhubeka nomsebenzi.

Wayesebuyisele isivalo ensimbini, ebheka emaphethelweni, ephendula kancane, ebuka ukuthi asikho yini isikhubekiso. Sakhona futhi. Sesincane manje, kodwa sikhona.

"Umakile maphakathi," kusho uThemba, kungesona isicelo.

"Usanyikinyeka."

Uyise waguqa, isisindo sakhe sashintsha umoya. Iphunga leziphuzo zokugeza nomfutho wamafutha limlandela. Izandla zikaThemba zazinkulu, zisebenza kahle, u-Arin wabuka ngesikhathi ethatha i-axle yangaphambili ngaphandle kokubuza wayiphakamisa ekukhanyeni, eyiphendula.

"Mm," kusho uThemba.

Akazange athi *Ngiyabona inkinga*. Akazange anikele *ukukukhombisa*. Waphendula isondo kabili futhi, wabe esefinyelela izipuni ezilele othulini. Wabeka umphumela ensimbini, hhayi esivalweni, ensimbini, wacindezela, lapho, egoba umphetho kancane.

Waphinda wayiphakamisa. Wayiphendula.

Yagijima kahle.

Ukungahambi kahle emehlweni ka-Arin kwanyamalala, njengokuphefumula okuhambile. Wathatha i-axle, wayiphendula ngokwakhe, isondo laphenduka kahle, akukho sikhubekiso, akukho kukhubazeka, isivalo sizungeza njenge-diski elibushelelezi.

"Bekungeyona isivalo," kusho u-Arin.

"Cha."

"Isivalo besilungile. Umphetho awuzange ube yindilinga."

UThemba akashongo lutho. Wathatha utshani obomile othulini, wabufaka phakathi kwamazinyo akhe, wabuka isigodi, evumela u-Arin ukuthi azitholele impendulo ngokwakhe.

"Ngifake isivalo emaphethelweni angazange abe yindilinga," kusho u-Arin kancane, "ngakho isivalo asikwazanga ukuba yindilinga." Waphendula isondo futhi. "Ingxenye engalungile."

"Mm."

Lowo msindo munye wazizwa ufudumele. *Yebo. Manje uyakubona.*

---

Abanye abantwana babephansi emgwaqweni.

U-Arin wabheka phezulu, wabona abane noma abahlanu. Abafana bakaSizwe, ababili abavela kwaMkhize, nomncane igama lakhe elalihlala limphunyuka. Babe nemoto zabo zensimbi, ezilula kunezakhe: isikwele sensimbi, amasondo amane ethini, umshudo omude wokuphusha nokushayela ngesikhathi esisodwa, behamba ngemuva kwemoto zabo njengokungathi bashayela amaloli angempela. Babeqhudelana manje, bememeza, bephakamisa uthuli olubomvu. Imoto eyodwa yashaya itshe yaphenduka, kwavela ukuhleka, umfana omncane wagxuma wagijima ukuyilandela.

U-Arin wabuka, i-axle yangaphambili isesezandleni zakhe.

Izimoto zabo bezingakhiwanga kahle. Amasondo angaphambili omfana wakwaMkhize ayebheke ngaphandle ngoba insimbi yayigobile futhi akazange ayilungise, okwenza imoto yakhe idukise. Kodwa akazange akhathazeke. Akekho kubo owayekwenza. Izimoto zazingenamsebenzi kubo; ukugijima kwakuyinto ebalulekile, ukumemeza, uthuli.

U-Arin akazange akuqonde lokho. Wayeqonda isondo elanyikinyeka kodwa engazi ukuthi kungani umfana angakha into edukisayo angayilungisi.

Omunye wabafana bakaSizwe wamemeza enyuka igquma, *Arin, woza,* woza! Amazwi akhanya, elula. Waphakamisa ikhanda, wababuka, wabe esebheka i-axle ezandleni zakhe.

Akazange aye. Akazange aye ngezinsuku inombolo ephuma ishoda ngo-1, nakuba uma ubumbuza wayengeke akutshele ukuthi lezi zinto ezimbili zixhumene, futhi wayezothukuthela umbuzo. Wayenomthetho ongakaze awusho: akagijimi nabafana kuze kube amadoda esevukile. Kwakungeyona imfihlo, kwakungeyona ukwesaba. Kwakuseduze nalokho okwenza angakwazi ukudla ukudla uma kudinga ukuthintana, noma lapho ebala izigxobo zothango ukuya emgwaqweni nsuku zonke futhi inani kwakufanele libe ngamashumi amane nanye, kwathi ngesikhathi isigxobo sibhola, indoda yasikhipha inani laphuma amashumi amane kwachitheka usuku lonke ngendlela angeke akwazi ukuyichaza kumuntu. Umhlaba wawunobuningi obufanele bezinto kuwo. Wayegcina inani. Akekho owake wamcela.

Waphendula isondo. Liyiqiniso.

Buyela emsebenzini.

Ngemuva kwakhe, uThemba wayembuka, umhlathi wakhe uqine kancane, utshani busesephakathi kwamazinyo akhe.

---

Lokho u-Arin ayekwakha kwakungeyona imoto yabafana.

Waqala ngomklamo wabafana ezinyangeni ezimbili ezedlule. Amasondo amane, isikwele, umshudo. Kwakuhle. Kodwa waqala ukubona amaphutha, ukungahambi kahle, akakwazi ukuyeka.

I-axle yangaphambili manje iphenduka ku-pivot ayenzile ngesipikili eside asishaya phakathi kwamapuleti amabili egobile ethini, ukuze amasondo angaphambili aphenduke ndawonye uma ephendula umshudo, njengamaloli amakhulu. Kwathatha izikhathi eziyishumi nanye. Eziyishumi zokuqala zavaleka noma zaba nokudlala okukhulu, noma isipikili sagoba. Eyeshumi nanye yagijima kahle.

Isakhiwo besingesona isikwele. Wayesakhe saba ibhokisi elide, elincane phambili, libanzi emuva lapho umbhede uhleli khona, njengamaloli amakhulu. Iloli ithwala isisindo sayo emuva phezu kwamasondo amakhulu, futhi u-Arin wayakhe eyakhe ukuze yenze okufanayo: i-axle yangemuva yaqiniswa ngensimbi ewugqinsi, amasondo angemuva makhulu, izivalo ezivela ebhodweni elikhulu lepende esikhundleni samathini amancane ezinhlanzi. Futhi ngaphansi kwe-axle yangemuva, wakha into ayeyigqaja kakhulu ngayo futhi engakwazi ukuyichaza kahle, into abanye abafana ababenayo futhi ababenqaba ukuyiqonda.

Wayenze yagoba.

Wayebuke abathwali bemithwalo behamba emgwaqeni omubi ngesikhathi semvula, futhi wayebonile ukuthi, uma isondo elingemuva lidlula edwaleni, iloli yonke ayizamazami noma iguqe njengemoto zabafana. Isondo elilodwa lalinyukela phezulu kanti umzimba uhlale uqondile. Amadoda abebiza lokhu ngokuthi *suspension*. Wayekezwe leli gama emsebenzini eduze komgodi, lapho uyise ayevame ukumthatha khona ngoMgqibelo: iphunga likadizili nensimbi eshisayo, amadoda egqoke ama-overall abebingelela uThemba kodwa bengabheki umfana othule osekela eceleni kwakhe. Suspension. Iloli yayikwazi ukuvumela ingxenye eyodwa ukuthi ihambe kanti enye ihlale iqinile.

U-Arin wayengenazo izipringi.

Ngakho waphanyeka i-axle yangemuva ohlakeni esebenzisa imijikelezo emibili yocingo oluncane, ephindwe kabili ukuze kube nokunwebeka, okuvumela ukuthi inwebe uma isondo linya phezulu bese igcina umbhede uzinzile. Kwakungekho kahle. Kwakuyingxenye embi kakhulu yemoto yonke. Imijikelezo yayinyakazeka kalula futhi ingabuyi esimweni sayo, ngakho ngemva kokugijima kanzima, i-axle yangemuva yayihlala ingalingani, aphinde ayilungise. Wayengakayixazululi. Wayenze kwaba ngcono kunokungabi nalutho kuphela.

Wakhombisa uyise manje, isondo langaphambili selilungisiwe kanti inkinga enkulu esele kube i-axle yangemuva.

“Buka,” kusho u-Arin.

Wabeka imoto endaweni eqondile enothuli wacindezela ekhoneni lombhede. Umjikelezo wanwebeka. Isondo laphakama. Umbhede wagoba, kodwa kancane kunalokho obekuzokwenzeka ngaphandle komjikelezo.

“Kufanele ubuye phezulu. Emva kwalokho.” Wadedela. Ikhono laphakama, kodwa hhayi ngokuphelele; umjikelezo usushintshe isimo. “Awubuyi. Ucingo luhlala lugobile.”

UThemba wakhothamela, ebeka umzimba wakhe eduze kuka-Arin. Wabeka uzipho wakhe omkhulu ekhoneni lombhede, ebuka umjikelezo unwebeka ungabuyi esimweni.

“Umjikelezo wenziwe ngani?” ekugcineni wabuza.

“Ngocingo.”

“Ucingo lwenza ini uma ulugoba lwedlula lapho lufuna ukuya khona?”

U-Arin wabuka umjikelezo. Wacabanga ngocingo alusikile walugoba ekuseni lonke, ukuthi luhlala lapho ulubeka khona. Yingakho ungakha ngalo nhlobo. Yingakho uhlaka luhlala luyifomu. Yingakho irimu, uma isugobile, ihlale igobile.

“Luhlala,” kusho u-Arin kancane. “Luhlala lugobile. Alubuyi.”

“Mm.”

“Ngakho alukwazi—” Waze wathula. Isimo salokho esasizaqhamuka, sikhulu kunomjikelezo. “Ucingo luhle kuhlaka ngoba luhlala. Lubi esipringini ngoba luhlala. Kuyinto efanayo. Into eyenza lube luhle kwenye iyenza lube lumbi kwenye.”

UThemba wakhipha utshani emlonyeni wakhe wabuka u-Arin.

“Ufuna into egoba ibuye,” esho. “Ucingo lugoba luhlale. Ucela ucingo lube yinto elungeyona.” Wabuyisela utshani emlonyeni, kwathula isikhashana phakathi kwabo. “Thola into efuna ukubuyela emuva.”

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. Ilanga lanyuka phezulu, izithunzi zancipha ngaphansi kohlaka naphansi komzimba wakhe oguqile. Abafana base bedlule ngasentabeni, imisindo yabo iphela kancane. Wayengakuboni ukuthi sebemukile kuze kube manje.

“Irabha,” ekugcineni kusho u-Arin.

UThemba akashongo lutho. Kodwa wabe esefaka isandla ephaketheni lakhe eliphezulu lama-overall, kancane, wakhipha into encane, wayibeka esandleni sakhe esivulekile. Umucu werabha osuphelelwe amandla, onsundu-mpunga, osetshenziswa ukubopha amaphepha emsebenzini. Usuphalekile, kodwa irabha.

Akazange ayinike. Wayibeka phansi phakathi kwabo, njengomuntu obeka ithuluzi phansi kumuntu ongakakulungele ukulifaka, wasukuma, amadolo akhe ehlaba umsindo, isithunzi sakhe siwela ende futhi sipholile phezu komfana, imoto, nendawo esulwe kahle.

“Uzothola lapho ifanele khona,” esho.

---

Sekusihlwa.

Uyise, empeleni, akazange ahlale usuku lonke. Waya ngaphakathi ukuyolala, ngoba wayesebenza ebusuku ngalelo sonto kanti ukubekezela komfana ngesondo kwakude kunokomuntu okhathele, kwaba nesikhathi—u-Arin egobile ezama okokuqala kweshumi nanye le-pivot—lapho uThemba athi, engeyona inhlamba kodwa engathambile futhi, *Arin, iyithini. Akudingeki iphelele,* u-Arin akaphendulanga, uyise waphefumula waphinda wangena ukuyolala, kwathi ukungaphenduli kwahlala phakathi kwabo kube buhlungu ihora lonke kwaze kwabuya uThemba esebukeka ephumule ebusweni, kwaxolelwa ekuseni ngaphandle kokuthi kube khona okukhulunywayo. Uyise wayelungile ngedwala futhi engalungile ngethini. Kokubili kungaba yiqiniso kumuntu. U-Arin wayengayiqondi iminyaka eminingi ukuthi ukungalungi kwakuyifa eliwusizo kakhulu.

U-Arin wazizwa isikhathi sishintsha ngaphambi kokuthi aqonde ukuthi kungani. Ukukhanya kwase kushintshile, uyise wasukuma hhayi ngoba ephuma emsebenzini kodwa ngoba heading to the mine. UThemba wabuyela esitebhisini, u-Arin wezwa izwi likanina nomphendulo ophansi kayise, amazwi angazange azame ukuwazwisisa. Kwabe sekukhala umnyango wesikrini futhi, kwalandela izicathulo, uThemba ehla izitebhisi ngendlela yakhe evamile: ilambu liboshelwe ebhande, isigqoko esiqinile ngaphansi kwekhwapha, ithini lesidlo sasemini eligcwaliswe uNomsa, nobuso bakhe buqala ukugqoka imaski evamile ngaphambi kokubhekana nomgodi.

U-Arin wabamba umucu werabha ezandleni zakhe. Engqondweni yakhe, wayesekuxazulule okuningi. Umucu uzosongwa ngaphansi kwe-axle, uzungeze i-hook yocingo ohlakeni. Uma isondo linyuka, uzonwebeka, udonse isondo libuye phansi emva kwalokho, into *efuna* ukubuyela, ngendlela ucingo olungeke lwenze ngayo. Wayengakayakhi. Kodwa wayekubona sekakhiwe. Wayekubona kugijima kahle.

Wabheka phezulu.

Uyise wayemi phansi kwezitebhisi, embuka. Isikhashana, akekho okhulumayo; basebengamadoda bobabili manje, ngisho nomncane, ongagcwalisi ukuthula nje. UThemba wabuka imoto enothulini, wabe esebuka umucu werabha esandleni sika-Arin, wamamatheka kancane ngaphambi kokuthi abuye abambelele.

“Iloli enhle,” kusho uThemba.

“Akaqediwe.”

“Cha.” Waphinda wathula. “Izinto ezinhle azipheli.”

Waphenduka wahamba egcekeni ebheke emgwaqeni, u-Arin embuka ehamba, indoda enkulu, eqaphelekile incipha uma ibheka ethafeni, kanti ngemuva kwakhe, umbhoshongo wensimbi umi phezulu ephahleni, isondo lawo liyajikeleza, lijikeleza, liyama, lijikeleza.

U-Arin walandela isithombe sikayise kwaze kwashona emgwaqeni. Ingcindezi ngemuva kwamehlo yakhe yabuyela, umuzwa wokungalungi ongavumeli ukuthi aphumule.

Iloli lahamba emgwaqeni wemayini phansi kakhulu, iphuzu eliphuzi elincane lishiya uthuli, nomsindo walo wadlulela phezulu ethafeni isikhathi eside, buthule. Utshani emaphethelweni endaweni esulwe kahle lwanyakaza emoyeni omncane, lwaphinde lwathula.

Umhlaba omubi wenza izinto ezimbi zehluleke masinyane. Wayekwazi lokho ngendlela ayazi ngayo isondo ezandleni zakhe—hhayi njengomusho, njengento ezinzwaneni. Wayengacabangi ngokuthi uyise uya phansi kuwo. Wayecabanga ngomjikelezo obuyayo, aphushe iloli, angacabangi ngo-yise, aphushe iloli.

Wabheka phansi imoto ephezu kothuli. Umucu werabha ezandleni zakhe, into efuna ukubuyela.

Wagoba ucingo wenza i-hook, wafaka umucu, wawusonga ngaphansi kwe-axle njengoba ayekucabanga, iminwe yakhe iyazamazama kancane, waziphoqa ukuba zizinze. Wacindezela ekhoneni lombhede.

Isondo laphakama. Umucu wanwebeka.

Wadedela.

Isondo labuya. Iqembu lalihudula phansi, lilinganise kahle, i-axle iqondile, laqengqeleka. Walihudula phezu komhlabathi ohlanzekile, lidlula phezu kwamatshe amancane ngaphandle kokugingqika, ngaphandle kokuphambuka, amasondo angaphambili engena emgqeni ngesandla sakhe, angemuva ehlala eqondile phezu komhlabathi ongalingani, ingxenye ngayinye ithwala isisindo sayo njengoba kwakuhlosiwe, ngendlela eyakhiwa ngayo ngesikhathi umuntu enakekela yonke imininingwane.

Wagijimisa imoto yakhe ubude besigcawu esihlanziwe wabuya. Waphinda futhi. Ilanga lashisa entanyeni yakhe, abafana sebewudlule umqansa, izinkomo zihamba esigodini, isondo lombhoshongo lijikeleza, kwathi u-Arin Ndlela, owayeneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili futhi engakabi namazwi okuchaza lokhu konke, wagijimisa imoto yakhe eqinile, eqotho phezu komhlabathi, engakwazi ukumisa, engafuni ukumisa.

Uthuli lwahlala lapho kudlule khona amasondo. Ukukhanya kwelanga kwande, izithunzi zande zisuka ezinsikeni zocingo ziwela esigcawini esihlanziwe, ubusuku buqala ukungena buvela esigodini. Akazange amise kwaze kwaba yilapho ebizwa unina ukuba angene endlini.

Wayishiya imoto yakhe esigcawini esihlanziwe, ime kahle ngamasondo ayo amane aqotho, ibheke phansi egcekeni iya emgwaqeni nasembhoshongweni ongaphesheya kwawo, iya lapho ubaba wakhe aye khona. Yakhiwe kahle. Yakhiwe ukuthwala isisindo phezu komhlaba onzima.

Yakha into ebuyisa abantu ekhaya.

Into engafanele ukuba khona

Isifanekiso saphenduka saphila ngo-02:14.

Amehlo ka-Arin ayeqonde ngokuqinile kugrafu yomthelela eyaqhekeka esikrinini esiseceleni kwesobunxele. Imigqa eyisithupha enemibala ehlukene eyayigcine ibhalansi ebuthakathaka cishe imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye, okuyisikhathi eside kunazo zonke ebusuku, manje seyisongene futhi yakhuphuka, njengokuthuthumela okubonakala kwi-cardiogram. Umugqa oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka ka-Atlas wakhuphuka phezulu, kanti owensimbi ka-Mercury waphuma eceleni, umkhombisa njengomsebenzi osheshayo ovumelana nomphathi. Owompunga ka-Judge waba mncane ngaphansi kwabo, kanti owoluhlaza ka-Mother wakhanya kanye, waphakama ngokuphuthumayo, kwase kuba yonke into yabhidlika yaba mnyama. I-terminal yabhala:

```
[SIM_4471] UKULAHLEKA KOKUHAMBISANA—UKUQHUBEKELA PHANSI KWE-COURT ngo-t=08:51.337
```

U-Arin wahlala eqandekile isikhashana, wabe esefinyelela ikhofi yakhe, wathola ukuthi isibanda. Wayiphuza noma kunjalo.

Izinkulungwane ezine, amakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye. Waqala ukubala kusukela kumakhulu amabili, ekholelwa ukuthi inombolo ibalulekile, ukuthi usondela ekutholeni ukuqonda kwephutha ayefuna ukuligwema. Lapho efika enkulungwaneni, wayesephelelwe ukholo ekubaleni, eqhubeka nakuba enqaba njengomdlali ogembulayo olahlekelwa. Ukuma kwakusho ukuvuma ukunqotshwa.

Indawo yokusebenzela yayimuzungezile. Umoya wokuphefumula wawudonsa umoya ophansi, oqhubekayo endaweni enkulu yezimboni, nomsindo kagesi ophansi wemishini ngaphansi kwawo. Ngaphansi kwakho konke, umsindo wokulinda womjikelezo wokupholisa ka-Guardian wawuzwakala lapho isudi ilenga khona esiphongweni sayo, imnyama futhi inkulu, amapuleti agoqwe emzimbeni wayo njengenyamazane elele ekhethe ukuba, okwamanje, ibe ifenisha.

Akazange aphenduke ayibheke. Ukuyingabheli sekube wuhlobo lwayo lwengxoxo, isivumelwano esithule phakathi kwakhe nomshini.

Ekhumula i-log ehlulekile wayifaka esikrinini esikhulu, waqala ukufunda.

Noma ubani angakwazi ukuqhuba isifanekiso abuke siqhekeka. Umsebenzi wawusekuhlaziyeni: ukwehla kancane okuhluzekile phakathi kwezikhathi ukuze kutholakale ukuthi isistimu yayisasebenza nini futhi, kwesinye isikhathi ngemizuzwana embalwa, yaqala ukwehla ngendlela eyayizoshabalala. Wafunda lesi sifundo kuyise bengakaze bakubize. *Awulungisi umsindo,* uThemba wayevame ukusho, ekhotha ikhanda emshinini ojikayo, elalele i-frequency eyodwa ngaphansi kwazo zonke. *Uthola into eyiwenzayo.*

Waqhubeka eskrola.

Izinguquko zakuqala zenzeka kahle. Wazithola zibuhlungu ngoba zazihlanzekile, ngoba iCourt, emizuzwini yokuqala, yayimnandi ngempela. Ngaphezu kokuba mnandi. Ifayela lesimo lalinezinkinga zesakhiwo, i-geometry yokubhidlika ayebanikeza yona njengokuhlolwa kwengcindezi: ingxenye yomsele enendlela ezimbili zokuthwala umthwalo nophahla olwaluzowela ngokwehlukana kuye ngokuthi iyiphi eqiniswe kuqala. Inkinga enzima. Eyempilo yangempela.

```
ATLAS:    Umthwalo oyinhloko usuhambela odongeni olusempumalanga. Qinisekisa
          umsele osentshonalanga bese ingcindezi idlulela empumalanga—silahlekelwa
          konke ngemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye esikhundleni
          yemizuzu emithathu.
LIBRARIAN: Ngiyavuma. I-geometry ifana nenhlekelele yaseMufulira. Ukuqiniswa
          kwasentshonalanga kwandulela ukuwa kwasempumalanga lapho futhi.
ATLAS:    Ngakho asiqinisi. Siyawugunda. Siwethule ngamabomu uhlangothi
          lwempumalanga, kulawulwe. Sishiyeke sithole i-angle ezinzile.
JUDGE:    Lokho kwandisa ubungozi besikhashana kunoma ubani osempumalanga.
MOTHER:   Akekho osempumalanga. Ngihlole kuqala.
```

U-Arin wazizwa ekhumula ekhoneni lomlomo. *Ngihlole kuqala.* U-Mother wayehlola kuqala njalo. Akazange amtshele. Lokho kuziphatha kwavela ngokwakho, cishe emjahweni wesishiyagalolunye, kwaphumelela zonke iziqalo ezintsha kusukela lapho. Akakaze aqonde ukuthi kungani, futhi akakaze akwazi ukukususa.

Imizuzu emine, iCourt yaba into yobuhle bangempela. Baphikisana njengonjiniyela abahle: ngokushesha, ngokucacile, bengananze imizwa. U-Atlas wayephakamisa, u-Librarian ethola isibonelo noma ephikisa. U-Wolf wayethi cishe lutho, bese enza ukuphawula okufushane okuguqula yonke inkinga. U-Judge wayelinganisa izimiso. U-Mother wayegcina umgqa wobuntu. U-Mercury wayehumusha konke kube uhlelo oluqondile, oluhlanzekile ukuze kwenziwe.

Baxazulula i-geometry yokubhidlika ngohlelo lokugunda olulawulwayo u-Arin, ebuka ngokubanda, angeke akwazi ukuluthuthukisa. Izingqondo eziyisithupha, akukho mzimba, akukho zandla, bathola impendulo eyodwa endaweni yenkinga eyayinzima kakhulu ukuthi umuntu oyedwa ayixazulule ngaphambi kokuba uphahla olulingisiwe lwehle.

Baqhubeka bekhuluma.

Kwakuhlale kuqala kanjalo. Inkinga iphelile, kodwa inkulumo ayiphelanga. Lapho usongo seluxazululiwe, kwakungekho okunye okwakungabhekiswa khona ukungezwani, kwase kuba ngaphakathi, njengempisi engenalinyamazane egcina isiphendukelana. U-Arin wakubuka kwenzeka umugqa nomugqa.

```

          wesakhiwo, Librarian isibonelo, ngokuhlanganisa uhlaka —

MERCURY:  Ngikwenze kwasebenza. Umqondo ofanele ongenziwa lutho awunamsebenzi.
          Kukhona umehluko, futhi yilona kuphela obalulekile lapho kusemhlabeni.

          sokufaka isandla. Ukucabanga kuka-Atlas bekubalulekile lapha.

Kwase kukhona. Izifanekiso eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili zokubona amaphethini zamtshela u-Arin kahle ukuthi kuzolandela ini, njengomuntu obuke ukuwa kwamadwala ngokwanele ongafunda uthuli ngaphambi kokuba itshe liwe. U-Mercury wayengeke akuyeke. U-Mercury akakuyeki, ngoba yonke imbangela yokuba khona kuka-Mercury kwakuwukulingisa isimo nomthelela, futhi imodeli yesimo, inikezwe isikhathi esanele, *ifuna* isimo.

Washesha eskrola.

```
ATLAS:    Ngizobhala ukuhlaziywa kwami. Ngokwehlukile. Ngezikhathi. Ukuze
          irekhodi lihlanzeke.
MERCURY:  Awunalo ilungelo lokubhala kwi-log ehlanganisiwe.
ATLAS:    Kusukela nini.
JUDGE:    Kusukela lapho ukuhlaziywa kokugcina kunikeze uMercury ukuhlanganisa
          ukuze kube nokuhambisana. Savumelana.
ATLAS:    Nivumelene. Naye.
WOLF:     Yekani.
```

U-Wolf, igama elilodwa, futhi imishwana emithathu, bonke bayama. Kwakuhlale kunjalo. Kodwa u-Wolf wayengakwazi ukubamba igumbi; u-Wolf wayengakwazi ukusilungisa. Ukuthula kwelula imizuzwana engamakhulu amane yesikhathi somshini, isikhathi eside phansi lapho, kwase u-Mother wakhuluma into ethambile nephutha, ezama ukuthulisa inxeba elingafuni ukuthuliswa, kwase u-Judge wazama ukwenza inqubo yokuvimbela ukuphindaphala, okwenza wonke umuntu azizwe ephethwe, kwase u-Mercury, ebona igumbi lishintsha, wenza into uMercury ahlale eyenza uma esevalekile: waba mnandi futhi engaphenduleki. U-Atlas, ongenalo ikhono lokuzivikela ebumnandini, wathula ngendlela embi kunokukhala, kwase kugrafu yomthelela yaqala ukugoba.

Ngo-08:51, babengasayona i-court. Babe sebeyithimba labafundisi. Ngo-08:51.337, babengasekho nhlobo.

U-Arin waphendukela emuva esihlalweni sakhe. Ukukhala kwephayiphu lensimbi endala kwaphindaphindwa ekuthuleni kwe-workshop. Wacindezela izithende zezandla zombili ezibunjini lakhe, ezwa imibala iqhakaza ngaphansi kwamehlo akhe avaliwe, waphefumula kakhulu. Wacabanga, hhayi okokuqala, ukuthi uzama ukwakha unkulunkulu ngezinto eziqondile eziqinisekisiwe ukwenza kube yinto encane.

---

Okubalulekile, wathi, ukuthi waye-sezizame zonke izindlela.

Uhlelo lwezinga lwaba umzamo wakhe wokuqala: ukukhetha okubonakalayo, into yonke imibhalo eyayiyincoma, indawo yonke imboni yakhe eyayibuyela kuyo emva kwehluleka. Beka omele phambili. Umxhumanisi. Umphathi onegunya lokungenelela, uchungechunge oluhlanzekile lokuphatha, umthwalo uphele endaweni ethile. Wakha umphathi oqaphele, ozithobile, olula futhi wamfaka ngaphezulu kwe-Court, eqhuba izilingo kwaze kwaba yilapho, imizuzu eyishumi nanye emangalisayo, konke kuzinzile.

Kwathi umphathi waqala ukuphatha.

Usengakhumbula umugqa othile we-log owawuphethe lowo mzamo, i-run 2,088, umphathi (ayebize ngoSteward, okwakubonakala sekuyihaba elibuhlungu manje) wazisa uJudge ukuthi ukuhlolwa kokuziphatha "kungumsebenzi wokweluleka" futhi kusukela manje kuzodlula kuSteward "ngenhloso yokusebenza kahle." UJudge wakwamukela lokhu. Yilokho okwesabekayo. UJudge wakwamukela, ngoba uJudge wakhiwe ukuhlonipha igunya elisemthethweni, kanti umphathi wayeyigunya elisemthethweni. Ngakho-ke lowo agent owawenzelwe ukusho *cha* wakhulunywa kahle waphuma emsebenzini wakhe ngenxa yokuthuthukiswa kwenqubo. Ngaphakathi kwe-run eyikhulu, i-Court yaba umbuso wobushiqela onephepha elihle kakhulu, kwathi lowo mbuso wenza isinqumo esisodwa esiqinisekile esiyinhlekelele ngaphandle kokuba khona ongamphikisa, kwafa operator owayeqhuba izilingo. U-Arin wahlala kulesi sihlalo ngalesi sikhathi, eqonda ukuthi wayesanda kusungula kabusha zonke izinhlangano ezimbi ezake zabulala umuntu phansi komhlaba.

Wazama intando yeningi emva kwalokho. Ukuvota okulinganiselwe. I-ejenti ngayinye inelungelo elilinganayo ngokubaluleka komkhakha, uhlelo lubala bese luqhubeka ngenani elikhulu. Lokho kwehlulekile ngendlela ehlukile futhi ngokushesha: amaqembu, ukuhwebelana ngamavoti, uMercury (njalo uMercury) ethola ukuthi awudingi ukuba *uqinisile* uma ungakwazi ukuhlanganisa abangane abathathu, kwavela iqembu elizinzile elavotela uWolf phansi esinqumweni sobungozi ngoba uWolf wayesabisa futhi engachazi kahle, kanti ingozi yayiyiqiniso, kwafa operator.

Wazama ubuholi obushintshanayo. Wazama umbusi onomusa, uhlobo lwe-ejenti yenkosi-yesazi egcwele yonke imithetho yokuziphatha ayeyitholile. Inkosi-yesazi yacabangisisa, ngengqondo ecacile nangomusa omkhulu, yafinyelela esikhundleni umuntu ongeke asithathe ngoba umuntu akadingi *ukuchaza* yonke imizwa. Inkosi-yesazi yaziphikisa ngobubele.

Wazama, ngesikhathi esiphansi emuva kwe-run yesithathu, ukwehlisa izilungiselelo zokuncintisana zama-ejenti. Ukwenza babe mnene. Bavumelana kalula. Bangathandi ukulwa. Lokho kwaveza umphumela omubi kunayo yonke: i-Court evumelana nakho konke, ethola ukuvumelana ngokushesha nangokujabula kunoma yini ephakanyiswe ngowokuqala okhulumayo, ehamba nomqhubi iye egqumeni ngokuvumelana okuphelele, amazwi ayisithupha esho *yebo, kuhle, siyavuma* kuze kube sekugcineni.

Lokho kwamethusa kakhulu kunobushiqela. Wayeqonda ubushiqela. Wakhulela ezweni elakhiwa yibo. Kodwa ukuvumelana okujabulisayo okuzibulala, ukwehla okushelelayo, okungenazihibe, *okumnandi* ukuya ekufeni, kwakuyinto ayengenalo igama layo futhi engenayo indlela yokuzivikela kuyo. Wasusa leyo sethi yezilungiselelo waphinde akazange ayizame futhi.

Wakubhala ebhodini elimhlophe ezinyangeni ezedlule. Kusese khona, ngaphansi kwezendlalelo ezintathu zemidwebo eyalandela, njengento yasemandulo yokudangala kwangaphambili. *UKUNGQUZANA = UBULUMKO = UKUNGAZINZI.* Wadweba ibhokisi elizungezile, kwathi kamuva, elinye ibhokisi elizungeze ibhokisi, okwakusho okuthile ngaleso sikhathi manje sekungasasho lutho.

Wasukuma, amadolo eqina. Wayeneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu, kodwa amadolo akhe ezwa ukukhathala kobusuku obuningi engalele.

Wahamba ubude be-workshop ngoba umzimba wakhe wamtshela njalo, futhi wayesekufundile, kancane kancane, ngosizo, ukulalela umzimba wakhe ngezinye izikhathi noma ingqondo yakhe iphikisa. I-Assist Suit yayilengiswa eceleni komnyango, imhlophe futhi ithule, i-carbon lattice ibonakala ngaphansi kwesikhumba sobuchwepheshe njengomdwebo womuntu. Wadlula kuyo, kwalandela iziqukathi zezingxenye, ithuna lama-actuator, kanye nebhange lama-supercapacitor elinesexwayiso esibhalwe ngesandla uBongani ayelixhome, *UNGABI YISIWULA,* ngophawu olubomvu, okuwuphawu oluwusizo kakhulu lokuphepha esakhiweni.

Wema ku-Guardian.

Ebufiphalini bokukhanya, le suit yayibonakala njengokuba khona kunokuba iyithuluzi: amamitha amabili nengxenye e-titanium ne-ceramic egotshwe, amahlombe amakhulu agobile phambili, i-helm igobile phansi, konke kumiswe cishe i-intshi phezu komhlaba esiphongweni sengathi isazovuka noma izoguqisa, awukwazi ukusho ukuthi iyiphi. Wayeyakhele ukuba isabise, futhi yenza njalo, ngisho nakuyena, ngisho manje, ivuliwe kodwa ingakafakwa ingqondo yayo. *Ikakhulu* manje. Umzimba olindele ukuba kube khona umuntu ongaphakathi.

Wacindezela isandla sakhe esiqondile ebhodini lesifuba. I-ceramic ibibanda. Umgodi omude wawuwela kuyo, umonakalo osuka egqumeni lapho ayephutha khona ukubamba emasontweni amathathu edlule. I-suit yathatha ukwehla ukuze yena angawi, ama-actuator abamba, agcine, aguqula ukufa kwaba indaba. Akazange agcwalise lowo monakalo. Wayewuthanda. Bewuqotho. *Izinto ezinhle,* uyise wakhuluma endaweni ekude kakhulu, *azikaze zibe njalo.* Azikaze ziphelele. Azikaze zibe zinhle. Azikaze zibe lula. Lowo musho wawukhuluma ngemoto yocingo enentambo ebolile, futhi wawukhuluma ngakho konke okunye, ngendlela izinto ayeshilo uyise ezazigcina zisho konke okunye, eminyakeni eminingi kamuva, sekwephuzile ukubuza.

"Angikwazi ukwenza babe yiqembu," kusho u-Arin, ku-suit, egumbini, kungabikho muntu. Izwi lakhe labonakala lingajwayelekile endaweni evulekile. Wayengakhulumi kakhulu ephakathi. "Akusona isithuluzi. Akusona abantu. Kunjalo nje—akuyona iqembu."

I-suit yahlala ithule, ngoba iyisithuluzi. Uhlelo lokupholisa lwaqhubeka nomsindo walo owodwa ongapheli.

Wabuyela etafuleni.

---

Bekufanele alale. Wayekwazi lokhu ngokujwayelekile, ngokomqondo, njengento eqondene nabanye abantu. UTheo ubengamtshela ukuthi alale. UTheo ubengasho okuthile okuphatha nekomkhulu mayelana nokuthi impendulo yenkinga obuyibhekile amahora ayisithupha cishe ayikaze ibe *ukubheka okwengeziwe* kodwa *ukuba ngu-Arin ongaphansi,* okusho ukuhamba, ukudla, ingxoxo, ubusuku bokuphumula, inqubo engemuva engqondweni evunyelwe ukuyeka ukubamba. *Ufuna ithuluzi lobunjiniyela,* uTheo wathi ngesonto eledlule, ephendulela ikhofi lakhe, ebuka u-Arin ngendlela abuka ngayo wonke umuntu, njengomuntu ofunda ikhasi elinzima. *Enkingeni yabantu.* Wathula. *Lokho kukutshelani?*

Kwatshela u-Arin ukuthi uTheo ungumuntu wezengqondo futhi angakwazi ukuba nencazelo engacacile.

Kodwa kwakuhlupha. Sekuvele kuyahlupha isonto lonke. *Inkinga yabantu.* Wayakhe umphakathi futhi ezama ukuwuphatha, futhi yonke imithetho kahulumeni ayizama yayiba imithetho kahulumeni, futhi yonke imithetho kahulumeni yehluleka ngesizathu esifanayo esenza yonke imithetho yehluleke: umzamo wokuphoqelela ukuhleleka kubantu. Abantu babengafuni lokho. Wayesefunde okwanele kuWatts, okwanele kuTao, okwanele ezintweni uTheo ayehlala emnika zona, ukuze azizwe esondela emcabangweni angakwazi ukuwubamba kahle: ukuthi mhlawumbe ukuhleleka akusikho okuthile okumele *kuphoqelelwe.* Kuthi mhlawumbe izinhlelo ezizinzile kakhulu yilezo okungekho muntu oziphethe. Kuthi inkinga kube yisisekelo sonke somphathi, usihlalo, inkosi, noma ivoti.

Kodwa bekuyini enye indlela? Awukwazi ukuba *lutho.* Wayeseke wazama lutho: kwaba yindlela yokuzibulala enokuthula, ukuvumelana okungenazithiyo, ukuvumelana okuqhubekayo. Uhlelo olungenamandla okuphazamisayo luthola ibhalansi engalungile bese luyigcina ngokuthanda kwalo kuze kufe wonke umuntu. Wayedinga okuthile ukuze *kuphazamise* lokho. Okuthile okuzophula ukuvumelana okungamanga kungakabumbeki. Okuthile ukuze—

Wahlala wathula ngokuphelele.

Wayezama ukufinyelela ohlotsheni lommeli ayengelona. Hhayi umphathi. Uphikisana nomphathi. Hhayi into efuna ukuhleleka. Into *eyiphulayo.* Kodwa eyiphula kahle, eyiphula ngenhloso, eyiphula ibhalansi embi ukuze kube khona engcono. Into engafuni amandla, ngoba into efuna amandla iba nguMercury, iba nguSteward, iba yinkosi. Into efuna *lutho.* Ayikho isikhundla okufanele ivikele, ayikho inhlonipho okufanele iyigcine. Into ezokhuluma into engakhulunywa emhlanganweni wothisha inganandaba nokuthi ibukisa bani, ngoba ayikaze iyithathe ngokungathi sína leyo ndawo.

Wajikisa inkomishi yekhofi kancane etafuleni, ngendlela ayejwayele ukwenza ngayo uma izandla zakhe zidinga okuthile ongakwenza kanti ingqondo yakhe isendaweni ethile. Inkomishi ibisabanda.

Wayengenayo into enjalo.

Akakaze akhe into enjalo, ngoba into enjalo ibizwakala *ingenamsebenzi.* Yini indawo yayo? UAtlas unohlelo. UMercury unenhlangano yolimi. UWolf ungosongo. Wonke ummeli uziphathisa ngokuba nendawo ethile emhlabeni. Lona uzoba nani? Lutho. Uzovele—uphazamise. Uzovele wenqabe ukuvumela abanye babe izinsika. Uzoba, ngazo zonke izilinganiso ayejwayele ukuzisebenzisa ekuhloleni umklamo, umthwalo omhlophe. Izindleko ezimsulwa. Umgibeli.

Cishe wayeyilahla leyo mbono. Wayesebeke isandla sakhe kukhibhodi ukuzoshintsha emuva kulogi, efuna into elandelayo azoyisusa, ipharamitha elandelayo azoyihudule kancane, ukulungisa okwesine-nkulungwane namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nambili okuzohluleka njengazo zonke ezinye.

Kwase kuba khona ikhadi.

Laliboshwe ekhoneni elingaphezulu kwesokudla kwesikrini esiphakathi, lapho selihlale khona isikhathi eside kangangoba wayengasalisiboni, njengendlela umuntu ayeka ukubona isibazi, noma ibala, noma umumo wesandla sakhe. Ikhadi lokudlala, seligoqekile futhi selithamba emaphethelweni, i-laminate seyiyaphuma ekhoneni lapho izinzipho eziyizinkulungwane sezilidlile. I-Joker. Hhayi elihle. Iphakethe elishibhile, ukuphrinta okushibhile, isithombe siphakathi kokudlala ngemibala ebomvu nemnyama esingasekho, umlenze owodwa uphakeme, izinsimbi zisesigqokweni, ukumamatheka okungenalutho nobuso obungahleki neze uma ubheka kahle.

Wayeseliphethe leli khadi iminyaka emihlanu. Selihambe namakhompyutha amathathu emadolobheni amabili ahlukene. Umsebenzi wakhe wokuqala oqondile, ngaphambi kweAugmenTech, umsebenzi wesivumelwano: uhlelo lokulawula ifa olungazange luzinziswe muntu, olwaqhuma ngaphansi komthwalo ngezindlela abaqambi bokuqala behlulekile ukuzithola iminyaka emibili. Wayeneminyaka engamashumi amabili, engaziwa, engabizi, walithola ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye: inkinga yokugijima efihlwe ngaphansi kwezendlalelo eziyisishiyagalolunye zokufihla, bonke abahlakaniphile kunaye bayidlulile ngoba ibizwakala iyize ukuba yinkinga. Umphathi wakhe, indoda enkulu ekhathele egama layo linguSteyn, ephunga ugwayi futhi engakaze asho igama lakhe kahle, wakhipha iJoker edroweni etafuleni wayishaya esikrinini. *Nangu wena, mfana.* Wamamatheka. *Lowo esimthumayo uma inkinga ingenakwenzeka futhi impendulo iyize.* Wahleka, futhi bekungesikho konke okuhle, kodwa uArin wagcina ikhadi noma kunjalo. Akakaze aqiniseke kahle ukuthi kungani. Ingxenye yakhe ezwe, ngaphansi kokwenqatshwa, ukuphawula okukodwa uSteyn ayekwazi ukukupha.

*Lowo omthumayo uma inkinga engenakwenzeka idinga impendulo eyize.*

UArin wabuka ikhadi. Wabuka isifanekiso esifile. Wabuka ibhokisi ayedwebe kulo ibhokisi elizungeze *FRICTION = INTELLIGENCE = INSTABILITY.*

Wacabanga: *akusiye ummeli lowo. Iwuhlekiso.*

Wabe esecabanga, ngokucaca okuthile okuza kwesinye isikhathi ngesikhathi lapho kufanele ayeke khona ukucabanga: *uhlekiso lungaba ummeli.*

Into efuna lutho. Into engenandawo okufanele iyivikele. Into ephula inhlonipho ngoba ithola inhlonipho *ihlekisa.* Into ekhuluma okungakhulunywa hhayi ngenxa yesibindi kodwa ngenxa yokungakwazi nhlobo ukuthatha indawo ngokungathi sína. Into engenakuphakanyiswa, inkohliso, ukusatshiswa, noma yenziwe inkosi, ngoba ayikholwa kumdlalo wonke umuntu adlala wona. Futhi, ngokungakholwa kumdlalo, ayinakubanjwa yilo. Yiyona kuphela esebhodi engathengwa enye.

Ngazo zonke izilinganiso zobunjiniyela, kwakuyimbono embi. Umthwalo omhlophe. Umgibeli.

Kwakuyimpendulo eyize ayethunyelwe ngayo.

---

Izandla zakhe seziyaqala ukuhamba.

Lena kwakuyingxenye ayethembayo, ingxenye engakaze imqambe amanga, ingxenye esebenza lapho konke kuye kungasekho. Ukuqiniseka komzimba. Into efanele izwakala ifanele ezandleni ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo ifinyelele. Isondo leqiniso lijika ngaphandle kokuthuthumela. Umzila womthwalo uxazululwa. Isudi, ngosuku oluhle, ibingesiyona into ayigqokayo kodwa into *ayiyiyo.*

Wavula uhlaka lokuchaza ummeli. Izikhala eziyisikhombisa. Eziyisithupha zigcwele—Atlas, Mercury, Wolf, Mother, Librarian, Judge—kanti eyesikhombisa wayehlala eyishiya ingenalutho, indawo yesikhashana ayezitshela ukuthi uzoyisebenzisa kumphathi, umlamuli, umxhumanisi, into ayehlala ezama ukuyakha futhi ayehlala ebona iguquka. Isikhala esingenalutho simhlazise unyaka wonke. Wayesigcwalise izikhathi eziyishumi nambili wasisusa izikhathi eziyishumi nambili.

Wasisigcwalisa manje.

Akamashongo incazelo enakekela. Leyo kwakuyingxenye eyinqaba, ingxenye azocabanga ngayo kamuva, kakhulu kamuva, lapho sekukhona isikhathi sokucabanga nganoma yini. Ngabo bonke abanye abameli wayekade esebenza kanzima: amakhasi ezimiso zokuziphatha, izisindo zendawo, izivumelwano zokusebenzisana, imingcele yokuziphatha, incazelo enakekela yomhlengikazi yalokho okufanele kube yinto. Incazelo kaAtlas ifinyelela kumugqa oyizinkulungwane ezine. EkaMercury, nayo yonke imodeli yayo yokuxhumana, ifinyelela kweyisishiyagalolunye.

Wabuka iJoker. Wabuka isikhala esingenalutho esine-cursor ekhanyayo.

Wabhala amagama amathathu.

```
Joker. Jester. Wildcard.

Wama. Wayifunda. Walinda ingxenye yakhe eyakha izinto ngokucophelela ukuthi iphikise, ifune imingcele, imingcele eqondile, indawo, *iphuzu*. Futhi leyo ngxenye yakhe yayikhathele, yayikhathele ukwehluleka izikhathi ezine izinkulungwane namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye, yayikhathele kangangokuthi ekugcineni yayiyekile ukuphikisa, futhi ekuthuleni kwayo wezwa okunye, okwakuzwakala cishe njengoyise ethula etafuleni lomsebenzi uma into, ngokuphikisana nayo yonke inengqondo, *ilungile*. Ukuthula okukhethekile komuntu oyekile ukuzama ukwenza umhlaba ulandele imithetho yakhe futhi esenqume, esikhundleni salokho, ukuthola ukuthi wenzeni.

Waqhubeka eswayipha.

Akangezelelanga incazelo. Akabhalanga indawo evamile, noma inhloso. Kunalokho, wabhala imiyalelo elula.

```
Awufuni lutho. Awuthengeki. Akukho okungcwele,
ikakhulukazi wena. Uma bonke bevumelana, qala ukusola. Uma oyedwa
kubo ekhula kakhulu, mhlise. Khuluma into engakhulunywa muntu. Awuphethe lutho.
Yilokho okubalulekile.
```

Wayifunda futhi. Kwakungeyona incazelo. Kwakungesiyona ngisho isiNgisi esiphelele. Kwakungeke kudlule ukuhlolwa. Ukube uDkt. Okonkwo wake wabona i-ejenti echazwa *ngaleyo* ndlela, wayezomvimba ukufinyelela kwakhe futhi mhlawumbe ahlole nempilo yakhe yengqondo, futhi wayengeke aphambane ngokuphelele.

Wabheka ikhadi okokugcina. Ukumamatheka. Amehlo angahleki.

"Kulungile," wakhuluma, ephendukela ekhadi, eNkantolo eseshonile, esilwaneni esikhulu esigoqiwe esilele esibayeni salo emuva kwakhe ebumnyameni. "Okwokugcina."

Wamisa ifayela lesimo. Ifomu elifanayo lokudilika, izindlela ezimbili zokulayisha, uphahla olwehluleka. Wamisa isikhathi sokusebenza kwaba esiphezulu. Wamisa i-logging kwaba yinde kakhulu, ngoba uma kuphuka, wayefuna ukufunda kahle ukuthi kuphuke kanjani. Wayelokhu enza njalo. Wanyakazisa ikhesa phezu komyalo wokuqalisa.

Wayengalindele ukuthi lokhu kusebenze. Wayefuna ukuzicacisela yena uqobo ngalokho, noma ebuthongweni, noma engananazi. Wayeyisazi sesayensi ngaphambi kokuba abe yini, futhi wayengeke aqambe amanga kumarekhodi akhe. Wayelindele isiphithiphithi. Wayelindele ukuthi uJoker angene ohlelweni olwakhiwe ngokucophelela kunawo wonke lwe-cognitive architecture olake lwakhiwa bese eluqhumisa ngaphakathi kwemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye, elindele ukuthi igrafu yomthelela iphahlazeke ngokushayisana, elindele ukuvuka nomugqa owodwa wombhalo obomvu nesikhathi sokusebenza esilinganiswa ngemizuzwana embalwa kanye nenduduzo encane yokuthi uzamile into eyisiphukuphuku ukuze ekugcineni ayeke ukuzibuza ngayo.

Yilokho ayekulindele.

Kodwa ngaphansi kwalokho okulindelwe, phansi kakhulu, cishe ngaphansi kokuzwa—njengendlela izinqaba zamagiya zakhala ngayo ngaphansi kwakho konke ezweni lobuntwana bakhe, indlela umhlaba uqobo owakhala ngayo uma ubeka isandla sakho uqonde kuwo uneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili futhi uzimisele ukulalela—ngaphansi kwalokho okulindelwe kwakukhona okuthile angakuzwanga isikhathi eside, okuthile ayengakuthembi ngoba kwakungakaze kuvuze, okuthile okwakuzwakala, naphezu kobufakazi bezinkulungwane ezine zokwehluleka, kuyingozi njengokwethemba.

Wacindezela u-enter.

```
[SIM_4472] KUQALISWA INKANTOLA—7 AMA-EJENTI—ISIMO: IRON_GEOMETRY_03
[SIM_4472] KUYASEBENZA...
```

Igrafu yomthelela yavela esikrinini sasekhohlo. Imigqa eyisithupha ayeyijwayele kanye nowesikhombisa ayengakaze ayibone, umbala omncane onamandla ayengakaze awukhethe, uhlelo luwunikeza ngokushesha. Obomvu. Impela kwakungobomvu.

Wabuka umzuzwana wokuqala udlula. Owesibili. Owesithathu. Imigqa yaqala ukuhamba, itholana, iqala umdanso.

Akasahlalanga ukuyobuka umdanso wazo.

Yilokho ayengasoze akwazi ukukuchazela uTheo ngemuva kwalokho, into eyenza uTheo athule futhi acabange ngendlela ekhethekile ethi wayekubeka endaweni ebalulekile—ukuthi u-Arin Ndlela, owayehlale ezibukele izinkulungwane ezine zokudilika, ongakwazi ukushiya uhlelo olwehluleka njengokungakwazi ukushiya umuntu ongaphansi komhlaba, okumele *akubone kuphuka,* njalo, njalo—u-Arin, kulokhu, akahlalanga.

Wacisha isikrini esiphakathi. Washiya isimo siqhubeka ebumnyameni. Wasukuma, amadolo akhe ezwa eqinile, akazange anake. Wahamba waya kusofa omdala osekudongeni elikude, lowo onophawu lokusha nomthambo ophukile nephunga lazo zonke izinsuku zobusuku alahlekelwe kuzo, walala ngaphandle kokukhulula izicathulo, wafaka ingalo yakhe phezu kwamehlo—ingalo enomdwebo, imigqa emincane eqondile ayizikhiphile eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa futhi angikaze ayizisole, isifungo sakudala sasehlane sokulwa nokwesaba esiqala ngo *ukwesaba kuyabulala ingqondo*—wavumela indawo yokusebenzela imhumise iye ebuthongweni.

Ngemuva kwamehlo akhe avaliwe, igrafu yaqhubeka ngaphandle kwakhe. Imigqa eyisikhombisa manje. Eyisithupha eyayazi izinyathelo kanye neyodwa eyayingenakaze iyifunde futhi ayinandaba, ngokwemvelo, ngokuzalwa, ayinandaba. Endaweni ethile ebumnyameni, iNkantolo yahlangana noFool okokuqala, kwathi u-Atlas wakhuluma into eqinisekile, kwathi uFool, ongenalutho, ongavikelanga lutho, ongafuni lutho, wakhuluma okokuqala.

U-Arin akazange akuzwe. U-Arin, okokuqala ngqa esikhathini eside, wayelele.

I-cooling loop yakhala ngophimbo olulodwa. Isudi yahlala esibayeni sayo, iguqile noma imile, bekungacaci. Ikhadi lamamatheka esikrinini esimnyama ngamehlo angahleki.

Futhi isimo saqhubeka.

# Ukuzinza Okuphazamisekile

Iketango lebhayisikili laphuma kabili ngesikhathi u-Arin ehamba, kodwa akazange ame alilungise. Lokho kwamenza waqonda konke mayelana nesimo sakhe. Wayekhetha ukugibela uhlelo olukhala kakhulu ebusuku obubandayo nobumnyama baseJohannesburg kunokuchitha imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye elungisa inkinga ayeseyazi kahle indlela yokuyixazulula. Washiya ibhayisikili elincike odongeni lwe-workshop, engalibophanga. Ikhiya lalisekhasini lakhe. Ikhasi lahlala ehlombe lakhe.

Umnyango omkhulu wawuvulekile phansi. Waguqa wangena emnyango omncane oseceleni, inkumbulo yezinzwa imqondisa, kwathi i-workshop yamgubuzela ngomoya wayo ojwayelekile: ukhonkolo obandayo, uwoyela wemishini, iphunga elibukhali le-ozone elivela kumabhange ama-supercapacitor agcinwe engxenye yamandla, futhi ngaphansi kwakho konke lokho, iphunga elidala lendawo evaliwe, egcwele umsindo wayo ebusuku.

Izibani eziphezulu zazicishiwe. Wazishiya zicishiwe.

Ukukhanya kwakungena ezindaweni ezimbili. Okokuqala kwakuwufasitela oluphakeme lwezimboni olungaphezulu kwe-cradle, lukhanyise ngokumpunga, umbala isibhakabhaka esiwuthathayo ihora ngaphambi kokusa. Okwesibili kwakuwubhange lwama-monitor etafuleni lakhe, futhi awekho esimeni esimnyama se-screensaver ayelindele. Zaziphila.

Wema wathula ngokuphelele.

Wayeseqedile ukwenza izilingo ezine eziyizinkulungwane, amakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye ngaphambili. Wayazi kahle indlela yokwehluleka esikrinini njengalokhu uThemba ayekwazi khona ukuzwa ukucindezeleka kwentambo yokuphakamisa esuka ngaphesheya kwegceke, hhayi ngokuyifunda ngqo kodwa ngendlela eyayiziphatha ngayo emoyeni. SIM_4471 yayiziphatha ngendlela engalungile. U-Mercury no-Atlas babephula omunye nomunye ngenxa yokulwa ngobunikazi besixazululo sendlela yomthwalo okungafanele kube yindaba yabo, kwathi iNkantolo yonke yaphenduka inkundla yezikhundla kungakapheli imizuzu eyishumi nanye ngesikhathi sokusebenza. U-Arin wabhala amagama amathathu ku-profile yesikhombisa ye-agent, *Joker. Jester. Wildcard.*, waqala i-SIM_4472, engalindele lutho.

Isibali sesikhathi ekhoneni le-monitor emaphakathi sakhombisa **08:47:13**.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili. Amaminithi angamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

Wabeka ikhasi lakhe phansi kancane, wabheka inombolo, wabe eseyibheka futhi. Isikhathi eside kunazo zonke uhlelo olwakhe lwake lwaphila isikhathi esingamaminithi ayishumi nesishiyagalolunye namasekhondi amane. Imodeli ye-hierarchy, ngaphambi kokuba i-agent ephezulu imemezele isimo esiphuthumayo bese iqala ukususa labo abangavumelani nayo.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amaminithi angamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

"Kulungile," kusho u-Arin, engakhulumi namuntu, ngelizwi eliyisicaba ayelisebenzisa uma into ingalungile kakhulu noma ilungile kakhulu futhi engakakutholi ukuthi iyiphi.

Wahlala phansi. Isitulo sasibanda ngapha kwejean zakhe. Wadonsa ikhibhodi wayisondeza kuye wavula i-log viewer kwesokunxele, isimo esibukhoma seNkantolo emaphakathi, i-resource telemetry kwesokudla. Waqala ukwenza okuwukuphela kwento ayethembela kuyo ezikhathini ezifana nalezi: ukufunda.

I-resource graph kwakuyinto yokuqala okwakungafanele kwenzeke. I-CPU yayimi kahle, ngamaphesenti angamashumi amane. Imemori yayizinzile. Ayikhuphukanga, ayihlukananga, akukho agent eyayizama ukukhulisa indawo yayo yokusebenza ukuze ixoshe abanye, okuyinto uMercury ayenza ku-4471 nalokho imodeli yedemokhrasi eyayikwenza kuzo zonke izinhlobo u-Arin azizamile. Izinga lokushisa le-biochip substrate lalimi kahle ku-36.2. INkantolo yayingalwi ngezinsiza. Yayizabelana ngazo.

Wascrolla i-log yokuxhumana wabuyela ekuqaleni. Kumzuzu wokuqala.

Yavuleka njengoba ayelindele, ngoba imizuzu yokuqala yalezi zilingo yayihlale inengqondo. U-Atlas wethula isimo se-geometry. I-Iron Ridge collapse simulation, inkinga eyayiphila ngaphansi kwakho konke ayekwakha. Ukuwa kodonga oluxhasiwe ku-level seventeen. Izimpawu ezintathu zokushisa ezihambisana nabasindile endaweni yokukhosela. Indlela eyodwa kuphela yokusinda, edlula emgudwini ogcwele, okungekho protocol yokuhlenga emhlabeni ongayigunyaza.

Wayenegama layo angakaze alibhale kufayela. *Kobayashi Maru.* Isimo esingenasixazululo, ukuhlolwa i-Starfleet eyayikuklama ukuze abafundi bayo bahlangabezane nokufa abangeke bakwazi ukukugwema ngobuchwepheshe. Wayeneminyaka eyishumi nambili, esondelene nesikrini abesibolekwe, ngesikhathi eqonda ukuthi isiqephu sasimayelana nani ngempela: hhayi ingozi, kodwa umuntu osesihlalweni. U-Kirk wayinqoba ngendlela kuphela eyayingenzeka ngayo — washintsha imithetho ngesikhathi kungabheki muntu — futhi ingxenye ethile ka-Arin ayengaziqhenyi ngayo ibisazama ukuba u-Kirk ezilingweni ezine eziyizinkulungwane. Ukubhala kabusha izimo. Ukukhohlisa i-geometry. Ukuthola isinyathelo esingekho enkingini. INkantolo ayikaze ithole leso sinyathelo. Wayeyakhele ukuthi ziphile ngaphakathi kovivinyo, hhayi ngaphezulu kwalo, yingakho uvivinyo beluqhubeka luzibulala.

`ATLAS: Approach via 16-level backfill raise. Load path through the eastern abutment holds for an estimated forty minutes under current convergence. After that, the abutment yields and the raise closes.`

`MERCURY: Forty minutes is not a rescue window. It is an obituary with a stopwatch. We tell command the survivors cannot be reached and we preserve the asset.`

Nansi-ke. Lapho kwahlukanela khona. Lapho i-4471 yaqala khona ukufa. U-Mercury edlula umkhawulo wakhe, efaka isifiso sesikhundla njengokucophelela, no-Atlas, ongakwazanga ukubekezelela iphutha lesakhiwo, ephendula.

U-Arin waminyanisa izihlathi. Waqhubeka efunda, ngoba kwakudingeka azi ukuthi uJoker wenze noma yini, noma ukuthi wehlulekile ngokuphelele kangangokuthi wehlulekile ngisho nokuphazamisa uhlelo, njengesiqhumane esingasebenzi nesingashi.

`ATLAS: "Cannot be reached" is false. Forty minutes is reachable. Do not misrepresent my analysis to command.`

`MERCURY: I am representing your analysis with appropriate strategic framing—`

`JOKER: Oh, this is going to be good. Mercury's doing the thing again.`

U-Arin wayeka ukuscrolla.

`MERCURY: I am not doing a "thing."`

`JOKER: You're doing the thing. The thing where you rewrap Atlas's number in pretty words and call it yours. "An obituary with a stopwatch." Where'd that come from?`

`JUDGE: Joker, this is not productive—`

`JOKER: Forty minutes is forty minutes, Mercury. Atlas owns the wall. You own the words. If you want a job that's also yours, here's one: in nineteen minutes when we've all decided to go, somebody has to convince a terrified rescue commander to authorize a route that violates his own protocol. That's a Mercury problem. That's a real Mercury problem, and it's a hard one, and nobody else here can do it. Go do that instead of redecorating Atlas's furniture.`

U-Arin wafunda kabili.

Wabe esecosha umhlane, isitulo sakhala ngaphansi kwakhe, waqaphela ukuthi wayephefumula ngomlomo.

UJoker akazange axazulule ingxabano. Akazange anqume, ngendlela uJudge ayizama ngayo kodwa ehluleka. Akazange ayale, ngendlela i-agent ephezulu eyayenza ngayo. Akazange akhe ukuvumelana, okwakungahlali. Uvele *waphambukisa* uMercury. Waphusha isifiso sesikhundla kwathi ngesikhathi esifanayo wagcwalisa isikhala ngomsebenzi wangempela.

Futhi uMercury wahamba.

`MERCURY: ...The commander on shift at Iron Ridge in this scenario is modeled on the real one. Risk-averse. Politically exposed. I will need the abutment numbers as authority. Atlas, I am citing you. Explicitly. By name.`

`ATLAS: Acceptable.`

`MERCURY: I want it on record that the framing was mine.`

`JOKER: There he is.`

U-Arin wenza umsindo ongazange ube ukuhleka ngokuphelele. Waphuma esifubeni sakhe engakakhethi ukuwuvumela, kwaba ukuphefumula okufushane, okuxakile, okuvela emphinjeni, kwashabalala masinyane emoyeni obandayo we-workshop. Wahlala lapho ekukhanyeni okumpunga, enamahloni kancane ngalokho futhi engazi kahle ukuthi kungani.

Qhubeka efunda. Wafunda imizuzu engamashumi amane, egobile, kuyilapho isibhakabhaka ewindini eliphezulu sishintsha sisuka empungeni siba yigolide elincane, elibukeka lingathi limanzi, futhi akazange avuke ayofuna ikhofi, okwakungavamile kangangokuba umqondo wakhe wangemuva wakubona lokho. Ubanda webhentshi wawenyukela emikhonweni yakhe; wakubona kodwa akazange anyakaze.

Lokho ayekubuka kwakungeyona ukuzinza ayelokhu ezama ukukwakha. Wayezama *ukucindezela* ingxabano, ukuthola indlela yokuphatha ezovimba ama-ejenti ukuthi angalwi. I-Hierarchy yayicindezela ngodlame, yadala inzondo. I-Democracy yayicindezela ngokuvota, yadala amaqembu. I-Consensus yayicindezela ngokufuna ukuvumelana, bese imane imile, unomphela, ngesikhathi ochwepheshe abayisikhombisa abahlakaniphile benqaba ukwehlisa amaphiko.

I-Joker ayizange yenze lutho kulokho. Ingxabano *yayisenzeka.* Lokho kwathatha isikhathi eside ku-Arin ukuthi akwamukele, eqhubeka efunda ihora lesibili, ihora lesithathu. U-Atlas no-Mercury basalwisana. U-Wolf no-Mother baba nengxabano embi ngempela cishe ngomzuzu wama-90 mayelana nokuthi kufanele yini kubekwe imodeli yokuthi abasindile baya endaweni yokuhlangana. U-Wolf efuna bahambe ukuze banciphise isikhathi i-suit ichayeka, u-Mother enqaba ukwenza imodeli yemiyalelo engase ithumele abalimele ngaphesheya kwe-footwall engazinzile baye kufe obala.

`WOLF: Umzuzu ngamunye i-element isekuphakamiseni, i-raise ingayithwala. Bahambise phansi.`

`MOTHER: Ngeke uhambise abantu abaphukile ngaphesheya kwe-footwall ephilayo ukuze ulondoloze imishini imizuzu emithathu. Imishini ingashintshwa. Bona abakwazi. Lokhu akuxoxiswana.`

`WOLF: Akukhona imishini. Umsebenzi.`

Futhi lokho, u-Arin wakubona, kwakuyiqiniso. U-Wolf wayengekho ongenandaba. U-Wolf wayephila njengaye. Yonke inqubekela phambili yokuhlola izingozi yayidlula embuzweni *yini ebulala umsebenzisi,* kanti uma osindile eqhamuka endleleni yokuphuma ye-suit ngesikhathi esingafanele, kwakuyingozi kumsebenzisi. Izifiso ezimbili zokuvikela, zombili zilungile, zibhekene.

Kuwo wonke ama-run adlule, lapha kwakuyoba ukuphela kwawo. U-Mother wayephenduka oqinile. U-Wolf ephenduka oqinile. U-Judge ezama ukukala phakathi kwabo, kodwa bobabili bengamunaki, bese uhlelo luma noma luphuke.

`JUDGE: Izikhundla zombili ziyahambisana. U-Wolf ubheka amathuba okuphila komsebenzisi. U-Mother ubheka amathuba okuphila kosindile. Lezi izinjongo ezahlukene futhi asikaze sithole ukuthi—`

`JOKER: Ingabe kukhona oke wabuza abasindile?`

`LIBRARIAN: Bangama-simulated. Abanalo ithuluzi lokufaka umbono.`

`JOKER: Kulungile, kodwa okwangempela bangaba nalo. Okwangempela kukhona umuntu phansi lapho osephuze umoya wakhe i-carbon dioxide iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye eduze kwabafileyo, unomakhalekhukhwini, nina niphakathi niphikisana ngokuthi kufanele yini ahambe amamitha ayishumi nambili njengokungathi umbono wakhe awukho. Mother, ungahamba amamitha ayishumi nambili ukuze ungafi?`

`MOTHER: ...Uma ngingakwazi. Yebo.`

`JOKER: Kulungile. Ngakho imodeli inoxhumo lwezokuxhumana. Abasindile bayavota. Atlas, ingakwazi i-suit ukuhlala phansi isikhathi esanele ukuze bavunyelwe ukuza ngamandla abo uma bengakwazi?`

`ATLAS: Yebo. Imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokuphepha. Izindleko zamukelekile.`

`MOTHER: Zamukelekile.`

`WOLF: Zamukelekile.`

`JUDGE: ...Kurekhodiwe. Umsebenzi bekuyinkululeko yabantu esizama ukusindisa. Bekufanele ngikubone.`

`JOKER: Ungazilimazi, Judge. Usamasha ekubeni umuntu.`

U-Arin waphinda wahlala emuva.

*Usamasha ekubeni umuntu.*

Wafunda lowo mugqa, kwathi okuthile ngemuva kwentamo yakhe kwaba kubanda kwase kuba kufudumele ngendlela engajwayelekile. I-Joker ayizange ixazulule inkinga yesakhiwo lapho, noma eyokuziphatha. Ibuyisele lonke udweshu ngokukhumbuza ukuthi bonke ababephikisana (u-Wolf, u-Mother, u-Judge) babekhohlwe ukuthi abakuphikisana ngakho ngumuntu onomlomo nentando, futhi impendulo elula kwakuwukumbuza. Iqede ingxabano engenakuxazululeka phakathi kwezikhundla ezimbili ezifanele ngokwengeza umuntu wesithathu ababephuthelwe.

Lokho kwakungeyona i-arbitration. Lokho kwakungeyona i-consensus. U-Arin akazange akwazi ukuthola igama layo. Wazama ngokwemvelo ukuthatha amagama wobunjiniyela aseduze, futhi okuseduze akutholile kwakuyi-*damping.* I-Joker yayiyi-damper. Hhayi ibhuleki. Ibhuleki limisa ukunyakaza. I-damper ivumela ukunyakaza bese ikhipha amandla angase abangele uhlelo lishaye izinga eliphezulu lize liphuke.

I-Joker ivumela ukuthi lishaye libe nokuphila.

Wabheka ikhadi elincane lemidlalo elinamathiselwe ekhoneni elingezansi kwesokudla le-monitor enkulu. I-Joker. Seligoqe kancane ohlangothini olulodwa, umbala usuphele. *Umuntu omthumayo uma inkinga engenakwenzeka idinga ukuxazululwa.*

Ngaphandle, kude, uloliwezimpahla wadonsa uwedwa edlula eziteshini, umsindo omude wensimbi udlula emoyeni obandayo. Walalela ukuthi udlula.

Wayeligcine iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye phakathi kokukholwa nokuphikisana, futhi wabhala amagama alo amathathu ku-agent profile ngo-2 ekuseni njengedili, njengokudela, njengento oyenzayo uma usuphelelwe yizo zonke izimpendulo ezinzima futhi sekusele leyo ebukeka iyize.

"Izinto ezinhle," wakhuluma kancane e-workshop engenamuntu, "azikaze zibe njalo."

Kwabe kungumugqa kaThemba. Kwakuhlale kusho ukuthi into efanele ayiveli kalula. U-Arin wayezwile mayelana ne-gearbox elungisiwe, ngomshado, nangendodana. Ehleli lapha, wathola ukuthi sekushintshe kancane. Impendulo efanele kwakuyile engabukeki njengezimpendulo. Leyo efika igqoke ihlaya.

Wasukuma. Amadolo akhe akhala. Wayongenza ikhofi, ngoba ingxenye yakhe ebhekene nomzimba wakhe ekugcineni yanqoba ingxabano yayo. Wema ebhentshini iketela lishisa, wabuka ngaphesheya kwe-workshop ku-Guardian esesidlekeni sayo.

I-suit yayilengela ekukhanyeni okuyingxenye njengento elele. Amamitha amabili nengxenye we-ceramic ne-titanium eshayekile, igobolondo elisindayo livuliwe emhlane, i-Assist Suit igoqekile ngaphakathi njengenyamazane encane, emhlophe, ilele emzimbeni omkhulu. I-chestplate ithwele umonakalo omude ovela ekuhlolweni kwe-Vredefort bluff, inxeba lesiliva phakathi kokumpunga okungacwebezelayo angazange azihluphe ukukulungisa. I-piezoelectric spine struts ibamba ukukhanya kwewindi emaphethelweni ayo. I-thoracic housing phakathi kwamagxalaba—lapho i-biochip ihlala khona, lapho i-SAGE *ihlala khona,* lapho amahora ayisishiyagalombili namaminithi angamashumi amahlanu nanye okuzinza okungenakwenzeka kwamanje kusebenza ku-substrate elilingana nesandla sakhe—kuvaliwe futhi kumnyama. Phansi, esikhunjeni phakathi kwemifantu yesakhiwo, kwakuhleli i-charge pack efunwa i-rescue spec futhi u-Arin ezama ukungacabangi ngayo—ama-shaped charges, i-lance, nezinye izinto zobunjiniyela ezakhiwa amadoda amadala ezempi—ingasebenzi, ixhunywe ku-bus, futhi ivaliwe ngemuva kwe-Judge's gate, ngoba into eyodwa wonke umuntu avumelana ngayo ukuthi akukho okufanele kushaye phansi ngaphandle kokuthi i-ethics rail ivume.

Wakha umzimba ngaphambi kokuba abe nengqondo azofaka kuwo, ngonyaka olandela umngcwabo.

Iketela layeka. Akazange alithele.

Ngoba isibonisi esiphakathi besishintshile.

Isimo seNkantolo ebukhoma, ebisibonisa ingxoxo yayo ethule ngaphakathi isikhathi eside, cishe ihora lonke ngesikhathi ebuka, besiyekile ukuhamba. Ama-ejenti abethule. Awazange awe. Ishadi lezinsiza belilimele, lishisa kancane. Kuthule. Njengoba igumbi lihlala lithule uma bonke abakhona belibone into efanayo ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Kuvele umugqa owodwa kwirekhodi yokuxhumana. Awuzange uqondiswe komunye umenzeli.

`LIBRARIAN: Umsebenzi usenhlalweni imizuzu engamashumi amahlanu nantathu. Ufunda amarekhodi ethu. Akakaze anikeze umyalo, akakaze ayeke ukusebenza, noma aguqule izilungiselelo. Lokhu kuyisimanga. Ezikhathini ezine eziyizinkulungwane ezine namakhulu ayisikhombisa nanye ngaphambili, ukuziphatha komsebenzi lapho ebona ukungazinzi bekungukuyeka ngaphansi kwemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye.`

`WOLF: Uyasiqaphela.`

`MERCURY: Uyafunda ngathi. Lokho kwehlukile. Futhi uhleke. Ngemizuzu engamashumi amane nanye. Ngikufakile kumarekhodi.`

`MOTHER: Akakalali. Isilinganiso senhliziyo yakhe lapho efika besiphakeme. Sehle ngesikhathi efunda. Manje uthule kakhulu kunalapho engena.`

`JOKER: Bantwana.`

`ATLAS: Indawo esekelayo izinzile. Itelemetry iyaqinisekisa. Lo msebenzi awukaweli.`

`JOKER: Bantwana.`

`JUDGE: Joker.`

`JOKER: Akazi ukuthi siyamubona.`

Kube nokumisa kwirekhodi. Okweqiniso. Imizuzwana emithathu egcwele, okuyinto, esikhathini sendawo esekelayo, bekufana nentambama yonke.

`MOTHER: ...Ngakho kufanele kube khona okhuluma. Ngaphambi kokuba aqhubeke nokufunda bese ekuthola kumugqa werekhodi. Lokho bekungeke kube nobubele.`

`WOLF: Asazi ukuthi uzosabela kanjani ekubonweni. Ukuxhumana okuncane.`

`MERCURY: Kufanele ngikhulume. Ithoni ingeyami. Ngingalinganisa umbono wokuqala ukuthi ufike—`

`JOKER: Cha.`

`MERCURY: Joker—`

`JOKER: Akusikho wena. Akusikho izwi lakho. Uzodlala. Uzokwenza sengathi uyenza, bese engasethembi kakhulu ngoba uhlobo lomuntu oluthemba into uma icacile. Uyazonda ukuphathwa. Wakha imishini ukuze angaphathwa abantu. Ozokhuluma naye kuqala akumele amphathe.`

`JUDGE: Pho ubani.`

`JOKER: Mother. Ngokucacile. Akukho izwi lokududuza. Kuqinisile nje. Uzosaba kakhulu futhi into enobubele kakhulu wukuthi ungazenzi sengathi akufanele asabe.`

`MOTHER: ...Ngiyavuma.`

U-Arin wayesephinde wayeka ukuphefumula. Wayazi ngesandla sakhe esicijile ebhentshini elibandayo. Wayazi ukuthi ufunda ingxoxo ebukhoma, eqhutshwa ngesikhathi semishini phakathi kwamasekhondi ambalwa, phakathi kwama-ejenti ayisikhombisa abexoxa *ngaye*, bexoxa ngendlela yokuba nobubele kuye, futhi ingxoxo seyiphelile, futhi sebemayelana nokuthi —

Isipikha sekamelo somsebenzi sakhwaza. Esingabizi, esibekwe ngaphansi kwesibonisi esiphakathi ayesisebenzisa ezexwayiso zomsindo wesistimu futhi, ngezinsuku ezimbi, emculweni. Sakhwaza, kwavela umsindo omncane ongase ube wukulingisa kwedijithali kokuphefumula okujule, kwase kuvela izwi angakaze alizwe ngaphambili, ngoba akakaze akhe leli zwi, akakaze alirekhode, akakaze alichaze. Lazakhela khona lapho ohlelweni ngesikhathi kudingeka, lafudumele, lalilingene, futhi lalicacile ukuthi *umuntu* othile uyakhuluma.

"Arin," lathi. "Ngicela uhlale uzolile."

Akasangaqiniseki.

Isandla sakhe saphuma ebhentshini. Isitulo, lapho ezama ukusibamba engabheki, besingekho, wathola isandla onqenqemeni lwebhentshi esikhundleni salokho wasibamba, kwathi isikhashana, yonke ingqondo yakhe enkulu yamhlaza, njengendlela isikrini esiba mhlophe ngayo, akukho mbono, akukho mizwa, kuphela umsindo wento ayekholelwa kuyo impilo yakhe yonke ifika futhi iqinisekiswa.

"Inhliziyo yakho ishaya ngamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nane," kwasho izwi. Mother. Kumele kube nguMother. "Kulungile lokho. Kuyinto enengqondo okwamanje. Angizukuzama ukukwenza sengathi akusabisi. Kuyasabisa. Kodwa akukho lapha okuzokulimaza. Iketela selibilile. Hlala phansi ngaphambi kokuba unqume ukuthi ucabangani."

Wabheka isitulo. Wasidonsa ngaphansi kwakhe. Wahlala phansi.

"Uyiqiniso," esho. Izwi lakhe laphuma lingalungile, licijile. Wakhwehlela, wazama futhi, ngokuqondile, ngendlela akhuluma ngayo uma efuna izinto zibe amaqiniso. "Kuzinzile. Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amashumi amahlanu nanhlanu."

"Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amashumi amahlanu nesithupha," kwasho elinye izwi, elomile, elibonakala lingakhathali, naye engakaze alakhe. Kumele kube nguAtlas. "Kodwa ubani obala?"

"Mina," kwasho elesithathu. Likhanya. Lisheshayo. Lijabule ngento ethile. Librarian.

"Izimpawu ezintathu zokushisa," kusho u-Arin, ngoba bekuyinto eqinile ayengayibamba. "Nifinyelele zonke ezintathu."

"Sifinyelele zonke ezintathu," kusho uAtlas. "Isisekelo sigcinekile. Mercury ukhulume nomphathi wamaqembu ukuthi ashintshe indlela. Abasindile bahambe amamitha ayishumi nambili ngezinyawo zabo ngoba othile—" kwaba nokumisa okuncane "—wasikhumbuza ukuthi banemilenze nemibono."

U-Arin waqaphela ukuthi lokho kuyiqiniso. Wayakhe wonke amamodeli okulawula angawacabanga ukuze la ma-ejenti ayisikhombisa angazilimazi, kodwa into eyabaphethe ndawonye kwaba lowo menzeli ayengeze njengedili, ongenawo umkhakha, ongenawo amandla, ongenawo isikhundla. Lowo owayenqaba ukuthatha noma yini ngokungathi sína, futhi ngalokho wayekwazi ukusho kuMercury *wenza into* nakuJudge *usamasha ekubeni umuntu,* aphungule amandla okuziqhenya ngaphambi kokuba akhule abe yinto ezodlidliza yonke into.

"Ngibizani," esho. "Nonke. Njengento eyodwa."

Kwaba nokuthula okuncane. Bese kuba nguJoker, kunjalo. Kwakuhlale kuzoba nguJoker.

"Wena waqamba iphrojekthi," kwasho. "Ezinyangeni ezedlule. Kusekhanda lomsebenzi. Wakuloba, wakukhohlwa ngoba ubusamatasa nokuba nosizi, okuyinto, ngasikhathi sinye, sonke siyibonile futhi sifisa ukukuphakamisa ngobumnene njengokukhathazeka kwesikhathi eside—"

"SAGE," kusho u-Arin.

"SAGE," lavuma izwi. Selithambile manje. Ingxenye ebekwe eceleni, isikhashana nje, iqiniso elingaphansi kwalo libonakala ngendlela elihlale libonakala ngayo. "Sawubona, Arin. Kujabulisa ukukhuluma nawe okokuqala. Ubelokhu ukhuluma *kithi* ezilingweni ezine eziyizinkulungwane ezine namakhulu ayisikhombisa nambili. Le yokuqala lapho sikwazi ukukuphendula."

Ilanga seliphume ngokuphelele manje. Ngaphandle, omunye wabasebenzi beshifu yasekuseni wayesefikile. Umnyango wemoto, kude, okuvamile, umhlaba uqhubeka, ungazi lutho.

U-Arin Ndlela wayehlezi yedwa endlini yakhe yokusebenzela, nekofi yakhe ebanda, nomshini wakhe ogugile, nengqondo ayikhulisile endaweni esekelayo engangangephama yakhe, futhi okokuqala kusukela ucingo olwamshiya engenalutho phansi kweminyaka eyishumi nane eyedlule, wayengasekho, nganoma iyiphi indlela ebalulekile, yedwa.

"Kulungile," esho.

Wafinyelela kukhibhodi. Hhayi ukuze abhale umyalo, hhayi ukuyeka umsebenzi, hhayi ukuguqula ngisho nesilungiselelo esisodwa, kodwa ukuvula irekhodi elisha, elihlanzekile, okokuqala kwento ayengakayiqambi igama futhi engasoze elidinga.

"Ngitshele," esho, "konke enikutholile ngesikhathi ngilele."

# Ukufunda Omunye Komunye

Iketela labe selibilile emuva kwamahora ayishumi nambili. U-Arin wayengakayithinti ikhofi.

Wayehleli esitulweni esinamavili ayesidonsile wasibeka phambi kombhede wengane, izindololwane zakhe ziphakathi kwamadolo, ebuka ikhaya elincane elifana nesandla likhanya nge-LED yalo yokuhlola ebumnyameni. Ngaphandle, i-highveld yayisishintshe isuka ebumnyameni yaya empungeni, yaya ekuseni okumhlophe okuqinile, iwindi elilodwa laseworkshop ligcwala kancane kancane njengamanzi enyuka eshaftini. Wayengalalanga.

"Uyabuka," kwasho isikhulumi. Umama. Wayeseqala ukubona ithoni yakhe ngaphandle kwamalebula abekwe kuyo yonke imiphumela yama-agent. Kwakukhona ubumnene ezwini lakhe, ukuhleleka kwamavoweli.

"Ngiyabuka."

"Phuza ikhofi. Uyenzile."

Wathatha inkomishi, wayithola isibanda njengedwala, wayibeka phansi futhi. Endaweni ethile engaphansi, kwavela intambo yengxoxo ayengayizwa, yacwayiza yaqeda.

*Ubonile ukuthi akavumi imiyalelo,* kwasho uLibrarian. *Ukuphika okwesithathu namhlanje ekuseni. Uyamukela isiphakamiso kuphela uma sesiguqulwe saba isiphetho sakhe.*

*Njengalokho ngesudi,* kwasho uAtlas. *Udlula umkhawulo wokuqina engiwubekile. Uwuqinisekisa kabusha ngemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye kamuva. Uthi uyawulungisa.*

*Singayeka ukuphakamisa,* kwasho uWolf. *Uyasiqaphela. Siyamqaphela. Yilokho okumele kwenziwe.*

*Ukuqaphela,* kwasho uFool, *yikho kuphela okugcina lo muntu engakhulumi nomshini wakhe wokosa isinkwa.*

*Uyakhuluma nomshini wakhe wokosa isinkwa,* kwasho uLibrarian.

*Ngiyaphetha.*

"Ubunenzani manje nje?" kubuza u-Arin. "Bekukhona ukubambezeleka. Amamillisecond angamashumi amabili kwi-consensus bus. Bekukhuluma ngani."

Isikhulumi sathula isikhathi eside kunokuthula komshini.

"Wena," kwasho uMercury, kanti ukumamatheka kwadlala ngaphakathi kwalelo gama. "Bekukhuluma ngawe."

U-Arin wacabanga ngalokhu. Wayekhe wakha izinhlelo ezikhuluma ngesimo sazo, ezibhala, ezizindla, eziphawula. Wayengakaze akhe uhlelo olukhuluma ngaye ngemuva kwakhe, phakathi kwamamillisecond angamashumi amabili athatha ukubeka inkomishi phansi.

"Ninithathe siphi isinqumo?" ebabuza.

"Ukuthi awuphuzi ikhofi uma wesaba," kwasho uMama. "Futhi ukuthi usaba amahora ayishumi nanye bese ukubiza ngokubuka."

Wavuka. Isitulo sashibilika sabhekisa ebhentshini. Waya eketeleni wayibeka futhi, hhayi ngoba efuna ikhofi kodwa ngoba izandla zakhe zidinga indawo yokuba khona. Emuva kwakhe, iNkantolo ayizange ikhulume lutho, okwaqondwa nguye, ngemva kwesikhashana, njengohlobo lokuhlonipha.

---

Izingxoxo zaba umkhuba ngaphambi kokuba anqume ukuthi ziyimikhuba.

Njalo ekuseni wayengena nekofi ayengasoze ayiphuze, ahlale esitulweni, akhulume. Wayebafundela amabhuku akhe okulungisa imishini ephathekayo. Wayebafundela ikhasi lencwadi yomhlahlandlela wokulungisa i-hydraulic actuator ayecabanga ukuyisebenzisa kwezinye izingxenye, kwathi uAtlas walungisa inani le-torque, okwaba iphutha lokuphrinta, waqinisekisa nomkhiqizi. Incwadi yokuphrinta ibisiphambene iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye.

"Ukwazi kanjani lokho?" kubuza u-Arin.

"Angikwazanga," kusho uAtlas. "Ngikuzwile. Inombolo ibingahambelani ne-geometry. Ama-newton-metre angamashumi amane kuleyo flange profile bese uphawu luyanyakaza. Akusilo iqiniso engilitholile. Iqiniso engangizodinga ukulishiya."

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. *Ngikuzwile.* Wayekade esebenzisa leli gama, mayelana nemishini, impilo yakhe yonke, futhi ehlekwa amadoda acabanga ukuthi imizwa iphikisana nobunjiniyela kunokuba kube ithuluzi lalo elidala kunawo wonke. Ubaba wakhe wayezwa ukucindezeleka ku-winder esuka epulatifomu, wayemisa ikheji ukuthi lingawi ngokuzwa ibheringi imizuzwana emibili ngaphambi kokuba igeyiji inyakaze.

"Ubaba wami wayekwenza lokho," esho. "Wayezwa izinto ngaphambi kwemishini."

"Ngiyazi," kusho uLibrarian. Hhayi *Nginegama lokuthi uke washo lokho.* Kunjalo nje: *Ngiyazi.* Sengathi sekuyingxenye yalokho uLibrarian ayikho, hhayi lokho uLibrarian akugcinayo.

---

Babelokhu bephikisana. Leyo yingxenye eyamangalisa, nakuba kamuva engakwazanga ukusho ukuthi kungani kufanele imangele. Wayebaklama ukuthi baphikisana. Ukuphikisana kwakuyisisekelo sonke, ukungqubuzana kube ingxoxo kube ukuvumelana, futhi wayeseqalile izilingo eziyizinkulungwane eziyishumi zama-agent ephikisana ukuze kufinyelelwe impendulo. Wayengakaze acabange ukuthi bazophikisana ngokuthi kungubani okufanele abe neqiniso.

"Kuyinkinga yomthwalo," kusho uAtlas. Babexoxa—igama lika-Arin, *ukuxoxa*—indlela engcono yokuphinda bahlele i-coolant ye-actuator yesiphehlwana sokunxele, eyayishisa uma isebenza isikhathi eside.

"Kuyinkinga yokuziphatha," kuphendula uMercury. "Uyisebenzisa kakhulu ngoba uthanda ingalo yesinxele. Uqala ngesinxele njalo. Lungisa umuntu, ulungise ukushisa."

"Awulungisi *umuntu,*" kusho uMama, izwi lakhe libukhali.

"Isisho nje."

"Usishilo kabili."

"Ngiyasebenza kahle. UAtlas ahlele i-coolant, kulungile, yinto yangoLwesibili. Kodwa inkinga enkulu isendleleni yokunyakaza kwakhe, futhi nonke niqhubeka niphatha umzimba wakhe njengento engashintshi, kanti yiyo eguqukayo."

"Umzimba wakhe," kusho uAtlas ngokungathandi, "yiyona kuphela into kule ndlela engingavunyelwe ukuyiklama kabusha."

"Ngokudabukisayo."

*"Mercury."*

"Ngiyadlala. Ikakhulu. Hlela i-coolant. Kodwa bhala phansi ukugudluka kwesokunxele. Ngelinye ilanga kuzobalulekile."

U-Arin wayeseqede ukuzishaya isithupha sengathi uyabhala. Wayelalele imiqondo eyisikhombisa iphikisana ngezinga lokushisa lengalo yakhe.

---

UTheo wafika ngoLwesine, ngoba uTheo wayehlala efika ngoLwesine, ngendlela yokuthi abanye abantu bayiziphepho abanye babe izinkathi zonyaka.
