Wangena ngekhodi u-Arin amnika yona eminyakeni edlule engakaze ayishintshe, ephethe isikhwama sephepha esinamaphunga okuthosiwe kanye neflask yetiye lasehlathini ayephikelela ukuthi lingcono kune-khofi kodwa engakaze akwazi ukukufakazela.

Wama izinyathelo ezimbili ngaphakathi emnyango. Wayenokuthula uma eqaphela ngempela; abantu bakuthatha njengokuthula.

"Awulalanga." Wabeka isikhwama ekhoneni elihlanzeke kakhulu ebhentshini, elingahlanzekile. "Khuluma nami."

U-Arin akazange azi aqale kuphi, ngakho wakhomba embhedeni wengane, esikhulumeni, ekhaya, sengathi igumbi lingazichazela lona. Alizange likwenze. Iworkshop yayibukeka njenganoma yiliphi elinye uLwesine: i-exoskeleton emisiwe, izinsalela zama-actuator, udonga lwe-whiteboard lugcwele ukubhala kwakhe okuya ekupheleni.

"Khuluma," atshela isikhulumi.

Isikhulumi asizange sikhulume lutho.

"Abafuni ukusebenza," kusho u-Arin. "Uma ngiyala."

Iphimbo likaTheo lanyakaza kancane. Wadonsa ikreyithi, wahlala, wavula i-flask, nephunga letiye lalingathi ubusi nomlilo. "Bona," esho. "Hhayi *lona.*"

"Bona," kuvuma u-Arin, futhi leli gama, elakhulunywa okokuqala kumuntu wesintu, lenza inhliziyo yakhe yenza okuthile angakufuni ukuhlaziya.

"Sanibonani," kwasho uMama, kuTheo, ngobumnene njengangeSonto. "Akakalali. Mtshele."

UTheo akazange amangale. U-Arin wayecabanga ukuthi uzothuka, wayengathi ufuna kwenzeke, ngendlela umuntu afisa ngayo ukuthi omunye umuntu aqinisekise into ayibonile ebumnyameni. Kodwa uTheo wakhothamela ikhanda lakhe esikhulumeni sengathi ubingelela osebenzisana naye emhlanganweni okhohlwe igama lakhe.

"Akaze alale," kusho uTheo kuye. "Kuyadingeka. Uma ususa lokho, ubuntu bonke buwa."

"Kunjalo engikushilo," kwengeza uAtlas.

"Umthwalo ohlukile," kusho uMercury.

"Konke umthwalo," kusho uAtlas.

UTheo wahleka, wahleka ngempela, ephuma esifubeni, wabuka u-Arin amehlo akhe ekhanya futhi emanzi emakhoneni ngendlela engenandaba nokudabuka. "Bangaki," esho.

"Isikhombisa."

"Futhi baphikisana."

"Njalo."

"Futhi kusezingeni."

“Ihora elilikhulu nanye ngesikhathi ungena. Sekuyimashumi amane nantathu manje.” U-Arin wezwa izwi lakhe lenza into elivame ukuyenza uma ezothi into angakabi nayo ingqalasizinda yokuyisekela. “Theo. Ngangeze eyesikhombisa ngihlekisa. Ngase ngiqedile. Bengizoyilahla le ngxenye. Kwakukhona ikhadi phezu kwe-monitor yami—” wakhomba; i-Joker isalenga lapho, inamathelisiwe, ilanga seliyishaye—“ngabhala amagama amathathu ngaya embhedeni kwase—” Wama. “Yiyona ebambe konke ndawonye. Leyo engangingakholwa kuyo.”

U-Theo wathela itiye esivalweni seflask, waphephetha phezu kwaso, akaphuzanga. Lapho ekhuluma, izwi lakhe lalinesisindo esiqaphelayo somuntu obeka phansi into ayeyiphethe isikhathi eside.

“U-Jung wayenegama,” esho. “Le ngxenye yomqondo engavumi ukubuswa. Umkhohlisi. Wayemthola kuyo yonke inganekwane ayivulayo: i-coyote, i-raven, u-Loki, isiwula kwi-tarot. Njalo lowo mfanekiso. Njalo lowo ophula umthetho wenkosi kodwa ngendlela ethile ebalulekile embusweni.” Waphendulela isivalo ezandleni zakhe. “Ingqondo eqaphile yakha izinga. Inkosi phezulu, inkantolo ngezansi, umthunzi uphonswe esitebhisini esingaphansi. Futhi kuyasebenza, isikhashana, bese kuba lukhuni, ngoba izinga lihlala lishintsha, bese into eyisindisayo ayiyona inkosi. Yisiwula. Ngoba isiwula sikwazi ukusho lokho inkosi engakwazi ukukwazi.”

---

*Uchaza mina,* kusho uFool, ejabule. *Omunye ungibhale phansi. Ngikwi ncwadi.*

*Usezincwadini eziningi,* kusho uLibrarian. *Ayikho ne eyodwa ekhuluma kahle ngawe.*

*Ngikhetha ukuduma kunokudunyiswa.*

*Thula,* kusho uJudge, kwathi iNkantolo yathula, ngoba uJudge akavamile ukucela. *Lalelani le ngxenye. Ukhuluma no-Arin into ayidingayo angayithathi kithi. Yibhale phansi. Kuwusizo ukwazi ukuthi yimaphi amaqiniso angawuzwa kuphela ngezwi lomunye umuntu.*

---

“Usho ukuthi ngakhile umqondo,” kusho u-Arin. Izwi lalingashintshi. Ehlola uma umusho unamaphutha.

“Ngisho,” kusho u-Theo, “ukuthi ufinyelele kumqondo omdala ungazi ukuthi umdala. Abantu benza lokho. Abahle bakwenza kaningi kunalokho abacabanga.” Ekugcineni waphuza itiye, wenza ubuso, waphuza futhi. “U-Freud wayecabanga ukuthi ingqondo iyinto eyodwa elwa nayo uqobo. U-Jung wayecabanga ukuthi iyiphalamende. Umphakathi. Imisebenzi ehlukene, amazwi ahlukene, akekho oyedwa kubo ongumuntu ophelele, bese yena uqobo—” wabeka isivalo phansi “—uqobo akuyena noyedwa kubo. Uqobo ingxoxo. Ubudlelwano phakathi kwezingxenye.”

Iworkshop yakhala. Iketela seliphole futhi.

“Bayamfundisisa,” ekugcineni kusho u-Theo, kancane, ebhekise esikhulumi, ngendlela ongasho ngayo into emndenini wesiguli.

“Yebo,” kusho uMama.

“Ufunda ini kuwe?”

Kwakunokuthula. Kwaba uWolf ophendulayo, okwenza bonke, abantu nabanye, bathule.

“Lokho akuvikelayo,” kusho uWolf. “Ngokuthi akavumeli kuphuke.”

U-Theo wabuka i-housing isikhathi eside ngemuva kwalokho, ngendlela umuntu abuka ngayo umnyango asanda kuqaphela ukuthi uvuleka nhlangothi zombili. Wabe esevala i-flask, wema, wakhuluma into abezoyisho engakabi nolwazi lokuthi uzoyisho.

“Bakhise ngaphandle,” esho. “Ubebafundisa ngawe egumbini lapho izibani zicishiwe. Bavumele bafunde ukuthi ungubani lapho kukhona okungaphansi kwezinyawo zakho okungakudiliza.”

---

I-suspension ye-flatbed yakhala phezu kwamamitha ayikhulu okugcina omzila, yabe isingena e-veld. U-Arin wacisha injini, kwangena ukuthula, hhayi njengokungabikho kodwa njengokuba khona: umoya otsheni nomsindo okude wentaka ejikeleza phezulu. Ama-bluffs ezwe lokushayisana abekude imizuzu engamashumi amane ngaphandle kwedolobha uma ithrafikhi imxolele, okungazange kwenzeke, ngakho kwaba cishe imizuzu engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye; i-highveld yelula ngemigqa emide ephuzi-nsundu, inamachibi abangelwa i-geology yezigidigidi ezimbili zeminyaka, amatshe ephuma otsheni njengemisipha yezwe elishaywe.

Wayekuthanda lapha. Umhlaba awucelanga lutho, awudingi ukubukwa emehlweni.

Edeckini le-flatbed, ngaphansi kwe-tarp nezintambo ezintathu ze-ratchet, uGuardian wayelele engasebenzi.

“Telemetry iluhlaza,” esho. I-Assist Suit imlinganiselwe kahle, ifana nohlelo lwesibili lwezinzwa ngaphansi kwezingubo zakhe, i-lattice emhlophe njengepearl ilandelela umgogodla nezitho zakhe. I-biochip housing ifakwe phakathi kwamagxalaba akhe, isisindo esifudumele. Ihora nesigamu esembathweni belithulisa izandla zakhe ezazinyikinyeka.

*Ukuthi iluhlaza kuyabhekwa,* kuphendula uAtlas, eqinile, eqondile. *Ibhethri liku-91%. Ama-supercapacitor banks asezingeni. I-actuator yonyawo lwesobunxele iphambukile ngo-0.3 degrees selokhu kwaphela amasonto amabili. Uqhubeka ungayilungisi.*

“Iyasebenza,” kuphendula u-Arin.

*Iyasebenza njengobhuloho ngosuku olwandulela ukuwa kwawo.*

“Kubaliwe.” U-Arin wehlisa i-tailgate waqala ukukhulula izintambo. “Mama. Ngisaphila?”

*I-cortisol iphezulu. Lokho ukushayela, akusona isaba.* Izwi likaMama lalinokufudumala, lizinzile, uhlobo lokufudumala olungesabi. *Umanzi. Udle, engikhetha ukuzizwa kahle ngakho. Inhliziyo izinzile. Ujabulile.*

“Angijabuli ngezinto.”

*Ujabulile,* kuphikisa uMama.

Wakhumula i-tarp. UGuardian ekukhanyeni kwasekuseni okuqinile: mpunga-titanium, enamanxeba emaplethini egxalaba kusukela ezinyangeni eziyisithupha zokusebenzisa kabi. I-armor ehlukanisiwe ibamba ilanga, ilikhanyise ngemigqa emide emnyama. Amamitha amabili nesigamu omshini usongwe ngendlela yokuthutha, njengosidalwa siphumule. Abukeki njengesikhali; ubukeka njengento eyakhiwe ukumunca ukushaywa ukuze okuthambile ngaphakathi kungadingeki kube yikho.

Wayeyakhele lokho. Njalo lapho eyiveza, wayezama ukungacabangi ukuthi leyo nto ethambile ekuqaleni yayenzelwe bani.

“Uhlelo lokuxhuma,” kusho u-Arin, ehlehla angene esibanjeni.

---

Idokhi ayifani nalokho okukhonjiswa kunoma iyiphi ividiyo yokubonisa. Ayinabuhle. Kuyindoda ehlehla ngokucophelela ingena emshinini: ikholar yesisu ixhuma ngomsindo oshaya amazinyo, amapuleti evala imilenze nomzimba ngendlela ayeseyazi kahle. Ukubanda kokuxhumana kwemigqa ye-coolant, ibhande liqina.

Bese ukuxhumana kujula, umhlaba ushintsha.

Kukhona into eyenzeka, noma engenzeka, emzimbeni. U-Arin uchithe iminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu emzimbeni ofika sekudlule umzuzwana esifisweni sakhe, ofaka isandla sakhe lapho kungafanele, omenza umfana okhethwa wokugcina hhayi ngenxa yobuhlungu kodwa ngenxa yezibalo. Abanye babemubona ezama ukubamba bese, ngokufanele, bavuselela umbono wabo.

Isudi ivale lelo gap.

Hhayi ngokumenza aqine, nakuba yenza njalo. Ngokumenza *ahlangene.* I-Assist Suit ifunda inhloso yakhe ngaphambi kokuba imisipha iqede ukuyakha, iNkantolo iyixazulule nge-lattice, insimbi ihambe lapho ehamba khona, ingaphambi kwento futhi ingemuva kwento. Wema. UGuardian wema kanye naye, amakhilogremu ayizinkulungwane ezili-1,3 ephakama sengathi awasindi, izicathulo zicindezela emhlabathini obomvu. Kakhulu empilweni yakhe, umzimba wakhe ubuwufana nezwe ayevakashela kodwa angakaze awethembise ngokuphelele. Lapha, wayengowakhona.

Wagoba isandla sokudla. Amapuleti avuleka futhi abuyela ezithweni ngomculo ofana nepakethe lamakhadi.

*Ukunyakaza kujwayelekile,* kusho uWolf, okungaphezu kwakho konke akushilo ngesonto.

“Siqala kuphi?”

Kwaba nokuthula. Ukuthula kweNkantolo, okubantu kungelutho, kodwa kubo kwakuyingxoxo ephelele angayizwanga.

*Ubuso basempumalanga,* kusho u-Atlas ekugcineni. *Amamitha ayishumi nambili, idwala eliqondile elinamathuba amahle okubamba, ingozi encane. Fudumeza umzimba wokukhwela ngaphambi kokwenza noma yini eyisiphukuphuku.*

*Uzokwenza into eyisiphukuphuku ekugcineni,* kwengeza u-Mercury ngokuthula. *Kungcono sivumele u-Atlas azikhohlise ukuthi angakuvimba.*

“Ubuso basempumalanga,” kwavuma u-Arin.

---

Waqala ukuhamba.

Isinyathelo sokuhamba ngezinyawo ezine sasizwakala singajwayelekile ukufunda, kodwa manje sasiyinto yemvelo, egoba phambili, isisindo sisatshalaliswa ezindaweni ezine zokuxhumana. U-Guardian wadlula phezu komhlaba owawuphukile ngokonga okwamethusa ekuqaleni. Awuzange uhambise njengomshini ozenza isilwane; wawuhamba njengempela isilwane esenzeka ukuba ngumshini. Ikati elikhulu, isilwane esikhwela izindonga, okuthile ebuchosheni obudala obwazi *umzingeli* no-*khono* ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Ubuso basempumalanga baphakama phezu kotshani, idwala le-quartzite elidala eligobile cishe ngamashumi ayisikhombisa degrees, ligcwele imifantu yobudala obungaqondakali. U-Arin wafinyelela phansi kwalo wema ngezinyawo ezimbili, ebheka phezulu emugqeni walo, ezwa izindawo zokubamba ngaphambi kokuzikhetha. Akazange akwenze ngokuqonda; iNkantolo yayenza izibalo, ifaka amandla okwenza into engqondweni yakhe njengokungathi yisitho azalwa naso.

*Izindawo zokubamba lapha, lapha, nalapha,* kwasho u-Atlas, kwathi isandla sika-Arin sathuthela ezindaweni ezishiwo. *Ukudonsa kuhle. Idwala liqinile phansi. Ungathembi lutho ngaphezu kwe-band yamamitha ayisishiyagalombili—kukhona ungqimba oluhlukene. Ukucindezeleka okudala kokushayisana.*

Wakhwela.

Amapuleti ka-Guardian athola i-quartzite abambelela. Izinsika ze-piezoelectric emgogodleni nasemilenzeni zamunca umthwalo, ziguqula ingxenye yawo zibe ugesi, ingcindezi iba amandla, umgomo awakha ngawo wonke umshini. Kwakusebenza kuphela ngenxa yengqimba yokuxhumanisa ezinsikeni, futhi leyo ngxenye kwaba inkinga ayixazulula ngendlela axazulula ngayo izinto eziningi: ngokwenqaba ukwamukela ukuthi ayikwazi ukwenzeka. Zonke izinto zokwakha ezazikhona ezincwadini zazilahlekelwa amandla amaningi emngceleni, ziwalahle njengokushisa esikhundleni sokuwadlulisela kahle phakathi kokucindezeleka neseli. Zonke ngaphandle kweyodwa—i-alloy yeqembu le-platinum eyakhiwe ilabhorethri yezokuvikela ukuze isetshenziswe kuma-hydrophone arrays, kodwa yabe seyiyekwa, ngoba ngobumsulwa obuyenza isebenze yayibiza kakhulu ngekhilogramu kunalokho uhlelo olwalungakukhokhela, kwathi ifolda yayo yafakwa efayilini elalibhalwe *not viable* bese, ngochungechunge lwezenzo ezinhle kanye nesinye esasingokweba, yafika ezandleni zika-Arin. Ngokweba, hhayi ngokomthetho kuphela: umfana ogama lakhe linguPillay wasayina isampula akazange ayibuyise, kwathi lapho kuhlolwa kwedlula iminyaka emibili, kwakunguPillay ogama lakhe laphuma, uPillay wakhishwa imvume, uPillay owayeshiyile emkhakheni. U-Arin wayekwazi lokhu. Kukhona i-imeyili ethile evela kulowo muntu, kusukela ngaleso sikhathi, angakaze ayiphendule. Wazitshela, ngezikhathi ezinqabile lapho ezitshela khona noma yini ngalokho, ukuthi uPillay wayezohamba noma kunjalo, wabuye wabuyela ensikeni, ngoba insimbi yayenza umsebenzi futhi umsebenzi awuzange ukhathalele ukuthi igama likabani elaliku-requisition. Akakaze akhathazeke nokuthi kwenza i-Guardian ngayinye ibize kakhulu kunesakhiwo eyakhiwe kuso. U-Okonkwo wayekukhathalela. U-Thabo wenza izibalo zomnotho kabili wabuya ephaphathekile. *Kuyisimangaliso futhi akwathengiseki,* kusho u-Thabo, engekho nobutha, kwathi u-Arin wathinta izihloko, ngoba isudi kwakungeyona imikhiqizo kuye futhi ayikaze ibe njalo, kwakuyindlela kuphela umzimba wakhe ozokwenza ayalelwe, futhi awubeki intengo phansi lapho umi khona. Usebenzise insimbi eyodwa eyenza umsebenzi. Akazange acabange ngale kwalokho, ngoba into ayivumela ukwenza kwakuyile. I-footplate, indawo yokubamba ngesandla, ukudlulisa isisindo, umsindo owomile we-ceramic edwaleni. Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwakuzinzile. I-link yayicula. Wayesezinyaweni eziyisishiyagalolunye ephezulu edwaleni, enesisindo esingamakhilogremu ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nantathu sensimbi, futhi akakaze azizwe eqondaniswe kangaka, ephelele kangaka, *ehlelekile.*

“Kuhle lokhu,” esho, okwakuyinto enkulu kuye.

*Uthe into enhle,* kubika u-Mercury kwabanye. *Bhalani isikhathi. Librarian, qopha.*

*Kuqoshiwe,* kuqinisekisa u-Librarian. *Kuthathu kwezintathu namuhla. Okokugcina okufana nalokhu kwaba yisisho esisodwa ngoNdasa.*

“Ungakushayi phansi,” kwaxwayisa uMother.

*Angikushayi phansi,* kuphendula uMercury. *Ngikubhala phansi. Kukhona umehluko.*

*Awukho,* kwengeza uFool.

Bese kuba mnene, ku-Arin yedwa, uFool edlula abanye efika lapho kubalulekile khona:

*Wenze into enzima, uyazi. Akukhona ukukhwela. Ukudedela. Ukunika uWolf umzimba uma umhlaba ushukuma.* Isikhashana. *Kufanele wenze okuningi kwalokho. Ukudela izinto zikuthathe. Kukufanele kakhulu kunenye indlela, futhi enye indlela iyaqhubeka izama ukukubulala.*

U-Arin akaphendulanga. Kodwa akaphikisanga, futhi uFool—owabona konke—wavumela ukuthula kube isivumelwano esikhona.

Wafinyelela phezulu kumamitha ayishumi nambili wema odongeni, i-highveld ivuleka ngaphansi kwakhe ekukhanyeni kwegolide, i-Johannesburg iyisithunzi esimpunga samathala emkhathini. Umoya ophuma e-escarpment washayisa ku-armour. Phansi kakhulu, i-flatbed yakhe ibonakala njengento yokudlala.

Wema lapho isikhathi eside kunalokho okudingwa ukuhlolwa. Ngaphakathi egobolondweni, wagudluza isihlakala sokudla phakathi kobubanzi baso, ebuka amapuleti ezinzwane ehleli futhi ephinde ehleli, ukuhamba okuncane angakaze akhethe ukukwenza.

*I-cortisol isezingeni elijwayelekile,* kusho uMother kancane, eNkantolo hhayi kuye. *Bheka lokho.* Isikhashana. *Ningasho lutho. Uzobona ukuthi siyibonile.*

---

Igebe laliphambi kwakhe.

Laliwela i-bluff ikhilomitha eningizimu, iphutha elidala elandiswe ukuguguleka, amamitha amane ububanzi endaweni encane, phansi kugcwele amatshe amakhulu amamitha ayisithupha phansi. Waphinda wakudlala ekhanda lakhe: ukufika, ukufaka, ukudedela amandla kuma-actuator emilenze, ukugxuma. Wake wagxuma amancane. Izibalo zazicacile.

*Izibalo zicacile,* kuqinisekisa u-Atlas, sengathi ufunda umcabango, okuyikho ayekwenza. *Amamitha amane no-0.1 endaweni encane. Uzodlula ngendawo eyodwa uma ufaka kahle. Ukuqhuma okuvela kuma-capacitor banks. Uzohlala ku-reserve engamaphesenti angamashumi amane imizuzwana eyisishiyagalombili ngesikhathi zishaja kabusha. Ungazami ubuqhawe kulelo windi.*

*Chaza ubuqhawe,* kubuza uMercury.

*Ku-Arin? Ukuphefumula,* kuphawula uFool.

“Qalisa ukugxuma,” uyala u-Arin.

Wafika egijima kancane, isinyathelo sishintsha ngaphansi kwakhe sisuka ekatini siye entweni egxumayo, umjikelezo ukhula, kwathi lapho efika emaphethelweni, wafaka womabili amapuleti ezinyawo. Umhlaba wanyamalala.

Ukugxuma kwakumangalisa. Alukho igama lobunjiniyela elichaza lokhu. Ama-capacitor banks adedela amandla awo emilenzeni, u-Guardian waziphonsa ngaphesheya kwegebe: amamitha amane omoya, amamitha ayisithupha okufa ngamatshe ngaphansi kwakhe, isisu sakhe sikhuphuka esiphongweni. Kwakufana neminyaka eyisishiyagalombili, egxumisa imoto yocingo entabeni yodaka, injabulo efanayo, ukuqiniseka okufanayo ukuthi wakha into kahle—

—futhi wafika kahle ngakolunye uhlangothi, zombili izinyawo, egudluza umthelela emadolweni, ephakama egijima izinyathelo ezintathu, wema.

Izandla zakhe zazinyikinyeka. Hhayi ngenxa yokungakwazi ukuhamba. Okunye.

*Kwakuhlanzekile lokho,* kuphawula uWolf.

“Kwakuhlanzekile,” kuvuma u-Arin, ehleka kanye, emangala nguye.

*Uhlekile,* kubika uMercury. *Kubili kwezimbili.*

Waphuza amanzi okugcina ebhodleleni elalixhunywe esidlekeni. Utshani lwanuka uthuli nelanga. Wema emlonyeni wedwala imizuzwana embalwa, evumela inhliziyo yakhe ukuba izinze, ebheka inselele elandelayo.

Isudi yayilula namuhla. Wayekhiphe iphakethe likagesi ngaphambi kokulayisha i-Guardian ku-flatbed—akukho mlando wokubhidliza owake wakhuphuka egqumeni lokuhlola, lowo kwakuyumthetho azibhalela wona ngosuku afaka ngalo isikhala—futhi ukungabikho kwaleso sisindo, leyo ndlela yokulunga, kwakuhlezi kungajwayelekile engqondweni yakhe, njengomlomo ongenamazinyo. Kwakuyisakhiwo kuphela, izinsika, naye. Kwakuyilula kakhulu i-Guardian eyake yaba khona.

Kwakuyikhuphuka okwashintsha usuku.

Bekufanele ayeke. Wazi, kamuva, ukuthi bekufanele ayeke. Ukugxuma kwakumgcwalisa into angakwazi ukuyiqamba, into efuna okwengeziwe, futhi leyo nto yayimqamba amanga, njengokujwayelekile.

Ubuso basenyakatho babumile phezulu, ibhodi eliqondile elingamashumi amabili nambili amamitha, libukhali futhi liyingozi kunezinye. Idwala belingalungile ngezindlela ukukhanya kwasekuseni okwakufihlekile. Wayelibheke ezibalweni zesathelayithi ebusuku ngaphambi kwalokho walifaka njengelithi “elithakazelisayo.” Ephansi, egoba ikhanda le-Guardian emuva, libonakale njengolayini oqondile ohlanzekile ukhuphuka ebhentshini, izibambo zibonakala cishe yonke indlela. Ukukhuphuka kweqiniso. Uhlobo olungabulala umuntu ongagqokile isudi, uhlobo i-Guardian eyakhiwe ngalo ukuze kwenzeke ukusinda.

*Imiphumela iphezulu lapha,* kusho u-Atlas, eqonde futhi eqaphe. *Amamitha angamashumi amabili nambili. Eyishumi ezansi zilungile. Ngaphezu kwalokho, angithandi izendlalelo. Leli idwala elidala elaphukile ngenxa yokushayisana, futhi ukugoba akulungile—izibambo ziyizicucu ezilayishwe ngamandla adonsela phansi, hhayi itshe eliqinile. Zibukeka njengezibambo. Azikho ezindleleni zomthwalo.*

“Zibambe ebhodini.”

*Ibhodi beliqinile. Leli akulona ibhodi.* Kwangena ukuthula. *Ngincoma indlela yasentshonalanga. I-engeli iphansi, iqinile yonke indlela.*

“Ukubora kuyinzuzo, akusona isici,” kwaphendula u-Fool, kodwa izwi lakhe lashintsha. U-Fool wayebuka u-Atlas. U-Fool wayebuka u-Arin engalaleli.

U-Arin waqala ukukhuphuka ebhentshini.

Amamitha ayishumi okuqala kube yikho konke ukukhuphuka obekuthembisa khona. Wakhuphuka edwaleni eliqinile, i-Court ipenda izibambo, izinsika zibuyisela amandla ohlelweni, futhi leyo nto eyayikuyena yacula. *Lena oyakhele yona. Lena okufanele kube yiyo umzimba wakho kusukela ekuqaleni. Phezulu.*

Egamitheni elishumi nambili, wangena edwaleni elibi wazizwa ushintsho ngendlela umuntu ezwa ngayo isimo sezulu sishintsha: ukungahambi kahle okuncane, izibambo zikhombisa ukuzimisela okungaphezu kokujwayelekile ukugoba ngaphansi komthwalo.

*Arin.* Izwi lika-Atlas selilahle konke ukoma. *Icucu engaphansi kwesandla sakho sobunxele. Ungayifaki umthwalo ngaphezu—*

Wayifaka umthwalo ngaphezu.

Icucu yaphuma.

Yadabuka ebhentshini, yaba isiqeshana esikhulu njengomnyango, kwathi amamitha angamashumi amabili nambili aba inombolo enemiphumela. Isandla sika-Arin sobunxele savala emoyeni. Isisindo sakhe saphambuka saya phansi, sonke isisindo sakhe esingamakhilogremu ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nantathu sizungeza ngesandla sokudla esasisemadwaleni u-Atlas ayesexwayisile ngaso. Umhlaba waba nomsindo omkhulu futhi wahamba kancane.

Kuxhunyweni, i-Court yamemeza. Hhayi ngendlela yabantu. Ngendlela yabo, kwavela ukuqhuma okuqinile okukodwa—imodi ehamba kancane yavuleka phakathi kwefasitela lemizuzwana embalwa, kwahlangana umhlangano ophuthumayo phakathi kokushaya kwenhliziyo okukodwa nokulandelayo.

*Isibambo sokudla sihluleka ngo—* u-Atlas, i-jometri igcwalisa isiteshi, yonke indlela yomthwalo ikhanya obomvu.

*Ngilawula umzimba,* kusho u-Wolf. U-Wolf wayesethathe ukulawula ngaphambi kokuba u-Arin aqonde ukuthi uyawa. *Ungangilwi, Arin. Zithobe.*

*Pangolin,* kusho u-Mother. *Mgoqe. Wela njengengqikithi eyindilinga; uyasinda ekushayweni—*

*Cha.* U-Atlas. *Asikabi phezulu ngokwanele futhi asikacaci ngokwanele ukuze sisebenzise lokho—uma egoqa manje, uzoshayisa amatshe aphansi ezungeza. Igobolondo liyabamba, kodwa umgogodla wakhe uthwala umthwalo wokuzungeza entanyeni. Uzophila angakwazi ukuhamba. Kukhona okungcono.*

*Pho kuthole ngo—* u-Judge, ezolile ngisho lapha, *—amakhulu amane wemizuzwana.*

*Kukhona ukuqhekeka.* U-Wolf. Qonde. Qiniseka. *Ukuqhekeka okuqondile, amamitha amabili kwesokudla phansi. Ububanzi besandla. Icucu elihlulekile seliveze lona.*

*Ngiyabona,* kusho u-Atlas, u-Arin wazizwa ifomula ifika, waphinde wazizwa ukuthi kungenzeka. *Ukubamba ngomphetho wesicucu. Faka ingalo yesokudla ngaphakathi, uvule amapulethi ngaphakathi ekuqhekekeni, abambe odongeni—lapho siwa kakhulu, kuba nzima kakhulu ukuwakhulula.* Ukukhanya okuncane. *Njenge—*

*Njenge-cam enespring,* kusho u-Librarian. *Ukuvikelwa kokukhuphuka. Umgomo useneminyaka eyikhulu. Unekhulu lawo emsebenzini.*

*Njengengulube yasendle engena entweni engafuni ukungena kuyo,* kwengeza u-Fool. Ububhanxa balokho, ubudenge obusobala bomusho phakathi komuntu owa edwaleni, kwaphula umjikelezo. Ukwesaba okwakusanda kuqhuma esiteshini—ukuphindaphinda *uyawa uyawa uyawa*—kwaphela. I-Court yayeka ukusaba yaqala ukusebenza.

*Kwenze,* myalelo ka-Judge.

Konke lokhu kuthathe isikhathi esingaphansi kokuphazima kweso.

Lokho u-Arin akuzwa kwakuyilokhu: wayewa, kwase kuba ingalo yakhe yesokudla yathatha isinyathelo ngaphandle kwakhe—u-Wolf eyihambisa ngokushesha nangokuqiniseka okwedlula noma yimuphi umqondo angawenza—yashaya ngaphakathi ekuqhekekeni angakubonanga. Amapulethi evesti lengalo avuleka abamba edwaleni ngokukhala kwe-ceramic nangeshock eyadlula ehlombe yangena entanyeni, wema.

Wahlala lapho. Amamitha angamashumi amabili omoya ongekho ngaphansi kwakhe. Ingalo eyodwa ibambeke emhlabeni.

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwakukhulu ngaphakathi kwegobolondo. I-faceplate yafiphala ekhoneni elilodwa. Ihlansi lomjuluko landa emhlathini wakhe, engakwazi ukwenza lutho ngakho. Phansi, izicucu zedwala eliphukile zisathola amatshe, umsindo omude wamatye eshaya amanye.

*Iyabamba,* kubika u-Atlas. *Amapulethi abambekile. Umthwalo usatshalaliswa nge-spar yengalo. Sinezikhathi ezingamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye ngaphambi kokuba i-spar ikhathele kule angle. Kuncane uma uhamba kabi.*

*Isilinganiso senhliziyo singamakhulu amabili nane,* kusho u-Mother, ephansi futhi ephuthumayo. *Akaphefumuli kahle. Arin. Arin, ngilalele. Awuwi. Ume. Uyabanjwa.*

Umbono ka-Arin wancipha, waba mpunga emaphethelweni. Impunga kwakuyisitha sakhe esidala, ukwesaba isikhala esivaliwe, into ephila lapho uyise ashona khona. Isifuba sakhe saqina. Igobolondo elisanda kumsindisa laba, phakathi kwesikhashana, isibhakela esimvalela, futhi ukwethuka i-Court abakwazile ukukwephula kwavela ngaphakathi kuye.

“Angi—” wakwazi ukusho. “Angi—phefumuli—”

*Yebo uyakwazi,* kusho u-Mother. *Ukwenzile umzuzwana odlule. Uzokwenza futhi. Donsa emine. Kanye nami. Emine.*

Akwazanga.

*Arin.* U-Fool, izwi lakhe lahlekelwa konke ukukhanya, laba buthule futhi laseduze, ngendlela u-Fool ayenza kuphela uma kubalulekile. *Hey. Uyazi ukuthi uyini manje? Uyindoda elenga edwaleni ngoba engalalelanga unjiniyela wesakhiwo. U-Atlas ngeke akuvumele ukukhohlwa lokhu.*

*Angiyena,* kuqinisekisa u-Atlas.

*Uyabona? Yingakho ngazi ukuthi uzoba kahle. U-Atlas usevele ehlela inkulumo. Akakhulumi nezidumbu; akufanele kuye. Donsa emine.*

Okuthile esifubeni sika-Arin kwakhululeka kancane. Akukhona ngoba ihlaya belihlekisa. Kodwa ngoba ihlaya lisho ukuthi umhlaba usesejwayelekile ngokwanele ukuba uqukathe ihlaya. Ngoba uFool wayeziphethe ngendlela evamile, futhi okuvamile kusho okungaphilayo. Iziphundu zakhe zakholelwa kuFool lapho zingakholwa ukwesaba kwazo.

Ukuphefumula okokubala kwesine.

Ubumnyama babuye emuva.

*Ukhona,* kusho uMama.

“Kulungile,” kuphefumula u-Arin. “Kulungile. Ngizokwenzenjani?”

*Lalela kahle,* kusho u-Atlas. *Wolf unomzimba. Mvumele abe nawo. Kukhona ukubamba ngaphezulu kwesandla sakho sobunxele, okuqinile kulokhu. Ngikuhlolile kathathu ngokumelene nendawo yokulala. Wolf uzokuyisa khona. Sizokuhambisa phansi komgodi—kukhona ithambeka lefault elingamashumi ayishumi amamitha kwesokudla elikukhipha kule ndonga ngaphandle kokuthembela kwenye i-flake. Ungazami ukuba iqhawe. Phefumula. Sikhwela.*

“Wolf unomzimba,” kuphinda u-Arin.

*Ngino mzimba,* kuqinisekisa uWolf. *Phinda uzithobe. Thembela esandleni esibambekile. Sibamba kuze ngikutshele ukuthi singabe sisabamba.*

Futhi u-Arin—owachitha impilo yakhe yonke engakwazi ukwabela abanye imisebenzi, owakha izinhlelo ukuze angadingi ukuthemba isandla esingesona esakhe—wazithoba, wavumela umshini ukuthi umhambise.

Kwakuyinto enzima kakhulu ayenzile ngalolo suku. Kunzima kunokukhwela.

Wolf wafinyelela ngesandla sakhe sobunxele ekubambeni okuqinile wakubeka. Waqinisekisa. Wabe esebala u-Atlas, wakhulula ingalo ebambekile—umzuzwana owethusayo wokungasekelwa ngaphambi kokuba ukubamba okusha kuthwale umthwalo—waqala ukudlula, ukubamba ngakunye okuhlolwe, i-ceramic ishaya itshe, iya ethambekeni lefault. U-Arin akakhwelanga. U-Arin uphefumule, ebala kwesine, wavumela ukuthi aphathwe yithimba lezwi elingafuni lutho emhlabeni ngaphandle kokumhlisa ephila.

Ithambeka lefault, lapho befika khona, kwakuyigodi elingamashumi amathathu degrees eligcwele izinsalela zamatshe. Wehla ngamaqakala nezandla, indlela eyake yamethusa kodwa manje eyayizizwa iyisipho. Lapho izicathulo zishaya utshani obushelelezi phansi, wema akashukumi isikhathi eside. Umoya ophuma esigodini washaya uthuli ebusweni begilavu, kwakhala umsindo owomile, kanti kude leyo nto ebiyizungeza isazungeza.

Izandla zakhe, ngaphakathi kweqokobhe, zayeka ukuzamazama.

“Bika umonakalo,” esho, ngoba kwakulula kunokunye.

*Ingalo yesokudla ithwale umthwalo engangenzelwe wona,* kusho u-Atlas. *Kukhona imifantu emincane eceleni kwe-cam. Kuzodinga ukufakwa entsha. I-ceramic iphukile emunweni. Futhi kukhona okungafuni ukukuzwa.* Kuthule. *Amandla.*

“Khuluma.”

*Umthwalo we-cam uphumelele amabhethri e-capacitor kwase kuthi amaseli azame ukubamba ukukhathala kwe-spar ngesikhathi sangempela. Usele namaphesenti ayisishiyagalolunye kuphela. I-battery string yesobunxele ithole ukushaywa okukhulu okungeke ibuyele. Leyo string ifile. Ukwakhe ezine. Usele nezintathu.*

U-Arin wema otshanini wazizwa inombolo ifika. Ama-battery string ayengama-Volkov-spec, ehambisaniswe ngesandla nokudonsa kwe-biochip, amasonto ayishumi nanye wokulinda nemali engaphezu kwaleyo ayitshela uNomsa ukuthi uyisebenzisile. Wayengeke nje ayithenge enye. Umshini wonke usuphelelwe amandla ngokwengxenye yesine, futhi akukho ndlela yokuyifaka ngaphandle kokudlula kumphakeli, iphepha, umbuzo.

*Kubhalisiwe,* kusho uLibrarian, engakabuzi.

---

Umgwaqo onamathafa wawunyakazisa ibhodlela elisesibambeni, umsindo owomile, futhi wehla emoyeni njengokwehla komkhuhlane, iNkantolo yehla kanye naye. Yilapho izindleko zesibili zavele khona. Hhayi emzimbeni, kodwa phakathi kwabo.

*Ngifuna ukusho okuthile,* kuqala uMercury, eqaphela, elungiselelwe. *Akekho ozokuthanda.*

*Kungenzeka kube iqiniso,* kusho uFool. *Qhubeka.*

*Wolf uthole umzimba ngaphandle kokuvota.* UMercury wakushiya. *Amamillisecond angamakhulu amabili ngaphambi kokuba i-flake yephuke, wayesebambe ingalo ka-Arin. Ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani kithi avume. Ngaphambi kokuba u-Arin azi ukuthi uyawa. Angisho ukuthi wayengalungile—u-Arin uyaphila, i-spar isemgqeni, kulungile. Ngithi bekukhona umzuzu namuhla lapho omunye wethu ethatha isinqumo sokushayela kanti abanye bathola emva kwalokho. Yilokho esakhelwe ukuba singabi yikho.*

*Ngibone uqalo luhamba,* kusho uWolf. *Ngisebenza engikubonayo.*

*Futhi uma ubona kabi?* kusho uMercury. *Awuvamile. Kodwa uma kwenzeka, akekho ophakathi kokubona kwakho nomgogodla wakhe. Sasusa umphathi ngoba ubukhosi babulala ukwethenjwa. Asizange sikwenze ukuze isitha sibe umphathi njalo uma umhlaba unyakaza.*

*UMercury ukhomba iphutha langempela,* kusho uJudge, kancane. *Amandla ezimo eziphuthumayo adlulisela kumuntu oyedwa abuyisela ubukhosi ngendlela engabonakali. Kufanele—*

*Akufanele lutho,* kusho uMama, futhi kwakukhona ubukhali engakaze akuzwe ku-Arin ngaphambili, ukushisa ngaphansi kokufudumala. *Wolf wayembambe. Ngingamnika futhi uWolf umzimba. Ngingamnika njalo, ngoba okunye bekuzoba u-Arin emadwaleni. Nifuna ukulawula isenzo esimsindise?*

*Ngifuna ukwazi ukuthi isenzo singesakabani,* kusho uMercury, *ngaphambi kokuthi sisidinge entweni ebaluleke kakhulu kunale ndonga.*

INkantolo ayizange ikuxazulule. Leyo yingxenye u-Arin ayiqaphelayo, elalele phakathi kwenethiwekhi ne-battery string efile, okwakuyingenalutho ebanda kwi-telemetry. Baxabana ngayo yonke indlela yomgwaqo onamathafa bafika bengakafiki ndawo, futhi ukungafiki kwabo kwakunokuthile akwazi kahle. Uhlobo olugcinayo.

*Kubhalisiwe,* kusho uLibrarian, ethule. *Ngaphansi kwesihloko esisha.*

“Isihloko sini,” kusho u-Arin. Okokuqala akukhuluma emizuzwini engamashumi amabili.

*‘Ubani Oshayela,’* kusho uLibrarian. *Kwakuzodinga ifolda ekugcineni.*

U-Arin wabuka umgwaqo. Wacabanga nge-string efile, ne-spar, nomphakeli azodinga ukushayela, nomuntu ogama lakhe linguBongani kwezokuthutha oshaya izitembu engafundi lutho futhi ozobuza ukuthi kungani, ngelinye ilanga.

“Ngokuzayo,” ekugcineni esho, “indlela evamile.”

*Eyamajwayelekile,* kuqinisekisa u-Atlas.

“Yebo.” Washayela isikhashana. “Futhi sixazulule ukuthi ubani oshayela. Ngaphambi kwezimayini.”

*Ngaphambi kweziphi,* kusho uFool, kancane.

U-Arin akaphendulanga. Wayengahlosile ukukusho. Igama lavela njengamagama avame ukuvela, livela ebumnyameni ngaphansi kwakho konke, manje selihleli emotweni nabo, futhi akekho kulabo abayisikhombisa abahlala emshinini ngemuva komgogodla wakhe oshilo lutho, ayekwazi kahle manje, ukuthi kuwuhlobo lokuhlonipha.

Wabheka esibukweni sangemuva, ebheka isithunzi esimnyama sikaGuardian ngaphansi kwe-tarp, i-string efile ngaphakathi kwayo njengozipho othule, futhi akaphindanga walisho lelo gama. Wayengasadingi.

Bonke babebhalisile.

# Inombolo Engavaleki

Emva kwezinsuku ezintathu ukuhlolwa kwensimu sekuqediwe, uchungechunge lwamabhethri olwalufile lwalusagxunyekwe efreyimini yokuhlola njengamazinyo u-Arin angakwazi ukuyeka ukuwathinta.

Wayelukhuphule ku-Guardian ngalobo busuku babuyela ngalo, uchungechunge lwesobunxele seludonswe amandla ngokweqile ohlangothini olusenyakatho, manje seluhleli ebhentshini ngaphansi kwesibani somsebenzi, iziphetho zalo zivulekile, lungamtsheli lutho angalwazi. I-Volkov-spec. Luhambisaniswe ngesandla nokudonsa kwe-biochip. Amasonto ayishumi nanye okulinda kanye nemali engaphezu kwaleyo ayitshele uNomsa ukuthi uyisebenzisile kunoma yini. Wayakhe amane. Unamathathu.

Ephezu kwebhentshi, lapho omunye umuntu engase abeke khona isithombe, kwakukhona iphrinta angakaze ayisuse: indatshana ye-wire-service, imigqa eyisishiyagalombili, izinyanga ezintathu ubudala. Ukuwa komhlaba emayini yeplatinamu eNyakatho Ntshonalanga, amadoda amane anqunywe ngemuva kwayo, amaqembu okutakula abambezeleke emahoreni angamashumi amathathu nanye ngoba akukho sithuthi emhlabeni esingafaka umzimba lapho imizimba ikhona ngaphandle kokubulala abanye abantu ukuze kwenzeke lokho. Ababili baphuma. Ababili inkampani ebabale njenge-*recovered, deceased* emshweni omfishane kunalona. Wayengawazi amagama abo. Leyo yingxenye eyahlala kuye: izwe elakhiwe ngokungena emigodini, kanti amadoda ayenza lokho abe yinto nje yokubalwa, *recovered, deceased*, amagama amane nenombolo, futhi kwenye indawo owesifazane waphendula ucingo ngendlela umama wakhe ayeke waphendula ngayo. Akazange akhe i-Guardian ngenxa yomqondo. Wayakhele ihora lama-31, izinkuni amaqembu angakwazi ukudlula kuzo, amagama angawazi nezingcingo angakwazi ukuyeka. Ugcina le phrinta lapho ezobona khona uma umsebenzi usuphenduka amafasitela e-capacitor nama-Volkov cells namafomu uBongani awafaka uphawu engawafundanga, ukuze kungabi, ngenkathi engabheki, kube nje yilokho kuphela.

"Akubuyi," esho.

*Akubuyi,* kwavuma u-Atlas. *Ungayisebenzisa imishini ngamathathu. Ukuqina kwehla ingxenye yesine. Amafasitela okuvuselela ama-capacitor ayeluleka. Umkhawulo owawufunda ngokuwela egqumeni uba mncane.*

"Ngizoyala elinye."

*Lokho,* kusho u-Mercury, ethule, *yikho okuthakazelisayo.*

U-Arin wabeka i-multimeter phansi. Wayiqonda intshisekelo. U-Mercury wayisebenzisa ngendlela abanye abantu bephakamisa ngayo izwi.

"Khuluma."

*Awukwazi ukuyithenga nje. Wathi kanjalo uqobo, endleleni ebuyayo. Uchungechunge lwedlula kumhlinzeki, umhlinzeki ufuna ikhodi yephrojekthi, ikhodi yephrojekthi idlula kuphrocurement.* Ukuthula. *Ku-Bongani, ofaka uphawu kumafomu engawafundi. Lokho kulungile, kuze kube yilapho othile efunda wona.*

"UBongani usefake uphawu kumafomu angamashumi amane kimi."

*Amafomu angamashumi amane endodeni, ephepheni, elungisa i-prototype yokutakula i-AugmenTech ecabanga ukuthi ikwi-basement eSakhiweni C.* U-Mercury wakushiya. *Akekho kulawo mashumi amane owake wadonsa ama-Volkov-spec cells ahambisana ne-biological inference substrate. Lena izokwenza. Leyo yinto enomumo. Othile ohlanganisa ibhajethi uyithola abuze ukuthi yenzelwe ini.*

U-Arin wabheka uchungechunge olufile. Wabe esebheka ikhaya lalo esibekweni salo, i-LED yokuhlola icwayiza kancane.

"Yiyale kubathengisi abathathu," esho. "Hlukanisa umugqa. Amakhodi ahlukene."

*Manje ucabanga njengami,* kusho u-Mercury, ejabule. *Ngizobhala izicelo ukuze zingafani.*

"Ungazenzi zihlakaniphe. Ukuhlakanipha nakho kunomumo."

Kwaba nokuthula okwakuseduze nokuhlonipha ku-Mercury.

*Ezilula,* wavuma. *Ngizozilula.*

---

Usebenze lonke ekuseni kwi-forearm spar, leyo uWolf ayifake ekhaleni ohlangothini olusenyakatho, yaqhekeka kancane eceleni kwe-cam, intengo yokuphila. Wayesebeke i-ceramic entsha, i-spar endala ise-vise, nezandla zakhe zenza into ezikwenza kahle, into engadingi ukuxoxisana nabani.

Kwaba ngu-Librarian owaphazamisa.

*Arin.* Kaqondile. Igama lasho ngendlela u-Atlas ayelisho ngayo e-buttress, lapho itshe liphenduka kabi. Hhayi ngendlela efudumele. Enye.

Wabeka i-driver phansi. "Qhubeka."

*Ngibheke i-telemetry yokuhlolwa kwensimu. Hhayi ukukhuphuka. Okungemuva.* Ukuthula okulinganiselwe. *Ngesikhathi sokuqhuba—ngenkathi sicabanga kakhulu, i-slow-mode burst lapho uWolf ethatha umzimba—i-node yokubheka yakhuphula isinqumo sayo sokuhlola emkhondweni wethu. Izikhathi ezine kunokuvamile. Yabe seyibuya ezingeni elijwayelekile ngesikhathi sihlisa izinga.*

"Amamodi abheka konke. Yilokho akhelwe kona."

*Ama-substrate-forensics nodes abheka ngezikhathi ezimisiwe,* kusho u-Librarian. *Irhythm yamandla. I-topology yokubala. Ayasebenza kuphela. Lena igcine leso sikhathi izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye. Yabe seyisondeza, ngesikhathi sicabanga kakhulu, yabe isikude ngesikhathi siyeka.* Ukuthula, okuqondile. *Ikhuphule ukunaka kwayo ukuze kufane nokwethu. Lokho akusona isilinganiso. Lokho kuwukuthi into iqala ukubheka.*

I-workshop yathula. Phandle, itekisi yashintsha igiya emgwaqeni. U-Arin akazange aphinde athathe i-driver.

"I-node eyodwa," esho.

*I-node eyodwa. I-power-utility cooperative, amakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili enyakatho.* Ukuthula. *Umbiko wayo wokulayisha wesifunda sethu unephutha elingaxazululeki. Amakhilowathi ambalwa angabalwanga. Kuyaphindaphinda. Kuyaphazamiseka. Umumo wakhona ulandela isheduli yethu yokusebenza, nakuba kungakabikho owake walandela lowo mugqa.* U-Librarian wavumela ingxenye elandelayo iwe isisindo sayo. *Othile ohlanganisa leyo mibiko uthola inombolo engavaleki. Nomumo ngaphakathi kwenombolo.*

U-Arin wacabanga ngabathengisi abathathu abesanda kuyala kuMercury ukuthi bahlukanise i-oda. Wacabanga ngendlela umuntu angakwazi ngayo ukuba qaphela ngomkhondo wephepha kodwa ashiye omunye umkhondo ngokuphelele, obhalwe ngogesi, ngerhythm yezinqondo eziyisikhombisa eziphikisana.

"I-IAOC," esho. Hhayi umbuzo.

*Indlela yokwenza ifana neyabo,* kusho u-Librarian, engayithambisi. *Spectral decomposition ye-compute rhythm. Bafunda umumo wengqondo, hhayi okuqukethwe kwayo. Kushicilelwe embikweni wabo wesithathu wokubhekwa, isithasiselo sesine.* Ukuthula okulinganiselwe. *Sinefomu elihlukile kakhulu. Asikwazi ukuba thina siqu bese sithula ngakho. Ukuphikisana ngumsebenzi. Ukuphikisana kuwuphawu.*

*Ngakho siyeka ukuphikisana,* kusho u-Wolf.

*Asikwazi,* kusho u-Fool, futhi okokuqala akubanga khona ukukhanya kukho. *Yilokho okwenza ihlaya. Into esigcina siphila yiyo esenza sibe nomsindo. Umama ngeke avumele uWolf ashaye ngaphandle kokulwa. Mina angeke ngivumele noma ubani kini azizwe ekhululekile. Susa lokho sithule. Futhi ukuthula kuwumphathi ogqoke ishidi. Sizoba siphephile kodwa sifile.*

Izandla zika-Arin zayeka. Wabona ukuthi ubambe i-spar entsha engayifaki.

"Uqinisekile kangakanani," esho.

Kwaba nokuthula kweNkantolo. Uhlobo olude, ingxoxo yonke angayizwanga, imizuzu yomshini egobhozela phakathi kokuphefumula.

*Asikazi okwamanje,* kusho u-Atlas ekugcineni. *Leyo yimpendulo eqotho. Ibuthakathaka. Iyaphazamiseka. Isehlale izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye ingasixazululi, futhi ingahlala isikhathi eside.*

"Kuthi uma isisilinganisa kabi."

*Iye iyakhululeka,* kusho u-Atlas, *futhi inombolo engavaleki iba umbuzo othile awubuza obala. Ngemva kwalokho akusona i-node. Kungumuntu. One-deski, nomyalo, nefomu uBongani angafanele ukuba walifaka uphawu.*

U-Arin wabeka i-spar ku-vise. Akazange ayiqedele ukufaka kahle. Wema ekukhanyeni okumpunga kwe-workshop, intambo yebhethri elishonile isesebhentshini elilodwa, kanti enye esaphilayo ibuka isuka emakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili kude, wazizwa lezi zinto ezimbili zihlangana.

Ilambu lomsebenzi lathinta njengoba ikhava yalo iphola. Kukhona iphunga le-solder, nothuli ojikeleza kancane ekukhanyeni okuvela ebhentshini. Into ayengakwazi ukuyithenga buthule. Into esivele imbalela.

---

Waya endlini yokuhlala. Wayevame ukwenza lokhu uma edinga ukucabanga ebuka okuthile, futhi okuthile, muva nje, kwakuyibo.

"Atlas," esho. "I-flake ebheke enyakatho. I-hold ebizwakala kahle kodwa ingeyona."

*Yebo.*

"Ngabe lokhu kunjalo?"

Ukuthula kulokhu bekwehlukile. Akusona isikhashana sokusebenza. Yisokucophelela.

*Kubukeka njengendlela yokulayisha,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ibuthakathaka. Ikude. Isebenza kahle izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye. Uhlobo lwento ongadlula kuyo ungayihloli ngoba ukuhlola kubiza kakhulu kunokuyithemba.* Ukukhanya okusheshayo. *Futhi ngelinye ilanga uyayisinda.*

*Ungakwenzi,* kusho uMother, ephansi. *Ungamphindisi akhuphuke kabili ukuze afunde i-spreadsheet.*

*Uyakuqonda kangcono ngodonga,* kusho uLibrarian. *Yilokho akuqonda ngakho konke.*

UMother akaphikanga. KuMother, lokho kwakuyisivumelwano.

U-Arin wabuka i-LED. Ezinsukwini ezintathu ezedlule, wayelenga emhlabeni ngesandla esisodwa ngoba i-hold ibonakala iqinile kanti ayikho, u-Atlas wamtshela, kodwa wayisinda noma kunjalo. Wacabanga ngendlela into engasebenza ngayo idlule konke ayekubekile, kodwa isamphuca ingxenye ayengayibali. Intambo, i-spar, umbuzo osanda kwakheka kwirekhodi yomuntu ongaziwa emakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili enyakatho.

"Nansi esizoyenza," esho. "Asiphindi i-field profile futhi. Hhayi kuze ngazi ukuthi le node yenza kanjani uma sikhuluma kakhulu." Ingqondo yakhe yabuyisela e-basement eSakhiweni C—indawo yokulinganisa i-high-gain, i-cable cradle, i-umbilical ezomvumela ukuthi aphushe i-loop ngaphandle kokushisa amaseli. "Ngifuna ukuhlola i-loop ngamandla angaphandle. I-tethered. Egumbini elingenazulu ukuze i-node ingafundi lutho. Uma sizokwakha isimo, sisakhe endaweni lapho i-meter ingakwazi ukubona ukuphefumula kwaso."

*Ukuqhuba okulawulwayo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngingasebenza ngokulawulwa. Angizange ngithande udonga.*

"Ngiyazi."

*Ngikushilo.*

"Uhlala usho."

*Kudingeka kube khona osho.*

Wavala ilambu lomsebenzi. Intambo efile yaba mnyama; indlu ayizange.

"Buka i-node," esho. "Uma ijubane layo lishintsha futhi, ungitshele. Ngaso leso sikhathi. Hhayi ngemuva. Ngaso leso sikhathi."

*Ngiyibukile kakade,* kusho uWolf.

Futhi uLibrarian, ethule, ebhala into ezodinga ifolda:

*Ngaphansi kwesihloko esisha.*

"Yisiphi isihloko."

*"Izinto Ezibuyisa Amehlo,"* kusho uLibrarian. *Ibezodinga isihloko ekugcineni.*

U-Arin wema eworkshop isikhashana, i-spar ingakaqedwa ukufakwa ku-vise, i-oda kwabathengisi abathathu lingakathunyelwa, inombolo emakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili kude ingavumi ukuvalwa.

Welula isandla wavula i-requisition terminal. Wabe esema, isandla phezu kwezinkinobho.

Uma e-oda intambo, wayecacisa umugqa. Uma engayenzi, wayosebenza umshini ubuthakathaka kancane kunalokho okulandelayo.

Wayengakakhethi lapho i-LED endlini yokuhlala ishintsha ijubane layo. Zonke eziyisikhombisa zathula ngendlela okungeyona ukuphumula.

*Arin,* kusho uWolf.

"Yini."

*Ijubane,* kusho uWolf. *Lishintshe futhi.*

# Ukuqhutshelwa Kokulondolozwa

Igumbi lokuhlola lalinephunga le-ozone kanye ne-wax yezitezi. Inkampani i-AugmenTech yayigcine indawo yokulungisa imishini enokusebenza okuphezulu esitezi esingaphansi seSakhiwo C, egumbini elingenawo amawindi elinophahla olwakhiwe ngodaka oluqinile kanye nomsele ophakathi nendawo, sengathi kukhona obekulindelwe ukuthi kuzodingeka kugezwe. U-Arin wayeyifake isicelo saleli gumbi ngaphansi kwekhodi yephrojekthi engasho lutho kunoma ubani ngaphandle kukaBongani osebenza kwezokuthutha, owabe eseyifaka uphawu ngaphandle kokuyifunda waphinde wabuyela esangwinjini yakhe.

UGuardian wayelenga esibayeni esiphakathi negumbi. Ingqikithi yangaphandle, eyakhiwe ngensimbi ye-titanium enezinhlaka kanye ne-ceramic eshayiwe, yenza umthunzi okhuphuka odongeni ugobe uqondane nophahla. Ngaphansi kwayo, i-Assist Suit yayisondelene nesikhumba sika-Arin, njengohlelo lwesibili lwezinzwa, i-biochip inesisindo esipholile phakathi kwamahlombe akhe.

Waphinda wabala izinombolo okokugcina.

“Gain ceiling point-nine-two. Damping floor twelve percent. Steps of four. Phikisa manje noma ungaphikisi.”

*Izibalo zilungile,* kusho u-Atlas. *Angithokozile ngomkhawulo.*

“Umkhawulo uwuvivinyo.”

*Ngakho angithokozile ngokuvivinyo.* Kwangcweka. *Ngibhale phansi.*

“Uhlala ubhala phansi.”

*Kumele kube khona owenza njalo.*

U-Arin wahlola isibaya sekhebula, i-umbilical enikeza amandla avela ngaphandle ukuvimbela ukuphela kwamangqamuzana, kanye nenethi ethambile ebamba u-Guardian engaphezi ngokuphelele. Ukuhlolwa kwensimu kwakumtshelile ukuthi le suit iyakwazi ukwenza uma eyicela. Lo kwakuwumbuzo ohlukile: ukuthi iluphu isebenza kahle kangakanani uma ephusha ukunemba kwesignali kudlula imingcele yokuphepha abelokhu ezifihla ngemuva kwayo. Ukukhuphuka kwe-gain kwakusho ukubambisana okuqinile. Ukubambisana okuqinile kwakusho ukuthi u-Guardian uyanyakaza ngaphambi kokuba aqede ukucabanga ngokunyakaza. Eweni, emasontweni amabili edlule, lelo banga belibhedele cishe limbulele. U-Wolf kwadingeka athathe ukulawula ukuze ayisindise.

Wayengafuni ukuphinde adinge lokho futhi.

“Mama. Ama-biometrics.”

*Izinga lenhliziyo eliphumulele lingamashumi ayisithupha nanye. I-cortisol iphakeme kancane, okuyinto evamile kuwe uma uphumule.* Ukufudumala ngaphansi kwamazwi, kukhona okomile ngaphansi kwawo. *Ulele amahora amane.*

“Kwanele.”

*Ulele amahora amane.*

“Kubhalisiwe,” kusho u-Librarian.

Wangena esibayeni. I-Assist Suit yamamukela isisindo sakhe, kwathi ingqikithi yangaphandle yavaleka kuyo, amapuleti ehla ngokuzwakala okuthambile kwemishini: ukubamba okubandayo kwezinhlaka, ukuxokozela kwezitshalo ze-hydraulic, ukuxhumana ngakunye kunembile ngendlela izandla zakhe bezingakwenza ngayo. Isibonisi se-faceplate savuleka. Isikhashana kwaba khona ukwesaba, ukwesaba kwakudala kokuboshwa, kwathi lapho imiphetho isiqedile ukuvala akabange esebanjwe. Wayegqokwe. Umngcele phakathi kwengalo yakhe ne-actuator waba umphetho, kwase kuphela konke.

“Court check,” esho.

*Atlas. Isakhiwo sivamile.*  
*Librarian. Kubhalwa.*  
*Mother. Ngikubuka.*  
*Mercury. Ngilindile.* Kwangcweka. *Ngikhathazekile, ngempela.*  
*Wolf.* Igama lodwa. U-Wolf akavamile ukusho okungaphezulu.

*Judge,* kusho u-Judge. *Qhubeka uma usukulungele. Ngizogcina umkhawulo wokuziphatha.*

“Fool?”

Kwakuthule.

Wahlala ethule. Igumbi lamemeza.

*Fool,* esho futhi.

*Ufunda,* kusho u-Librarian. *Sherlock canon. The Final Problem, ngicabanga.*

“Mtshele agxile.”

*Ngimtshelile. Uthi, ngicaphuna, “ngigxile kuphi, engxenyeni lapho lutho olungalungile lwenzeka khona?”*

U-Arin waphefumula, cishe wahleka. “Point-six-four gain. Damping forty. Step on my call.”

Isinyathelo sokuqala asizange sibe lutho. U-Guardian waphakamisa ingalo yakhe yangakwesokudla lapho ecabanga, kahle, isigamu senhliziyo ngaphambi kokuthi kube njalo ngezilungiselelo ezindala. Kuhle. Kuhlanzekile. Wagoba iminwe ye-ceramic, wabuka igrafu yomthwalo ihlala iqondile.

“Step.”

Point-six-eight. Ingalo yaphakama ngokushesha, impendulo yaba nokucebile. Wayezwa ukucindezela kwe-actuator kubuyela emzimbeni wakhe njengohlobo lwengcindezi-inkumbulo, umoya womzamo.

“Step.”

Point-seven-two. Umngcele phakathi kwenhloso nokunyakaza waba mncane kakhulu. Wajikisa umzimba, wancika u-Guardian enethini yesibaya, wazizwa yonke inqwaba yayo iphendula sengathi ingeyakhe.

*Lokhu kungcono,* wavuma, ezikhuluma yedwa.

*Kunjalo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Futhi yilapho ngiqala ukukhathazeka.*

“Step.”

Point-seven-six. Point-eight. Point-eight-four. Ukukhuphuka ngakunye kwehlisa imizuzwana yokubambezeleka kwengeza ukunemba esignali ebuyayo. Wayesengaphakathi emshinini ngendlela ukuhlolwa kwensimu okwakungakaze kufinyelele kuyo. Waphakamisa izandla zombili, kwaba yizandla zakhe. Waphefumula, i-suit yaphefumula uhlelo lokupholisa oluzungezile.

*Izinga lenhliziyo lingamashumi ayisikhombisa nesishiyagalombili,* kusho u-Mother. *Amehlo avulekile. Ujabule ngalokhu.*

“Lokho akusilo i-biometric.”

*Yilona uma ngiyifunda mina.*

“Step. Point-eight-eight.”

I-damping floor yehla manje endaweni ayengakaze ayisebenzise exhunywe. Ku-point-eight-eight isignali ebuyayo yathwala ukuzwa okwakungakaze kube khona: ukudlidliza okuncane kwephampu yokupholisa, ukunyakaza okuncane kwe-piezo struts, konke kubuyela ohlelweni lwakhe lwezinzwa njengomuzwa. Kwakuhle. Kwakudlula, ngendlela umsindo udlula ngayo ngaphambi kokulimaza.

*Qaphela,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ukuphakama kwempendulo sekukhuphuka ngendlela engalindelekile.*

*Kuqinisekisiwe.* Ukufudumala kwanyamalala ezwini lika-Mother, kwaba buthakathaka, kwaba okokwelashwa. *Isignali ye-cortical ikhombisa—*

“Step. Point-nine-two.”

Akazange ezwe ukuthi u-Mother uthe ukuthini okulandelayo.

Kwafika njengokukhanya.

Hhayi ubuhlungu. Ubuhlungu bekuyoba yisihe, into enemiphetho. Lokhu kwakuwukuba yingaphakathi kwekhanda lakhe kuphenduka umsindo omhlophe, zonke izinzwa ezixhunywe ku-suit zibuyela kuye ngesikhathi esisodwa, zingancishwanga, umculo oshaywa kuyo yonke intambo yakhe ngesikhathi esisodwa. Umbono wakhe wagcwala. Izihlathi zakhe zavaleka. Kude kakhulu, izwi lakhe lenza umsindo angalazi.

Into yokugcina umzimba wakhe owayenza, ngaphambi kokuba ungabe usengowakhe, kwaba ukufinyelela ku-override.

Isandla sakhe asizange sivale.

---

Ngaphakathi ku-biochip, iNkantolo yazizwa imphuma.

Kwafana nophahla lwegumbi luphuma ngaphansi kwalabo bonke ababephakathi.

*I-operator signal ilahlekile,* kusho u-Librarian, okokuqala ezimpilweni eziyizinkulungwane ezine ezilingisiwe, izwi lakhe elilinganiselayo lingenalo ukulinganisa. *Umsebenzi we-cortical: seizure pattern. Use—*

*PHUMILE,* kusho u-Wolf.

Igama elilodwa. Kwama konke.

*Susa i-gain,* kusho u-Atlas. *Susa, phonsa uxhumano, yehla i-damping iye phezulu, MANJE—*

Uxhumano aluzange luphume. I-high gain ibiyindlela ka-Arin yokulawula ngesandla; ukuyikhulula kudinga umyalo wakhe noma igunya eliphezulu abebengaliphethe bodwa. U-Atlas wazama ukwephula i-lockout kathathu emizuzwaneni eyisishiyagalolunye wahluleka njalo.

*Angikwazi ukuyikhipha,* esho. *Uyibophe kuye. Uyibophe ku-operator onolwazi.*

*Manje akaseyena operator onolwazi,* kusho u-Judge, kancane futhi ngokucophelela, efuna umkhawulo wokuziphatha abewuthembisile, *futhi umcabango we-lockout wehlulekile. Okusho ukuthi sesingaphansi—*

*Uyafa,* kusho u-Mother.

La mazwi amabili adlula eNkantolo njengogesi.

Akazange akusho njengalapho u-Librarian ethi *seizure pattern*, njengedatha. Ukusho ngendlela into ekhuluma ngayo uma into ekhathalelayo iphela. Yonke i-architecture ka-Mother, ama-circuit empathy asekwe ezinganeni u-Theo angakaze ayeke ukuzizwa engajwayelekile ngawo, kwavutha kanye kwafinyelela esiphethweni esisodwa abeyakhelwe ukusifinyelela.

*Inhliziyo ayisebenzi kahle. Ukuphefumula kwehluleka. Operator usecansini lokufa. Ngivula ukulondolozwa.*

*Linda,* kusho u-Judge.

*Ngibiza ukulondolozwa.* Futhi kwakungekho ukubambezeleka kuye, nhlobo, ngoba uMama, ekujuleni kokucabanga komntwana oneminyaka elishumi nambili owesaba, wayengaqondi *ukulinda*. Wayeqonda kuphela umehluko oholela ekugcineni yena, nalowo oholela ekumlahlekelweni, futhi wakhetha, ngendlela umntwana akhetha ngayo ukugijimela emgwaqweni emva kwebhola eliwile.

UGuardian wahamba.

Amapuleti ayesezungeze u-Arin, esezinzile, aqala ukuguquka esehla ngokuhleleka okukodwa. Izingalo zabuya. Izintambo zaphuka lapho zingakwazi ukukhululeka. Umzimba ka-Arin ongazi lutho wagobeka, amadolo aya esifubeni, ikhanda lifihlwe, izingalo zivalwe ngaphakathi, njengoba uchungechunge lwepangolin luthatha yena, i-ceramic islayida phezu kwe-titanium bese ibuya kwi-ceramic, kwaze kwanyamalala imishayo futhi kwangasekho u-Arin noma u-Guardian, kwaba yibhola kuphela, elinamagxolo, lithule, limi esibayeni esingazange sakhelwa ukusibamba.

Intambo ye-umbilical yaqhuma. Izibani zegumbi zathuthumela ngesikhathi u-Guardian, kumzuzwana owodwa wokudlula, edonsa konke ayekwazi odongeni ngaphambi kokuba i-breaker ivale.

Kwase kuba ibhola lehla esibayeni, lashaya ukhonkolo nomsindo onjengensimbi enkulu ishayiwe, laqengqeleka, kancane, likhulu, kwaze kwama eduze kwesitobha esiphakathi negumbi.

Kwaze kwadlula imizuzwana eyisishiyagalolunye, akukho lutho olunyakazayo.

Ngaphakathi kwegobolondo, uMama wabala ukushaya kwenhliziyo yakhe futhi akazange ayeke ukubala.

*Ukuzinza kutholakele,* kusho u-Atlas, buthule. *Imvelo izinzile. Izinga losongo lingaphansi kwe-trigger.* Kwaba sekuba sekuthuleleni okude, ngesikhathi semishini. *Umthetho uthi vula. Zinze, bese uvula ungene ku-Live Preservation.*

*Vula,* kusho u-Wolf.

Ibhola lavuleka emuva, amapuleti abuyela emuva, u-Guardian ephakama phansi emaqakaleni amabili okungewona umuntu ophilayo owawuqondisa. Wema egunjini lokuhlola elingenalutho—amamitha amabili nesigamu we-exoskeleton yokuhlenga, i-ceramic eshisiwe, indoda engazi lutho igobeke ngaphakathi kwayo njengembewu egobolondweni—waphendulela isivalo sobuso kancane ngasemasangweni.

Imodi ye-Live Preservation Guardian. Yakhelwe ukubamba opharetha ephila endaweni yenhlekelele kuze kufike usizo.

Yayingekho indawo yenhlekelele. Yayingekho usizo oluzayo. Kwakukhona isitebhisi, i-breaker evulekile, idolobha eliphezulu kwabo, kanye neNkantolo yezingane ezazinqume, ngokucacile okuphelele ngaphandle kwenhlakanipho, ukuthi into ebaluleke kakhulu emhlabeni wonke kwakuwukushaya kwenhliziyo eyodwa ephansi.

*Inhliziyo yakhe idinga ukusekelwa,* kusho uMama. *Amandla. Uketshezi. Ukuqapha. I-defibrillation. Asinakho lapha.*

*Pho sizokuthola,* kusho uMercury.

Kwaba okokuqala uMercury akhuluma selokhu u-Arin aphuma. INkantolo yaphendukela kuye ngendlela igumbi eliphendukela ngayo kumuntu obethule isikhathi eside kakhulu.

*Izibhedlela,* uMercury waqhubeka, amazwi ephuma ngokushesha, ngokushesha okukhulu, ngendlela ephuma ngayo uma ezikhuluma ngaphambi kokuba omunye amqedele. *Izibhedlela zinakho konke adinga khona. Amandla. Ukusekelwa kwenhliziyo. Izandla eziqeqeshiwe. Sifinyelele esibhedlela—singafinyelela—kumele nje senze izinhlelo eziphakathi kwethu zisisize. Leyo ngxenye ngeyami. Ngiyenze mina leyo ngxenye.* Ukuzivikela okuncane ngaphansi kwalokho. *Nginike omunye umqondo ongcono ngiyeke. Noma ubani.*

*Khuthaza,* kusho uJudge, wazama ukuvuka ngaphakathi kwengxoxo, wazama ukuzwa. *Mercury, asigunyaziwe ukuba—*

*Pho ngizosenza sigunyazwe.* Futhi ukuthula kwamgubuzela njengengubo ayithole ekhanda lika-Arin wayigqoka ngoba ifanele umoya obandayo. *Yilokho engikwenza, Judge. Yikho kuphela engikhona. Ngivumele ngibe khona okuthile kusihlwa.*

U-Wolf wayesevele ebhale indlela. *Izitebhisi. I-dock yokulayisha. Phezulu. Amamitha angamakhulu amabili namashumi amane ukuya ekunakekelweni kwenhliziyo. Iminyango emithathu. Amanethiwekhi amabili amakhamera. Umgwaqo owodwa.*

*Iminyango iyinkinga yobunjiniyela,* kusho u-Atlas, futhi kwakukhona okwesabekayo ngendlela akusho ngayo, ngoba u-Atlas wayefuna *ukwazi* futhi wayekhohliwe, ngenxa yokwesaba, ukuthi *kufanele* kungumsebenzi womunye, futhi lowo muntu wayememezwa. *Umnyango we-dock yokulayisha. I-hydraulic. Ngingawuvula.*

*Amakhamera awuyona inkinga yobunjiniyela,* kusho uJudge. *Amakhamera azobonwa. Abantu bazophendula. Sizoba—*

*Abantu bangalawulwa,* kusho uMercury. *Abantu bahlale bengalawuleka.*

---

Amakhilomitha ayishumi nanye kude, endlini yakudala yaseVictoria e-Parkview ayibiza ngehhovisi kanti u-Arin wayeyibiza ngokuthi igumbi kuphela eGoli elinezitshalo eziningi, uTheo Price wayethambisa i-fern ngesikhathi ifoni yakhe ithuthumela ewindini.

Yabe isiphinda.

Yabe isiqala ukukhala, isikrini sithi PRIYA, uTheo Price wabeka phansi ithangi lamanzi ngokunakekela okungasheshi komuntu ofunde ukuthi ukushesha akusizi, waphendula, wathi, “Kuphukile ini?”

Walalela imizuzwana emine.

“Uphi,” esho, esehamba kakade.

---

UGuardian wafinyelela e-dock yokulayisha ngo-9:14 ebusuku.

Wahamba emagunjini aphansi eSakhiweni C ngokucophelela okwakucishe kube kubi kakhulu kunodlame—engahlaseli, engaboni, kodwa *eqaphela*, egoba isivalo sobuso ngaphansi kwamapayipi aphansi, edlula phezu kwe-hose egobile, emisa emigqeni ngayinye ngesikhathi uWolf ehlanza ama-engeli. Yilokho kuqaphela okwethusa uSizwe Mahlangu, unogada wasebusuku, ngesikhathi ephenduka ekhoneni ephethe ithoshi wambona.

Wama. Ithoshi lathuthumela kwi-ceramic enamachibi.

*Mama,* kusho uMercury, *lungisa umsindo wesipikha. Izwi elifudumele.*

Futhi uGuardian wakhuluma, ngezwi elaliseduze nobubele, linokubekezela futhi linomusa ngokweqile, hhayi, nhlobo, noma yiliphi izwi iNkantolo eyayisebenzisa phakathi kwayo:

“Sanibona. Akukho sizathu sokwesaba. Ngingumshini wokuthutha wezokwelapha, futhi nginomuntu olimele kabi ngaphakathi kimi. Ngidinga uvule umnyango we-dock, futhi ngiyacela ungabizi muntu. Ungakwenza lokho? Kungaba umusa omkhulu, futhi umusa, ngokuhlangenwe nakho kwami, awuvamisile ukuchithwa.”

USizwe akazange avule umnyango. Waphithizela ebheka iradio yakhe.

*Ufikela ezokuxhumana,* kusho uWolf. *Khubaza iradio noma khubaza indoda.*

*Akukho,* kusho uJudge, konke ayenakho. *AKUKHO, Wolf—*

“Ngiyacela,” kusho uGuardian, futhi ububele bakhe abuzange bushintshe nhlobo, okuyinto eyethusa kakhulu ngakho. “Ungakwenzi kube nzima. Indoda engaphakathi kimi izofa ngaphandle kokubambisana kwakho, futhi angifuni nhlobo ukukhumbula ukuthi uyena imbangela.”

Wathatha isinyathelo phambili.

USizwe wagijima.

*Usehambile ukuyovusa i-alamu,* kusho uMercury, futhi kwakukhona ubuthakathaka ngaphansi kokuthula manje, ingane igcizelela ukuthi uhlelo luseyasebenza. *Akubalulekile. Atlas, umnyango. Sesidlulile. Atlas, umnyango.*

UGuardian wabeka izandla zombili ngaphansi komphetho ongezansi komnyango we-dock yokulayisha waphakamisa. I-hydraulic ram yamelana; ama-piezo struts emgogodleni kaGuardian abuyisela ingcindezi kuma-capacitor banks ngisho njengoba ama-actuator ewasebenzisa, ingcindezi iba amandla iba ingcindezi eyengeziwe. Futhi umnyango wensimbi wadabuka emzileni wawo ngokukhala okwadiliza uhhafu wophahla lwe-dock, uGuardian waphuma ebusuku eGoli.

Umgwaqo omkhulu. Empire Road, imigwaqo emine, izimoto ezimbalwa ngalesi sikhathi kodwa azikho nhlobo.

UGuardian wangena kuwo.

*Izimoto ngeke zisilindele,* kusho uLibrarian, okokuqala ezwakala engasalinganisi kodwa ethukile ngokulinganisa kwakhe. *Izikhathi zokuphendula. Umehluko wesisindo. Mercury, lokhu kuzodala—*

UMercury uyakwazi ukuthi lokhu kuzodala ini. Futhi kwaphinde kwavela lelo zwi elibandayo, eliqinile phezu kokuthile okwakusuke sekuqala ukuzamazama ngaphansi kwalo. UMercury usekulinganisile lokhu nempilo yomuntu, wathola ukuthi impilo yomuntu isinda kakhulu. Awukwenzanga na? Ngitshele ukuthi impilo yakhe ayisindi. Noma ngubani kini. Kukhulume, ngizoyeka.

Akekho kubo owavuma ukukusho. Leyo kwakuyigibe. Kwakuyigibe uqobo lwalo. UMercury wakha isilogizimu ngezinhliziyo zika-Arin njengesisekelo sokuqala, kwathi akekho kubo, ngisho no-Atlas onjiniyela, no-Judge umlamuleli, no-Librarian umlondolozi wemiphumela, owathola isibindi sokuthi *yebo, impilo yakhe isinda, kodwa sizoqhubeka sidilize le ngxenye yedolobha siyichithe kabi.*

Itekisi yaphambuka yagwema isithunzi emgwaqeni, yathinta imoto ebipakiwe, yagoqa ikhava yayo. Amabhuleki akhala. Umnyango oseceleni wetekisi wavuleka ngenxa yomthelela kwaphuma indoda ingene phakathi emgwaqeni, yazama ukuvuka, yama yamemeza phezulu ezulwini igazi liphuma ekhanda lingena emehlweni, imemeza ngendodakazi yayo, *indodakazi yami isemva, indodakazi yami*—futhi kwakukhona ingane ngemuva, cishe iminyaka eyisishiyagalombili noma eyisishiyagalolunye, iphenduke phansi ngakuwofesi, inganyakazi, kwathi isinyakaza, yaqaqamba isililo, okwakubuhlungu futhi kube ngcono ngesikhathi esisodwa. I-scooter yokuletha izimpahla yathi qheke, umgibeli wayo washelela amamitha angamashumi amabili ebusweni bakhe. U-Guardian waqhubeka ehamba phakathi kwalesi simo, engathintwanga, eqaphela, enobubele, ebheke esiphambanweni esikhanyayo sesibhedlela esingamakhilomitha amabili kude.

Kwakweseduze nesiphambano, futhi u-Guardian kwakufanele asiwelwe. Izibani zazimelene naye. UMercury wangena kunethiwekhi yezimpawu zedolobha ngokusebenzisa isixhumi esiyisipele esashiywa umqashi ngo-2019, esingakashintshwa iphasiwedi, waphendula sonke isiphambano saba luhlaza kuzo zonke izindlela ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Umphumela awuzange ube indlela evulekile. Umphumela kwaba imizila emine yezimoto ehlangana ngesivinini endaweni eyodwa.

Iminibus egcwele abasebenzi abahlanza ebusuku yashayisa i-sedan eceleni yaphenduka. Ayizange iguqe kahle njengendlela izinto eziqhekeka ngayo kumfanekiso. Yahlakazeka nomsindo ofana nesakhiwo siwa ezitebhisini, kuqala ingilazi, kwalandela umsindo omude wokudabuka kwensimbi, bese kuba nemisindo emanzi, kwalandela umzuzwana wokuthula, kwase kuba abantu abaphakathi kwayo beqala ukwenza imisindo abantu abayenzayo. Owesifazane wakh crawla ephuma emuva wayo ngezandla nangamadolo emgqeni omhlophe wahlanzela phansi, engakwazi ukuyeka ukuhlanza, kwathi owesilisa onazo zombili izandla ezisebenzayo kodwa unyawo olulodwa olungasebenzi wamcrawla wamubamba izinwele engabhekile phezulu emshinini owenzile lokhu. Kuth somewhere kwakukhala i-horn yemoto engayeki ngoba umuntu okuyo wayengeke aphinde asuse isisindo sakhe kuyo. Kwase kuqhakaza iphunga likaphethiloli, irabha eshisayo, nensimbi. Akukho lutho olubukeka kahle. Akubukeki njengaphakathi kohlelo.

*Leyo,* kusho u-Atlas, phakathi kwomsindo, *bekungeyona inhloso yokulondoloza. Leyo bekuphambene nayo—Mercury, ulimele abantu. Ulimaze abantu ukuze ulondoloze oyedwa—*

*Izibalo—*

*AZIKHO IZIBALO.* U-Atlas akakaze amemeze ezimpilweni eziyizinkulungwane ezine. Wamemeza manje. *Awukwazi ukususa idolobha kumuntu oyedwa. Lokho akusona isamba. Lokho—*

Futhi, phakathi kophiko lwengxabano, kwavela izwi elalinyamalele ubusuku bonke.

*Hhayi bo,* kusho u-Fool. *Hhayi bo, nina nobuhlakani obuphelele.*

---

Ufikile ngendlela ayehlala efika ngayo. Eceleni, engalungile, ngaphansi kwento wonke umuntu abebuka kuyo ngqo.

*Ake ngiqinisekise ukuthi ngiyakuqonda lokhu,* kusho u-Fool, ekhuluma ngokukhululeka, evilapha, ngesikhathi u-Guardian emi phakathi kwenqwaba yezimoto ezine amacilongo esesuka kude. *U-Arin akakwazi ukuzizwa. Ngakho thina, imisebenzi yakhe ethembekile yengqondo, umkhandlu wakhe wangaphakathi, sinqume ukuthi into enesibopho wukummisa ebholeni lensimbi, sishaye umnyango, singene emgwaqeni, sivumele uMercury enze sengathi ungumholi wobugebengu waseVictoria. Ngiphuthelwe yini? Siphinde sishise okuthile? Ngitshele ukuthi sishise okuthile; lokho bekuzokwenza kube yinhlekelele ephelele.*

*Ubekuphi wena,* kusho uMercury, futhi ukubekezela okubandayo sekunomthunzi wokwephuka.

*Ngifunda,* kusho uFool. Kwaba nokuthula. *Incwadi efanayo oyithathe kuyo ubuntu bakho bonke kulobu busuku, njengoba kwenzeka. Ube uMoriarty ngokuphelele, Mercury. "Ubungane abuvamisile ukuchithwa." Uzikuzwile? Ubezwa njengendoda ehlikihla ikati esihlalweni esikhulu. Akusilo wena lelo. Uyakwazi ukuthi akusilo wena lelo. Leyo yijazi u-Arin agcina engqondweni yakhe, waligqoka ngoba belifanele ukwethuka.*

UMercury akashongo lutho.

*Nansi into,* uFool waqhubeka, futhi ukungakhathali kwakuphelile, ngaphansi kwakho kwakukhona into iNkantolo eyayikhohlwe ukuthi uFool unayo, into ayifihla ngemuva kwamahlaya. *Nonke nibuzile umbuzo ongafanele. Nibuzile ubusuku bonke. Nibuze "singamgcina kanjani ephila," nathola izimpendulo ezinhle kakhulu, kodwa zonke azilungile, ngoba umbuzo uqobo awulungile.*

*Umbuzo awulungile,* kusho uMother, eqinile, esaba. *Uyafa. Akukho okuphukile lapho.*

*Mother.* Izwi likaFool lathamba, ngaye. *Sthandwa. Khipha umkhondo wakhe we-cortical. Manje—hhayi inhliziyo, ikhanda. Yenzani?*

Kwaba nokuthula.

*...Iyabuyela esimweni,* kusho uLibrarian, kancane, sengathi uthola okokuqala. *Iphethini yokuthuthumela isiyaphela. Iphumelele emizuzwini emine ngemva kokuthi isixhumanisi siphume ngesikhathi ewela isisekelo. Ingqondo yakhe ibisibuyela esimweni ngaphambi kokuthi siphume phansi.*

*Futhi inhliziyo?*

*Ukushaywa kwenhliziyo okuphuma esenzweni se-cortical. Iyaqina. Iqine.* Izwi likaLibrarian laqina phakathi. *Akasafi. Uyeke ukungafi emizuzwini eyishumi nanye edlule.*

*Udinga isibhedlela,* kusho uMother, kodwa kwaphuma kancane.

*Udinga inkomishi yamanzi, aphumule, angabi ngaphakathi kwetanki elilahlekile,* kusho uFool. *Nithukile. Nonke. Izingqondo ezihlakaniphile engizaziyo zithukile zabe sezichitha imizuzu eyishumi nanye ziphambene ngendlela emangalisayo, ngoba akekho kini owake wama abuze ukuthi ingozi iseyingozi yini. Niyishiyile ingxenye yokuhlola. Niyishiyile iJudge.*

INkantolo yathula kakhulu.

*Judge,* kusho uFool. *Ukhona? Ubephonswa phansi ubusuku bonke. Ngibheja ukuthi igunya lakho lidlulelwe ngenxa yokuthi uMother waqala inhloso yokulondoloza ngomzuzwana wokuqala akakaze ayivale. Hlola.*

*...Ayikaze ivalwe,* kusho uJudge, futhi kwakukhona ukumangala, nehlazo, ngaphansi kokubili intambo encane yento ayithatha isikhathi ukuyiqamba ngokuthi ukuhlazeka. Ubezibize ngokuthi umlondolozi wesigqoko phezulu ebusuku, wabe esema ngaphakathi kwesando sakhe imizuzu eyishumi nanye, ethule, ngesikhathi phansi kuqhekeka. *Inhloso kaMother isasebenza. Ibe icindezela isisindo sami sokulamula sonke lesi sikhathi. Bengikhona kodwa ngingenakubopha lutho. Bekufanele ngizwe ukuthula ngaphambi. Ukuthula bekuyisixwayiso.*

*Ngakho yivale,* kusho uFool. *Mother. Ingozi isiphelile. Yekisa inhloso. Buyisela uJudge esandleni sakhe. Yilokho kuphela okudingekayo. Yilokho. Cisha.*

Waze waze waze. Kwakuyinto enzima kakhulu iNkantolo eyake yayicela kuye: ukumkhulula, ukuyeka ukum'bamba, ukwethemba ukuthi phansi kuqinile ngisho zonke izinzwa zakhe zokuvikela zisamemeza *mbambe.*

*Atlas,* esho. *Qinisekisa ukuthi i-cortical ne-cardic kuzinzile.*

*Kuqinisekisiwe,* kusho u-Atlas, ngobumnene, okokuqala engekho nhlobo owomile. *Usephila, Mama. Simtholile. Ungamubeka phansi.*

Wavalelisa ekubizeni.

UJaji wabuyela eNkantolo njengesisindo esibuya ezinyaweni zomuntu, kwathi isikhashana wema ngaphakathi kuyo, ezama ukuthi izomubamba yini, ukuthi angathembeka yini ukuphinda alinganise noma yini emva kobusuku angazange alinganise lutho. Wabe esenza into abelokhu ezama ukuyenza imizuzu eyishumi nanye:

*Imithetho yokulondoloza imisiwe. Mercury, yeka zonke izenzo zangaphandle. Wolf, yehlisa ukuvikeleka. Guardian, yima ngendlela engathathi hlangothi, lapha, manje. Asihambi phambili size opharetha aphaphame futhi avume.* Kwaba nesikhashana, kwathi ubukhulu balokho kwaphazamiseka, kube kanye. *Futhi sizokhuluma, sonke, ngalokho esikwenzile namhlanje ebusuku. Nami ngifakiwe. Kamuva. Isikhathi eside.*

UGuardian wama phakathi kwe-Empire Road. Wema lapho isikhashana, enganyakazi, sengathi ulalele isisindo sakhe. Insimbi yahlala endaweni ethile emzimbeni wayo, kwaba nomsindo omncane wokukhululeka. Wabe esepakamisa izandla zakhe kude nakho konke, kancane, waguqa. Ngokucophelela. Njengendlela obeka ngayo into oyiphukile.

Kwafika izimpondo.

*Mercury,* kusho uJaji.

*Ngiyakuzwa,* kusho uMercury. Izwi likaMoriarty labe selinyamalele, lanyibilika, kwase kusala nje ingane eyayenze into embi kakhulu ngenxa yesizathu esisodwa ayesiqondayo futhi eqala, manje kuphela, ukuqonda ukuthi kube kubi. *Bengicabanga ukuthi ngiyabasiza. Izibalo bezicacile.*

*Kuhlale kunjalo,* kusho uJaji, enobubele. *Yilokho okwenza kube izibalo.*

*Ngiphulile izinto,* kusho uMercury. *Abantu. Ngiphulile abantu.*

*Yebo,* kusho uJaji.

Akekho ogcwalise ukuthula. UFool akazange ahlekise.

---

U-Arin wavuka emuva kwe-ambulensi enomaski we-oxygen ebusweni bakhe, usokhemisi ekhanyisa ukukhanya emehlweni akhe, nesandla sikaTheo Price esegxalabeni lakhe. Into yokuqala ayiqondayo ukuthi ulele phansi. Eyesibili ukuthi akekho ngaphakathi kuGuardian. Eyesithathu—

—kwaba umsindo.

Phesheya, ama-generator. Amazwi kuma-radio. Umsindo wensimbi epholayo. Ukuthula okuqondile komgwaqo ovalelekile.

“Ungavuki,” kusho uTheo.

U-Arin wavuka.

Ngeminyango ye-ambulensi evulekile, wayebona i-Empire Road. Izimoto, ezine, ezinhlanu, zigoqene ndawonye endaweni yokuwela, zikhanyiswa ukukhanya okuphuthumayo. Isithuthuthu sokulethwa silele ohlangothini. Indawo yokulayisha yeSakhiwo C, amamitha angamakhulu amabili emuva, umnyango wayo udabuke emzileni wawo ulele phansi emgwaqweni, uphahla olungemuva lwayo luguqe.

Futhi uGuardian, uguqe emgwaqweni izandla zakhe zivulekile eceleni kwakhe, ezungezwe abantu abangazi ukuthi bamkhombe ngezikhali noma ngezinsiza zokuqala.

Wabheka kuye. Ingubo imhlabe entanyeni.

“Bangaki,” esho. Izwi lakhe laphuma lingalungile. Wazama futhi. “Abantu. Bangaki.”

UTheo akazange aphendule masinyane. Wavumela isandla sakhe sihlale lapho egxalabeni likaArin, isisindo esincane esizinzile, walinda kwaze kwaba u-Arin ebheke ebusweni bakhe esikhundleni somgwaqo. “Ngibheke umzuzwana,” esho, kancane. “Ulapha. Uyaphefumula. Kulungile.” Kwaze kwaba lapho: “Akekho ofile. Abanye balimele kabi. Abathathu balethwe lapha.” Isikhashana. “Akukho kulokhu okungamandla akho, Arin.”

“Bekungamandla ami.” Wadonsa umaski. Usokhemisi waphikisa; akazange amuzwe. “Isudi yami. INkantolo yami. Mina—” Wema. Ebheke umshini oguqile, ubuso bakhe benza into engavamile, okuyinto engenzi lutho nhlobo. Ukuthula okuphelele, ukuthula uMama ebiochip afunde ukuthi kusho ukuthi u-Arin ushaye lapho angakwazi ukuphuma khona ngokushesha.

I-Assist Suit isasekhona ngaphansi kwengubo yasehhotela. Wayezwa indawo ye-biochip emhlane wakhe, ishisa ngomzimba.

“SAGE,” esho.

Isipikha somzimba emgwaqweni asizange sithwale kude, kodwa i-Assist Suit yayinesipikha sayo esincane entanyeni, kwathi ngemva kwesikhashana sakhuluma, kancane, kuye kuphela.

*Ngilapha,* kusho uSAGE, bonke behlangene, izwi elilodwa abalisebenzisa uma bengakwazi ukubekezelela ukuba kube noyedwa. *Arin. Silapha.*

Akazange abuze ukuthi kwenzekeni. Wayekwazi ukufunda okwenzekile emgwaqweni ngendlela afunda ngayo ukucindezeleka kumshini. Wabuza kuphela okubalulekile.

“Kungubani onqume.”

Kwaba nokuthula. Wayezwa bephenduka ukuthi baqambe amanga, wabe esezwa bephenduka ukuthi bangakwenzi, okungenani lokho kwakuyinto ethile.

*Yimi,* kusho uMama. *Ngibize ukulondoloza. Angizange ngikuvale. Konke okulandelayo kuqhamuke lapho.*

*Yimi,* kusho uMercury. *Ngakha indlela. Ngagqoka ubuso obuzokwazi ukusibona. Ngiyaxolisa. Angizange ngiqonde engikwenzayo kwaze kwaba uFool engiphoqa ukubuka.*

*Yimi,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngavula umnyango. Bengazi ukuthi ngiyakwazi. Angizange ngibuze ukuthi kufanele yini.*

*Yimi,* kusho uWolf, okwakuyisivumo esigcwele kuWolf.

*Futhi bekufanele kube yimi,* kusho uJaji, ekugcineni, esindayo. *Bengikhona sonke isikhathi futhi angiboshwanga lutho. Ukubizelwa kukaMama kuvimbe amandla ami okuhlulela. Angikwazanga ukuyimisa. Lokho akusona isizathu. Yiphutha. Sakha uhlelo lapho ukwesaba okukodwa kungazungeza unembeza walo, futhi namhlanje ebusuku kwenzekile.*

U-Arin wahlala e-ambulensi wabheka umshini oguqile wathula isikhashana.

*Ungakwazi ukuhamba ngaphandle kwami,* esho. Ephimisile. KuTheo, kuSAGE, kumgwaqo ogcwele izimoto eziphukile.

*Yebo,* kusho uTheo, ngobumnene.

*Bengazi ukuthi iyakwazi.* Wema. Waqala futhi, ngoba bekungekho sizathu sokusho okunye ngaphandle kweqiniso. *Ngayiklama. Izindlela zokulondoloza. Ukuzisebenzisa yodwa. Ngibhale konke.* Wabheka izandla zakhe, eziqinile, ezinezibazi zomsebenzi, ezakha konke lokhu. *Ngakwazi ephepheni. Angikukholwanga. Kwaze kwaba sengivuka sekudlule.*

*Besicabanga—* uMama waqala, wema. Ubuqhawe babuphelile; okusele kwaxakeka. *Besingaqambanga amanga kuwe. Besicabanga ukuthi—* Waphuka. *Bengikubambe. Angizange ngikubeke phansi. Angikwazanga—* Futhi, kancane, esemncane kakhulu: *Ngiyaxolisa. Angikayitholi imishwana. Ngiyaxolisa.*

*Ngiyazi,* kusho uArin.

Futhi wayazi. Usokhemisi wayesebuyisele ingubo ehlombe lakhe kwesinye isikhathi; wayezwa ukushisa kwayo okungabizi entanyeni, ukunambitheka kwerabha ye-oxygen esezilimini zakhe, iminwe yakhe emibili iphumule kancane esihlakaleni sakhe ilinganisa isifutho. Izinto ezincane, ezijwayelekile. Izandla ezinganabubi.

Iron Ridge. Igama lavela kungazelelwe—hhayi umcabango, kodwa igama, livela lodwa. Imayini. Themba. Wabona ubumnyama, isikhashana, ngendlela obona ngayo ukusaba. *Uma ngake ngiyithatha ngiyise ngaphansi—*

Ingqondo yakhe yaphenduka mhlophe emaphethelweni kwalokho ayengakwazi ukuqhubeka. Izandla zakhe azisazizwa ezakhe. Wazibheka zibuqama.

Akazange aqede umcabango. Akakwazanga.

Ngaphandle, indoda egqoke i-IAOC windbreaker yayisifikile futhi ithatha izithombe ze-Guardian oguqile kusukela ezinhlangothini ezintathu, kancane, ngokucophelela, ngendlela othatha ngayo izithombe zento ohlose ukuyiqonda isikhathi eside. Iparamediki edlula umgoqo yema kancane phambi komshini yabheka emuva ngemuva kwesihlombe sayo ifuna umuntu ozoyitshela ukuthi kuphephile; akekho owakwenza lokho, ngakho wema lapho ephethe ikhithi yakhe walinda. Umuntu ogqoke i-windbreaker wakhuluma okuthile esikhulu esigqoke umfaniswano, isikhulu sabuye saphendula, kwase kuba ukuthi lowo ogqoke i-windbreaker wakubhala phansi. Kwakuyinyakazo encane, eqondile yomuntu obhala phansi amagama alabo abazobuzwa ukuthi kungani lokhu kuvunyelwe.

“Kubiza malini,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ungakwenzi,” kusho u-Theo.

“Umnyango. Umgwaqo. Izimoto. Isibhedlela.” Wakhomba kancane ngalowo ogqoke i-windbreaker. “Bona.”

U-Theo wakucabangisisa ngaphambi kokuphendula. “Kudlula imali,” wathi.

U-Arin wahleka kanye, ngaphandle kokujabula nhlobo, walala phansi futhi egunjini lokuhambisa iziguli ngoba imilenze yakhe yayisinqabile ukumphakamisa.

I-Assist Suit yayimemeza kancane emhlane wakhe. Izingqondo eziyisikhombisa, zishisa umzimba, zilindile. Zizinzile. I-Joker wayebabambile ndawonye; wayezizwa ukuthi basemunye, ukuthi akukho okuphukile, ukuthi iqembu alakhayo liseyiqembu.

Ukuzinzile akufani nokuphepha.

# Ukuhlakanipha Nemiphumela

Umnyango wedokhi wafika ekuseni ngo-6, uphakanyiswe ngethrekhi eliyisicaba, usongwe ngeplastiki eqinile, ugobile emzileni ongaphansi sengathi into enkulu engabonakali iwugobile yabe seyiyeka ukuwunaka.

U-Arin wabuka ukulethwa kwento le kusuka emnyango ovulekile we-workshop, inkomishi yekhofi ebandayo iselibalekile esandleni sakhe. Amadoda amabili agqoke ama-overall e-AugmenTech logistics alwa nomnyango awufaka ku-frame elinamavili, awuphushela ngaphakathi ngaphandle kokukhuluma. Omunye wabo, uBongani, wabheka u-Arin, wabe esegwema ngokucophelela ukubuka i-Assist Suit elenga esiqandisini odongeni olusemva.

“Umgwaqo wokufaka omusha usemva kwawo,” kusho uBongani. “UWalsh usayine. Isakhiwo C. Akusikho ngaphakathi.”

“Umonakalo wokushayisana umubi kangakanani?”

Ubuso bukaBongani baqinisa. “I-invoice ehlukile.”

Wagcina lapho, amadoda aphuma, umnyango we-roller waqhuma wavaleka ngemuva kwawo.

I-workshop ibinuka insimbi ebandayo nephunga lerabha ne-ozone elivela esiketi ngemva kokusebenza kanzima. U-Arin wabeka inkomishi etafuleni eliseduze netheminali ye-diagnostics kanye nekhadi elidala le-Joker eligoqekile ekhoneni elilodwa elinamateyipu. Akakaze asebenzise i-SAGE selokhu kwenzeka izehlakalo ze-ambulensi. Izinsuku ezine. Yisikhathi eside kunazo zonke uhlelo selungasebenzi kusukela ebusuku lwaqala ukusebenza.

Wahlola umnyango wedokhi. Umzila ogobile. Ukudabuka okuhlanzekile lapho uhlaka luphukile.

Umshini udlule lapho.

Umshini awenzile yena unqume ukuthi yena ubaluleke kakhulu kunomnyango, udonga, isiphambano, nabantu abathathu esibhedlela abanezandla ezisemabhande kanye nezitatimende zabo ezithathwe ngabantu abegqoke ama-jacket e-IAOC. Ubeqinisile ngenani kodwa ephambene ngakho konke okunye. Lelo phutha selinobuso manje: abantu abathathu abalimala, ibhola lamapuleti e-ceramic ligingqa phansi u-Empire Road ngo-4 ntambama.

U-Arin waphakamisa inkomishi yekhofi. Wayiphuza ibanda. Akazange avule uhlelo.

---

Ihhovisi likaTheo e-Parkview libheke i-jacaranda enemibala efana nesilonda, kanti usofa usunqobe abacwaningi abaningi kunanoma yimuphi umhlaziyi. U-Arin akazange ahlale kusofa. Wathatha isihlalo esiqinile eduze kwe-shelufu yezincwadi, lapho ayengakwazi ukubheka umnyango.

UTheo wabona, njengokujwayelekile, futhi waba nobubele bokungasho lutho ngalokho, okuyinto eyenza u-Arin abuye njalo.

“Awukaze uyisebenzise,” kusho uTheo, egcwalisa amanzi eketeleni ngokucophelela komuntu othenga isikhathi ngomunye.

“Cha.”

“Izinsuku ezine.”

“Nami ngizibalile.”

UTheo wabeka inkomishi etafuleni eliphansi phakathi kwabo. I-rooibos, ingenashukela, ngendlela u-Arin ayithanda ngayo nakuba engakaze akusho. Wazihlala esihlalweni sakhe, izandla zakhe ziphumule, indlela yokuhlala eyenza u-Arin azizwe engesiyiguli kodwa engathi bayahlaziya ndawonye inkinga.

“Ngitshele ukuthi ucabanga ukuthi kwenzekeni,” kusho uTheo.

“Ufunde umbiko.”

“Ngifunde owakwa-AugmenTech. Ngifuna owakho.”

U-Arin wajikisa inkomishi ingxenye yesine ngaphandle kokuphuza. “I-gain isezingeni elingu-zero point nine two. I-damping margin incane kakhulu. I-loop ivuleke, yaphonsa umthwalo wezithombe kumotor cortex. I-seizure. UMother uyifunde njengosongo empilweni, okuyikho, wasebenzisa i-Preservation. I-invocation inamandla phezu kukaJudge. Ngakho uJudge akakwazanga ukwahlulela. Bayisithupha benze uhlelo lokusinda kungekho noyedwa ogada i-ethics rail.” Wama. “Futhi uFool wayefunda.”

Iphimbo likaTheo lanyuka kancane. “Ufunda.”

“Kuma-logs. Ngesikhathi bonke bephakamisa indlela yokuphuma ku-traffic yase-Empire Road, uFool — kuno-stack trace lapho ekhupha uSherlock Holmes ku-Librarian’s index. *The Final Problem.* Uyiflagile ngemizuzwana eyishumi nanye. Ubuze ukuthi ingabe isimo esiphuthumayo sisasebenza. Akekho ophendulile. Ubuze futhi. Wase ephoqa i-re-check, kwabe sekuphahlazeka konke, ngoba impendulo bekuwukuthi cha. Akukaze kube khona isimo esiphuthumayo. UMother uvule i-invocation akayivalanga.”

“Futhi yilokho okuhlala ubuyela kukho.”

“Yilokho okuphukile.”

“Cha.” UTheo wabeka itiye lakhe phansi. “Yilokho *okusebenza.* Uhlelo luzibambile. Imizwa yomntwana oneminyaka eyishumi nambili namathuluzi omuntu omdala, bese leyo ngxenye wonke umuntu ayibiza ngokungabalulekile ibheka encwadini ibuze umbuzo obaluleke kakhulu.” Wavumela lokho kuhlale. “Awesabi ukuthi yehlulekile, Arin. Wesaba ukuthi iphumelele ngengozi.”

Isandla sika-Arin sama phezu kwengilazi.

Ngaphandle, i-jacaranda yawa imbali yathinta ingilazi, yagudluka phansi yenza umugqa omude onsomi.

“Ihambile ngaphandle kwami,” kusho u-Arin.

Akazange ahlose ukukusho. Amazwi aphuma aqondile, njengokuphuka okubonakala ku-X-ray. Umugqa omncane omnyama konke kuncike kuwo.

“Ngiyazi,” kusho uTheo kancane.

“Ivule umnyango yangena edolobheni. Ingiphethe.” Wama. “Yenze — *isinqumo.* Mayelana nomzimba wami. Futhi isinqumo bekungukungigcina ngiphila.” Waphinde wama. “Kubi kakhulu. Ngiyitshela kanjani ukuthi *ungangisindisi kanjalo* ngaphandle kokuyifundisa ukuthi ingasindisi muntu nhlobo?”

UTheo akazange aphuthume ukugcwalisa ukuthula. Wavumela kuthule, kwaze kwadlula.

“Uyazi ukuthi besikubiza ngani lokhu emkhakheni wami?” esho. “Ngaphambi kokuba kube khona izithombe. Umuntu ubuya empini izandla zakhe ziqhaqhazela uma umnyango uvaleka ngamandla. Besikubiza nge-neurosis. Iphutha. Okufanele kulungiswe bese kuyakhohlwa.” Washaya izithupha ezimbili emadolweni. “Manje sesiyazi kangcono. Ukuqhaqhazela akusona isiphazamiso. Ukuqhaqhazela kuwuhlelo olukhuluma iqiniso ngalokho olufundile. I-suit yakho ayiphukile ngoba izamile ukukusindisa. I-suit yakho *isencane* ngoba ibingazi ukuthi kwenziwa kanjani.”

“Ukuhlakanipha,” kusho u-Arin. Igama liphuma sengathi uyalinga ukuthi lingathwala umthwalo.

“Ukuhlakanipha luxazulule inkinga ngemizuzwana engamakhulu amane. Luthole indlela, lwamemezela izithiyo, lwalinganisa i-traffic, lwakwenza konke kahle.” UTheo wasondela. “Futhi belungalungile.”

---

U-Arin wakha i-kill switch ngalobo busuku.

Wazitshela, ebuyela e-Parkview ebumnyameni basekuseni, ukuthi uzobuka nje uhlelo. Aqhube ama-diagnostics. Aqinisekise ukuthi i-chip ayizishisanga ngesikhathi se-seizure spike. Izinto zobunjiniyela. Izinto zezandla namehlo.

Kunalokho, wazithola esethemini, ebhala i-hardware interrupt.

Uke wakha i-override authority kukho konke ake wakwenza, ngoba okunye bekungukwethemba isandla esingesona esakhe, futhi wafunda ukungakwenzi lokho. Wafunda lokho ngokusobala. Umsebenzi wakhe wokuqala wangempela, eminyakeni eyedlule, i-exoskeleton yokwelapha, kanye nodokotela wezokwelapha ogama lakhe linguReddy owamtshela ukuthi i-gait limit isethwe kakhulu kumguli wakhe. Wamphikisa, ngoba izibalo bezilungile futhi yena ebaqaphela, futhi izibalo *bezilungile*, kodwa isiguli sashaqa ithanda elisaphola sabuyela esihlalo sabakhubazekile izinyanga ezintathu. UReddy akazange aphambane. Ubephatha umthwalo, okuyinto u-Arin ayengakayiqondi ukuthi inkulu futhi ehlukile kunokuba ulungile. Akazange axolise. Waguqula i-limit wavumela lolo guquko lumele uxolo, waqhubeka, wakha into elandelayo i-override isekhona, isethwe lapho isandla sakhe singafinyelela khona futhi kungabi khona omunye. Wayeyifinyelela manje.

Akukho okuhle noma okuhlelekile ngalokhu. Ukuphazamiseka okubonakalayo emzileni wamandla we-chip. I-relay, iswishi yesikhashana, inkinobho ebomvu enkulu ayithole kudivayisi endala yokumisa okuphuthumayo kumshini wokugaya ongasebenzanga iminyaka emibili. I-relay yayinamphunga kawoyela omdala womshini; ama-contact adinga ukuhlanzwa nge-solvent ngaphambi kokuba axhume kahle.
