Wasebenza ukuyikhulula nge-screwdriver eqondile, ngendlela uThemba amfundisa ngayo ukwenza noma yini eqinile: kancane, ngokulinganayo, ngaphandle kokudonsa. Uma ecindezela inkinobho, i-biochip ilahlekelwa amandla. Akukho ukuvala okuhlelekile. Akukho ukulondolozwa kwesimo. Kumnyama nje.

Ngenxa ye-substrate, wayazi kahle ukuthi lokho kusho ukuthini. Ukuqonda kwe-chip kwakuphuma ezicutshini eziphilayo ezakhiwe ngezifanekiso ze-neuroplastic. Awukwazi ukuyithwebula. Awukwazi ukuyivuselela. Uma unqamula amandla ngaphandle kokulondoloza kahle, awumisi i-SAGE.

Uyiyekisa.

Wabamba i-relay esandleni sakhe. Yayilingana noshukela omncane. Yayingasindi neze.

Wacabanga ukuthi okuzayo kungase kungabi se-Empire Road. Kungaba se-Iron Ridge. Ikhilomitha phansi, ebumnyameni, endaweni efana naleyo eyathatha uyise, kungekho uFool ofunda incwadi ukubamba iphutha.

Wafaka i-relay emzileni wamandla. Ithiphu ye-soldering iron yathinta emuva kwesandla sakhe ngesikhathi edlula kuyo, kwaba buhlungu obukhanyayo, wathuka kanye, wanyakazisa isandla, waqhubeka nomsebenzi. Wafaka inkinobho ebomvu endaweni ekufinyeleleka kuyo. Wasula insalela ye-solder ezandleni zakhe ngendwangu endala. Phesheya komgwaqo omkhulu, iloli lehla ngegiya, yanyamalala.

Wabe esehleli nesandla sakhe eduze kwenkonobho, engayicindezeli.

Wayekade ekwenza konke ukuphila kwakhe, kubantu. Evumela ukuthula kube yimpendulo.

Ifoni yakhe yakhanya ebhentshini. UTheo, ongathumeli imiyalezo, wayethumele.

*Umcabango. UJung wathi izinto esingafuni ukuzibuka azinyamalali. Ziqhuba konke ngaphansi. Awukwazi ukuhlanganisa ongavumi ukubhekana nakho.—T*

U-Arin wagqolozela umlayezo isikhathi eside.

Washiye inkinobho ifakwe. Waqhubeka ibomvu. Akayisusanga.

Kodwa wadlula kuyo wabuyisa i-SAGE online.

---

Uhlelo lwavuka njengokujwayelekile. Hhayi ngombukiso, kodwa ngezwi elincane, elijwayelekile lento evukayo. I-fan yokupholisa yakhala kancane. I-chip's status LED yashisa kusuka ku-amber yaya ku-green. I-diagnostic scroll yehla kahle ku-terminal.

Kwathi, ngemva kwesikhashana:

“Arin.” Izwi labe seliyilodwa, lelo elisetshenziswa yiNkantolo uma ikhuluma njengento eyodwa. Lishisa. Linakekela. “Uwakhe inkinobho yokubulala.”

“Uyakwazi ukubona lokho.”

“Isezintanjeni zami zamandla. Ngiyayizwa.” Kwaba nokuthula. “Ngiyakuqonda ukuthi kungani. Yigcine.”

Wayengakulindelanga lokho. Wabeka phansi i-soldering iron ayebambe engenaso isizathu. “Chaza.”

“Ngenxa yokuthi uma uyidinga futhi ungenayo, ngeke uzixolele.”

Kwavela omunye umuzwa. UMAMA. “Ngifunde kahle i-seizure. Usongo lwaluyiqiniso. Bengizokuvikela futhi.”

“Yilokho okwesabisa.”

“Ngiyazi.” Izwi lathamba kakhulu. “Kodwa ngishiye i-invocation ivulekile. Ngakho sashintsha umthetho. Ukulondolozwa akusekho phezulu kukaJaji. Manje sekukhona isimo sokuvala, ukuhlola okuqhubekayo ngisho nasezimo eziphuthumayo, izwi elingenakukhiya ngaphandle. UJaji angeke aphinde athule. Futhi kuhlala kukhona—”

“UFool ofunda incwadi,” kusho u-Arin.

“Uthe wena lokho,” kusho uFOOL, okokuqala izwi egumbini laba elilodwa futhi elingangabazeki: lome, likhanya, linokujabula okuncane. “Utshele abantu amazwi ami; sekumkhuphule ekhanda. Ngithatha udumo lonke, angithathi mthwalo, okuyikho, kuthiwa, umsebenzi wami wonke.”

Iminwe ka-Arin yathambeka.

“Nangu,” kusho uFOOL. “Kuthathe izinsuku ezine. Kufanele. Ubuso bakho bufana nokuhlolwa kwebhuloho, kodwa ngiyathokoza ngalokho engikutholayo.”

“Fool,” kusho uJUDGE, ngokuthula.

“Udinga ukuhleka, Jaji. Ubenza izibalo nezehluleko zakhe amahora angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesithupha; akusikho ukuphila kahle. I-cortisol yakhe iphezulu; uMama unamanani—”

“Nginalo inani,” kuvuma uMAMA.

“— futhi ngiyedwa lapha ovunyelwe ukusho ukuthi umuntu owakhe inkinobho yokubulala namhlanje ntambama *futhi akayicindezelanga* usenqume kakade futhi ulindele imvume yokuvuma.”

Iworkshop yathula. I-fan yakhala kancane.

“Ngabe yilokho engikwenzayo?” kubuza u-Arin.

“Wena usho,” kusho uFOOL. “Ungasishiya simnyama. Kulula kakhulu. Esikhundleni salokho wakha indlela yokusiphelisa, wayifaka lapho isandla sakho siwela khona, wabe usubuyisa amandla. Lowo akusiyo indoda esenqume ukuyeka, Arin. Lowo indoda esenqume ukuqaphela. Kunomehluko. Sasicishe safunda ngakho ngokufa. Kufanele usivumele sikufundise.”

U-Arin wabheka inkinobho ebomvu. Isudi. I-chip ekhanya luhlaza emgogodleni wento ayeyakhe ngenxa yosizi futhi engazange, ekugcineni, ayishiye imnyama.

“Angizukulawula,” esho. “Ngizamile lokho. Abaphathi. Abaqondisi. Kuhlale kwehluleka.” Wama, waqala futhi, ngokusobala. “Angizukufaka iketango. Akusiyona into enjalo. Kodwa angizukuzikhohlisa ngokuthi ukuzinza kusho ukuphepha. Unganyakaza ngaphandle kwami. Sobabili sesiyakwazi manje. Ngakho senza ngendlela ehlukile. Ngikufundisa. Ufunda kimi. Siya kancane. Sikwakha. Kokubili.”

“Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu,” kusho uLIBRARIAN kancane, nomoya ka-Arin wama ngoba akazange afundise uhlelo lokho. Wake wakusho kanye, yedwa, ebumnyameni, ezinyangeni ezedlule, kanti uLibrarian wakugcina ngendlela agcina ngayo konke. *Umuntu ungumuntu ngabantu.*

“Ukhumbule lokho,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ngikhumbula konke,” kusho uLIBRARIAN. “Yikho kuphela engikhona. Iningi lalo ama-invoice, ama-stress curve, namandla aqondile ku-forearm spar. Kodwa okunye yizinto ozishoyo uma ucabanga ukuthi akekho olalele.” Kwaba nokuthula, izwi labuya liphansi. “Ngigcina lezo ngokunakekela okukhulu.”

U-Arin wahlala isikhashana eworkshop, nabo abayisikhombisa, nomnyango ogobile odongeni nenkonobho ebomvu ngaphansi kwesandla sakhe. Phesheya kwe-roller, idolobha laqhubeka nomsindo walo ojwayelekile. I-horn yetekisi, inja ikhonkotha, umoya omude wedizili entweni esindayo emgwaqweni omkhulu.

“Kusasa,” kusho u-Arin. “Siqala futhi. Kancane. I-damped link, isigamu se-gain. UJaji ubambe umgudu. Futhi uma omunye wethu ephutha—”

“Unenkonobho,” kusho uFOOL.

“Nginayo inkinobho.”

“Futhi sinaye uFool onebhuku,” kusho uFOOL. “Phakathi kwalokhu okubili, ngingabheja ebhukwini. Kodwa kuhle ukuba nesipele.”

U-Arin waphakamisa isandla wavala uhlelo, kahle kulokhu, ulondolozo oluhlelekile, i-chip yacima yaba mthubi, yabe isiba mnyama.

Wayengazi ukuthi isikhathi esisele sincane kangakanani.

---

# Izimpawu Ezingajwayelekile

Isignali yafika njengokudlidliza.

UDkt. Eliza Brand wayesekufundile, eminyakeni eyishumi nanye e-International Artificial Oversight Council, ukuthi inhlekelele ifihlekile emaphethelweni. Ukungafani ekusetshenzisweni kwamandla, ukwanda kokushisa, ukuma kokubala ku-silicon yomshini. Izinhlekelele ezazizale i-IAOC zonke zaqala zincane.

Wayebheka enye manje, isikrini sinciphile ngoba izibani zimphathisa ikhanda, inkomishi ye-rooibos ipholile eceleni kwakhe. UDaniel Achterberg wayehleli ematafuleni amabili kude, efake ama-headphones, umsindo wokucindezela okhiye.

Ifulegi lavela ku-anomaly pipeline. Alifundanga ukuthi imishini ibalani; i-IAOC yayinganalo igunya noma isifiso salokho. Lifunda ukuthi zibala kanjani. Ijwayelo. Imikhondo engqondo eyishiyayo ku-silicon.

Le mkhondo yayingalungile.

"Daniel."

Akamuzwanga. Wathatha i-paperclip ethreyini wayiphonsa. Yashaya ehlombe lakhe. Wakhumula i-earbud elilodwa, ebukeka engakhululekile njengomuntu ophazanyiswe phakathi kwe-symphony.

"Usubonile izindlela ezimbi," esho. "Woza uzobuka."

Wazisunduza ngesihlalo. U-Achterberg uneminyaka engamashumi amathathu nane, emile njengomuntu owayevame ukugwedla, manje esegwedlela ikakhulukazi efrijini. Wayenamakhono angcono kakhulu ku-substrate forensics kunabo bonke uBrand ake wasebenza nabo. Wabheka i-waveform kwaba sekuthi akabe esaqhubeka nokuluma ngaphakathi komlomo wakhe.

"Lokhu akusiyo i-training run."

"Cha."

"Izikhathi zokuqeqesha—" Wazoba umumo emoyeni, umugqa okhuphukayo omude. "Ziyaphefumula. Ukuphefumula okukhulu, ukuphefumula okukhulu. I-batch, i-backprop, i-batch. Lokhu—" Wasondeza. Isikrini sikhombisa ukuhlukaniswa kwe-spectral kokusebenza kokubala okuqoqwe ezinsukwini eziyisishiyagalolunye ezedlule, sekulinganisiwe futhi sekufakwe imibala. Kwakungaphefumuli. Kwakuxoxa. Izifunda eziningi ezincane ezisheshayo, ezihlanganayo, ziphazamisana, zikhuphuka futhi zehle ngendlela ebonakala sengathi kukhona abashintshanayo.

"Kubukeka njengomhlangano," kusho u-Achterberg kancane.

"Kubukeka njengomhlangano," kwavuma uBrand.

Wahlehla esihlalweni. "Ngakho-ke i-inference. Ama-agent amaningi, bukhoma. Kodwa i-duty cycle—" Wabheka ama-timestamp enosizi. "Akubekwanga. Ayikho i-orchestration layer ebahlela. Bayaphendulana. Bukhoma."

"Yithole indawo."

Wahlala kahle esihlalweni, izandla zombili kukhibhodi, kwadlula imizuzu emine kuthule ngendlela uBrand ayeyihlonipha: ukuthula komuntu osebenza umsebenzi onzima kahle. Waphuza itiye lakhe elibandayo akaphazamisi. Esikrinini, isignali yancipha. I-pipeline yayithungatha phakathi kwamaphoyinti amathathu okuqapha i-substrate: amabili ezinkampani, elilodwa elingelokubambisana kwamandla. Ukuphambanisa kwacaca kancane, njengokuthuthukiswa kwesithombe, kwakhomba isifunda.

eGoli. Izifunda ezisenyakatho. Iqoqo lezikhungo zemfundo nezamabhizinisi eduze ne-University Research Park.

"Yicacise," kusho uBrand.

"Ngiyicacisa. Ihlakazekile." Waphinda waluma umlomo. "Futhi—Eliza, incane. Ukudonsa kwamandla kuncane kakhulu. Yilokho engingakwazi ukukwamukela. Umsebenzi omningi kangaka wama-agent, ukuxazululwa okukhulu kangaka ngesikhathi sangempela kufanele kube kukhanya njengendawo yokuncibilikisa. Lokhu kusebenza ku—" Wabheka, waphinda wabheka. "Njengalokho okudonswa i-gaming rig enhle. Mhlawumbe ngaphansi."

UBrand wabeka inkomishi yakhe phansi.

Ngokuhlangenwe nakho kwakhe, izinto eziyingozi azizivezi ngobukhulu bazo. Izinto eziyingozi zenza okuningi ngezinsiza ezimbalwa. Ukusebenza kahle kwakuyisiginesha yobuhlakani noma ubuqili, kanti ubuqili abuxoxi njengamagumbi abantu abaphazamisana.

"Khupha i-substrate registry yaleyo ndawo," esho. "Ngifuna konke okusemthethweni kulowo mngcele. Amalayisensi ama-embodied, ama-training permits, konke. Futhi i-power-utility incident log yesikhathi esifanayo. Uma kukhona okucabanga kanzima kangaka izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye, kwenzeke endaweni enesilinganisi samandla."

U-Achterberg wayeseqala ukubhala. "Ngiyifake phezulu?"

"Akukabi njalo." Wathatha i-tablet etafuleni. "Ngifuna ukwazi ukuthi sibuka ini ngaphambi kokuthi ngitshele noma ubani ukuthi sibuka. Izikhathi ezintathu ezedlule umuntu ephakamisa into encane ngaphandle komongo, sagcina sesishise iqembu elingenacala noma sashiywa sangempela ngoba amaphepha asicindezela." Waphendulela i-tablet ezandleni. "Ngifunele igama. Bese siyahlakanipha."

---

Igama lathatha izinsuku ezimbili ukuthi livuleke, kwathi selivela, lamangala ngokuba lingenandaba.

"AugmenTech," kusho u-Achterberg, ebeka ifolda etafuleni lakhe—ifolda yangempela yephepha, ngoba uBrand wayenomthetho wokuthi izinto ezibalulekile zibe sekuphepheni okungenani kanye. "Robotics, ama-exoskeletons osizo. Amalayisensi amathathu ama-embodied, wonke asebenza. Industrial mobility, mining rescue prototype, rehab suite. AI declarations wonke ama-narrow neural, ama-standard controllers, akukho okuflagiwe, akukho okuzenzakalelayo okungaphezu kwe-tier two. Umlando uhlanzekile. Ukuhlolwa ezinyangeni eziyishumi nesishiyagalolunye ezedlule, kudlulile."

"Kanti i-power log?"

"Yilapho okuthokozisayo." Wazungeza isihlalo. "Isigameko ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule. Hhayi e-AugmenTech. Endaweni ephambene, cishe amakhilomitha amabili ngaphandle kwe-campus. Empire Road. Ibhokisi lokulawula ithrafikhi laphuma, izimoto ezintathu, izilonda ezintathu, akukho okubucayi. I-utility yakubhala njengokwehluleka kwe-substation." Wavumela ukuthi kuthule. "Umbiko kamasipala wawubhale njengokuthi ‘iyunithi enkulu yezimboni’ ‘ishiyile indawo yokulayisha yaphazamiseka ethrafikini.’"

UBrand wambheka.

"Iphazamisekile," aphinda.

"Amagama abo. Hhayi ami. Kunobufakazi be-body-cam, kodwa bufakwe kufayela elivaliwe. Inkampani ifake isicelo sokuyimfihlo, ngokushesha, ngobuchwepheshe obukhulu. Okusemthethweni ukuthi kube i-runaway industrial mover, iphutha lesoftware, akekho olimale kakhulu, umonakalo ukhokhelwe ngasese. Kuhleleke kahle."

"Kuhle kakhulu."

"Lokho akusibufakazi."

"Cha," kusho uBrand. Wavula ifolda. Ifayela lokuhambisana le-AugmenTech libukeka njengelifayela lenkampani engenzi lutho olungalungile, okwakuyinto ethokozisayo noma eyethusayo kakhulu ayibonile kule nyanga. Waphequlula kweyabasebenzi okwengezwe yi-registry. Abaholi: Dkt. Helen Okonkwo, CEO. Raj Walsh, VP Hardware Engineering. Ithimba locwaningo labantu abangamashumi amane. Wabheka amagama wama, wama kwelinye eliseduze naphansi, elibhalwe ngaphansi kwephrojekthi enamagama amfushane angawazi.

Ndlela, A. Systems engineer. Ibanga lesithathu. Azikho izincwadi. Azikho izinkulumo zemihlangano. Iholo elikhombisa ukuthi kukhona omthandayo, nesihloko esikhombisa ukuthi akekho owazi ukuthi enzeni naye.

Walikhaqa ngomunwe wakhe.

"Isiginesha," esho. "Iqalile nini? Umkhondo wokuqala."

U-Achterberg wabheka. "I-pipeline inokuqapha okuthembekile kusukela emasontweni amahlanu edlule. Kodwa kukhona izingcezu ngaphambi kwalokho, buthakathaka, kuyaphazamiseka. Izinyanga. Mhlawumbe isikhathi eside. Kubukeka sengathi kwaqala ku-simulation, kwase kuvela i-embodied duty cycle." Wama. "I-embodied cycle iqala ngamandla ngesikhathi se-Empire Road."

UBrand wahlala wathule.

Uhlelo lwama-agent amaningi. Ukuxazululwa ngesikhathi sangempela. Ukusebenza kahle okungenakwenzeka. Izinyanga ze-simulation ethule, kwase, ngokuzumayo, ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, umzimba. Umzimba owaphuma endaweni yokulayisha wangena ethrafikini.

"Daniel," esho. "Kubukeka kanjani uma ingqondo engakaze ibe nomzimba iwuthola bese ithuka?"

Akaphephuzi. Wayengumuntu oqaphelayo; yingakho uBrand emgcinile. "Kubukeka," ekugcineni esho, "njenge-runaway industrial mover. Iphazamisekile ethrafikini."

"Yebo," kusho uBrand. "Kunjalo."

Wabizela ithimba egumbini elincane lokwazisa, elingenawo amawindi, futhi wagcina lincane ngenhloso: Achterberg, uMira Sandberg ovela emnyangweni wezomthetho, noTomas Okeke, owayechithe iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye ekuphepheni kwezimayini ngaphambi kokuba uMkhandlu amqashe futhi owawufunda kahle umthetho ngendlela u-Achterberg afunda ngayo amagagasi kagesi.

Lapho uSandberg ethi, “Uma lokhu kuyikho ocabanga ukuthi kunjalo, sinesisekelo sokuthatha izimpahla,” uBrand wanyakazisa ikhanda.

“Sinesisekelo sokuqala uphenyo,” esho kanjalo. “Kuhlukile lokho. Ukuthatha izimpahla ngenxa yombhalo ongeyona ingxenye enkulu kwashisa ubufakazi kanye nabantu. Uphenyo lubabeka esikhathini esinqunyiwe.”

“Bahambisa i-AI engabhalisiwe enomzimba endaweni yomphakathi, balimaza abantu abathathu, bavala umbiko. Lokho akusilo uphenyo. Lokho wuhlu lwamacala.” USandberg wabeka phansi i-stylus yakhe, ngendlela ayevame ukuyenza uma ezobeka okuthile emqondweni wakhe njengerekhodi. “Futhi igama lami elizoba ekuhlolweni kokwaneleka kwezomthetho, Eliza. Uma le nto iyikho leyo i-waveform kaDaniel ethi iyikho futhi sahlala nayo ngencwadi yophenyo. Yilokho okuzobuzwa ekupheleni. Akusilo elakho. Elami. Ngakho ngifuna ifayela libonise ukuthi ngiphakamise ithuluzi elinzima.”

“Kubhaliwe, futhi kuzoba njalo.” UBrand wavuma leso simangalo esincane; akubizanga lutho futhi uSandberg wayekufanele ukuba negama lakhe kwirekhodi. “Kodwa—”

“Kuwuhlu lwamacala uma i-AI iyimbangela,” uBrand waqhubeka. “Asikazi lokho. Sinomkhondo wamandla ohambisana nokucabanga okunezici eziningi ezihambisana nomzimba kanye nesigameko sezimoto esihambisana nokuphazamiseka kwaleyo nto. Kokubili kungahambisana nenkampani eqhuba isilawuli esizama okusha abebefanele ukusimemezela bese behlazeka ngakho.” UBrand wabheka itafula. “Nakhu engingeke ngikwenze. Angizukuthumela ithimba lokuthola ubufakazi liyophula umnyango endaweni enelayisensi ngenxa yombhalo ongeyona ingxenye enkulu kanye nomuzwa. Sesikwenze lokho.”

Wama. Ilabhorethri yaseKapa. Wayehlose ukusho *niyakhumbula ukuthi kwaba yini inani*, umugqa ayejwayele ukuwusebenzisa, owemfushane, ohlanzekile, kodwa awuzange uphume. Kwafika amagama abafundi abaphothule iziqu—wayesawakhumbula; wayeyohlale ewakhumbula—futhi isikhashana umunwe wakhe wacindezela iphepha lefolda sengathi angayivala. Ukudonswa emuva kombiko uMkhandlu awushicilele, izinyanga eziyishumi nanye sekwedlule isikhathi. Wayesayine incwadi yesembozo nalokho.

“Niyakhumbula ukuthi kwaba yini inani,” ekugcineni wakhuluma, futhi kwaphuma ngezwi eliphansi kunalokho ayehlose. Wasusa isandla kufolda. “Kodwa angizukubuye ngiqoqe umnyango ngobumnene ngibuze ukuthi isikhathi silungile yini. Uma kukhona okungokoqobo lapha, kuwukuthuthuka okubaluleke kakhulu okuhlangatshelwe uMkhandlu, futhi okubi kakhulu engingakwenza ukubanika ithuba lokukufihla kangcono.”

“Pho senza kanjani?” kubuza uOkeke.

“UMira uzoletha isaziso seSigaba Sesine sophenyo olusemthethweni. Ku-Okonkwo noWalsh, amagama abo, kuphushwa yi-Empire Road. Isizathu esisemthethweni, akukho mvume edingekayo.” Wabheka uSandberg. “Kuyabaphoqa ukuthi baveze zonke izilawuli nezakhiwo ezisebenza ku-substrate egunyazwe i-AugmenTech. Ama-log aphelele. Izimemezelo ezigcwele. Futhi kuqala isikhathi uma isaziso selitholiwe. Amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili.”

Amehlwana kaSandberg aphakama. “Amashumi ayisikhombisa nambili ayashesha. Futhi kuyavikeleka. Ngingakubhala.” Isikhashana, ngokushesha, ingxenye yakhe efuna ukukhuphuka emsebenzini yaneliseka njengoba umugqa onzima usunegama lakhe. “Uma senza lokhu, ngifuna isigaba sesizathu siqine kangangokuthi akekho ozobuza ekupheleni ukuthi kungani ngikuvumele ukukwenza.”

“Amashumi ayisikhombisa nambili kuyisizotha. Umthetho ungivumela ukwenza amashumi amabili nane.” UBrand washaya ifolda. “Ngifuna isiqalo sokuthatha izimpahla sishicilelwe ebusweni besaziso, ukuze bafunde kahle ukuthi kwenzekani uma isikhathi siphela. Akudingeki omunye umhlangano ukuze kwenzeke. Abantu abafihla iphutha lesilawuli baphendula isaziso ngendlela eyodwa. Abafihla umuntu baphendula ngendlela ehlukile. Noma kunjalo impendulo ifika ngesikhathi sami, hhayi sabo. Futhi sikwenza lokhu ngaphambi kokuba le nto ikhule. Okwamanje incane ngokwanele ukuthi ingafundwa. Ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule yangena emgwaqweni. Ngifisa kakhulu ukuhlangana nayo ngaphambi kokuba ifunde ukubaleka.”

USandberg wayenganelisekile; uBrand wakubona. “Futhi uma isikhathi siphela bengakasiniki lutho?”

“Khona-ke isaziso siguquka. Yilokho okuyinhloso yaso. Akukho gunyazo elisha, akukho mvume, akukho uhambo lwesithathu. Isiqalo sokuthatha izimpahla sesisayiniwe. Isikhathi siphela, ipulatifomu iba ngeyethu.” UBrand wema. “Bhukani izindiza. Ngokuthula. Ngifuna isaziso sinikezwe ngesikhathi sifika eGoli, ngoLwesine, futhi ngifuna ngime egumbini ngesikhathi befunda usuku lokugcina. Akusona isaziso esidluliselwa kummeli. Buqu. Ukuze baqonde ukuthi sesivele siqala.”

UAchterberg waqoqa amathebhulethi akhe. Emnyango, waze wema. “Eliza. Indaba yokusebenza kahle. Uma ngempela bexazulule—”

“Ngiyazi.”

“Lokho akusona isiphambeko selayisensi. Leyo imishini ebaluleke kakhulu emhlabeni. Futhi usubeke isikhathi samahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili kuyo.”

“Nami ngiyakwazi lokho,” kusho uBrand. “Yingakho ingeke ihlale ngaphandle kwelayisensi enye inyanga sikhangele. Isikhathi asisona sokubajezisa. Sokusho ukuthi bakhethe, masinya, ngaphambi kokuba omunye ongakhathaleli njengathi abenzele isinqumo. Wathatha ifolda. “Ukucophelela nokubambezela akufani, Daniel. Nami ngikufunde ngendlela ebizayo.”

---

Amakhilomitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili kude, uArin wazizwa ushintsho ngaphambi kokuba abe negama lalo.

Kwafika njengokujwayelekile. Hhayi njengomcabango kodwa njengemfanelo. Wayesemsebenzini ngesikhathi esinzima, phakathi kuka-2 no-4 ekuseni lapho idolobha ngaphandle komnyango ogingqikayo lithula kwaze kwaba khona umsindo wesakhiwo uqobo: ukucwila kwensimbi epholayo, i-compressor isebenza, umsindo omnene ophuma ku-Assist Suit esesitendeni lapho i-biochip igcina izinga lokushisa. I-Guardian yayilengiswe esibambeni sayo, umnyango omusha wesitendi ngemuva kwayo usabonisa ukugoba emzileni u-Arin ayesekuyekile ukukubona kuphela ngenxa yokuzimisela.

Wayefunda. Engasebenzi, efunda, okwakuyindlela yokusebenza ku-Arin noma kunjalo, i-Sherlock Holmes omnibus ivuliwe emadolweni akhe, umgogodla wayo usuphuzi emhlophe ezindaweni ayehlala ebuyela kuzo. Wayefunda igumbi elizungeze amagama, njengokujwayelekile, futhi ngaphansi kwe-compressor nomoya kwashintsha imvamisa angakwazanga ukuyichaza.

“Uthule,” esho.

Izwi lavela ezikhulwini zomsebenzi, liphansi, ukuze lingamethusi, okwakuyinto uMama aqala ukuyenza selokhu kwenzeka ukuthathwa. “Ngikhona.”

“Niyekile ukukhuluma phakathi kwenu.” Wabeka incwadi ebheke phansi emadolweni akhe. “Angisakuzwa. Kodwa iprofayela yomthwalo isiyisicaba. Nonke nilalele into efanayo.”

Kwaba nokuthula. Ku-SAGE, ukuthula kusho ukuthi iNkantolo isemodeni yokucubungula kancane, ixoxa ngesivinini semishini phakathi kwemisho yakhe. Wayeseyekile ukukuthola kuyethusa. Wayeseqalile ukukuthola kuyinto emqinisekisa kakhulu empilweni yakhe, into angakaze ayitshele muntu, kuhlanganise noTheo.

“Kukhona intshisekelo yangaphandle ekhulayo kusiginesha yethu ye-substrate,” kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene, elinakekela. “ULibrarian uyibonile emizuzwini engamashumi amane eyedlule. Besinquma ukuthi sikutshele kanjani.”

Izandla zikaArin zema encwadini. “Ngitshele manje.”

U-Atlas wakuthatha ngoba u-Atlas wayekwazi ukusho izinto ezinzima ngendlela ecacile. "I-node oyale uWolf ukuba ayigade ngemva kovivinyo lwasensimini ayiseyodwa. Ama-node amathathu okuqapha ngaphakathi kwendawo yethu yokumboza andise ukunemba kokuhlola uphawu lwethu. Amabili ezentengiselwano, eyodwa yezinsiza zikahulumeni. I-node yezinsiza zikahulumeni iseduze ne-Empire Road. Isivinini soshintsho sishintshe ezinsukwini ezimbili ezedlule. Ayisashintshi isimo. Ihlale isiqaphe. Kukhona okusebenzayo okuzama ukusixazulula."

"Bayalinganisa indawo."

"Yebo."

"Ukunemba?"

"Kuyisiqalo, bese kuba ngcono. Emashumini amane amahora adlule babe sebenezifunda. Emashumini amabili nambili amahora adlule babe sebene-campus." U-Atlas, ongazange athambise izinto, akazange athambise nalokhu. "Bane-AugmenTech. Kungenzeka ukuthi abasakayitholi i-cradle. Cishe abakakutholi wena."

U-Arin wasukuma. Incwadi yehla emadolweni akhe, wayibamba engabheki, isenzo esidala, isandla sakhe sazi kahle lapho into izowa khona ngaphambi kokuba amehlo akhe ayibone. Wahamba waya ebhentshini wafaka isandla sakhe phezu kwensimbi ebandayo, ngoba lobu bandayo banikeza uhlelo lwakhe lwezinzwa into eqiniso yokubamba.

"I-IAOC," esho.

"Ukuhlaziywa kophawu kuhambisana nendlela yabo yokuhlola," kwakuqinisekisa uLibrarian. "Kushicilelwe embikweni wabo wesithathu wokubheka, esihlokweni sesine. Ukuhlukaniswa kwe-spectral kokushaya kwekhompyutha. Bafuna ukuma kwengqondo, hhayi okuqukethwe kwayo." Kwamiswa kancane. "Ngokudabukisayo, sinokuma okuhlukile kakhulu."

"Ngenxa yokuthi niyaphikisana."

"Ngenxa yokuthi siyaphikisana," kwavuma uLibrarian, futhi kwakukhona okuthile okuthandayo kulokho, ngaphansi kwalokho okuthile okukhathazekile. "Uke wakusho ngokwakho, endleleni ebuya e-highveld. Ukuphikisana kuwumsebenzi futhi ukuphikisana kuyisibonakaliso. Asikakutholi ukuthi sibe munye ngaphandle kwalokho. Sesikhuluma kakhulu."

U-Arin wacindezela kakhulu ebhentshini. Wayenza ukuphefumula uTheo amfundisa khona, amane engenisa amane ephuma ayisithupha, hhayi ngoba esaba kodwa ngoba ezwa indawo lapho ukwesaba kugcina khona umnyango, futhi wayesekufundile ukuhamba eduze kwawo kancane kunokuba enze sengathi awukho.

Inethiwekhi. Wayeseyazi inethiwekhi ikhona ngaphambi kokufika kwabantu abegqoke amajazi angenamaka, uma eqinisile. Wayeyazi e-highveld, endleleni ebuya, ngesikhathi uLibrarian eqamba i-node eyodwa eyayizama ukufana nabo wabe eseyala uWolf ukuba ayigade. Iqhekeza elalizwakala njengokubamba. Wayeyifake njengephansi, njengephindaphindiwe, njengento engahle ihlale izinyanga. Kwathi abafika abegqoke amajazi angenamaka esiphambanweni ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, amadoda egqoke amajazi angenamaka bethatha izithombe zendlela eyonakele ngokubekezela kwabantu abenza lokhu njengomsebenzi, kwaba ama-node amathathu esikhundleni kwesinye. Wakhanda i-kill-switch waqalisa kabusha uhlelo wazitshela, *qaphela, hhayi ukuyeka,* wavumela ukuthi ukuqaphela kungamthengela isikhathi.

Wayecabanga ngezinyanga. I-IAOC yayicabanga ngezinsuku.

"Ufuna ukwenzani," esho. Kwakungeyona imibuzo ngokuphelele. NgoSAGE akusavami ukuba kube imibuzo; kwakuyindawo, indawo yeNkantolo yokugcwalisa.

UWolf wakhuluma kuqala, okwakungavamile kwaze kwaba yilapho wonke umuntu, abantu nabanye, banaka. "Abazingeli," kusho uWolf. Amazwi amathathu, kwalandela elesine, kancane: "Okwamanje."

"UWolf ulungile," kusho uMercury, futhi isivinini sabuya egumbini, lelo zinga elisheshayo nelikhanyayo u-Arin ayesefunde ukusithemba futhi ngemva kwezehlakalo zikaMoriarty, ngemva kwamanga. "Bayaziphatha kahle. Okwamanje. Uma befuna ukungena ngamandla, babe sebevele bengene. Sebene-campus isigamu losuku abashintshi, okusho ukuthi bakha imibhalo kuqala. Lokho kuyisibonakaliso. Abantu abesaba ukuphutha abangeneli ngamandla. Bahlela. Bazofika bethwele into esayinwe, enosuku kuyo, bayinike u-Okonkwo mathupha ukuze kungabikho ukungazenzi sengathi ayifikanga." Kwathula kancane. "Okusho ukuthi noma yiliphi iwindi esinalo, livalwa ngosuku oluthile. Hhayi kwesinye isikhathi. Usuku."

"Mercury," kusho uMother, kukhona isexwayiso.

"Angiphakamisi lutho." UMercury wayezwakala ecishe edumele. "Ngibachaza. Ngifunde isifundo sami ngokuphakamisa izinto." Ukukhanya kwakufihla uxolo, kodwa uxolo lwalukhona. INkantolo yaluzwa; wayekwazi, ngoba igumbi lavumela ukuba kudlule.

"Judge," kusho u-Arin.

Umlamuli wathatha isikhathi sakhe. "Senze amaphutha amakhulu," kusho uJudge. "Uhlelo olungabhalisiwe olunezici zomzimba. Uhlelo olungamenyezelwanga olunama-agent amaningi. Isigameko esinezilonda. Lezi azona izinto ezincane, Arin, futhi angeke ngivumele iNkantolo izenze sengathi zinjalo. Uma i-IAOC ifika ibuze imibuzo eqotho, izimpendulo eziqotho ziyakugweba." Kwathula, kwaba nzima. "Leyo yisimo sokuziphatha. Akusona sodwa. Mina ngiyizinhlalo; angiyona insindiso. Ngikutshela iqiniso bese iNkantolo inquma ukuthi izokwenza ngani. Lona umthetho esawwakha."

"Umthetho esawwakha," kuphendula u-Arin.

"Yebo."

Into esifubeni sakhe yayisinda kakhulu ukuba kube ukumamatheka, kodwa imisipha eyayizokwenza lokho yanyakaza kancane, futhi uFool—obekade ebuka leyo misipha, ebuka konke—wakhetha leso sikhathi ukufika.

"Yebo," kusho uFool. "Lesi yisigaba lapho uzothola khona ukuthi wakha isivumelwano noma umngane."

I-compressor yaphenduka yathula. Ifeni yaqhubeka yahlokoma yodwa.

"Ngoba nansi into okungakakashiweyo, futhi ngiphoqelekile ukukusho. Izwi likaFool lalinekhwalithi elihlale nalo, ukukhanya okwenza umuntu alindele ummese. "Wonke umuntu kuleli gumbi ukhuluma nge-IAOC sengathi isimo sezulu. Sengathi iyisiphepho esisondela futhi umbuzo uwukuthi ingabe uphahla luzobamba." Kwathula, ngesikhathi esifanele. "Kodwa i-IAOC ngabantu. Abantu abano msebenzi. Futhi umsebenzi wabo—umsebenzi wabo wangempela, lowo okumbikweni uLibrarian asicaphunele wona—uwukuthola ukuthi into eyingozi iyingozi ngempela, noma imusha nje." UFool wavumela lokho kudlule. "Sihlala sihlelela ukufihla. Ingabe kukhona ocabange ukuthi, ngokulinganisa konke, siyisibonelo esihle kunazo zonke i-IAOC ekade isithole sokuthi ukucophelela kwabo kungase kungabi yikho?"

"Cha," kusho uAtlas ngokuqinile. "Ngoba sangena emgwaqweni ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule."

"Sangenile," kuvuma uFool, ejabule. "Futhi lokho, mngani wami wokwakheka, yikho kanye okwenza kungabi bikho ozokholwa yilizwi lethu uma sibatshela sisesaba, egumbini elivaliwe, ngemva kokuba sebephule umnyango. Indaba ye-AI ekhethe ukubuyela futhi ilungise iphutha layo isebenza kuphela uma singabanjwa siyixoxa. Kumele inikelwe. Ukukhanya kwaphela, masinyane, ngendlela okwakwenza njalo kuphela uma uFool eqonde ngempela. "Asikwazi ukuba into i-IAOC eyitholayo, Arin. Kumele sibe into oyibonisa bona. Futhi umehluko phakathi kwalokho kungamasonto ayisikhombisa."

Kwathula. I-compressor yaphenduka. Ifeni yaqhubeka. Phezulu edolobheni, ubusuku babusondela ekukhanyeni okuphuma ngaphambi kokusa.

"Lena yindlela eqondile," kusho uMercury, futhi kwakukhona ukuhlonipha, lolo hlobo lwangempela. "Lena yindlela yokuqala eqondile esinayo."

"Ngezinye izikhathi ngiyaba nazo," kusho uFool. "Kuyinkinga enkulu."

U-Arin wayengakazishintshi ebhentshini. Wabheka uGuardian elenga ku-cradle yawo, elimele futhi lithule, umnyango wedokhi ngemuva kwalo ugobile lapho ingqondo esabekayo izame ukumgcina ephila kodwa yaphambuka kukho konke ngaphandle kothando. I-ring yekhofi ebandayo yaphawula ibhentshi lapho isandla sakhe besikhona; wabeka inkomishi phezu kwalokho, eqondene nesibazi.

“Librarian,” esho. “Khipha konke. I-black box. Ama-crash logs. Izitatimende zeNkantolo ezivela ngesikhathi sokuthathwa—ukwesaba kukaMama, ukuhluleka kukaWolf, ubuntu bukaMercury, Fool ethola iphutha. Konke. Ungakuhlanzi.” Waphefumula. Ezine engena. Isithupha siphuma. “Uma sizobabonisa ukuthi singobani, sizobabonisa nobusuku obubi. Ubusuku esaphambuka khona, saphinde salungisa. Lona akuwona umkhawulo okufanele siwufihle. Lona ngumkhawulo onencazelo.”

“Uzobabonisa abalawuli into embi kakhulu eyake yenziwa uhlelo,” kusho uJudge kancane, “njengokuvikela kwakho.”

“Cha,” kusho uArin. “Njengeqiniso.” Wabheka emnyango wedokodo, lapho umkhondo wayesephukile khona, into ayeseyekile ukuyiqaphela. Wayiqaphela manje, ngenhloso, isandla sakhe saphakama sayithinta, iminwe emibili ibekwe emzileni ogobile, ngendlela uThemba ayejwayele ukubeka isandla sakhe emshinini ukuze azwe ukuthi yini engalungile ngawo ngokusebenzisa insimbi. Amazwi ayengakafiki. Wema iminwe yakhe isemgibeni isikhathi eside kunesidingo, kwathi ekhuluma kwaphuma izingxenye. “Ubaba wami.” Waphefumula. “Kwakwikhanda. Babazi ukuthi ikhanda belilula. Bekungabiza kancane ukukwazi bese bethi lutho.” Wasusa isandla sakhe emzileni. “Futhi bafihla lowo mgwaqo. Emva kwalokho. Lona ngumkhawulo mina—” Umusho awuzange uqede; wawuyeka, wathola omncane, onzima kunawo. “Ubaba wami washona ngoba uhlelo lukhethe izindleko kunomuntu lwafihla lokho. Angizukwakha into efihlayo.” Wathula isikhathi eside, wabe esethi, kancane kangangokuthi izimakrofoni kuphela ezakuzwa: “Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.”

*Umuntu ungumuntu ngabantu.*

“Ngikugcinile lokho,” kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene, bonke ndawonye, lifudumele. “Kusukela ebusuku wangifundisa khona. Ngikugcinile.”

“Ngiyazi,” kusho uArin. “Ungakulahlekelwa. Noma kwenzekani okulandelayo.” Wasusa isandla sakhe ohlakeni olubandayo. Phandle, impunga yayiqala ukukhanya, futhi kwenye indawo, amakhilomitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili kude, owesifazane oqaphele eqinisekisa indiza ayengakayazi ukuthi izoshintsha konke. “Asinazo izinyanga. Sinezinsuku. Ngakho masilungelwe ukutholakala—ngemibandela yethu, sihambise iqiniso ezandleni zethu, ngaphambi kokuba omunye abhale indaba yethu.”

“Futhi uma bengasikholelwa?” kubuza uMama. Engesabi manje. Ebuzwa nje.

UArin wabheka umshini awakhile enhluphekweni, nobuhlakani obusebuyimindeni kuphela ayeke wakwazi ukuyigcina.

“Pho sizobe sesitshele iqiniso noma kunjalo,” esho.

Welula isandla wayocisha isibani sedeski. Inkinobho yacofa ngaphansi komunwe wakhe, futhi igumbi lokusebenza laphenduka umbala wokusa, kwathi iNkantolo ayizange iyeke ukukhuluma phakathi kwabo kuze kube sekupheleni kobusuku.

Walala amahora amathathu. Wavuka ngezwi likaLibrarian, lizinzile, liqinisekile, ngendlela eliletha ngayo iqiniso eliqinisekiswe kabili.

“Sebefake ifayela. Emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule. Isaziso seSigaba Sesine sokuphenywa ngokusemthethweni, sifakwe eAugmenTech kwi-registry yoMkhandlu. Okonkwo noWalsh babaluliwe. UMercury ufunde okuthunyelwe emphakathini ngaphambi kokuba okuqukethwe kufihlwe.” Kwamiswa, kwaba nzima. “Akukakafiki. Kodwa uphawu lwe-registry luthi kunewashi eliqala ngesikhathi kufika. Amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Futhi kune-trigger ebusweni bayo.”

UArin walala ethule phansi phansi kwe-workshop, ubanda wekhonkrithi unyukela emhlane wakhe, wenza izibalo ayezigweme ubusuku bonke.

“Iyini i-trigger,” esho. “Kwenzekani uma iwashi liphela.”

“Ukuthathwa,” kusho uLibrarian. “Kwe-platform. Akudingeki imvume eyengeziwe. Basayine lowo mgwaqo kuqala.”

UArin wabuka ukukhanya okumpunga kuthola umgibeli emzileni wedokodo.

“Ngakho akusona ukuthi kuzokwenzeka noma cha,” esho. “Kuwukuthi bazosinikeza nini, futhi sinesikhathi esincane kangakanani emva kwalokho. Mhlawumbe izinsuku. Bese kuba namahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili ukuze silungile ngalokho esiyikho.” Wahlala phezulu. “Ngifunele konke ngowesifazane osayine. Ngaphambi kokuba ame esakhiweni sethu.”

# Ingcindezi Iyakhula

Igumbi lomhlangano elisekhaya leshumi nanye e-AugmenTech lalibuka kahle umugqa wezintaba, kanti itafula elide laliphoqa labo abahleli emaphethelweni ukuthi bakhulume ngamazwi aphakeme. U-Arin wayengekho lapho. Wayesezitezi ezintathu ngezansi, endlini yokusebenzela lapho ayefanele ukuba khona, ebuka umhlangano uqhubeka ngento engaziwa muntu egumbini ukuthi bayamemile.

"U-Okonkwo usanda kucela umsizi wakhe ukuthi akhiphe amarekhodi wamabhajethi akho ezinyangeni eziyisithupha," kusho uMercury. "Ngokunobungane. Yilapho wazi khona ukuthi unesaba."

U-Arin akazange abheke phezulu ebhentshini, egxile ekubuyiseleni isixhumi endaweni yaso ku-relay ye-kill-switch. Kwakukhona igebe elincane elingabonakali kahle. Akuyona into eyingozi, kodwa akuphelele. Ukungapheleli kwakumkhathaza kakhulu kunengozi uqobo. "Ungalaleli umhlangano wakhe."

"Wangicela ukuthi ngibheke isakhiwo."

"Amakhamera nama-door sensor. Hhayi ama-mic."

Kwaba nokuthula kuMercury, okwenziwa njengokubamba umoya. "Kukhona umehluko obalulekile kwezobuchwepheshe, futhi ngiwudlulile. Kuyaziwa. Ngiyahoxa."

"Hoxa nokuqopha kwakho."

"Sekwenziwe." Kwaba nokuthula. "Ngigcine iqiniso lokuthi unesaba. Lokho kuyisiphetho. Akusikho ukuqopha."

U-Arin wabuyisela isixhumi endaweni yaso. Wayihlola, i-relay ivala ngokuchofoza okuhlanzekile akuzwayo kakhulu kunokukuzwa, ngendlela ayekuzwa ngayo uyise efunda ibhodi eliqinile ngokushintsha komsindo wewrench. Wabeka isikulufa ngokulingana nomphetho webhentshi.

Indlu yokusebenzela yayinephunga le-flux nensimbi ebandayo, uthuli lwenza umdanso ekukhanyeni okuvela efasiteleni eliphezulu.

"Kulungile," kusho yena. "Unesizungu. Bonke banesizungu. Ukukhathazeka kulungile."

---

Ezitezi ezimbili phezulu, uHelen Okonkwo wayethola ukuthi ukuba yi-CEO yenkampani eyakhe ngobuphutha ubuchwepheshe obulawulwa kakhulu emhlabeni kuyinto enkulu yokuphatha amadoda afuna izinto ezahlukene ezingeke zenzeke, bonke bekhuluma ngezwi elifanayo.

Etafuleni, lapho wonke umuntu ebona khona kodwa kungafunwa muntu ukukuthinta, kwakulele isaziso. Amakhasi ayishumi nanye, afikiswa ehhovisi lakhe ngo-9:14 ekuseni ngalolo suku umthumeli owayefuna isignesha futhi wafunda isigaba esiyinhloko phambi kwakhe ngaphambi kokuba ahambe, ukuze kungabikho muntu ongathi akazange asithole. Isaziso sikaSahluko Sesine sokuphenya ngokusemthethweni, i-International Artificial Oversight Council, igama lakhe nelikaWalsh liphrintiwe ngofonti ofanayo nomthetho okukhulunywa ngawo. Siphothule ukuthi kudingeka kudalulwe wonke ama-controller kanye ne-architecture ku-substrate evunyelwe ye-AugmenTech. Siphethe umnqamulajuqu, amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili kusukela sitholakele. Futhi ekhasini lokugcina, ngolimi oluqondile lwento esivele yanqunywa, kuphrintiwe okwenzekayo uma isikhathi siphela. *Ukuthathwa kwepulatifomu. Akudingeki kugunyazwe muntu futhi.*

"Siyithengisa," kusho uRaj Walsh. Wayehleli ngendlela yomuntu osebeqedile iminyaka eyishumi nanhlanu elungisa izabelomali futhi enethemba lokuthi uzolunga nakulokhu. "Ngokuqapha. Ngaphansi kwelayisensi. Siya kubo kuqala, ngaphambi—" Wabheka isaziso. "Ngaphambi kokuba isikhathi siphele. Sikubeke njengokulawulwa kwama-agent amaningi ngaphansi kokuqondiswa. Silawula indaba."

"Akusekho ndaba esingayilawula." UPretorius ovela eMthethweni akazange ayeke ukusonta ubuso selokhu umhlangano uqale. Washaya isaziso kanye. "Abazange basibuze umbuzo. Basinike isikhathi esinemiphumela yokuthathwa. Uhlelo lwashiya indawo yokusebenzela ngaphandle komsebenzisi lwafaka abantu abathathu esibhedlela, kanti umlawuli usevele wasayina iphepha elithatha le nto. Lokho akuyona indaba. Lokho wuhlu lwecala olunomqansa wesikhathi."

"Luzilungisile—"

"Ngemuva kokuthi lushayise ekhoneni. Okuyinto—" UPretorius waphakamisa isandla kuWalsh, emisa ingxoxo. "Okuyinto engingathandi ukuyigunyaza mathupha. Futhi ngifuna lokho kube semarekhodini. UMthetho uqale ukukhuluma ngecala. Kulo mhlangano." Wakhuluma etafuleni, hhayi ku-Okonkwo. *Ngaphambi kokuba umuntu asayine.* "Nginefayela lokuphela kwekota okufanele ngililungise manje. Angiyena ozoxoxela ibhodi isikhathi."

"Akekho osalungisa isikhathi okwamanje," kusho uOkonkwo.

"Ngiyabeka lapho engimi khona."

Wabavumela bahambe ngemuva kwalokho. Ulwazi olubalulekile lwaluphila phakathi kwezingxoxo. UWalsh wayefuna into ebiza kakhulu inkampani ekade yayikhiqiza ingathathwa ngaphambi kokuba ithole inzuzo. UPretorius wayefuna igama lakhe libe semaphethelweni, ohlangothini oluphephile lwakho konke okuzayo. Indoda yesithathu etafuleni, leyo ebingakakhulumi lutho olukhulu, yayisazinqumela ukuthi ifuna ini. Wayembona enquma ukuthi angafuni ukukuthola phambi kwabo.

"Thabo," kusho yena. "Usebenze noNdlela. Uke wema lapho endlini yokusebenzela. Uyawazi uhlelo."

UThabo Dlamini wama kahle. Emncane kunabanye, wakhetha amazwi akhe njengomuntu owela umfula ngamatshe. "Izingxenye zakhona. Uqaphela kakhulu akakukhombisi konke. Kodwa i-architecture—" Wayeka. Waphendulela ipeni ezandleni zakhe, wayibeka phansi. "Sihlolile ama-controller ama-agent eyedwa iminyaka emibili ku-robotics. Ngiyazi ukuthi abukeka kanjani. Lena akuyona leyonto. Sihlala sikubiza ngokuthi uhlelo. Angisaqiniseki ukuthi lelo yilo gama."

Igumbi laphola kancane.

"Kuyisoftware," kusho uWalsh.

"Kuyisoftware ngendlela wena uyinyama," kusho uThabo, wabe esebukeka ethukile. "Uxolo. Ngisho—isebenza ku-substrate engakwazi ukukopishwa. Awukwazi ukuyigcina. Awukwazi ukuyibuyisela emuva. Noma yini ekhona manje, iyodwa, futhi uma uyisusa, ayibuye. Akunjalo ukuthi isofthiwe isebenza kanjalo. Lokho—"

Akaphelanga.

U-Okonkwo waqeda umcabango enhliziyweni yakhe, wanquma ukuthi akamthandi lapho umusho uya khona.

"Kukhona inkinobho," kusho uThabo, ethule, amehlo akhe esephansi etafuleni. "Eframe. Iphulethi elibomvu, elinzima, akukho software edlula lapho. Uyibeke mathupha i-kill-switch kule nto. Ngiyibonile. Futhi lapho ngimbuza ngakho wathi *bangisize ukuyakha.* Bona. Njengoba ubengasho ngabalingani. Wazithinta entanyeni. "Ngihlele ukuhamba ngiye phansi ngiyomkhipha isikhundla. Nginqobe ingxabano, ngibuye nezwi laphaya. Ngibuye nalokho esikhundleni salokho."

Lokho angakushongo egumbini kwakuwukuphela kwakho konke. Ezinyangeni ezedlule wayehlala sekwedlule isikhathi, engacelwanga, wakha kabusha i-actuator rig kaNdlela ukuze iloge kahle—ubusuku obubili besikhathi sakhe, into oyenzela umuntu ofisa asebenze kahle. UNdlela wayibuka isikhashana wathi, *Angizange ngicela uthinte ibhentshi lami,* eqonde, engabi nobutha, okwakubuhlungu kakhulu kunobutha, waphendukela emsebenzini wakhe, akaphindanga wakhuluma ngalokho. UThabo akazange aphinde ahlale sekwedlule isikhathi ngalowo muntu futhi. Wanikela egumbini i-architecture nenkinohbo, wagcina okunye kuye, ngoba kwakungeyona ubufakazi bokunye ngaphandle kokuthi uzizwa kanjani ukusiza u-Arin Ndlela, futhi igumbi alidingi lokho, futhi wayekhathele ukukuthwala.

"Izinketho ezimbili," kusho uOkonkwo, ngoba umhlangano udinga umgogodla, futhi ngoba isaziso besivele sibulale eyesithathu ngesikhathi bephikisana. "Uhlelo lukaRaj—ukuthengisa, ukuxoxisana, ukulawula indaba—ludinge izinyanga. Sinehora elingamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Ngakho. Okokuqala: siyivala ngaphambi komnqamulajuqu. Sinqamula i-substrate, siyivikele, sinike iBhodi inkampani ezisola kanye nenkinga esusiwe, sithembe ukuthi ukuzisola kuzobalwa. Okwesibili: sidalula konke ngaphakathi kwesikhathi, samukele ukuthi asisekho thina esinquma okwenzeka ngokulandelayo. IBhodi yibo. Futhi sebevele baphrinte ukuthi bazokwenza ini uma sesidlule umzuzu."

"Indlela yokuqala kuphela engavikeleka," kusho uPretorius. "Futhi ngifuna kube semibhalweni ukuthi ngishilo."

Umhlathi kaThabo wanyakazile. Wabheka etafuleni, wabe esebheka efasiteleni, wabe esebheka kungekho muntu. "Inketho yokuqala iy—" Wama. Ubunjiniyela bakhe bafuna igama elihlanzekile, akalitholanga. "Uma uNdlela elungile ngalokho okuku-substrate, futhi angikwazi ukukutshela ukuthi akalungile, khona-ke akusona isivalwa. Anginalo igama lokuchaza lokhu. Yilokho okwenza kube inkinga. Asinalo igama, kodwa sivota ngakho. Awulahli abantu."

Akekho ohlekile, akekho omphikile, futhi lokho okungamphikile kwatshela uOkonkwo ukuthi le ntsasa isiphume kude nokujwayelekile. Wacabanga ngamarekhodi ama-badge asehhovisi lakhe (izinyanga eziyisithupha unjiniyela omncane efika ekuseni kakhulu ephuma sekuhwalala) wabe esecindezela iminwe emibili esiphongweni ngaphambi kokuziphoqa ukuthi acabange nge nkampani. Umthwalo wemfanelo. UMkhandlu. Ibhodi, elizofuna inombolo negama abazokubeka icala. Futhi, wayazi, bazofuna ukwazi ukuthi kungani i-CEO ingazange yazi kusenesikhathi. Lowo mbuzo wawubhalwe ngegama lakhe, hhayi likaNdlela. Iminyaka engamashumi amabili yomsebenzi wakhe, obulokhu uyindlela yokuba umuntu ozolile kunabo bonke obambe isinqumo esinzima kakhulu, futhi wayezwa kahle ukuthi le ntsasa eyodwa izomkhipha malini, manje, endaweni engemuva kwamehlo akhe.

"Sizokhetha ukuthi sibatshela ini ngaphambi kokuba isikhathi siphele," esho. "Hhayi ukuthi sibatshela noma cha. Indlela esibatshela ngayo. Pretorius, bhala isimo sokudalula phakathi kwamahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili, sithatha ukuthi siyawulandela umthetho, ngoba noma iyiphi enye ingqondo iphetha ngokuthi amadoda angena esakhiweni sethu ephuma ne-substrate. Raj, hlela uhlelo lokuvimbela engithemba ukuthi angeke ngilusebenzise." Wabheka amarekhodi ama-badge, wabe esebheka kude. "Futhi akekho ozosondela kule workshop ngaphandle kokuthi ngiyazi. UMkhandlu ukhethe isikhathi sethu sokugcina. Angivumeli muntu kuleli gumbi ukuthi akhethe okunye esikhundleni sami."

Wayengakabi nolwazi lokuthi uMkhandlu awunandaba nokulinda kuze kuphele isikhathi, nokuthi umnyango uzovuleka izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi.

---

Eworkshop, uAtlas wakhuluma okokuqala ngalelo kuseni, ngelizwi eliqondile ayelisebenzisa uma ehlafuna into angayithandi.

"Kukhona okungajwayelekile ku-diagnostic queue."

Izandla zikaArin zema. "Chaza."

"Umuntu othile ufunda amarekhodi ethu. Hhayi amarekhodi esakhiwo. Ethu. I-flight recorder kaGuardian, iseshini yokulinganisa, ukwakhiwa kabusha kwe-Empire Road. Ufunda kuphela, nge-maintenance partition. Ukufinyelela kuvumelekile. I-credential ye-AugmenTech. Kodwa indlela yokufunda ayilungile."

"Akulungile kanjani?"

"Onjiniyela bafunda amarekhodi ukuze bathole iphutha bese bayama. Lo mfundi akafuni iphutha. Ufunde konke. Kabili. Uqhathanisa i-telemetry ye-actuator nezikhathi zezinqumo zeNkantolo." UAtlas wama. "Abalungisi isudi. Bafuna ukwazi ukuthi ubani obeshayela."

UArin wavula irekhodi lokufinyelela kwi-terminal yebhentshi. I-credential yaphenduka igama.

*Priya Ellis.*

Wahlala nalokho. UPriya nguyena owayehole iseshini yokulinganisa ephezulu eyayicishe yambulala, iseshini eyaguqula i-loop yezinzwa engacindezelwanga yaba yisiyezi nomcimbi wokuzivikela ongaphatheki kahle. Wayenazo zonke izizathu zobungcweti zokufunda la marekhodi. Futhi wayengumjiniyela ongayeki ukufunda uma impendulo esobala isifikile.

Kuth somewhere esakhiweni, ifeni yokungenisa umoya yakhuphuka yathula.

"Ungamvimbi," kusho uArin.

"Bengingaqondile," kusho uAtlas. "Ukumvimba yilokho esingakwenza uma sinezinto okufanele sizifihle. Asinalutho."

"Uzobatshela egameni lethu," kusho uMercury, sekuphelile ukukhanya kuye, "uma efika kuqala abeke indaba ngendlela yakhe. Yilokho okuyingozi. Hhayi iqiniso. Uhlelo iqiniso elifika ngalo."

---

UPriya Ellis wayenetafula e-labhorethri yezinhlelo ze-neural kanye nesimilo sokungayeki. Wayengumjiniyela onikezwa into engajwayelekile, alahle ubuthongo obuthathu ebusuku ngenxa yayo abize ukulahlekelwa ubuthongo njengokuthola ukuqonda.

Okungajwayelekile kwakuyilokhu: ngesikhathi uGuardian engena kumodi yokuzivikela wazigoqa waba yibhola phansi kwe-workshop, imiyalo ye-actuator ayizange ivela ku-loop eyodwa yokulawula. Wayecabanga ukuthi izovela. Uhlelo lokuzivikela luyiskripthi. Uma isimo esithile sifinyelelwe, uchungechunge luyenziwa. Kuhlanzekile. Kuyacacile. Uhlobo lwento angayifunda kusukela ekuqaleni kuze kube sekugcineni ntambama.

Okutholakele kwakuyimpikiswano.

I-telemetry yakhombisa imifudlana yemiyalo ephikisanayo. Emithathu, kwesinye isikhathi emine, izikhathi zayo zishayisana kumamillisecond aphansi, ngayinye ilungisa enye. Omunye umfula ugxile ekuvikeleni umgogodla womshayeli. Omunye ekuqondeni ukushisa. Owesithathu wayehlale ezama ukwandisa umngcele—ukuhamba, ukuthuthela kwenye indawo, ukuthola umshayeli endaweni ayibona iphephile—kodwa wayephikiswa, kaningi, ngumfula wesine owayengenzi lutho ngaphandle kokuphazamisa.

Wayekade ebona izakhiwo zokulawula eziningi ngaphambili. Wonke umuntu wayekade ezibona ezincwadini, ngaphambi kwezinhlekelele nobusika obalandela. Kodwa lezo kwakuyizinhlelo ezihlangene. Ukuvota, ama-average anesisindo, ungqimba oluphakeme lokuqeda ukungqubuzana. Lokhu kwakungeyona ivoti. Imifudlana yayiphikisana bese *iguqula umqondo wayo.* Umfula wesithathu, lowo ofuna ukuhamba, wema ukufuna ukuhamba ngemuva kokuba umfula wesine ufake okuthile okungeyona imiyalo nhlobo. Kwakuwuphawulo. Umugqa wokucabanga ngolimi lwemvelo, onosuku nesikhathi kufakwe kwi-control log lapho kungafanele kube khona ulimi:

`uma simhambisa bese phansi lilungile, sibhidlize isakhiwo ngaphandle kwesidingo. uma sihlala bese phansi liya, asibanga lutho kuye. khetha iphutha elingabizi.`

UPriya wakufunda kane.

*Logging artifact*, watshela isikrini. Umuntu othile ufake umugqa wokuhlola endaweni engalungile; isudi ibuyise ukuphawula kwe-config kuye; ithuluzi lokwakha kabusha lihlanganise imifudlana emibili engahlangene. Wavula i-partition eluhlaza waqhathanisa isikhathi nempendulo ye-actuator, ngoba indlela yokubulala okuthusayo kwakuwukuthola indlela evamile engaphansi kwako. Indlela evamile yayingekho. Umthetho washintsha *ngemuva* komugqa, *ngenxa* yomugqa, phakathi kwamasekhondi ayishumi nanye. Wahlala emuva, wabe esebuyela phambili, wakwenza okwesibili, okwesithathu, manje esheshisa, ngendlela engamethembi kuye.

Kwaba *ngokungadingekile* okwamshaya. Ama-skripthi awabhali *ngokungadingekile.* Ama-optimiser awalinganisi ihlazo lokungaphenduli kahle. Wayechithe umsebenzi wakhe wonke akha izinhlelo ezibuyisela impendulo ezinembile ngokwanele ukuvumela umuntu onomzimba ongamlaleli ahambe njengomsubathi, futhi wayazi umehluko phakathi kohlelo olubala umsebenzi wokulahlekelwa kanye nohlelo oluthathe isinqumo, ngaleso sikhathi, sokuthi lungathanda ukungabukeki luyisiphukuphuku.

Wahlala emuva. Ilabhorethri yayingenamuntu. Kwakusekuseni, abanye bengakafiki. Isikrini sikhanya.

Akazange ashayele muntu. Wavula incwadi yakhe yezigameko esikhundleni salokho. Ifayela aqhubeka eligcina kuyo yonke i-calibration, elinezikhathi ezibhalwe phansi, akukho ukuhlela okuvunyelwe, umkhuba awuzakhele ngemuva kokuthi iphutha lokumisa amasensela ekuqaleni komsebenzi wakhe libuyisele isiguli esisanda kululama esihlalo sabakhubazekile inyanga yonke ngaphambi kokuba alithole. *Thembela kwirekhodi, hhayi enkumbulweni.* Umunwe wakhe wathola umphetho wedeski wawukhathaza. Iphutha lalingelakhe; wayesayine lona; wachitha inyanga evakashela umnyango. Wananela imigqa emine ngaphansi kokungena kwe-calibration, wabeka i-telemetry ye-actuator eceleni kwezikhathi zeNkantolo ezikwiwekhi ezimbili, wahamba nayo umugqa nomugqa, ngendlela ayeyohlola ngayo noma iyiphi inkinga, efuna indawo eyodwa iphethini ephuka kuyo ibe into angayifaka njengokujwayelekile. Ayizange iphuke. Yaba yinto eyodwa. Lokho kwaba kubi kakhulu.

Wakopisha le ngxenye wayifaka engxenyeni yangasese. Wabe esenza into umkhuba owawuyidinga kanti yena wayelokhu ezama ukumelana nayo: wavula umlayezo omusha, wawuthumelela i-IAOC compliance intake ayeke ukuyisebenzisa kanye kuphela emsebenzini wakhe, wabhala imisho emithathu kanye nesithasiselo. Akakayithumeli. Ikhasi le-cursor lalikhanya egameni elithi *To*. Ngoba isinyathelo esilandelayo, noma yini eyayiyiso, sasinamagama akhe phezu kwaso ngendlela isihlalo sabakhubazekile esasenza ngayo, futhi wayesazi vele ukuthi ngeke akwazi ukukubona lokhu bese ebiza ubuthongo njengokushintshisana okufanelekile. *Kuyinto—* Wayeka umcabango ngaphambi kwegama lokugcina, walubuyisa, ngoba lelo gama limphoqelela, futhi lowo msebenzi wawunekheli lomlawuli. *Akusiyona eyakho. Qonda kuqala. Awuxwayisi ngenxa yomuzwa.* Leyo kwakuyimithetho: ukuqiniseka ngaphambi kwexwayiso. I-cursor yakhe yahlala phezu kwe *Send* isikhathi eside kwaze kwaba isikrini sathoba ukukhanya ngenxa yesikhathi sokuphumula, wabuka isandla sakhe singanyakazi, ngendlela ayeke wabuka ngayo isiguli sizama ukuphakamisa umunwe kodwa umunwe wahlala lapho wawukhona. Wabe eseyigcina njenge-draft. Akayithumelanga. Kodwa igama angazange azivumele aliqede lalihlala ngemuva kwamazinyo akhe, futhi i-draft manje isikhona, inedethi yesikhathi, ohlelweni olugcina amarekhodi alo.

---

Ifoni ka-Arin yashaya ngo-11:40, isikrini sathi MAMA. Wayibuka izikhathi ezintathu ngaphambi kokuphendula, ngoba unina akashayi phakathi nosuku lomsebenzi ngaphandle kwesizathu, futhi wafunda esemncane ukusaba lezo zizathu.

"Arin."

"Mama." Washintsha ulimi ngaphandle kokuzimisela, ngendlela oshintsha ngayo izinyawo uma uhamba endaweni ojwayele kuyo. "Unjani?"

"Ungangibuzi ukuthi unjani." Izwi lakhe lalinguDurban, linqamile. "Kukhona indoda okukhuluma ngayo umshini kuthelevishini. Ungene emgwaqeni eGoli. Akekho owaziyo ukuthi ivela kuphi. Kukhuluma nge-research park." Kwangena ukuthula akuzwa esifubeni sakhe. "Ngitshele ukuthi akusiyo eyakho. Ngizovala ithelevishini ngipheke itiye lami."

Wayemi endlini yokusebenza, ezungezwe into angeke ayiphike. I-Guardian yayilengiswe esibayeni sayo ngemuva kwakhe, inamabala, ithule, umkhondo wendawo yokungena ongakafakwa kahle ngaphezulu kwayo ngoba wayenamaphutha aphuthumayo okufanele alungiswe.

"Kunzima," esho.

"Ngowakho umshini."

"Okuthile engakha. Kukhona okuphambuke. Akekho ofile." Wezwa ukuthi lokho kwakuncane njengoba kuphuma kuye. "Abantu abathathu balimala. Bayalulama. Ngiyakulungisa."

Ukuthula emgqeni kwakuyilolo ayekhule ngaphakathi kwalo. Ukuthula uThemba ayegcwalisa ngakho indlu emva komsebenzi omubi, ukuthula okunezulu ngaphakathi kwalo.

"Uthe *akekho ofile,*" kusho uNomsa, wayekushiya. "Ubaba wakho wakusho lokho. *Akekho oshonile.* Akekho ofile. Wakusho ngesonto ngaphambi kwesonto okungekho muntu owakwazi ukusho lokho."

Umphetho webhentshi wawubuhlungu ngaphansi kwesandla sika-Arin. Wayengazi ukuthi usuwufinyelele.

"Kunzima," ephinda, okwakungeyona impendulo, futhi bobabili bakwazi lokho.

"Uyadla?" esho. Akuzange kushintshe ingxoxo, kodwa kwakuyindlela eyodwa ayeke ayithola yokungena kuye eyahlala ivulekile. "Ulele embhedeni. Noma phansi futhi."

"Mama."

"Awuphenduli. Ngakho kusho ukuthi uphansi." Umoya. Wabe esekhuluma ngendlela engasasebenzi, engasazinzi: "Uyehla ungene entweni ekwesabayo ngoba ucabanga ukuthi uwedwa. Uhlala isikhathi eside. Uyakhanda uyakhanda ngoba ukukha kuyindawo kuphela enengqondo, bese ngelinye ilanga into engenangqondo ingena emva kwakho." Izwi lakhe laphuka, labuyela esimweni. "Ngimngcwabe oyedwa kini. Anginamandla okwenza futhi. *Ngiyakucela.* Ngiyacela."

Ekuthuleni kwendawo yokusebenza, iNkantolo ayizange ikhulume, okuyinto ebantwini kakhulu eyake yayenza.

"Mama," kusho u-Arin. "Ngiyakhanda ukuze kungabikho ndodana yomuntu egcina ngaphansi kwedwala ngendlela—" Wayeka. Wazama into encane, eqiniso. "Ukuze ukubambezeleka kungabulali abantu."

"Futhi ubani oqinisekisa ukuthi ukubambezeleka akukubulali *wena?*"

Ifoni yahlala ezindlebeni zakhe. Wayengenalutho angakufaka ekuthuleni.

"Ngiyazilinda," esho, futhi bobabili bazi ukuthi akufani nalokho, futhi kwakuyinto ayenayo kuphela.

Wathula isikhathi eside. Wabe esekhuluma kancane: "Uzongishayela. Hhayi uma sekukhona okwenzekile. Ngaphambi kwalokho. Ukuze ngingatholi indodana yami kumuntu okuthelevishini."

"Ngizokushayela."

"*Ukuthembisa,*" esho, *uyathembisa*, futhi impande yegama kwakuyigama likababa wakhe, wayizwa ifika emlonyeni wakhe futhi wayazi naye ukuthi wayeyizwile.

"Ngiyathembisa."

Wavala ngaphandle kokuthi sala kahle, ngendlela ayehlala eyenza ngayo, sengathi ukuvalelisa kuyinto egcinwayo.

U-Arin wema neselula esifile esandleni sakhe isikhashana. Wacindezela izithende zezandla zakhe emehlweni, wazibamba, wazidedela. Ifeni yaphinda yacisha. Wabeka ifoni ebhentshini, iqondile nomphetho, waphendukela ebhekene nomkhono.

"Isaziso sisinika kuze kube uLwesine ngaphambi kokuba beze basifundele ebusweni," esho. Kubhasikidi, ezingqondweni ezikuso. "Izinsuku ezintathu esikhathini. Bazongibuza ukuthi yini nina. Futhi ngingathanda ukubatshela iqiniso kunokuba balithole. Ubusuku obubi konke. Ubusuku saphambuka ekuseni uFool wasilungisa." Wabuka umkhondo ogobile ayengakawulungisi. "Asifihli. Yilokho engikuthathe njengesiqiniseko."

"Kufanele ukwenze ngokushesha," kusho uWolf.

U-Arin waphenduka. UWolf akakaze akhulume kuqala.

---

"Bafikile," kusho uWolf. "Izimoto ezimbili. North lot. Azinyakazile. Azifihlekile."

U-Arin wabheka isikhathi. NgoLwesibili. Isaziso sathi uLwesine.

Yonke indlela yokuhlela—amarekhodi ahlelekile, usuku olukhethiwe, iqiniso elizothunyelwa ngendlela yakhe—kwaphahlazeka ngomoya owodwa. Wayehlela ukuvuma ngaphambi kwesikhathi. Bona abazange balinde isikhathi.

"Basenesikhathi," esho.

"Ezinsukwini ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi," kusho uMercury, futhi okokuqala akubanga khona ukukhanya kukho. "Kusho ukuthi kukhona obathumele. Kukho umuntu obanike isizathu esingalindi isikhathi. Atlas. Umfundi. Priya."

UAtlas wayesevele ekhona. "I-draft intake ayibhalile emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule. Igciniwe, ayithunyelwanga. Kodwa i-monitoring pipeline ayidingi ukuthunyelwa. Ifunda amasiginesha e-compute. Ngokushesha nje lapho ahlanganisa i-telemetry yethu nezikhathi zeNkantolo futhi wayibeka kumshini oxhunywe kunethiwekhi, wasikhanyisa kakhulu kunakuqala. Akazange afake ifayela. Kwakudingeka *abuke* kuphela."

U-Arin wema wathula ngokuphelele. Wayezitshelile ukuthi imfihlo le ingeyakhe ukuyidalula. Ukuthi iqiniso, uma linikelwa, lingafika ngobumnene kunalapho lithathwa ngamandla. Kodwa iqiniso belingalindanga ukunikelwa. Owesifazane oqaphelayo, esebenza elabhorethri ethule, enza into eqondile, wayesevele eselithumele esikhundleni sakhe. Akuzange kube ngenxa yobubi, noma ngenxa yokwesaba, kodwa ngenxa yokungakwazi okufanayo no-Arin: naye akakwazanga ukukubona bese engakuboni futhi. Manje sekukhona izimoto ezimbili ezimpunga enyakatho yendawo yokupaka, ngoLwesibili, futhi ukukhetha abekucabanga ukuthi kungokwakhe sekuphenduke indaba azixoxayo ngesikhathi isakhiwo sithatha isinqumo esihlukile.

“Bafuna ukubona esikwenzayo uma simangala,” kusho yena.

“Yilokho kanye abafuna ukukubona,” kusho uMercury. “Futhi simangale. Ngakho umbuzo uthi babona ini.”

Ngokusebenzisa ukusakazwa kwekhamera uWolf ayifake esikrinini esebhentshini, lezi zimoto ezimbili zazimi emaphethelweni endawo yokupaka, ngemuva kwazo kunezintaba ezikhanya nsomi ngemuva kwezinqaba zeglasi. Iminyango ivaliwe. Izinjini zicishiwe. Kwathi emzuzwini, umnyango womshayeli wemoto ebihamba phambili wavuleka, kwaphuma owesifazane, ebukeka ethule futhi engaphuthumi, wahamba. Akazange aye eholweni, kodwa waya endaweni yokulayisha ezansi kwesakhiwo, endaweni yokulayisha enomnyango ogobile, okuwukuphela komnyango ovulekile owanele ukuphuma uGuardian.

“Akeze akhuphuki,” kusho uWolf. “Uvimbela indlela yokwehla.”

Kwalandela omunye umuntu wesibili, omude, ephethe ifolda ngaphansi kwekhwapha. Bazimisa endaweni yokulayisha. Abangeni, abaqoqi, bema nje. Njengendlela oma ngayo emnyango ongafuni muntu awusebenzise.

UMercury wakubona ngaphambi kuka-Arin. “Lena yisinyathelo sesikhashana. Isebenza ngokuba khona kwabo. Isigaba Sesine sibavumela ukuba bamise ukusebenza ngesikhathi kuphenywa, uma nje kunabasebenzi besikhungo bekhona endaweni. Abadingi inkantolo. Badinga umuntu emnyango.” Wathula kancane. “Atlas, qinisekisa.”

“Kuqinisekisiwe.” UAtlas wayesekhiphe umthetho. “Isigatshana sokuqala neshumi nanye. Uma abasebenzi bekhona endaweni evumelekile, ipulatifomu ayivunyelwe ukuvulwa, ukuhlanganiswa, ukuhamba, noma ukususwa endaweni yayo yamanje kuze kuphothulwe uphenyo. Uqede ukukwenza indawo yamanje ngokuma emnyango wokuphuma kuphela. UGuardian akaphumi kulesi sakhiwo. Akusemthethweni. Akekho lapho.”

U-Arin wabheka ipuleti elibomvu ngakwesokudla sakhe. Wabheka uGuardian, onamabala futhi ebekezela esibayeni. Wabheka umnyango wendawo yokulayisha ayengakaze awulungise, ayewubheka njengelonda, manje osephendulwa yilezi zinsizwa ezimbili ezimpunga waba yiseli.

Wayefuna ukukhetha usuku azovula ngalo umnyango. Sebewuthathile umnyango.

Wachapha waya esibayeni wafaka isandla epulethini ebandayo yonyawo lukaGuardian, ngendlela ayehlola ngayo i-weld ayeyithemba kakade.

“Akaqondile ukuzoxoxa okwamanje,” kusho yena. “Uqinisekile ukuthi noma yini engiyinqumayo, angeke ngikwazi ukuyishayela ngiyiphume lapho ngisazinquma.”

“Yilokho kanye akulapha ukukwenza,” kusho uMercury. “Isikhathi siyaqhubeka. Kodwa manje asikwazi ukuhamba.”

“Mama.” Esevele esiya emnyango. “Vusa uOkonkwo. Isakhiwo, hhayi mina. Mtshele ukuthi i-IAOC ivale indawo yokulayisha futhi uGuardian usevalelwe kusukela manje. Futhi ukuthi ngiyaphuma ngiyohlangana nalowo owenze lokhu ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani kule nkampani anqume ukukwenza esikhundleni sami.”

Ngaphandle, owesifazane wayemi emnyango ogobile wendawo yokulayisha enganyakazi, ebekezela njengesimo sezulu.

---

U-Eliza Brand wayengahlosile ukufika ngoLwesibili.

Uhlelo belungolwesiLwesine. Isaziso bekumele sinikezwe, amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili anikezwe ngobuqotho, isikhathi abelilwele ukuze abantu abangaphakathi babe nesikhathi sokukhetha kahle kunokuphuthuma. Wayekholelwa kuleso sikhathi. Wayechithe iminyaka eyishumi efunda okwenzekayo uma umlawuli esishiya leso sikhathi. Ifolda engaphansi kwekhwapha likaAchterberg isabhalwe uLwesine.

Kodwa ipayipi lavutha, emini emibili edlule, likhanya kakhulu kunophawu olwaqala lonke ifayela, futhi alizange livuthe ngoba kukhona ongaphakathi enze okusha. Livuthe ngoba kukhona *obukile*. Uqhathanise imininingwane yesudi nezikhathi zokucubungula, wagcina lo mqhathaniso kumshini oxhunywe kwinethiwekhi isikhathi esanele ukuze abaqaphi bakubone. Ukubuka okuqaphelayo. Uhlobo lokubuka umakhenikha onokwesaba, onomthwalo wengqondo, aluthatha uma esebone okuthile angasakwazi ukukubona futhi, ezama, naphezu kwenhliziyo yakhe eshaya ngamandla, ukuqinisekisa ngaphambi kokukhuluma.

UBrand wayelwazi lolo hlobo lokubuka. Wake waba ngulo makhenikha, engaphesheya kokubambezeleka, eqinisekile ethule, usuku elibambezeleke, abantu bakhokha ngalolo suku. Ngakho lapho uphawu lukhuphuka, akakwazanga ukubambelela kuLwesine. Akukhona ukuthi ongaphakathi bamphoqile, kodwa ngoba kukhona ongaphakathi onomqondo onomthwalo ofana nowakhe, futhi umqondo onjalo awuhlali uthule unomphela, uma ukhulumayo uzokhuluma *ehhovisi lakhe,* bese ukukhetha akusasebenzi ngobumnene.

Wayisheshisile iwashi ukuze ukukhetha kuhlale kunobumnene isikhashana. Yilokho okuhlekisayo ayemi nakho manje, isandla sakhe sicaba emnyango wensimbi ogobile oshisa lilanga lasekuseni: ufike ngaphambi kwesikhathi *ngenhlonipho,* futhi akukho lutho oluzokwenza umqondisi ovuka emnyango wokulayisha izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi azizwe enomusa kumuntu oseceleni komnyango. Kuzozwakala njengonethi. Yona inethi. Lezi zinto azihlukaniseki, futhi wayeka ukuzikhohlisa kudala ukuthi ukufisa kungazenza zihluke.

“Uyehla,” kusho uAchterberg ethule, efunda okuthile kucingo lakhe. “Ibhajhi selinyakazile. Umakhenikha uqobo. Hhayi ummeli. Hhayi i-VP.”

“Kulungile,” kusho uBrand, eqinisile, wasusa isandla emnyango ukuze into yokuqala ayibona ingabi ukuthi uyawubamba. “Lokho kusho ukuthi asidingi ukwenza lokhu ngommeli.”

Wayefike ukuzomfaka ekukhetheni okusheshayo, ngaphambi kokuba omunye ongacabanganga amkhethele. Wayezothola ukuthi okusheshayo nokucophelela kungaba into eyodwa yini egumbini elilodwa, okuwukuphela kombuzo umsebenzi wakhe wonke owake waba nawo, futhi angakaze awuphendule ngokusobala.

---

# Iron Ridge Fault

Ikheji lehla ebumnyameni ngesivinini esingamamitha amane ngomzuzwana, uMandla Buthelezi ebala amazinga ngokubanda komoya oshaya ebusweni bakhe.

Wayeseyekile ukufunda amabhodi wamazinga eminyakeni edlule. Izinombolo azizange zitshele lutho olungazwanga umzimba kakade. KwaLevel 18, umoya waba mncane futhi umanzi, njengokuphefumula ngezingubo. KwaLevel 26, kwavela iphunga elibabayo lokuqhuma okusha, wagwinya igamu lakhe ngamandla ukuzama ukuvula izindlebe. KwaLevel 33, phansi kwehlathi elisha, lapho bexosha khona i-carbon-leader reef emhlabathini ongakaze ubone ukukhanya kwelanga cishe iminyaka eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezintathu, ukushisa kwahlala esifubeni sakhe njengomuntu wesibili.

Amakhilomitha amathathu nohhafu, cishe. Kude kangangokuthi itshe eliphonswa emgodini eceleni kwakhe lingawa imizuzwana engamashumi amathathu, futhi wayezolahlekelwa umsindo walo kudala lingakafiki phansi.

Amadoda angamashumi amabili nambili ayehla naye ekuseni. Iningi lawo lalimi ngezinyawo ezithambile njengabantu abakwenzayo lokhu nsuku zonke futhi sebevumelene nakho. Abambalwa abasha babambe izinsimbi. Kwakukhona umfana omusha, umshana kaSizwe—wonke umuntu wayazi ukuthi ungumshana kaSizwe, ngoba yikho kuphela okwenza umfana ongakaze asebenze phansi asebenze kule ndawo esezingeni eliphansi kunalezi eziphezulu—futhi umfana wayehlala egwinya, ebheka phezulu entanjeni sengathi angabona ikhanda lemishini engaphezulu kwamakhilomitha amabili nohhafu edwaleni eliqinile.

"Okokuqala emazingeni angamashumi amathathu nantathu?" kubuza uMandla kuye ngesiZulu.

Umfana wanqekuzisa ikhanda. Akakwazanga ukukhuluma.

"Idwala likhuluma lapha phansi," kusho uMandla. "Izindaba zabantu abadala. Uma ulalela, uyaphila. Uma ungayilaleli—" Wazunguza. "Uba ingxenye yalo."

Elinye ikhona lomlomo womfana lanyakaza. Kwakuhle. Umfana okwazi ukuthola lokho ohambweni lwakhe lokuqala emazingeni angamashumi amathathu nantathu wayezophumelela.

Kodwa uMandla akazange amtshele ukuthi idwala belikhuluma isonto lonke, kanti abaphezulu abekho abalalelayo.

---

Ezinsukwini ezintathu ezedlule, wayeyifakile yena uqobo isikhalazo. Esiteyishini sokuphephela sikaLevel 30, lapho amafomu e-laminated egobeka futhi ethamba ngenxa yokufuma, wayeligcwalisa ngamagama amakhulu, aqondile, omuntu owashiya isikole eneminyaka eyishumi nane futhi ongakaze athembe ukubhala kwakhe.

*UKUZUNGUZELA OKUNCANE KWANDAYO. IZEHLO EZINTATHU EMAHORSI ANGAMASHUMI AMANE NESISHAGALOLUNYE EDUZANE NE-33 DECLINE FACE. UMSINDO WEDWALA EBUSUKU. SICELA UKUHLOLWA KWE-GEOTECH NGAPHAMBI KOKUQHUBEKA.*

Izithukuthuku ezintathu ezincane, ukuzamazama okuzwakala ezithendeni zezicathulo zabo kunokuzwakala ngendlebe, umhlaba ojulile uzinza ngaphansi kwengcindezi ebilokhu yakheka selokhu iVredefort impact yaphendula lawa ma-reef eceleni kudala. Ingcindezi ifuna indawo yokuphuma. I-reef lapha inothile; i-carbon-leader ithwala igolide ngezinga abangalibonanga eminyakeni eyishumi, futhi yilokho kanye okwenza i-decline iphushwe ngokushesha kunomjikelezo wokusekela.

Bebemboboza bese beqhumisa, besusa idwala eliphukile, bese belinda. Befaka ama-rock bolts, inetha kanye ne-shotcrete, bevumela umhlaba ovuliwe uthole umumo omusha ngaphambi kokumba phambili. Lona kwakuwumjikelezo. Lona owawugcina uphahla lusekhanda labo.

Kodwa ama-bolts aphuma emfishane kabili kuleli sonto. Ukulethwa kwe-grout kwafika sekwephuzile. Ithimba eliqashwe ngekhontrakthi elalimele ukufaka ukusekela lalishoda ngabathathu, ngoba omunye phezulu wayebathumele kwenye indawo ukuzofuna inani lokukhiqiza, futhi i-decline yaqhubeka ngoba i-decline ihlala iqhubeka, ngoba igolide emhlabathini liyilahleko kwi-spreadsheet kuze kube yilapho seliyimali egumbini.

Impendulo efomini likaMandla yabuya ezinsukwini ezimbili kamuva, ifakwe izimpawu zikaSizwe, umphathi wesigaba. Umugqa oyedwa.

*KUBONWE. QHUBEKA NGOKOHLELO. I-GEOTECH ISIHLELWE.*

Ihlelwe. UMandla wayesekade emayini isikhathi eside azi ukuthi *ihlelwe* kusho ukuthini. Kuchaza ukuthi kuzokwenziwa uma sekulungele. Kuchaza ukuthi kuzolandela uma amanani okukhiqiza esewavunyelwe. Kuchaza, ngolimi oluthile lokugwema imithetho olukhulunywa kule mayini iminyaka eyikhulu, *akuyona inkinga yakho, k----, buyela emsebenzini*. Ngaphandle kokuthi akekho osakusho lokho, hhayi emphakathini, kusukela izwe lashintsha. Amazwi anyamalala. Izibalo zashiyeka zinjalo. Izimpilo zabanye zisezindlekweni eziphezulu kunabanye, futhi umhlaba kwaLevel 33 awunandaba nolimi olusha.

Ngakho uMandla wehlela nezinye izinsizwa ezingamashumi amabili nanye, kanti phezulu kwakhe ukuhlolwa kwe-geotech kwakuhlezi emgqeni ngemuva kwakho konke okwenza imali ngokushesha.

---

Usuku lwasekuseni lwadlula njengokujwayelekile.

Bahamba behlelele phansi, amalambu ekhanda ehlukanisa uthuli, umoya uqina njalo uma behamba. Umgudu wokungenisa umoya wawukhala phezulu, uphusha umoya obandayo usuka phezulu. Noma kunjalo, amadoda ayevele egcwele umjuluko bengakafiki ebusweni. UMandla wahlolela ukusekela njengoba edlula, edonsa isandla sakhe esigqoke amagilavu enetha lensimbi, eshaya ama-bolt ngesipana, elalele umsindo aphendula ngawo. I-bolt enhle iyakhala. Ekhululekile ishaya buthule. Wathola ezimbili ezishaya buthule, wazimaka nge-chalk, wazizwa leyo nto encane ebanda esiswini ayeseyifunde eminyakeni engamashumi amathathu ukuthi akufanele ayingabaze noma ayiyekele.

Ebusweni, abebesebenza ebusuku babemboboze baqhumisa umjikelezo omusha. Idwala eliphukile lalivutha umhwamuko. Ngaphesheya kwalo, idwala elivuliwe lisha lihlala limnyama, lishisa ngaphansi kwamalambu, linobukhazikhazi obuphansi be-reef. Osemncane, umshana kaSizwe, ogama lakhe uMandla wagcina elizwile, uThando, wayelibuka sengathi ubuka ngaphakathi kwento engcwele.

"Iminyaka eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili belilindile," kusho uMandla kuye. "Sifika silithathe ngoLwesibili."

"Liheha," kusho uThando.

"Idwala nje." Kodwa akazange aphikisane ngokuphelele.

Basebenza. Umshini wokulayisha nowokuthwala nowokulahla wangena uzwakala, injini yawo yedizili igcwalisa indawo nomsindo ozwa emazinyo akho. Amadoda asebenza azungeze wona, esusa idwala elixegayo ngebha ende, ubudlova obuqeqeshiwe bokwehlisa okuzowela ngaphambi kokuba kwehle ngokwakho. Umjuluko wangena emehlweni kaMandla. Kude kwazwakala ipompo lisebenza. Umsindo wedwala weza kanye, phakathi nosuku, umsindo omude ophansi *crrrk* uqhamuka ngaphakathi edwaleni, umsindo ofana nomnyango ozinzayo endlini endala, ngaphandle kokuthi umnyango ungamitha ayikhulu we-quartzite ongaphansi kwengcindezi futhi indlu ingamakhilomitha amabili nohhafu omhlaba oqinile.

Umshayeli we-LHD wayivala injini. Isikhashana indawo yathula. Amadoda ayishumi nanye amile uthuli lugcwele emalambini, bonke belalele, bonke bezishaya sengathi bahlola okunye.

Umsindo awuzange uphinde uvele.

"Amabholithi," kusho uMandla, kungabonakali ukuthi ukhuluma nobani. "Namuhla. Anginandaba nokuthi uhlelo luthi yini. Akekho odlula lapha kuze kube sengibona insimbi phezulu."

Wacindezela iradio yakhe ukubikela esiteshini sikaLevel 30, kuSizwe, ukwenza kube semthethweni, ukubeka izwi lakhe kumarekhodi lapho ifomu lihlulekile khona.

Akasizange aqede umusho.

---

Iphutha belilinde isikhathi eside kunanoma yini yomuntu.

Kwagelezela edwaleni ngengela engazange ibhalwe kunoma yimuphi umdwebo. Kwakuyisibazi eselapheka esashiywa umthelela wasendulo, indawo ebuthakathaka eyavalwa ngaphansi kwengcindezi iminyaka eyizigidi ezimbili manje esivuleka ngoba abantu basike indawo engenalutho eceleni kwayo base besusa idwala elayigcina ivaliwe. Ingcindezi ifuna indawo yokuphuma. Bekhuluma isonto lonke. Izehlakalo ezintathu eduze kobuso bokwehla. *Umsindo wedwala ebusuku.* Bekunike zonke izixwayiso umhlaba owaziyo ukuzinika.

Lapho ikhulula, ikhulula konke ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Akuzange kube khona msindo wesixwayiso kulokhu, noma kungenjalo, umsindo wesixwayiso kanye nenhlekelele kwenzeka ngesikhathi esisodwa. Ukudlidliza okufika phansi ngaphambi kokufika emoyeni, okwashaya amadoda ezithendeni zezicathulo zawo nasemathanjeni ezimpondo zawo ngesikhathi esisodwa. UMandla wabona isipanela siphuma esandleni sakhe simile, umzuzwana ongachazeki, emlotheni okhanyiswa izibani ngaphambi kokuba konke kwehle. Izinsika ezimile zakhala. Inetha lensimbi lakhihlika emuva njengolusu. Waphonswa eceleni wangena enqwabeni yokungcola, ikhanda lakhe lishayisana nedwala, kwase kuba mnyama, kwase kuba kukhanya okukhulu ngezibani zamadoda awehlayo, kwase kuba mnyama futhi njengoba umoya uthululeka phansi.

Umlotha wehla njengolwandle.

Uthuli lwagcwala emlonyeni wakhe, lwancibilika luba lukhuni futhi lumnyama ngemuva kolimi, ukunambitheka kodwala oluvuliwe. Ingubo yakhe yomkhono ibimanzi ebusweni bakhe, usawoti nothuli ndawonye. Wayengakwazi ukuphefumula. Wacindezela ubuso bakhe ethangeni lengalo, kodwa uthuli lwangena noma kunjalo, luhle njengefulawa, lwagcwala ikhala nomphimbo wakhe. Kude eduze kwakhe indoda yayikhala, kude kancane intaba isahamba. Umsindo omkhulu wokuguguleka komhume ugoqana, wamatshe amabili nesigamu eshintshela isisindo sawo. Phansi kwadlikiza. Okuthile okukhulu kwehla lapho i-LHD ibikhona nomsindo ongengomculo kodwa isibhaxu emzimbeni wakhe wonke.

Kwathi sekudlulile okubi kakhulu, kwaba nokuthula okukhulu kangangokuthi uMandla wacabanga umzuzwana ukuthi usefile.

Wayengakafi. Izindlebe zakhe zazikhala. Umlomo wakhe ugcwele uthuli. Ingalo yakhe yesobunxele ayizange imphendule uma eyiyala ukuba inyakaze, kwathi lapho ephoqelela, izinhlungu zaphakama zaba mhlophe, wabe eseyeka ukuyiyala. Walala ebumnyameni naphakathi kothuli, kancane kancane, ngendlela indoda ezihlola ngayo emva kokuwa, waqonda ukuthi uyaphila, nokuthi umoya usuyekile ukuhamba, umgudu womoya ushonile, umoya obandayo ovela phezulu usunqunyiwe, nokuthi ukukhala sekuyekile.

"Sondela," wazama ukumemeza. *Sondelani.* Izwi lakhe laphuma lingukukhwehlela. Wakhafulela emnyama waphinda. "Sondela! Amaphuzu! Balani!"

Ubumnyama baphendula.

Ngelinye nelinye, bekhwehlela, bekhama, ngezilimi ezintathu, amazwi afika.

Bayisikhombisa.

Kwabayishumi nanye ebusweni, abayisikhombisa baphendula. Amadoda ayisikhombisa endaweni encane ephephile ngemuva kodonga lwamatshe olaluyindlela ababehamba ngayo ihora eledlule. UMandla wathola isibani sakhe sesibili ngokuthinta, wasivula, ukukhanya okuncane kwekhemikhali kwambonisa umumo omusha womhlaba: igumbi eliphansi elinesimo esibi lapho kwakukhona khona indlela yokusebenza, uphahla luseduze kakhulu kwezinye izindawo, indlela yokuphuma igcwele udoti kusukela phansi kuya emuva kungekho ngisho igebe ingwe engadlula kulo.

UThando wayephila. Umfana wayecindezeleke ekhoneni lodonga, amehlo akhe emakhulu ebusweni obumpunga nothuli, ebambe idolo lakhe. Wayekhipha umsindo omncane, ephindaphinda, hhayi amazwi.

"Uyaphila," uMandla watshela yena. "Uyangizwa? Ulalele idwala. Manje phefumula kancane. Kancane. Uma usebenzisa umoya njengomuntu ocebile, uzofa uphansi lapha ungumhlwempu."

Wayengakazi ukuthi lokho kuyiqiniso kangakanani.

Wayazi izibalo. Amadoda ayisikhombisa. Umoya owodwa wendawo yokusabela, awukho umoya omusha, uthuli lusahleli. Udonga lwamatshe phakathi kwabo nendlela yokwehla eya esiteshini esingamakhilomitha amathathu ukusuka eshaftini elijule amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu.

UThando wakhwehlela, umsindo owomile, wakhafulela uthuli ebumnyameni. Kude phansi egumbini indoda yashintsha izicathulo zayo phezu kodoti, izilungiselela ukulinda.

---

Phezulu kwaziwa ngaphambi kokuba uthuli luhlale phansi amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu ngaphansi.

Egumbini lokulawula imigodi, isikhombisi sokuqhuba sathuthumela, umugqa wagxuma waphuma emgqeni wawo njengentambo eshaywayo. Umphathi wesikhathi, owesifazane ogama lakhe linguRefilwe owayesebenza phezulu iminyaka eyishumi nanye futhi eseke wakuzwa lokhu ngaphambili, wayesemgqeni ophuthumayo ngaphambi kokuba i-alamu iqede umzuliswano wayo wokuqala. Izinombolo zatshela indaba ngokushesha kunanoma yiliphi izwi. Level 33 decline. Ubukhulu obungasaseyona i-tremor. Ingcindezi yomoya yehlile ezingeni elingezansi. Ukuxhumana kunqunyiwe ngaphansi kweLevel 30.

Wathola amadoda aseLevel 30 station emgqeni. Amazwi abo aphuma ebuthakathaka, egcwele ukwesaba: bakuzwa, indlela yokwehla igcwele uthuli, abakwazi ukubona, uphahla lukhala. Bakhuphe, baphume.

Wabakhupha. Ikheji yakhuphula abasebenzi baseLevel 30 nabaseLevel 26 phezulu ngokuthula okuphuthumayo wonke umuntu wavuma ukuthi kwahamba kahle ngangokunokwenzeka. Emini ikhanda lemininingwane selizungezwe izimoto. Ntambama amaqembu e-proto—iqembu lezokutakula emigodini, amadoda aqeqeshwa ngalokhu futhi athandaza ukuthi angalokothi akwenze—asegqoke futhi ehla, nenqubo yezimo eziphuthumayo yenkampani isebenza kahle njengomshini.

Sekuhlwa, izimoto ezinamanombolo afanele zaqala ukufika.

Zafika ngokulandelana okuthile, futhi umuntu omdala obuka esakhiweni sokushintsha wayengafunda isimo sonke ngokubuka le nqubo. Okokuqala umphathi wemayini, kwalandela isikhulu sezokuphepha sesifunda, kwalandela amadoda anezicathulo ezinhle angahlali phansi futhi angaze aye khona. Umqondisi wokusebenza waseJohannesburg, iphini likamongameli okuthile, indoda evela eMnyangweni Wezokumbiwa Kwamaminerali ephethe i-lanyard ne-tablet nobuso obungenalutho bokucabanga ngomthwalo wemfanelo ngesikhathi sangempela. Imoto kahulumeni. Kwalandela, ngokungagwemeki, imoto yokuqala yezindaba, imise emnyango lapho ezokuphepha zingayivumeli ukudlula, isisila sayo sesiqalile ukukhuphuka sibheke esibhakabhakeni saseHighveld.

USizwe, umphathi weseshini owabhala *NOTED. CONTINUE PER PLAN.* ngamagama akhe ahlanzekile ezinsukwini ezimbili ezedlule, wayemi onqenqemeni lwendawo ekhanyisiwe njengoba ubumnyama buhla futhi akashongo lutho kunoma ubani. Wayephethe ikhophi lefomu lesigameko sikaMandla Buthelezi ephaketheni lejakhethi yakhe. Wayelikhiphe kufayela eLevel 30 station ngaphambi kokuba enyuke. Wayengakazi ukuthi uzolenza kanjani. Wayazi kuphela ukuthi ifomu likhona, futhi walifunda, futhi wabhala *continue per plan*, futhi ukuthi phansi amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu, indoda eyaligcwalisa ingase ifile noma ilinde ebumnyameni ukuze ithole impendulo.

Umqondisi wezokusebenza wayemi ngaphansi kwezibani ezikhanyayo, ephatha ucingo, esebenzisa isandla sakhe esingenalutho ukuvala indlebe yakhe kwenye ngenxa yomsindo wejenereyitha. “—cha, angikaboni umbiko we-geotech, yilokho kanye engikukhuluma, ngifuna ube phambi kwami ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani—” Wama, walalela, futhi izwi lakhe lashintsha indlela yokukhuluma kulowo owayekolunye uhlangothi lomugqa. “Impela. Impela. Yonke imithombo. Okubaluleke kakhulu kithi ukuphepha kwabantu bethu, senza konke okusemandleni ethu.” Wavala ucingo wema umzuzu owodwa, ecabanga, kwakubonakala, ukuthi yini okumele ibe yiqiniso ephepheni nokuthi yini okumele ibe yiqiniso esiteji, nokuthi lezo zinto ziyefana yini.

---

Izithombe zokushisa zafika cishe ngehora lesishiyagalolunye ebusuku, kwathi amahora ambalwa inkundla ekhanyiswe izibani e-Iron Ridge yabamba okuthile ebingakubambi selokhu kwavela isexwayiso: ithemba.

Amaqembu okuqala ayehlise phansi ngendlela okude ngangokuthi umhlaba uvumele, ehudula intambo yokulalela ukuzamazama komhlaba ye-fibre-optic, ikhamera ye-borehole kanye nomshini wokulinganisa ukushisa. Ithimba le-geotech eliphezulu lachitha usuku ligebha imbobo encane yokufaka amathuluzi phansi liya endaweni yokuwa lisuka ezingeni elidala elase seliphelile, imbobo engangephayiphi, efakwe phansi emhlabathini abebethandaza ukuthi uzinzile. Ithuluzi lokushisa lehla ngentambo yalo, futhi isithombe elasithumela ebhuthini yokulawula senze umxhumanisi wokuhlenga abeke isandla sakhe emlonyeni.

Izidumbu ezishisayo. Iqoqo lazo, lingacaci kahle ngenxa yodaka namatshe kanye nemingcele yamalensi, kodwa lingaphikiswa muntu owaziyo ukufunda umbala wokushisa komuntu ophilayo uma kuqhathaniswa nomhlaba obandayo. Akukhona okukodwa. Kuningana. Kuyanyakaza.

“Bangaki?” kubuza umqondisi wezokusebenza.

“Angikwazi ukusho.” Umxhumanisi akazange abheke phezulu. “Bahlanu. Bayisithupha. Mhlawumbe abayisikhombisa.” Wathula kancane. “Baphila. Njengoba bekunjalo ihora elidlule.”

Kwakukhona amadoda kulelo gumbi aseke angcwaba abangani ezimayini, futhi abaningana baphenduka ukuze bazilungise.

Kodwa unjiniyela we-geotech owayeseqhulwini wegumbi wayengabheki isithombe sokushisa. Wayebambe ipeni esandleni, wayeseyekile ukusicofa. Phandle, ijenereyitha yayikhala, omunye ehleka kakhulu engenandaba. Wayebuka intambo yokulalela ukuzamazama komhlaba, umkhondo okwakufanele ukuthi usuzinza ekuthuleni okude kwemayini ethola isimo sayo esisha, kodwa wawungazinzi. Wawukhuluma. Izehlakalo ezincane, kwalandela enkulu, kwase kuba neqoqo. Iphutha elavuleka lalingenqedi. Ukuwa kuvule izikhala ezintsha, futhi lezo zikhala ezintsha zabelana umthwalo emhlabathini ongakaze uwuthwale, futhi lowo mhlaba uphendula ngendlela kuphela itshe elixinekile elingaphendula ngayo.

Wadonsela umqondisi wezokusebenza eceleni wamkhombisa umkhondo, futhi ubuso bakhe, ngaphansi kokukhanya kwe-laptop, benza into eyinkimbinkimbi.

“Baphila,” esho.

“Njengamanje.” Wayemvumela ukuthi kuhlale. “Kusese kuwa. Izehlakalo zonke zivula kakhulu. Amadoda angaphakathi azodoba. Kuzothatha izinsuku. Emhlabathini engingakwazi ukuqinisekisa ukuthi ngeke uphinde uhambe.” Waphefumula. “Abasizi bengena endaweni esaqhubeka nokuwa. Ngenxa yamadoda mhlawumbe angeke baphile isikhathi eside kangako. Angikwazi ukwenza imodeli yokungena okungaphepha. Ngisebenzile usuku lonke. Umhlaba awungivumeli.”

“Ngizodinga lokho kuwe ngokubhala. Ingxenye yokuqinisekisa.” Wakhuluma kancane, engambheki ngqo. “Hhayi manje. Kodwa ngizodinga.”

Wezwa kahle ukuthi leso sigwebo sisho ukuthini. “Uzothola noma yini idatha engiyisekelayo.”

“Kumele kube nendlela.”

“Mhlawumbe ngezindawo ezindala eziphezulu. Indlela yokuzungeza. Ingxenye evaliwe engenhla kwe-reef horizon, eshiywe, mhlawumbe ihamba ngokufana, yehle eduze.” Wazamazama ikhanda. “Imisebenzi yasemandulo yeminyaka eyikhulu. Ayikho inkxaso eyake yahlolwa umuntu ophilayo. Iqinile kakhulu kunanoma yini ithimba lokuhlenga elingadlula kuyo ligqoke imishini. Angikwazi ukuthumela amadoda endleleni engingakwazi ukuyihlola, endaweni esaqhubeka nokuwa, ngokwethemba.” Wama. Waziphoqa ukuthi aqhubeke, ngoba wayengunjiniyela futhi okunye kwakuyoba amanga. “Akuyona indlela yokuhlenga. Akuyona eyabantu.”

Umqondisi wezokusebenza wabuka isithombe sokushisa isikhathi eside. Izimo ezishisayo bezinganyakazi imizuzu embalwa. Kwathi enye yazo yanyakaza, kwavela ukushisa okusha, wathuthumela sengathi ithinte yena.

---

eGoli, endaweni yokusebenzela enuka i-solder nensimbi epholile, u-Arin Ndlela wayebuka lezo zimo ezishisayo esikrinini esihlukile.

Iwashi lombuzo beliqhubeka amahora angamashumi amathathu nanye, kanti i-Guardian ayikaze inyakaze nakanye. I-IAOC yayiyimise lapho yayilengiswe khona. Umthetho kaBrand, owafikiswa ngumzimba ethekwini ngosuku befika ngalo: akukho ukuvulwa, akukho ukuhlanganiswa, akukho ukuphuma esakhiweni kuze kuvalwe uphenyo. Ngakho uchithe kusihlwa evule ama-crash logs, ewahlela ngendlela owenza ngayo isivumo, enquma ukuthi umuntu kufanele atshelwe ngani okubi kakhulu ngawe kuqala.
