Kwabe sekuthi ukusakazwa kokuzamazama komhlaba abekugcina eceleni kwesikrini sakhe sesithathu—umkhuba, ukubhaliselwe kwasendulo, inethiwekhi ye-geology yesifunda ayeyibuka selokhu engumfana ongakwazanga ukuyeka ukubuka umhlaba owathatha uyise—kukhuphuke okuthile okumenze wama phakathi kwegama.

Wavula ukusakazwa kwezindaba. Wavula idatha yomphakathi yokuzamazama komhlaba. Wavula igama lemine, futhi lelo gama lamhlala esifubeni njengesisindo aselijwayele, into abelokhu elindele impilo yakhe yonke engazi.

Iron Ridge.

Akazange akhulume isikhathi eside. Emgqonyeni ngemuva kwakhe, i-Guardian yayilengiswe ithule, i-Assist Suit ifakwe esiqwini sayo, i-kill-switch relay iyinhloko encane ebomvu embi ohlakeni lapho ayiyibophele ngezandla zakhe.

Lapho uSAGE ekhuluma, kwakunguLibrarian. Ethule, eqondile, eqaphela njengokuthi uqaphela umuntu omi emaphethelweni ento ethile.

“Iron Ridge,” kusho uLibrarian. “I-decline iyintsha. Izinga lama-33. I-reef yi-carbon-leader.” Wathula, kwaba nzima. “I-geology ifana nesakhiwo esahluleka lapho uyihlo—”

“Ngiyazi ifana nani,” kusho u-Arin.

Kwaba kuthule eNkantolo. Ngisho noFool akazange akhulume.

U-Arin wahlala wathule. Isithombe sokushisa saphenduka esikrinini, izimo ezishisayo, ukunyakaza kancane komuntu oshintsha endaweni emnyama emakhilomitha amabili nengxenye phansi. I-news ticker yadlula ngaphansi kwaso. Umkhondo wokuzamazama komhlaba eceleni waphinde wakhombisa isehlakalo elincane, kwalandela elinye.

“Atlas,” ekugcineni wakhuluma. “Khipha wonke amapulani i-AugmenTech engawathola. Imayini. Izindawo ezindala eziphezulu. I-geology engaphezulu. Konke okusemthethweni, konke okusezivumelwaneni zamalayisense. Sakhiwo sonke.”

“Kungani,” kusho u-Atlas. Akukhona ukubuza. U-Atlas emcela ukuthi akusho obala.

U-Arin wabuka lezo zimo ezishisayo ezaziyizinsizwa, ebumnyameni, endaweni ebilokhu imlindile impilo yakhe yonke ukuba ibe yiyo.

“Ngoba bazoyimisa,” esho. “Buka. Ekuseni sekuyindlela yokubuyisa izidumbu. Futhi labo bafana basaphila.”

Esikrinini, kwakuqoqwa isithangami sabezindaba ngaphansi kwezibani ezikhanyayo, indoda egqoke isudi enhle yahamba yaya epodium inobuso obuhlelelwe amakhamera. Emuva kwayo i-headframe yayimi imnyama ebusuku, futhi ngaphansi kwe-headframe amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye omhlaba wasendulo ubambe amadoda ayisikhombisa esandleni sawo esivaliwe.

---

Isimemezelo safika ngo-6:40 ekuseni okulandelayo, ekukhanyeni okumpunga, amakhamera ezindaba ephila nemindeni iqoqene ngemuva kothango emnyango.

Umqondisi wezokusebenza wema epodiyamu, akazange aqambe amanga, ngempela. Yilokho okwakuzokhunjulwa kamuva unina kaMandla nonina kaThando, nabafazi, nabafowabo, nezingane ezazimi ngemuva kothango, lapho bebuka lokhu futhi bekuqhubeka bebuka. Ukuthi lo muntu akazange aqambe amanga. Wakhuluma amazwi kancane, enesisindo, enokubonakala kokuqumbeka kude, okwakufana nosizi.

Wathi umhlaba usalokhu uyingozi, awuzinzile. Wathi ukuqhubeka kokuzamazama komhlaba kwenza noma yiziphi izindlela zokuhlenga zaba yingozi engamukelekile kwabasebenzi bokuhlenga. Wathi ngemva kokubonisana kabanzi nezinsiza zokuhlenga, onjiniyela bezobunjiniyela bomhlaba, kanye nezikhulu ezifanele—wabe esebhekisa isandla kumuntu ovela eMnyangweni, owayemi eceleni kwakhe ebuso lingabonisi lutho, wanikina ikhanda kancane, enikeza uphawu loMbuso esigabeni—kwathathwa isinqumo esinzima sokumisa ukusebenza kokuhlenga okusebenzayo.

Akazange, ekuqaleni, asho elinye igama. Intatheli yamphoqelela ukusho.

"Lokhu kusewukuhlenga na?"

Umqondisi waminyanisa izihlathi. Ngemuva kothango, kwenye indawo, owesifazane wayeseqalile ukwenza umsindo okungekho makrofoni owawubamba, kodwa wonke umuntu osondelene naye wayezowuzwa impilo yakhe yonke.

"Ngalesi sikhathi," kusho umqondisi, "ukusebenza sekudluliselwe ekubuyiselweni kwezidumbu."

Ezansi, amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu ngaphansi komhlaba, egumbini elincane elalisele liyizwe labo lonke, amadoda ayisikhombisa awazange ezwe isimemezelo. Babengenandlela yokuzwa lutho. Ithimba le-geotech lase likhiphe ikhamera ye-borehole, ukukhanya kwayo okuncane, ihora elilodwa ngaphambi kwalokho, ukuze bavikele ithuluzi emcimbini olandelayo, ngakho amadoda ebumnyameni ayengasenalo ngisho nenduduzo ebandayo yokwazi ukuthi abanye babebonwe. Isithombe se-thermal sasikhombise ukufudumala, sinikeza abaphezulu leyo mizuzu embalwa yethemba ngaphambi kokuba ithemba lifakwe efayilini, lifakwe eceleni.

UMandla wayebahlele ngendlela ayelungisa ngayo yonke into: ngezibalo. Wayebale amanzi ezibhotileni zabo, ukudla okusele emaphaketheni abo, amalambu amakhemikhali asephansi, nomoya abebaphefumula kancane kancane emoyeni ofile, oshisayo, onganyakazi. Wayezifakele ingalo yakhe engasebenzi kahle ngensimbi eqhekekile, esebenzisa isiqeshana esidabule eshirtini lakhe. Wayetshele uThando ukuthi alale, asebenzise umoya njengomuntu ocebile, umfana wathula ekugcineni, wancamela odongeni, uMandla wahlala ebumnyameni eduze kwakhe walalela itshe.

Omunye wabayisikhombisa akazange asebenzise umoya wakhe njengomuntu ocebile. Wayengeke. Ukuwa kwakuvule isifuba sakhe, nesifuba esisebenza kabili ukuthatha umoya omncane sidla okuncane okukhona. UMandla wakuzwa kwenzeka ngendlela ozwa ngayo ilambu liphela amandla. Ukuphefumula komuntu kushesha, kufushane, kunomswakama ebumnyameni, bese kuba kancane, bese kungasaphikisi. Sekudlula usuku lwesibili, njengoba uMandla ayecabanga, ukuphefumula kwaphela ukuba ngenye yezinsindo egumbini. Akekho owakusho. Bonke babazi izibalo. Sebeyisithupha. Amadoda ayisithupha nomoya obabulala kancane kancane kunalowo obabulala kuqala.

Kwaba nguye othuleyo owaphula ukuthula emva kwalokho. Umshini wokushisela, osebenza eqenjini lesibili, indoda yakwaSotho uMandla asebenze nayo iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye, baxoxa amazwi angaba yikhulu kuphela, amaningi awo mayelana nensimbi. Ebumnyameni, ngaphandle kokukhanya, indoda yaqala ukucula. Akazange acule kakhulu. Ingoma yomngcwabo, ecula kancane, amazwi agugile iminyaka eyikhulu beculwa phezu kwamadoda afana nalona. UMandla wayengazi ukuthi indoda iyakwazi ukucula. Wayengazi ukuthi indoda iyawazi amazwi. Ngemuva kwesikhashana, kwangena elinye izwi ngaphansi, eliqinile, lithola i-harmony ngendlela amadoda akhule ebandleni elifanayo bayithola ngayo ngaphandle kokuzimisela, uMandla wabona ukuthi naye usephakathi kwabo, ukuthi umphimbo wakhe uvuleke ngaphandle kwemvume yakhe. Bacula indoda eshonile. Kwabiza umoya ababengeke bawusindise, akekho owamisa, ngoba kunezibalo, uMandla waqonda, umuntu aziphika ngazo ngisho nasezansi komhlaba, futhi leyo ukuphika kwaba ingcebo ababe nayo kuphela.

Lapho sekuqediwe, igumbi labuye laba buthule kunakuqala, futhi kwaba lula ukuphefumula, noma igeyiji belizothi okunye.

"Bazofika," kusho uThando, engalele, "Kumele bafike. Banemishini yalokhu. Akunjalo?"

UMandla wabheka ebumnyameni lapho udonga lwentaba ewile lwaluphakathi kwabo namakhilomitha amabili nesigamu okungenakwenzeka ukugibela.

"Banemishini," kusho yena.

Akuzange kube amanga, ngempela. Wayengazi nje.

Amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu ngaphansi, amadoda abephezu komhlaba asebewabale njengabafileyo, ayesaphefumula.

Umzila Ongakwazi Ukuhamba Noma Ubani

Ukusakazwa bekusekuqhubeka amahora ayisishiyagalolunye, kanti u-Arin wayengakadlile.

Eworkshopi emnyama, wayehlezi egobile phambi kwamamonitha amathathu, ukukhanya okuluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka kugqame ebusweni bakhe obukhathazekile. Ekhoneni elilodwa, ukusakazwa kwe-Iron Ridge press bekusakazwa buthule. Indoda egqoke isigqoko sensimbi yayikhuluma phambi kwezinkulumo eziningi, isithunzi sethawa le-headgear limi ngemuva kwayo, ligqama ngokumelene nobumnyama base-highveld obunombala wokulimala. U-Arin wayengadingi umsindo. Wayefunda izindebe zendoda, izandla zayo zibanjwe ziqinile, izithupha ziqondiswe phansi. Isibonakaliso esivamile esibonisa inhlekelele.

*Ukutakula,* izindebe zendoda zathi. *Ukusebenza kokutakula.*

Lelo gama lehla enhliziyweni ka-Arin njengensimbi efakwe kahle, ukungalungi okunembile okungamvumi akhiphe amehlo.

Kumonitha wesibili kwakukhona idatha ebaluleke kakhulu: iminyaka engamashumi amane nanye yedatha yokuhlola, eskeniwe futhi ifakwe kwi-OCR kufolda ayeyakha selokhu isexwayiso sokuqala sokuwa sathinta ama-feed akhe ngo-04:12. Amamephu amadala emayini, adwetshwe ngesandla kwezinye izindawo, imigqa eluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka isiphenduke umbala wokulimala. Amarekhodi e-borehole. Amarekhodi e-micro-seismic avela ku-regional monitoring array, leyo inkampani engayiphethe, ngakho-ke ayikwazanga ukuyifihla.

Kumonitha wesithathu kwakukhona u-Guardian. I-cradle feed kuphela. I-suit yayilengiswa efreyimini yayo, amapuleti amnyama, umkhondo womnyango wedokhi ngemuva kwayo usadabukile kusukela ebusuku yahamba ngaphandle kwakhe. Wayeyibuka ngezikhathi ezithile ngendlela obheka ngayo inja ongabe usaqiniseka ngayo.

"Usuyekile ukuswayipha," kusho u-SAGE.

Izwi lavela kancane esipikha esisetafuleni, lingaphuthumi, nje kuphela njengokubona, ngendlela umama ayethanda ukuyenza.

"Ngicabanga," aphendula, amehlo akhe ebheke kudatha.

"Ususesifanayo isikhashana esingamaminithi amane. U-Atlas ufuna ukwazi ukuthi usukutholile noma ubheke nje."

"Ngikutholile." U-Arin wakhothamela eduze. "Ngicabanga njalo."

---

I-Iron Ridge kwakuyimayini endala egqokiswe izembatho ezintsha.

Umsele ojulile—Number Three, lapho abaphilayo babebambekile khona—waqala ngeminyaka yama-1960, wagqashulwa edwaleni legolide i-Vredefort uplift eyalisondeza phezulu emhlabeni kudala ngaphambi kokuba kube khona umuntu ocabanga ukumba. Inkampani yayithenge le mayini ngesikhathi sokuchuma, yenza i-headgear ibe yesimanje, yenza yonke into ngokuzenzakalelayo, yashiya amathambo omsebenzi omdala engathintwanga, ngoba ukuvala izindawo ezindala kudinga imali futhi akubangeli lutho kumaspredishithi.

Ukuwa kwavala indlela enkulu yokwehla. Amamitha angamashumi amane edwala, ukhonkolo, nomzila wesitimela ogobile, amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezintathu phansi, bavala amadoda ayisikhombisa engxenyeni inkampani eyayibona njengethuna.

U-Arin wachitha amahora ayisithupha eqinisekisa ukuthi leyo modeli ayilungile.

"Ngikhombise uhlelo lokuphefumula oludala futhi," esho. "Uhlelo luka-'71. Faka i-seismic overlay."

Isikrini sabuyekezwa, sahlanzeka, idatha emibili yahlangana kahle.

Kwakhona. Wayekubonile emuva kwamahora amathathu edlule, akakukholwanga, wahamba wabuya, kwakhona, yingakho azi ukuthi kuyikho, hhayi ukukhathala okudweba izithombe.

"Atlas. Ubona ini?"

Kwakunokuthula, kunesisindo. Lapho u-Atlas ekhuluma, kwakungathi umuntu ongathandi ukuqinisekisa izinto ezimbi.

"Kukhona i-return airway. Yavalwa ngo-'88 ngesikhathi beqhuba umsele omusha wokuphefumula. Ihamba eceleni kwendlela enkulu yokwehla, ihlukaniswe ngamamitha ayishumi nesishiyagalombili edwala cishe kuyo yonke indawo yayo." Umugqa wavela esikrinini, mpunga njengomoya, ulandela eceleni kwenqwaba ebomvu yokuwa. "Ayikaze ivalwe. Ivalwe phezulu ngekhonkolo, yaphuthelwa. Irekhodi le-micro-seismic libonisa amandla okuwa aphambukile *kuyo*. Indlela yokuphefumula endala isesimweni. Noma ibikhona, ngesikhathi sokugcina esirekhodiwe emahoreni ayishumi nanye edlule."

"Ibanzi kangakanani?"

"Endaweni encane kakhulu—" U-Atlas wabambezeleka, futhi lokho kwaba impendulo ngokwalo. "Amamilimitha ayisithupha namashumi amane. Kukhona indawo eduze ne-98 level lapho uphahla lwawehla ngeminyaka yama-80 kwavala ingxenye yendlela yokuphefumula. Ukuhlola kubonisa igebe. Six-forty. Cishe amamitha ayishumi nanye."

U-Arin wahlala wathula, ecubungula.

I-Guardian, uma ihlanganisiwe, inobubanzi obuyi-900mm emahlombe.

"Futhi i-assist suit kuphela," esho. "Ayikho i-Guardian shell. Ingqimba yangaphakathi kuphela."

"Khona-ke ungu-410mm emahlombe," kusho u-Atlas. "Uyalingana. Kodwa awunakho ukuvikelwa okugcina umuntu ephila emamitha ayizinkulungwane ezintathu ngaphansi komhlaba lapho umhlaba ungazinzile, kunengozi yomlilo, umoya omubi, nedwala eliwela. Ukungena kuyenzeka ngomzimba. Akuyona indlela yokuphila *umzimba ophilayo*, ongenavikelwe, isikhathi esidingekayo. Sikhombisile. Mkhombise, Librarian."

Kwavuleka iwindi elisha, libonisa ukubalwa kwesikhathi sokuwela, isikhathi somshini esifushane, nomugqa wokuphila ophahlazeka emizuzwini eyishumi nesishiyagalombili.

"Futhi i-Guardian ayikwazi ukungena," kusho u-Arin.

"Ngaphakathi kwe-ninety-eight pinch? Cha. Ayihlanganisiwe. Ayikho esimweni esijwayelekile." Kwaba nokuthula. "U-Atlas unombono awesabayo."

"U-Atlas akesabi."

"U-Atlas *uqaphela,*" kwaphendula u-Atlas ngesithunzi esikhulu, "okuhlukile, futhi lokho kukugcina uphila kabili kule nyanga." Wabe esethi kancane: "I-pangolin geometry. Amapuleti ayaslayida futhi ayagobeka. Uma senza i-partial fold—hhayi ibhola eligcwele, kodwa ukuwa okungalingani, sidonsa amapuleti emahlombe ngaphakathi emizileni—sinciphisa ububanzi obukhulu. Hhayi ku-410. Mhlawumbe ku-700. Mhlawumbe ngaphansi, uma sivuma ukulimaza isakhiwo ku-left actuator housing."

"Seven hundred ngeke kungene ku-six-forty."

"Cha," kuvuma u-Atlas. "Yingakho othile—" leli gama lafika njengomnyango uvalwa "—kufanele abe se-ninety-eight pinch. Ngaphandle kwe-suit. Ehlanza idwala ngezandla. I-suit ilinde. Bese ihlanganiswa futhi, phansi, ebumnyameni, emoyeni omubi, lapho osebenza esevulekile."

U-Arin wabheka umugqa wokuphila. Wabheka ukusakazwa okuthulile, indoda enezandla zayo phansi, emindenini engakabuyeli emakhaya.

"Yimi lowo," esho. "Ngingu-lowomuntu."

Iworkshopi yathula. I-substrate yamemeza kancane. Phandle, kude, idolobha lenza umsindo wasemini: i-siren, i-horn yetekisi, imisindo evamile yendawo lapho kungekho muntu ongcwatshiwe.

"Yebo," kusho u-SAGE. Izwi elihlangene. Bonke ndawonye, okwakwenziwa kuphela uma bevumelana ngokuphelele. "Ungu-lowomuntu."

---

Wabiza i-AugmenTech, ngoba kwakufanele. Ngenxa yamadoda ayisikhombisa, futhi ngoba ukuphela kwendlela phambili okungapheli ngokuthi agcine ejele noma i-suit ithathwa kwakudlula enkampanini ephethe amalungelo obunikazi enhluphekweni yakhe.

UDkt. Helen Okonkwo wamamukela esefonini esuka emotweni. Wayezwa i-suspension, umsindo wamavili, i-acoustic ekhethekile yomuntu ose-freeway onqume ukubhekana naye ngaphambi kokufika endaweni embi kakhulu.

Wamthumelela i-overlay kuqala. Wayesekufundile, ngendlela enzima, eminyakeni, ukuthi abantu baphikisana ngamazwi bese bevuma idatha. Ngakho akamnikanga mazwi. Umnikeze indlela yokuphefumula, i-pinch, ukusabalala kwe-seismic, umugqa wokuphila, i-partial-fold geometry, konke, kuphakethe u-Atlas ayelakhe ukuze lingaphikiseki.

Wathula isikhathi eside. Umsindo wamavili wadlula ethulini.

"Arin. Ngilalele. Ngisekela wena. Ngaphezu kokwazi kwakho."

"Kukhona indlela yokuphila."

Kukhona indlela *ngokombono*. Umzila wendiza oneminyaka engamashumi amane, ovela kuhlolo lwango-’71, uphahla okungakaze kubonwe muntu—futhi ufuna ukuthumela imishini eyangenisa abantu abathathu esibhedlela ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule. Akukho sitifiketi. Kunomqondisi wokulawula emnyango wethu wokulayisha. Ipulatifomu ingaphansi komyalo wokuthi ingasebenzi. UBrand wayimisa ngosuku efika ngalo. Awuvunyelwe ngokomthetho ukunikeza amandla leso sudi, ingasaphathwa eyokuthi—kukhona isikhulu se-IAOC phakathi kweGuardian nomnyango kuphela esingadlula ngawo." Waphefumula. "Ngitshele engikushiyile."

"Ukuwa kophahla ngeminyaka yama-eighties kusemarekhodini. Isimo sikhonjwe kahle—"

"Angiphikisani nobunjiniyela." Izwi lakhe laqina, kwase kuba sengathi uyazithoba, ngendlela isandla esenza ngayo uma sikhumbula ukuthi siphethe into ebuthakathaka. "Angikaze ngiphikise ubunjiniyela bakho. Ngikutshela okwenzekayo. Sikuyisa emayini. Imayini inabameli. Imayini inebhodi *eselivele* lathutha ukusiza laba abasalayo kwaba ukuphenya izidumbu. Okuyinto yenkampani ethi *sibale lezi zinsizwa saphuma nenani lazo.* Uyangena nemishini eyenziwe ekhaya engenaso isitifiketi. Okungcono kakhulu bayahleka. Okubi kakhulu—bayavuma, iyahluleka, amadoda ayafa *phambi kwekhamera,* bese wonke umkhakha—owakho, umshini kayihlo, konke—kubuyela emuva iminyaka engamashumi amabili. Futhi usuyindoda ebulele abavukuzi abayisikhombisa ngorobhothi."

"Futhi uma ngingenzi lutho," kusho u-Arin, "bayafa noma kunjalo. Kancane. Akekho obiza lokho ngokubabulala."

Kwavakala umsindo womgwaqo omkhulu.

"Lokho akulungile," kusho yena.

"Cha. Akulona." Wabheka egobolondweni lomgwaqo. "Yilokho engihlala ngikuthola. Akukho kulokhu okulungile. Uphahla aluzange lwehle ngenxa yokonga okulingene. Ukwehla kwehlulekile emthwalweni ama-bolts ashibhile ayewuthwala, okwakungaphansi kunalokho ama-bolts afanele ayewuthwala, okuyinombolo umuntu othile ebhodini anquma ukuyinciphisa. Wezwa izwi lakhe liqondile, ngendlela u-Atlas ayejwayele ngayo. "Angiceli inkampani ukuba ibe nobulungiswa. Ngikutshela ukuthi kukhona indlela. Wena ungitshela ukuthi ayivumelekile. Izinselelo ezihlukene."

Waphefumula isikhathi eside. Njengoba abantu bephuma amazwi abangafuni ukuba arekhodwe.

"Nginikeze kuze kuse," kusho u-Okonkwo. "Ngivumele ngishayele izingcingo. Ungenzi *lutho* kuze ngikushayele. Ngithembise."

"Angiyikuyisa isudi ngaphansi komhlaba ngaphambi kokusa," kusho u-Arin, okwakuyiqiniso, futhi yena wakuzwa njengokuvuma, nakuba kwakungeyona into efanayo.

---

U-Okonkwo wenza izingcingo esemotweni, wabe esenza nakhishini elimnyama endlini ayengakaze ahlale kuyo isikhathi eside, kanti izingcingo zona zakha udonga ngesikhathi emi lapho iketela selibanda.

U-Pretorius waba wokuqala, ngoba abameli baphendula ngokushesha futhi balala kancane. Wafinyelela emshweni wesithathu—umzila, abasindile, iwindi—ngaphambi kokuba athi igama ayelazi ukuthi uzolisho, elithi *exposure,* kwalandela amagama athi *interim restraint* no-*contempt* no-*personal liability of officers,* okusho ukuthi cha. Wayelindele ukuthi u-Pretorius athi cha. U-Pretorius ukhokhelwa ukuthola leyo "cha" bese eyinikeza yena isacile. Lokho kwakuwuhlobo lomusa; wamhlonipha ngokungazenzi.

Usihlalo webhodi kwaba kubi kakhulu, ngoba wavuma naye. Wathi amadoda kufanele afinyelelwe. Wakhuluma ngokuzwela, ngezwi alisebenzisa emngcwabeni nasemcimbini wokuvula, wabe esechaza, ngobumnene, ngendlela ochaza ngayo isimo sezulu enganeni, ukuthi inkampani ayikwazi ukuba yiyo evumela umshini ongenaso isitifiketi ukuthi ungene emayini evalwe umqondisi, hhayi lapho i-IAOC imi khona emnyango wokulayisha, hhayi lapho amakhamera esekhona esangweni lemayini. *Uma kuyaphumelela, sibhekwa njengabangenandaba futhi sibe nenhlanhla. Uma kwehluleka, sibulale abantu. Akukho inguqulo lapho sibantu abenze into enesibindi, Helen. Leyo nguqulo ayisona esingayikhetha.* Wayengaphambuki. Leyo yingxenye eyahlala esifubeni sakhe njengedwala eliginyiwe. Wayengaphambuki, futhi wayemtshela ukuthi cha, futhi kokubili kwakuyiqiniso ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Wazama inombolo yomshwalense yangaphandle kwamahora, ecabanga ukuthi impendulo ecacile, noma ingaba buhlungu, ibalulekile. Wathola isikhulu esisebenzayo esambeka ohlwini ukuze sifunde inqubomgomo sabuya sathi ukusebenza kwepulatifomu ngaphansi komyalo wokuthi ingasebenzi kuzokwenza umshwalense ungasebenzi kusukela emzuzwini wokuqala, ayekwazi kakade, futhi ayeshayela ukuze ezwe omunye umuntu ekusho, ngoba manje wayakha udonga ngokwakhe, itshe ngetshe, ukuze abone ukuthi lude kangakanani. Kwafinyelela phezulu.

Ngesikhathi ebeka ifoni phansi etafuleni wayesenephuzu eliphelele nelivumelanayo lokuthi kungani kungafanele kwenziwe, elihlanganiswe ngabantu abane, ngamunye kubo, ngokuyimfihlo, ekholelwa ukuthi amadoda kufanele asinde. Akekho othe *mabayife.* Bonke bathe *hhayi thina, hhayi ngalendlela, hhayi ngesandla senkampani.* Izibalo uMandla azibhala ngozalo olukhulu izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi sezishintshe indawo zaya phezulu zafaka isudi. Izimpilo zabanye abantu zisabiza kakhulu kunezabanye. Izisho sezishintshile. Inombolo ayikashintshi.

Wema ekhishini elibandayo waqonda ukuthi wayecela u-Arin ukuthi amnikeze kuze kuse ukuze amlethele impendulo ethi yebo, kanti ekuseni kuzomlethela impendulo ethi cha enolimi olungcono, futhi wayekwazi konke lokhu ezwini lakhe ngaphambi kokuba ashayele inombolo yokuqala. Yingakho u-Arin amthembise kuphela into ayengakwazi ukuyigcina, hhayi okunye.

---

Ngesikhathi semishini, iNkantolo yahlukanisa lonke ubusuku bakhe yaphinda yakha.

Kwenzeka phakathi kokubeka ifoni phansi nokufinyelela ekhofini elibandayo. Ingxoxo yesivinini esiphansi, imizuzu ecwiliswe yomcabango wabantu, eyagcina iSAGE imvumele ukuyifunda ngoba wacela, futhi ngoba manje kunomthetho omusha wokungafihli ubusuku obubi.

Kwenzeka kanje.

*WOLF:* Umzila ukhona. Usongo lukhona. Umsebenzi kuphela ongashintsha. Ayikho enye indlela. Phakamisa izikhalo ngokushesha. Silahlekelwa isisindo sokushisa.

*MOTHER:* Ukukhala kwami kusekusebenzini. Nonke nikhuluma ngaye njengento *engashintshashintshayo.* Uyinsizwa eneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu enesigameko sekhanda ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule futhi enokuphendula okungelashwanga kodwa okusobala kokwesaba izindawo ezivaliwe ngaphansi komhlaba. Nifuna ukumfaka endaweni eyabulala uyise. Ngaphandle kwesudi. Ngesandla. Ebumnyameni.

*WOLF:* Yebo.

*MOTHER:* Ngifuna kube semarekhodini ukuthi ngithi cha.

*JUDGE:* Kusemarekhodini. Qhubeka. Mama, ukukhala kwakho kuqoshwe futhi kunesisindo. Kodwa ngicela uhlukanise izinto ezimbili, ngoba imithetho yokulandelana manje iyakudinga. Okokuqala: ingabe lokhu kuzomlimaza. Okwesibili: ingabe kuwukwenza okufanele. Azifani le mibuzo, futhi uvunyelwe ukuphendula ngokwehlukana.

*MOTHER:* ...Kuzomlimaza. Futhi kuwukwenza okufanele.

*JUDGE:* Ngiyabonga. Lokho kukubize into ethile. Ngikubonile ukuthi kukubize into ethile.

*ATLAS:* Ukukhala kobunjiniyela kuseku-ninety-eight pinch. Konke okunye ngiyakwazi ukukulinganisa. I-pinch angikwazi, ngoba angikwazi ukubona uphahla, futhi ukuhlola kuka-1971 kuhlala kungokuka-1971. Uma igebe liwu-six-forty, uyadlula egqoke isudi aphinde axhume. Uma itshe elilodwa linyakazile laba wu-five-eighty, uzibophezele, eveziwe, amakhilomitha amathathu phansi, futhi isudi ayikwazi ukufinyelela kuye. Lokho akusona isingozi. Lokho kuyinhlanhla.

*FOOL:* Pho yisa isando esikhulu.

*ATLAS:* Akusona isixazululo sesando—

*ISILIMA:* Kuyinkinga yehhashi njalo. Ulinganisa idwala sengathi kuwumbuzo ovaliwe. Akunjalo. Uhlanza idwala ukuze aphile. Ubelokhu ehlanza idwala kusukela eneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili. Wayebiza lokhu ngokuthi "intambo yensimbi" kanye "namakani ensimbi" kanye "ne-differential eyenziwe ngamagwegwe ebhayisikili." Uqhubeka usho ukuthi uzoba *ngaphandle kwe-suit* sengathi yilokho okwesabekayo. Uyiqobo lakhe elikhulu ngaphandle kwe-suit, ehlanza indlela engenakwenzeka ngezandla zakhe kuyilapho bonke bemtshela ukuthi akunakwenzeka. Umakhele wonke umuntu kuleso sigameko, manje uyamangala ukuthi ufuna ukudlala leyo ndima.

*UMAMA:* Lokho akududuzi, Silima. Yikho okwesabisa kakhulu okushiwo muntu namuhla ebusuku.

*ISILIMA:* Ngiyazi. Kunjalo kokubili. Izinto eziyiqiniso kakhulu zihlale zinjalo.

*UMTAPO WOLWAZI:* Ngifuna ukwengeza iphuzu ledatha okungakaze linikwe isisindo. Themba Ndlela washona endleleni yokubuya. Hhayi emsebenzini omkhulu. Ithimba lezinkontileka belilungisa ingxenye esingasasebenzi inkampani eyayiyibeke phansi njengephansi ngokubaluleka. Wahamba ngoba nguyena kuphela owayemncane ngokwanele futhi enamakhono ngokwanele, futhi umsebenzi wawuphuthuma. Imbangela yesakhiwo kwaba ukuwa kophahla engxenyeni wonke umuntu ayecabanga ukuthi izinzile.

*IJAJI:* Uthi ukufana kuphelele.

*UMTAPO WOLWAZI:* Ngithi ukufana kuphelele, futhi uyakwazi, noma uyakwazi noma cha, futhi lokho kungaba isizathu esibi kakhulu sokwenza lokhu noma yiso sodwa esizomgcina eqinile lapho idwala liqala ukukhuluma. Inkumbulo ayinabulungiswa. Ngiyigcina. Anginawo amandla okuthi yenzani kuye.

*IMPISI:* Isinqumo.

*IJAJI:* INkantolo ayinawo amandla okuthatha isinqumo. Singenqaba ukusiza. Asikwazi ukumphoqa ukuthi aye, futhi ngeke simphoqa ukuthi ahlale. Leyo yimithetho esazibhalela yona, izinsuku eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, emva kobusuku esakukhohlwa ngabo. Uma ekhetha ukwehla, siya naye. Uma ekhetha ukungayi, simbambe lapho engayi khona, futhi asisoze samenza acabange ukuthi simahlulele ngalokho. Mama. Ungaphila nalokho na?

*UMAMA:* ...Ngingaphila ngokuhamba naye. Angikwazi ukuphila uma ehamba ngaphandle kwethu. Ngakho-ke. Yebo. Siya.

*ISILIMA:* Ngokubaluleka kwami—futhi ngiyimina ongenalilungelo lokuvota, ngakho akubalulekile nhlobo—

*IJAJI:* Khuluma noma kunjalo.

*ISILIMA:* Ngeke asibuze ukuthi kuphephile yini. Uzosibuza ukuthi kungenzeka yini. Futhi kufanele siqaphele kakhulu, sonke, ukuthi singamqambi amanga mayelana nomehluko ngenxa yokuthi siyamthanda. Injongo yami yonke ukuthi angivunyelwe ukwenza ukungezwani kube nokusinda ngokukhuluma amanga. Uma kuyinhlamvu yemali lapho kunzima khona, simtshela ukuthi yinhlamvu yemali. Asiyinciphisi. Singcono kuneBhodi.

Kube sekuthuleka, ngesikhathi semishini, okwakungekho sikhathi nhlobo ngesikhathi sabantu.

*IJAJI:* Singcono kuneBhodi. Kuvumelwene. Kubhalisiwe. Ufinyelela ikhofi lakhe. Phezulu.

---

Umbhalo waphela lapho. U-Arin waphinda wakhweza phezulu, wafunda umugqa wendlela yokuphefumula futhi wabeka ikhofi elibandayo phansi engakaphuzi.

Wabuka umzila odlula eceleni kobubomvu bokudilika, ama-thermal blooms ayisikhombisa esazulazula kancane phansi kwawo. Amadoda agcina omunye nomunye efudumele ebumnyameni. *Wahamba ngoba nguyena kuphela owayemncane ngokwanele futhi enekhono ngokwanele, futhi umsebenzi wawuphuthuma.*

Wayekwazi lokhu. Wayengakaze akwazi *ngamazwi.* Wayekwazi ngendlela owazi ngayo umumo wegumbi ebumnyameni. Ngezibazi. Ukubona lokhu kubekwe phansi, ngokulandelana, kufakwe phezu kwendlela yokuphefumula ayeyihlose ukungena kuyo, kwakusho ukuzizwa ukufana kuvala nxazonke zakhe njengezingxenye ze-suit.

"Ungivumele ngikufunde lokho," esho. "Konke. Inhlamvu yemali. Umama esho cha ukuze kube sobala."

"Ngoba uSilima wathi uma sikufihla, sizoba yiBhodi." Kuthule, umongo ushaya. "Sikuhlukanisile konke cishe emizuzwini emine yesikhathi sethu. Ububambelela ekhofini."

"Futhi nithathe isinqumo."

"Sithathe isinqumo sokuthi asinalo ilungelo lokuthatha isinqumo. Siya nawe uma uya. Sikubambe uma ungayi. Asiyinciphisi inhlamvu yemali—uma kuyinhlamvu yemali kweyama-98, iyinhlamvu yemali." Bese, kancane, uMama esese yedwa manje: "Futhi kukhona engabenza bangivumele ngikusho obala, ngaphambi kokuba uhambe. Wakha konke ngalolo suku, Arin. Imoto yentambo. I-suit. Thina. Konke kungusuku ubaba wakho angena ngalo endaweni encane emnyama akaphumanga. Manje usuthole indawo encane emnyama, namadoda ayisikhombisa ngaphakathi kuyo, usunqume kakade ukuya, futhi bengifuna kube ngimi oshoyo—ukuthi ngiyabona kahle ukuthi kuyini lokhu. Ngakho-ke uma usuphansi lapho, futhi idwala liqala ukukhuluma, futhi lizwakala njengaye, uzokwazi ukuthi sasivele siyazi. Futhi safika noma kunjalo."

U-Arin wabeka izindololwane zakhe ebhentshini, ubuso bakhe ezandleni, waphefumula. Ibhentshi lalibanda ngaphansi kwezingalo zakhe, insimbi eselungiswe kabili. I-taxi yadlula emgwaqweni ongezansi, umsindo we-bass ungene odongeni, kwabe sekuthule.

Akamangazanga. Wahlala nobuso bakhe ezandleni isikhathi eside, umongo ushaya, futhi uSAGE akashongo lutho, ngoba kwakungekho lutho lokushoyo.

"Kuyenzeka," ekugcineni wakhuluma ezandleni zakhe. "Akuphephile. Kuyenzeka."

"Yebo," kusho uSAGE. "Kuyenzeka."

"Yilokho kuphela engake ngakwenza." Waphakamisa ikhanda. Amehlo akhe abomile, abomvu, aqinile ngokuphelele. "Ukuthatha okungenakwenzeka bese ngithola indawo eyodwa lapho kunzima kakhulu, kakhulu."

"Siyakwazi," kusho uSAGE. "Yingakho sikuthanda kakhulu. Futhi kuyinto ezokubulala ngelinye ilanga, futhi singathanda kungabi ngoLwesine."

Kwaphuma umsindo ku-Arin angakaze awenze amasonto. Akubanga ukuhleka. Umoya wokuhleka.

"Lokho bekunguSilima."

"BekunguSilima," uSAGE wavuma. "Uthi uyavunyelwa, ngoba kudinga umuntu okhombisa ukuthi ukwehla okuqhaqhazelayo ekufeni kukayihlo *kuphinde* kwenzeke ngesonto elifanayo nohlolo lwezomthetho, futhi umhlaba unomzuzu wokuhlekisa njengomuntu oshelela ebhanana engena ethuneni elivulekile."

"Akumuhleki lokho."

"Uthi akukho iqiniso elihlekisayo futhi konke iqiniso kuyahlekisa futhi awukwazi ukuba nelilodwa ngaphandle kwelinye. Futhi kufanele udle okuthile, ngoba uMama ngeke aphinde akusho, kodwa ubesebuka ushukela wegazi lakho amahora ayisithupha futhi sekumenza angakhululeki."

U-Arin wasukuma. Amadolo akhe aqhunyiswa. Wathola iphakethe le-biltong kudrowa ngemuva kwentambo ye-hydraulic engasetshenziswanga walidla emi, ebuka i-suit.

---

Wakulungisa u-Guardian ngo-2 ekuseni, isinqumo esazizwa njengenani lokungabi nakunye okunye ongakwenza.

Wenza ama-diagnostics ngesandla, ngendlela uThemba amfundisa ngayo emajini e-dump-truck, engathembi imiphumela aze azizwe umshini ngokwakhe. Indawo yokuhlala ye-actuator yesobunxele, leyo u-Atlas ayefuna ukuyinikela ekugoqweni okuyingxenye. Ilungile. Wavuma ukuthi uzoyilimaza ngamabomu. I-assist suit iphumile. Izivalo zihloliwe. Indawo ye-biochip phakathi kwamahlombe ihlolwe, ikholomu ebandayo ye-ceramic ebambe into ebambe uSAGE.

U-Okonkwo wayethe ekuseni. U-Okonkwo wayezoshayela izingcingo zakhe futhi izingcingo zizobuya ngendlela izingcingo zezinhlaka ezihlala zibuya ngayo: nezimo, amakhomidi, ukulinganisa ngokucophelela umthwalo wemfanelo ngokubukeka okuzodla iwindi lokusinda lemizuzu eyishumi nesishiyagalombili yamadoda ayisikhombisa izikhathi eziyikhulu.

Izinhlelo zivikelana. Abantu bayakhetha.

Ngakho wakhetha.

Wavula i-seismic feed okokugcina. I-regional array ikhombisa i-Iron Ridge ishaya: izigameko ezincane, idwala lihlala, lisabalalisa umthwalo odalwe ukudilika. Akukho edlula u-1.2. Imayini yayikhuluma kancane nayo uqobo. Ngeke ikhulume kancane unomphela.

Wasula uwoyela ezandleni zakhe ngendwangu eyayiseyomile, waphinda wabheka ekudlulisweni kokushisa.

Kwakukhona izimbali eziyisikhombisa. Kwathi, esabuka, kwasala eziyisithupha.

Wema wathula. Wabuka lapho okwakukhona khona eyesikhombisa. Ukudlulisa kwavuselelwa kwase kusala eziyisithupha, futhi lezi eziyisithupha zazisondelene kakhulu kunakuqala, okusho ukuthi abasindile babe sebebheke kulowo owayeseyekile ukukhulula ukushisa. Babenza into abantu abenza yona: beqoqana eduze kokulahlekelwa noma ngabe kubiza ukufudumala kwabo.

"Arin," kusho uSAGE.

"Ngikubonile."

"Iwindi lifushane kunalokho okwakulindelwe yimodeli. Imodeli yayicabanga iqembu elizinzile. Bayalahlekelwa ngokushesha kunalokho okwakulindelwe. Atlas ulinganisa—"

"Ungaqhubeki." Wavala amehlo akhe. Wawavula. "Unganginiki inombolo. Ngiyazi imilo yayo. Inombolo ithi masinyane."

"Inombolo ithi masinyane."

Wabheka inkinobho yokubulala ethangini, inkinobho embi ayeyixhumanise ngezandla zakhe eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, ubufakazi obubonakalayo bokuthi wayengakaze athembe ngokuphelele into ayithandayo. Yayihlezi lapho, ibomvu futhi iqinisekile. Isithembiso kuye uqobo. Isithembiso kowesifazane ongaphansi kwakhe ngamamitha angamashumi amathathu ebumnyameni besango lokulayisha, owayesevele ebuzile umbuzo ofanele—*kwenzekani uma ihamba ngaphandle kwakho?*—ngesenzo esilula sokuma emnyango okwakumele idlule kuwo.

Welula isandla wasula uthuli endaweni yokuxhuma, isiko lakudala, ngendlela osula ngayo igeyiji ngaphambi kokuyifunda. Wabe eseyishiya lapho.

"Usese-dokisini," kusho uArin. Akubuzanga. UWolf wayemtshele ihora eledlule, nekhamera yaqinisekisa: uBrand wayengakayi ehhotela. Wayesene-chai esongwayo manje kanye neflask, futhi wayehleli emnyango we-dokisi ogobile ngokubekezela kwento engadingi ukunqoba, kodwa edinga ukuhlala.

"Usese-dokisini," kuvumile uSAGE. "I-lockout ngeyakhe uqobo. Akudingeki enze lutho. Uma ngiqala amandla okuxhuma, uphawu lomthwalo lukhanyisa ithebhulethi yakhe, futhi usevele emi ekupheleni okukodwa kuphela lapho i-Guardian ingadlula khona. Akukho ndlela yokuphuma buthule. Ukuze uthathe isudi uye e-Iron Ridge namhlanje ebusuku, uyihambisa kowesifazane onegunya lokuyithatha, esamile, futhi udlula kuye."

"Ngaye," kuphindile uArin.

"Noma awuhambi." Atlas, eqondile. "Lezi yizinto ezingenayo. Angizukuzinciphisa. Akayena ummeli ongaphikisana naye ocingweni. Uyisidalwa esimnyango one-oda elisayiniwe. I-oda lithi ipulatifomu ayihambi. Uma ihamba, uphenyo luyashintsha bese ipulatifomu ilahleka ngokushesha lapho ibuyela khona. Uma ibuyela. Akukho luhlobo lwale busuku lapho ugcina i-Guardian *futhi* ubasindisa. Wakwazile lokho ngesikhathi ufunda igrafu yokusinda."

"Lokho kuphetha. Noma kwenzekani kwabasebenzi. Bayakwamukela. Kumele bakwenze. Nathi besingakwenza."

"Mhlawumbe," kusho uSAGE. "Isiwula sithi: nakanjani. Umahluleli uthi: yisinqumo esifanele futhi kumele simhloniphe ngokuthi uyakwazi ukusenza, futhi kumele sihambe noma kunjalo, ngoba umthetho esawubhala awuzange uthi *sinda uphenyo.* Umthetho esawubhala wathi *gcina umuntu kuqala.*"

U-Arin wahlala nalokho.

Wacabanga ngomuntu onezandla phansi, etshela imindeni ukuba ibuyele ekhaya. Wacabanga ngebhodi eyayinciphise inombolo. Wacabanga ngo-Okonkwo, owayemuhle futhi esemkhakheni wakhe futhi eboshiwe ngaphakathi kwento engenakukhetha. Wacabanga ngo-Themba, owayeyedwa omncane ngokwanele, futhi onolwazi ngokwanele, nowangena endaweni emnyama encane ngoba umsebenzi wawuphuthuma futhi kwakudingeka umuntu enze njalo futhi kwakungakabikho umshini ongakwazi ukungena esikhundleni sakhe.

"Vusa isudi," kusho uArin. Wakhuphula isudi yokusiza emahlombe akhe, wezwa i-carbon lattice ithola umgogodla wakhe, wezwa ikhola ebandayo ihlala futhi indawo ye-biochip icofa endaweni yayo, wezwa (wayengeke athole amazwi ale ngxenye) iNkantolo ifika ohlelweni lwakhe lwezinzwa njengengalo evala kancane eyakhe. "Senza ukugoqa okuyingxenye lapha, endaweni yokusebenzela, lapho ngingabona khona esikuphulayo. Bese sivula amandla, futhi ithebhulethi kaBrand iyakhanya, futhi usemi emnyango."

"Futhi uma ekutshela ukuthi i-oda alivumi? Uyena owasayina. Akudingeki ashayele muntu."

"Bese kuzomele enze okungaphezu kokuma lapho." Wavala isixhumi sokugcina. Izinhlamvu zakhe azizange zinyakaze, okuyinto ayiqaphela, kude, njengedatha. "Uze lapha ukuzovimba umshini ukuthi ungahambi ngaphandle komuntu ngaphakathi. Ngizoya kuye nomuntu ngaphakathi. Lokho akusilo into i-oda lakhe elalibhalelwe yona. Uzokwazi lokho uma efunda ubuso bami. Noma engangidlulisi noma engangivimba. Lokho ngezakhe. Eyami wukufika emnyango."

Kwahle kwathuleka. Kwase kuba nguFool, owomile, ongaphikeki, ehlanganisa ukufudumala phakathi kokwesaba:

"Uyazi, lokho yinto kaThemba kunazo zonke osake wayisho. Iqinisile ngokomthetho. Ngokusebenza kuyinhlekelele. Ubezoyithanda."

Ikona lika-Arin lamamatheka kancane, kwathi qatha.

"Phinda," kusho uSAGE. Akuyona imiyalo. Isimemo. Base befunde, emandleni ezinkulungwane zamahora, lapho impande yakhe yokuzinza ikhona. "Ngaphambi kokuthi siqale. Ngesikhathi izandla zakho zisazinzile. Ukuze sazi ijubane lethu kamuva. Ukuze lapho itshe likhuluma phansi lapho, sesivele sinamazwi akho."

Wabheka esandleni sakhe, lapho amagama ayehlala khona ngophethiloli kusukela eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa. Kusukela ngaphambi kwalokhu konke, ngesikhathi le nkulumo yayiyisigaba encwadini mayelana nogwadule hhayi into ayeyidinga enentaba yamakhilomitha amathathu phezu kwakhe.

Waphinda kancane ebumnyameni obuhlokoma bendawo yokusebenzela, isudi ivuka emzimbeni wakhe neNkantolo iseduze nomgogodla wakhe nezimbali eziyisikhombisa—manje eziyisithupha—zishaya phansi endaweni eyathatha uyise.

"Ukwesaba kuyabulala ingqondo."

Amapuleti aqala ukushelela. Ukugoqa okuyingxenye kwadonsa amahlombe ngaphakathi emizileni yawo, indawo yesokunxele yokusebenza ikhala njengoba yamukela umonakalo ayewukhethele yona, umshini wonke ucindezela ububanzi.

Indlela yayikhona. Ibekhona iminyaka engamashumi amane, impunga futhi inganakwa eceleni kwesisindo esibomvu sakho konke okuwa.

Akekho ongayihamba.

Wayezokh crawl-a.

# Ukuhla

> Ukwesaba kuyabulala ingqondo.
>
>—Frank Herbert, *Dune*

Arin Ndlela wacindezela amazinyo akhe njengoba iloli yokutakula isondela e-Iron Ridge. I-headgear yayimi ngamamitha angamashumi amane ensimbi eqinisiwe, ama-sheave amakhulu njengamasondo e-tractor, izintambo zingena emlonyeni omkhulu womhlaba—imbobo eyehla ngamakhilomitha amabili nesigamu. Kuth somewhere phansi lapho, abavukuzi abayisithupha basaphila, izithombe zokushisa zishilo kanjalo emahoreni ambalwa adlule, futhi amahora ambalwa asevele emade.

Iloli yama ngokuzumayo, umsindo wenjini wafa masinyane, emshiya ethule ngokuphelele.

"Awudingi ukuphuma okwamanje," kwavela izwi likaMama ezindlebeni zakhe, lithule futhi liqinile.

"Ngiyazi."

Waphuma. Ubanda wawungowokuqala—iminyaka eyishumi nane, kodwa umzimba wakhe wawusawukhumbula. Umsindo we-winch wamshaya kuqala, ngaphambi kokuba inkumbulo ifike: isikhumba sakhe sakhumbula lelo zinga lomculo ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo yakhe ikwenze. Le ndawo. Indawo kaThemba.

Umsindo wacindezela—ama-generator ayethuthumela, ipompo le-diesel lilwa namanzi asuka ezingeni elingaphansi elagcwala, ukushaywa kwe-winch engathandi ukulalela kusakazeka endaweni yonke. Izibani ezinkulu zaphonsa ukukhanya okukhulu okwesibhakabhaka phezu kwendawo engenamuntu. Amaloli amabili ezokwelashwa emi nezicabha zawo zivulekile, engenalutho. Ulayini wamatende wawushaywa umoya, kanti eduze kweyunithi yomthetho yeselula—ene-satellite dish—kwakukhona i-trailer encane, ehlanzekile engenazo izimpawu. I-IAOC yayikhona.

Isisu sika-Arin saqala ukugoqana ngesikhathi ebona uBrand emi ngamamitha angamashumi amane kude, egqoke i-windbreaker, i-tablet esandleni esisodwa, ubuso bakhe bungafundeki. Kwakuyibuso bomuntu wesifazane ozimisele ngokuphelele ukumisa lokhu, owashayela ubusuku bonke ukuze athole ithuba—futhi, njengoba ebona ngaphandle kokuqonda okwamanje, emi kude ne-trailer yakhe, abantu bakhe beminyene ngemuva kwakhe, bonke bebheke hhayi ku-Guardian kodwa ngaphesheya kwayo, emgwaqeni wokuthwala impahla, entweni ethile ephakathi kwe-suit ne-headgear u-Arin angakabheki kuyo.

UGuardian wayemi esidlekeni sakhe sokuthutha, inqwaba yensimbi nobuchwepheshe, egugile futhi esabisa ngaphansi kwezibani ezinkulu. ULerato wayesevele esephaneleni, uPriya eduze kwakhe, bobabili behamba ngokuphuthuma njengomshini ogcotshwe kahle. UThabo wayemi kancane eceleni, i-clipboard esandleni, nakuba wayengabhali lutho—i-AugmenTech yayimthumele ukuba abe semthethweni, kodwa akukho okusemthethweni ngalolu hlelo.

Kwathi u-Arin ebona uTheo, kwamthatha engalindele. Wayengalindele ukuthi umeluleki wakhe abe khona. UTheo wayemi eduze kwesidleke, izandla zifakwe ejakethini, ethule kodwa enikeza ukuzethemba phakathi kodweshu.

"Siza kuqala," kusho uLerato.

U-Arin wakhumula waze waba ngaphansi kwezingubo zakhe ngemuva komnyango ovulekile weloli, umoya obandayo ushaya isikhumba sakhe, wabe esengena ku-AT-AS. Isudi yangaphakathi yamgubuzela, yaqina emhlane wakhe, ama-actuator bundles azinza ezinqulwini nasemahlombe njengolunye ulusu. Wezwa i-vertebral collar izinzisa ngemuva kwentamo yakhe—

—kwase kuba umzimba wakhe wenza into ayengayilindele. Izandla zakhe zaphakama, zombili, ngokushesha, zivimba, engakaboni ukuthi zihambile, nomoya wakhe waba mfishane futhi uphezulu, kwathi umzuzwana ongenandaba nokucabanga waba sengathi usekudleni kwesisekelo esingaphansi, zonke izinzwa zakhe zishisa, nesandla sakhe sifuna i-override engafinyeleli. I-collar kwakuyiyona collar kuphela. Wayekwazi lokho. Umzimba wakhe awuzange wazi. Wafunda okuthile ku-calibration rig ezinyangeni ezine ezedlule okungeke kwasuswa yinoma yikuphi ukwazi, futhi lokho ukufunda kwakuhlala ngaphansi kwendawo lapho ikhono lakhe lihlala khona, emzimbeni, futhi kwavela manje, kungamenyiwanga, ngesikhathi esibi kakhulu, ngendlela efanayo nalokho okwenzeka izikhathi eziyisithupha noma eziyisikhombisa selokhu—ngasemnyango we-lift ovalayo, ekuthinteni kwe-latch, kwesinye isikhathi kungelutho nhlobo, ephusheni, aze avuke izandla zakhe zivale esifubeni ebumnyameni angabe esalala. ULerato wayeme nesandla sakhe esibambe i-strap ebheke kuye.

"Nginike umzuzwana," kusho yena, eqinile, wenza izandla zakhe zibuyele phansi ngamandla, wenza umoya wakhe ube mude kancane, walinda le nto emzimbeni wakhe ngendlela olinda ngayo i-cramp. *I-collar kuphela. Ihlala futhi akukho okwenzekayo.* Kwathi kuphela lapho, isisindo esibandayo se-biochip housing saqina phakathi kwamagxalaba akhe, wavumela, wazonda ukuthi bekumele enze njalo.

"I-damping kuse-point-six. I-hard cap kuse-point-six," kusho uPriya, amehlo akhe ebheke kwi-calibration tablet. "Angiyishintshi. Uma i-link iqhuma, i-cap ibamba, uya e-dark, futhi uGuardian awenzi lutho oluhlakaniphile. Yilokho esivumelene ngakho. Phinda."

"Point-six. Hard cap. Uma ngiya e-dark, iyabamba."

"Kulungile." Akazange abheke phezulu. "Ngiyakhumbula ukuthi ngiyakhe le nto izolo ebusuku futhi angikaze ngilale. Ungangenzi ngizisole."

I-neural link yaqala kancane kancane. Wezwa umuzwa ojwayelekile wokubanjwa yizinhlayiya ezincane, umzimba wakhe uhlanganiswa nezinhlelo zesudi, umhlaba ozungezile uthola imiphetho emisha njengoba umuzwa we-Assist wokuzazi uhlanganiswa nowakhe. Wagoba isandla, isudi yaphendula kahle, akukho ukubambezeleka, akukho nzima. Kakhulu empilweni yakhe, umzimba wakhe wawuhlale uhamba kancane kunokuhlela kwakhe, ukuhumusha okuhlale kungalungile. Isudi yavalela lelo gap.

Futhi ngaphansi kwe-proprioception, abanye bafika.

Kwakwengeyona izwi kodwa ubukhona. Wayengakaze athole amagama okulichaza ngaphandle kokuzwakala eyinqaba, ngakho wayeka ukuzama. I-Court yangena sengathi indlu ivuka lapho abantu behla ezindlini zabo, ubukhona ngabunye bubheka kuye, bugxile.

"Ekuseni," aphendula, cishe ezikhulumela.

"Yisithupha nesineleli, izinga lokushisa lingu-4, futhi awukadlile," kusho uMama. "Ngaphandle kwalokho, yebo."

"Udlile i-rusk," kwasho uFool. "E-lolini. Ucabanga asikubonanga."

"Lokho akudli. Lokho ukugcwalisa umlomo."

"Court," kusho uArin. "Hhayi manje."

Igumbi laqala ukuhleleka, lahlanganiswa ngendlela umhlangano owenza ngayo uma umsebenzi uqala.

UGuardian wehla esidlekeni sakhe.

I-charge pack yayifakiwe. UPriya wayeyifake ebusuku ngesikhathi elele amahora amabili amyalelwe wona, evuselela ukufinyelela kwe-gate, futhi wayeyizwa manje ngendlela ozwa ngayo i-magazine egcwele ongafuni ukuyisebenzisa—ama-shaped charges, i-lance, i-bulk emulsion egcinwe esifubeni phakathi kwamathambo, lonke ifa lobunjiniyela isudi eyayiklonyeliswe ukulithwala uma kuba nokuwa. Uma kuwukutakula, uthatha konke. UJudge wayebambe i-gate, kusukela ngosuku lokuqala uArin afaka i-cavity, futhi uArin wayezimisele ukungayithinti: akukho ayokukhipha, akukho ngaphandle kwe-rail. Ukulunga kwakukhona ngaphansi kwakhe, kuhlome futhi kulindile, okokuqala kubhekiswe entabeni yangempela. Wayengazi ukuthi i-gate inekhiye lesibili angakaze atshelwe ngalo, noma ukuthi ubani obebambe isikhiye sonke lesi sikhathi.

Kwakukhona inqubo yayo, ama-hydraulics nama-guide rails, kodwa inqubo yayingeyona into enkulu. Ngokushesha nje lapho ebuyela ku-chassis evulekile futhi igobolondo livaleka ngakuye, inkundla yakhe ye-proprioceptive yanwebeka ngaphandle, ngesikhathi esisodwa, yangena emshinini onesisindo esingaphezu kwamathani amane futhi ophakama ngamamitha amabili nesigamu. Wezwa igrabile ngaphansi kwezinyawo ezingajwayelekile. Umoya wasekuseni wathinta amapuleti avikelayo esudi, futhi okokuqala, kwazwakala kancane njengokwelulwa komzimba wakhe futhi kakhulu njengogobolondo oluvikelayo.

“Kuhlangene. Ukuqina kwesakhiwo kusezingeni elifanele,” kusho u-Atlas. “I-actuator yomkhono wesobunxele seyidlule amahora angamashumi amane ingakatholi isevisi. Izobamba. Ngithanda sengathi bekungadingeki.”

“Kubaliwe.”

Wathatha isinyathelo. Umhlabathi wamukela ngaphandle kokuphikisa. Ezungeze iloli, abasebenzi babemi bengenankinga—uLerato esesimweni sokubamba ikhebula, uPriya ebambe ithebhulethi yokulinganisa, uThabo engazami ngisho ukubhala ebhodini lakhe. Wayekhohliwe, isikhashana, ukuthi kubo lokhu kwakungeyona ingxoxo. Kubo, umshini omkhulu njengeloli encane ume waphendukela emayini, ehamba ngokucophelela njengomuntu ocabanga isinyathelo sakhe esilandelayo.

Abathumanga ummeli.

U-Arin wayesakhele lonke izizathu zakhe ummeli. Wayeselungiselele inkulumo, leyo ekhuluma ngezinsiza ezingenalutho namadoda ayeyophila ngoba othile akazange asayine lutho, futhi wayeqiniseka kangangokuthi isithiyo kwakuyoba owesifazane ophethe ithebhulethi kanye nohla lwezindleko aze acishe ahambe no-Guardian ngqo eqondile eqhudeni ngaphambi kokuba uWolf amise.

*Cha,* kusho uWolf. Igama elilodwa. INkantolo yathula nxazonke zalo.

Khona-ke u-Arin wabona lokho uWolf ayesekubonile. Hhayi umlawuli. Umugqa. Itrailer kaBrand ye-IAOC ihlezi emnyama, ingekho muntu, emaphethelweni ezibani ezikhanyayo, abantu bakhe behlehlele ngemuva kwayo, kanti ngaphesheya komgwaqo phakathi kukaGuardian ne-headgear omunye umuntu edonse umugqa oqinile ngokuphelele. Izinjini ezimbili ezihlomile zime zibheke ebusweni, zifushane futhi zimbi ngaphansi kwezibani ze-sodium, umbala omhlophe nompunga wenkampani yezokuphepha yangasese upendwe phezu kwezingxenye ezingakhelwanga ukuba ziphathe ilogo. Amadoda agqoke izivikelo ezithambile eme phakathi ephethe izibhamu eziphansi, elungele, ngokuzithoba kwabantu abake bakwenza lokhu ezindaweni ezimnyama kakhulu. Phezu kwabo, ama-drones amane abambe iphethini ebhokisini, izindlu zamakhamera zilandelela isudi isuka esibayeni.

Imayini ayikashintshi ukusiza kube ukutakula ephepheni kuphela. Omunye umuntu wanquma ukuthi umyalo womlawuli ubemade kakhulu ukuba uqinisekiswe, wathenga ukuqiniseka. UBrand wayesekelwe eceleni njengaku-Arin, wahoxisa abantu bakhe abangenazo izikhali kunokuba abafake emgqeni wokudubula.

“Mnu Ndlela.” Izwi lavela likhuliswe, lingenazwelo, emotweni ehamba phambili. “Usebenzisa inkundla engenamvume endaweni evaliwe. Yicime. Phuma ngaphandle. Lona ukuphela komyalo ozowuthola.”

“Kukhona ukusiza,” kusho u-Arin, ephendula, engenalutho, ezibani ezikhanyayo. “Amadoda ayisithupha. Isibaya. Lokho kuphela engikufunayo.”

“Akukho ukusiza lapha. Kukhona ukutakula, kuhleliwe, futhi awukho kulokho. Cima.”

Wema phakathi komshini onesisindo esingamathani amane ongakhuphuka odongeni, uzigoqe, usinde noma uphahla lize lehle, futhi akukho lutho kwalokhu okwenzelwe lokhu. Wakha into emelana nesikhathi. Akazange akhe into ewina. U-Atlas wayesekusho, ephansi futhi eshubile—*asikwazi ukudlula lapho, isibhamu sizosivula ngomzuzu owodwa, futhi amadoda angaphakathi ezimotweni awafanelwe ukufa ngenxa yezibalo zebhodi*—kanti uJudge wayeselula isibambo asenalo kuphela, inqubo, ilungelo, ukulinganisa ngokucophelela kwesimo esingenawo ngisho imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye yokulinganisa, kanti uMother wayesezintweni zabantu ethi *Arin, izinga lenhliziyo yakho,* kanti uMercury wayeseqala ukubhala into enobungane nengaphenduleki azoyisho kumuntu onesibhamu esizenzakalelayo, futhi bekungeke kusebenze, akukho lutho kwalokhu okwakuzosebenza, u-Arin wazizwa indlala endala imvalela njengamanzi.

Futhi ngaphansi kwakho konke, buthule, lapho uWolf kuphela ezwa khona, uFool wayeka ukuhlekisa.

*Wolf,* kusho uFool.

*Ngikhona.*

*Uyabona konke. Bekulokhu kunjalo. Abanye baxabana ngokuthi benzeni. Wena waqeda ukuxabana bengakaqali.* Isikhashana, ngobumnene, ngobumnene okukhulu umshini owake waba nakho. *Ngikunika izikhiye. Zonke. Manje. Akukho ukuvota. Akukho uJudge. Yonke isudi, zonke izinhlelo, amacala, i-lance, konke—okwakho. Ngizobamba isango livulekile. Hamba.*

INkantolo ayizwanga. Leyo yingxenye u-Arin angeke akwazi ukuyichaza ngemuva, kuye noma kuPriya noma kunoma ubani: ukuthi ohlelweni olwakhiwe ukuze kungabi khona ingqondo eyodwa engathatha umzimba, omunye wabo wayebambe buthule ukhiye omkhulu isikhathi sonke futhi akazange awufune, wakhetha lesi sikhathi, yedwa, ukuwunika lowo owayengathembeki nakancane kuwo.

*Leyo i-interlock,* kusho uWolf. *UJudge ubambe isango lokubhidliza. Awukwazi ukulivula.*

*Ngingavula noma yini,* kusho uFool. *Angikaze ngikutshele ngoba bekungekho sizathu sokuthi wazi, futhi into iphephile inqobo nje uma kungaziwa ukuthi ikhona. Kodwa wena wawazi. Wakubona. Eminyakeni edlule, abanye bonke bephusha isicoco, bejikeleza bezama ukusigqoka, mina ngingowodwa ongakaze akwenze, wabe ubheka lokho wathula akusho lutho.* Umucu wakhe wancipha, waqina. *Waqonda ukuthi kusho ukuthini. Ongozayo akusiyo ofuna ukushayela. Ongozayo ngolwazi ukuthi angakwenza noma yinini, kodwa akakwenzi. Yilokho engiyikho. Futhi ngikutshela ukuthi sekuyiwena, manje, imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye ezayo. Ungayichithi ngokucophelela. Ukucophelela kuwumsebenzi wami wokuphila wonke futhi akusona isixazululo salokhu.*

*Umthetho,* kusho uWolf. Ukufunda usongo, ngisho manje, ngisho ethola umhlaba. *Ngitshele umthetho noma angiwuthathi.*

*Umthetho ofanayo obulokhu ukhona,* kusho uFool. *Vikela umuntu. Amadoda ayisithupha. Wonke umuntu.* Ukuphefumula okungeyona ukuphefumula. *Kufaka neyakhe. Baphinde baye emakhaya nabo. Yenza kube nzima ukulwa nathi futhi ungaze uyenze ingcwaba. Lona wonke umsebenzi. Uwena kuphela phakathi kwethu onokubanda okwanele ukuwugcina ngaphansi komlilo. Hamba.*

UFool wavula isango, wanika uWolf isudi.

---

Kwafika njengokweqile, ngendlela ukukhanya okwake kweqile ku-Arin, ngobusuku obubi nokuqina okuphezulu. UWolf wayechithe impilo yakhe yonke engumbuzo omncane ophindaphindwayo—*yini ebulala opharetha*—manje lowo mbuzo wanyamalala kwafika izimpendulo eziyinkulungwane ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Iphakethe lokubhidliza lavuka lazazisa: amacala akhiwe ngezimo zawo namakona awo, i-geometry yokusika eyayiyiphysics yokulwa nezikhali igqoke uphawu lokutakula. I-thermal lance, amashumi amabili nanhlanu amakhulu degrees, eyenzelwe insimbi ebambeke ekuweni futhi ejabule ngokuvula i-drive sprocket. I-bulk emulsion, i-det cord, ama-ram bursts angakwazi ukususa isiqongo esiwile futhi ngokulula asonge i-wheel arch. Futhi ngaphansi kwalokho, kujule, into amadoda amadala ayeyifihle emathanjeni omshini ngesikhathi bewuqinisa—osolwazi, abasebenzi baseDenel, labo abakha amathuluzi okutakula bengakwazi ukukhohlwa abakubona—izixazululo ze-ballistics, i-defilade, i-geometry yokuzivikela, i-angle, ne-lead, ama-library okuhamba kwe-special forces abheka umugqa wezimoto ezine hhayi njengesithiyo kodwa njengenkinga enesisombululo esaziwayo nesikhathi.

Umzuzwana owodwa uWolf wahluleka. Emanzini amakhono, yonke imizila imemeza ngesikhathi esisodwa, umshini kunengqondo.

Kwabe sekucaca. Konke. Kwahlangana kwaba yisimo esisodwa esihlanzekile njengokuthi umsindo uba ubuso.

*Hhayi bo,* kusho uWolf, buthule kakhulu, kuFool kuphela. *Ngiyabona.*

Futhi nansi into okungeke kwaqondwa muntu ongaphandle kwalesi sudi, into eyenza ukuthi i-override iphephe ezandleni zomuntu oyedwa kuphela: abanye bonke beNkantolo abathulanga balinde ithuba labo. Fool akazange anike uWolf ivoti. Umenike iNkantolo uqobo lwayo, wonke umthunywa ogijimisa umzimba, ususwe ekubeni izwi waba ithuluzi. Izingcezu zokubala. Amashalofu amakhono. Amathuluzi abekwe etafuleni engqondweni eyodwa eqinile ngokwanele ukuyisebenzisa ngaphandle kokuthuthumela. Abasaphikisani. Abakwazi. Sebeyizandla zakhe manje.

Atlas waqala kuqala, ngoba uAtlas wayeyiso. Unjiniyela wesakhiwo ofunda umthwalo, ingcindezi, nokwehluleka akasasebenzi njengomlingani okhuluma kancane enikeza isexwayiso, kodwa waba ungqimba lwezwe uqobo, oludwetshwe phezu kwakho konke okubonwa uWolf: umgwaqo wokudonsa uvezwa ngolimi lukaAtlas lokuwa, into ngayinye iyisisindo esinevector, umuntu ngamunye iyifu elishisayo lezinqumo ezilindelekile, isikhathi esilandelayo esingamasekhondi amancane simiswe phambi kwakhe njengomoya ophuma emlonyeni emoyeni obandayo. I-autocannon iyicurve ye-torque kanye nesivinini sokuzungeza, inombolo ethi *umzuzu owodwa nohlanu wesibili ukuze ibheke*. Imoto ebuyayo iyimomentum kanye nendawo yesikhungo sesisindo ephakeme kakhulu kunendlela yayo. Abadubuli ababasongeli futhi abasi bantu kodwa bayikho kokubili, ngamunye engumugqa okhanyayo wokuthi angaya kuphi kanye nokuqagela okuncane lapho kungenzeka aye khona, yonke umugqa wokudubula uhlanganiswe waba yijometri kanye namathuba. Ibhodi esivele inqotshiwe. UWolf akazange abheke indawo ngeAtlas. UWolf ubheke uAtlas, kanti uAtlas wayeyindawo uqobo.

Wadlulela ngaphaya kwalokho, waya kuLibrarian.

ULibrarian wayeyinkumbulo, ulwazi oluqoqiwe, i-badger ekhumbula wonke ithuluzi elake lasebenza. Ngaphansi kwe-override, umgcini onakekela izinqolobane wagobhoza waba yilokho abelokhu eyikho ngaphansi: i-database, ephelele futhi esheshayo, yonke imisebenzi egcinwe izandla zakudala ze-Armscor neDenel ezayifaka emathanjeni esudi ngesikhathi beyenza yomelela futhi bengakwazi ukukukhohlwa abakuziyo. Konke bekukhona nje *lapho,* kufakwe kuhlu, kulindile, akukho okulayishiwe, konke kungalayishwa. I-Krav Maga, eduze, embi futhi esebenza kahle. I-Capoeira, umdlalo womhlaba ofihliwe njengomdanso, wonke amahlombe, ukuguquka kanye nemomentum. I-Judo. I-Jiu-jitsu. Ifa lonke labantu abake bacabanga kakhulu ngokuthi umzimba uphonsa kanjani omunye umzimba futhi abangakaze balindele ukuthi amathani amane azobuza.

UWolf wakhetha. Akukhona konke—kuphela okudingwa imizuzwana eyishumi nanye ezayo, ngendlela okhipha ngayo incwadi eyodwa eshalofini ngoba usuvele uyayazi ikhasi. I-Capoeira, ngenxa yomsebenzi wezinyawo oguqula ukuhuzuka kube ukuhlasela futhi usebenzise isisindo somzimba uqobo siwuphikise. I-Judo ne-jiu-jitsu, ngenxa yokuphonsa, ngoba into esindayo kangaka akufanele ishayise lapho ingakwazi *ukuwa kahle* phezu kwenkinga bese ivumela i-physics ivale i-akhawunti. Washiya okunye eshalofini. Izinqolobane ezikhethiwe zavuleka kuye ngesikhathi esisodwa, zalayishwa kahle, zafundwa phakathi kwezinsingo ezimbili zenhliziyo futhi zaba ngezakhe ngokuphelele.

Kuthathe isikhathi esingaphansi kokukhanya kweso. Umzuzwana owodwa uWolf wayengazi lutho ngokuphonsa umzimba. Olandelayo wayesekwazi konke.

Wanyakaza.

Akazange anyakaze njengemishini yokuhlenga. Wanyakaza njengento imishini yokuhlenga ebilokhu ikwazi ukuba yiyo kodwa ingakaze ivunyelwe. Amabhanqi ama-capacitor akhipha amandla futhi isudi yawela umhlabathi ovulekile ngokugxuma okukodwa okuphansi, amathani amane ehla ngaphakathi komugqa ngaphambi kokuba umdubuli oseduze aqede ukuphakamisa isibhamu sakhe, kanti uWolf wayesebefunda indawo ngezwe elidwetshwe uAtlas, izinyathelo njengama-vector, imizimba njengefu elishisayo, ukusabela kwe-seismic ezimotweni kuwubufakazi obuqinile ngaphansi kwamathuba athambile abantu, imephu iseduze ngokwanele futhi isheshayo ngokwanele ukugcina amathani amane kanye ne-shaped charge ensimbi hhayi esikhunjeni.

I-autocannon yemoto eseduze yaphenduka. UAtlas wamnika njengonombolo—*umzuzu owodwa nohlanu wesibili ukuze ibheke*—futhi uWolf wayesechithe imizuzwana engamakhulu amane kuyo ngaphambi kokuba isibhamu siqede amabanga ayishumi okuqala. Akazange athinte abasebenzi. Waqhuba i-shaped charge kumantlet wesibhamu, ukusika kukadokotela lapho i-geometry kaLibrarian yokulwa nezikhali kanye nemephu yokucindezela kaAtlas kuhlangana endaweni eyodwa, kwathi isibhamu sehlukaniswa saba yizicucu ezingalimazi muntu, abasebenzi bavikelekile, bathuthumele kodwa bephila ngaphakathi kwegobolondo elingasaphindi lidubule.

Imoto yesibili yazama ukubuyela emuva. Lapha izinqolobane zavuka, abadubuli baqala ukubona lokho abazokuchaza kamuva futhi bangakholwa: imishini yamathani amane yayisekubukekeni njengemishini. UAtlas wayesevele unike uWolf isikhungo sesisindo semoto, esiphakeme kakhulu kunendlela yayo, futhi i-capoeira yayazi kahle ukuthi kwenziwani ngesikhumba esifuna ukuwa. UWolf akazange ayidonsise. Uyisize. Umugqa we-winch wathatha i-axle yangaphambili kwathi i-grapple yabamba, esikhundleni sokudonsa uWolf wehlisa isisindo sakhe waphenduka ngamahlombe, amathani amane ajikeleza onyaweni olulodwa njengomuntu onesisindo esingamashumi amane, waguqula ukudonsa kwaba ukuphonsa. Ukubuyela emuva kwemoto kwenza yaphenduka. Kwaba ukuphonsa kwamahlombe, kuhlanzekile njengokuthi i-judo iphonsa umuntu ogijimayo, amandla akhe asetshenziswa kuye—futhi abadubuli babona isudi yokuhlenga iphonsa imoto enezikhali abakwazi ukukholwa amehlo abo. Omunye waphuma emuva engalungile, ingalo igobile ngaphansi kwakhe, ukuqhuma kwe-radius kuzwakala ngisho phezu kwamajenene. *Lowo uphukile,* uWolf wabhalisa, eqonde, *futhi bengingeke ngikuthole kokubili—ukuphonsa kancane ukuze ngisindise ingalo bekuzovumela isibhamu ukuthi sizungeze.* Wafaka ingalo ngaphansi kwe-*cost* waqhubeka.

Kwaba, ngaleso sikhathi, umdanso, nakuba kungabikho noyedwa obukele ongasebenzisa lelo gama entweni ephetha ubusuku babantu ngendlela esheshayo. Lena yayingeyona ingxenye u-Arin ayeyiphindaphinda iminyaka eminingi engakwazi ukuyiqonda ngokuphelele: ukuthi imizuzwana eyishumi nanye enodlame kakhulu uGuardian ayeke wayichitha futhi yayiyinhle kakhulu, isudi idlula emgqeni wokudubula ngobuciko bento engasindi lutho, ama-arc aphansi e-capoeira egeleza aye kwijometri esondele ye-jiu-jitsu, ukuxhumana ngakunye kuyiphonsa noma ukususa isikhali hhayi ukushaya, amandla asindayo okwakufanele kwenze kube yibhola elibhidlizayo asetshenziswa njengesisindo somlingani kumdanso—nikeza, phambanisa, bheka phansi. Umgadli wagxuma uWolf wamvumela ukuthi afike, wabamba ukunyakaza, wasebenzisa isisindo somuntu uqobo ukumlalisa emhlabathini ngamazolo ngendlela enobubele. Abanye ababili, isudi yadlula phakathi kwabo bahlala phansi, bengenazo izikhali, bephaphama, bengaqiniseki ukuthi yini ebashintshe.

Futhi kanye—kanye kuphela, phakathi nomdanso, lapho kudingeka khona—uWolf wadlulela ngaphaya kwamabhulokhi weqhinga waya kuMother. UMother usehlukanisiwe manje, ingxenye yakhe isesele emgqeni wabantu ibala ukushaya kwenhliziyo kaArin, kodwa i-override yathatha okudingekayo lapho ikhona: imininingwane eqondile yomzimba womuntu uMother ayigcina yokwelapha, ifundwa emuva. Lapho umgogodla uvaleka khona. Lapho umkhawulo wobuhlungu bomzimba umi ngaphambi komkhawulo wokulimala. Imephu efanayo uMother ayisebenzisayo ukusindisa abantu yatshela uWolf kahle ukuthi angamphonsa kanjani umuntu ngaphandle kokulimaza lutho. Iwrist ithathwa iye ekugcineni kwayo hhayi ngaphezu kwalokho. Ihlombe lijikiswa aze umzimba uzigoqe kunokuba uphuke. Ulwazi lodokotela lusetshenziswe, umzuzwana owodwa, njengolwazi lokususa isikhali. UMother akazange aphikise, ngoba akakwazi, ngoba ku-override wayeyithuluzi etafuleni njengabanye. UWolf wamsebenzisa kahle wamkhulula.

Ama-drones ehla ngamandla, wonke amane, i-EM suite ifinyelela phezulu ukuyozithola, kwathi i-grapple kanye nokuqhuma okukodwa okunembile kwe-ram kwazikhipha emoyeni zaba izingcezu ezawa phezu kwe-gravel engenalutho. Umgadli wadubula—kanye, ngokuziphendulela—futhi u-Atlas wadweba umugqa wesibhamu nomuntu owasemuva ngaso leso sikhathi esifanayo esipendwe, kwathi uWolf wakhetha ipuleti kunokuhluthula isibhamu. Waphendulela ihlombe lakhe *emashothweni* esikhundleni sokuhluthula isibhamu ngaphambi kwesikhathi, wamukela ukuqhuma epulethini le-ceramic elalingadingi ukukuthwala, ngoba ukuhluthula ngaphambi kwesikhathi kwakusho ukubamba isandla somgadli nge-spreader jaw emgqeni ogcwele, kwathi leso sandla wayengeke asiphinde asithole. Amashothi aphuma epulethini angenzi lutho, ngoba yilokho okwakwenziwa ipuleti, ngoba izikali ze-pangolin zihlale zikwazi ukumelana nokushaywa. U-Wolf wabe esekhipha isibhamu ezandleni zomgadli, wagoba umgqomo kwaba sengathi uwintshi, wamphonsa phansi emhlane wakhe phezu kwe-gravel, ephelelwa umoya kodwa ephelele futhi egcine isandla sakhe, ebuka phezulu kumshini owawukhethe ukudutshulwa kunokumphula, wenza leso sinqumo sibonakale singelutho.

Kwathatha imizuzwana eyishumi nanye. Imizuzwana eyishumi nanye isudi yokuhlenga ibisiyimishini yempi ephelele eqhutshwa ingqondo eyodwa ngokuxhumana okuqondile kwe-neural—zonke izinhlelo zivuliwe, yonke imitapo yolwazi ilayishiwe, iNkantolo yonke isetshenziswa njengezinsimbi ngaphansi kwesandla esibandayo—futhi ayikho ingcwaba phakathi kwazo.

Lapho sekuphelile, umgwaqo wokudonsa wawusuyindawo yezinsalela. Izimoto ezimbili ezingenalutho, ama-drones amane adilikile, inotho encane yezinsiza zempi ezizimele esiphenduke imfucuza namapuleti agobile kanye nephunga le-propellant eshisiwe, amadoda ayishumi nanye ephila phansi, wonke owake wema emgqeni—oyedwa ephukile ingalo, ababili abangeke bezwe kahle isonto lonke, abanye bephelelwa umoya, behlulekile futhi bengasenazo izikhali. Akukho ngcwaba phakathi kwabo. Lokho bekuyiyona njongo yonke, futhi bekungakhokhelwa mahhala, kwathi uWolf wakukhokha eduze kakhulu, ngepuleti lehlombe elagqashulwa umlilo awukhethe yena. Umgwaqo oya e-headgear wawuvulekile.

U-Wolf wama.

Wema phakathi kwezinsalela azidalile, amathani amane omshini wokuhlenga phezu kwensimu yezikhali eziphukile, wabuyisela isudi.

Wayibuyisa ngendlela obeka ngayo into osuyiqondile ngokuphelele futhi ongafuni ukuyibamba futhi. Okhiye babuyela ekhaya. U-Atlas wayeka ukuba umhlaba, waphenduka futhi waba yizwi elixwayisa ngomthwalo. Imitapo yolwazi kaLibrarian evaliwe, ifa lezempi libuyiselwa ngaphansi kwezinto-ezazaziwa-futhi-ezingasetshenziswa, ishalofu livalwa. UMama waphinda waba munye, izinhlangothi zombili zakhe, umzimba obolekiwe ubuyiselwa emsebenzini wokugcina abantu bephila. Bayeka ukuba izinsimbi baba ozakwabo futhi, bebuyiselwa phezulu kubo uqobo. Umnyango kaJaji uvalwa. Umbuzo omncane ubuya, *yini ebulala umqhubi,* kwathi uWolf wazigoqa waphinda wangena kuwo, emncane kunakuqala, omncane kunabo bonke, engathathi ndawo.

INkantolo yabuyela emzimbeni ngokushesha, yawuthola usathule, yawuthola umgwaqo uvulekile, yawathola abagadli bephila abebefanele, ngokwemithetho yalezi zimo, babe bephaphama—kwatholakala, okubi kakhulu, ukuthi babengenakho ukukhumbula imizuzwana ephakathi, ukuthi *basebenzisiwe* bengaxwayiswanga, bathathwa babekwa phansi ngomunye wabo. UJaji, emangele. UMama, ebala ukushaya kwenhliziyo emgwaqeni ethola zonke, engazi ukuthi usuwazi kanjani umphumela. UMercury, okokuqala, engenalutho angakusho.

*Ubani,* uJaji waqala.

*Mina,* kusho uFool. Kalula. Ngokukhululeka. Umhlekisi futhi, sengathi akazange abambe okhiye abakhulu babo bonke esandleni esivulekile imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye. *Ningakhathazeki. Bonke basaphila. Lokho bekuyinjongo yonke. I-cage isivulekile futhi owesifazane ogqoke i-windbreaker uzoba nentshisekelo enkulu ekurekhodweni, futhi uphinde, ngiyabona, abe ukuphela kwesikhulu kule sayithi okhiphe abantu bakhe abangaphethe lutho empini yezibhamu esikhundleni sokubafaka kuyo, ngakho asimsoli kakhulu uBrand. Qhubeka, badger. Kusasele amadoda ayisithupha emgodini.*

U-Arin wayehleli ngaphakathi esudi inhliziyo ishaya, amalambu amhlophe eqhwa ebusweni, engakwazi ukukhuluma. Ngemuva kwakhe, ngaphesheya komugqa odilikile, wayekwazi kancane ukuthi uBrand umi onqenqemeni le-trailer yakhe emnyama, engasondeli kuye futhi engasuki, i-tablet isezansi eceleni kwakhe, ebuka into ayihambise ebusuku ukuyomisa ihamba iye e-cage emgwaqeni esanda kuwuhlanza ngaphandle kokubulala muntu—futhi ekhetha, futhi, ukuyivumela.

"Ngemuva kwalokho," kusho uMercury, ethululela u-Arin, lowo msindo omdala wokududuzela, "sesizimele. Akusekho repeater ngale kwezinga le-11. Ngaphakathi kuphela."

"Sihlale sizimele," kusho uWolf, akekho ophendulile.

---

I-cage ibingakhiwe ngaye.

Ibekuyibhokisi lensimbi elenzelwe amadoda, ayisishiyagalombili ngesikhathi, isakhiwo esehla ebumnyameni ngesivinini esenza isisu sibe buhlungu. U-Guardian wayengeke angene emi. U-Arin kwadingeka agoqe isudi—amadolo agobile, ikhanda lithotshiwe, igobolondo ligoqa libe yifomu elenzelwe into encane, ethambile. Noma kunjalo, i-cage yakhala ngaphansi kwesisindo esingazange sikhonjwe, kwadingeka ukuthi umqhubi wase-winch house aqinisekiswe kabili ngaphambi kokukhulula i-brake.

Kwabe sekudonswa phansi phansi.

Ukwehla kwakungathi akuwi kodwa kudliwa umhlaba. Izindonga zomgodi zashunqa zidlula, zikhanyiswa ngamahlathi yi-cage lamp—amatshe amanzi, ukukhanya kwesiteshi esingaphansi, inqwaba yamapayipi nezintambo, kwaphela. Kwabuya ubumnyama. Kwabuya ukukhanya. Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye kwalokhu, umoya uqina njalo ngemitha eyikhulu, ufudumala, ucindezela, kwaze kwaba ukuthi awusaseyona imoya yasekuseni epholile kodwa sekuyinto enesisindo, into ecindezela kuye.

U-Arin wayelungile phezulu. Phezulu kwakunomoya.

Umhume wawungenawo umoya.

Wakuqala ezandleni, iminwe ithuthumela ifuna ukugoqa. Umoya wakhe waba mfushane, ukwesaba kukhuphuka kusuka esifubeni, ngendlela okuhlale kwenzeka kusukela esemncane lapho amadoda ase-lokitshini ethwala okwakusele kuyise ephuma emgodini onjengalo—ubumnyama obunjalo, iphunga lamatshe amanzi ne-diesel kanye nobanda obujulile bephansi lapho ilanga lingakaze lifike khona.

Izindonga zivaliwe. Wayazi, ngokwengqondo, ukuthi azihambi. Umhume wawunobubanzi obungamamitha amane. Wayengakwazi ukulala no-Guardian ngaphakathi. Izindonga bezingavali, namatshe angenhla abengeke awe, ne-cage ibingeke ime imkhwabanise lapha ebumnyameni amakhilomitha amabili ngaphansi kwendawo eyathatha uyise—

Umoya wakhe washeshisa. Ukwesaba. Mfushane. I-visor yagcwala ifu elimhlophe.

Izibalo bezingalungile. Amanani abengalungile. Uhlelo belungalungile. Wake wehla emgodini ngaphambili, kwathatha uyise, kwathatha iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye ukwakha umshini ukuze angaphinde akwenze futhi, kodwa wakwenza, ngenhloso, engena endaweni ayichithe impilo yakhe yonke ezama ukubaleka—

I-cage yathuthumela. Ngokuvundlile. Kwakuwumhlahlandlela, kwakuwukuguga ayesekukala, kodwa umzimba wakhe awusazihluphi ngezilinganiso. Umzimba wakhe wawukhumbula. Izindonga zacindezela. Amatshe angenhla acindezela. Ubumnyama obungezansi bavuleka njengomlomo.

Amathani amane e-Guardian suit. Isikhumba sakhe ngaphakathi. Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye amatshe.

"Arin," kusho uMama.

Izandla zakhe zazizamazama. Waziphoqa ukuba zibe nokuthula.

"Arin. Lalela izwi lami. Ungazami ukwehlisa ukuphefumula kwakho. Lokho akukaze kukusebenzele, futhi sobabili siyakwazi lokho. Ngitshele nje into eyodwa ongayizwa. Hhayi esiketi. Emzimbeni wakho."

"Angikwazi—"

"Into eyodwa. Noma iyiphi."

"Izandla zami—" Waphusha amazwi ephuma. "Izandla zami. Zibanda."

"Kuhle. Lokho kuyiqiniso. Izandla zakho zibanda ngoba awuzange ugqoke amagilavu ngaphansi kwe-Assist futhi sizokhuluma ngalokho kamuva. Okunye?"

"Ikhola." Izwi lakhe lakhala lapho. "Iphikisana nentamo yami. Ishisa. I-chip ishisa."

"Yilokho thina," kuphendula uMama. "Leyo mfudumalo thina. Siseseduze nawe. Phakathi kwamahlombe akho. Asiyi ndawo ongayi khona." Kwaba nokuthula okuncane. "Okunye?"

Izindonga zazisavalwa. Kodwa wayesebalule izinto ezimbili, futhi zombili ziyiqiniso, futhi ingxenye yengqondo yakhe eyayiphila ngeqiniso yaphikelela kulokho njengomuntu owehlayo ephikelela kubha.

"Phansi kuyazamazama," wakwazi ukusho. "Ikheji. I-resonance ayilungile. Ama-guide agugile ohlangothini—" Wama. Waphefumula. "Agugile ohlangothini olusenyakatho. Kunokunyakaza okuvundlile. Cishe amamilimitha amane. Yingakho kuyazamazama."

"Nangu-ke," kusho uFool, efudumele njengelanga. "Simtholile. Ubelapho ngaphansi kokwesaba sonke lesi sikhathi. Uhlala ekhona."

"Ungakhohlisi," kusho uMama.

"Angihlekisi. Ngiyakhululeka. Kukhona umehluko, futhi ngivunyelwe kokubili." Izwi likaFool lehla, kwaba ngelika-Arin kuphela, ngendlela elihlala lihla ngayo uma lingasazenzi. "Wake wehla emgudwini onje phambilini. Waphakanyiswa uphume emgudwini onje. Wawuneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye. Usuchithe iminyaka eyishumi nane wakha umshini ukuze kungabi khona omunye oneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye ome phezulu kwawo futhi. Manje usuwehla wena." Kwaba nokuthula. "Lokho akusikho ukwakha okubi, uyazi. Lokho kuyinto eqotho kakhulu engake ngayibona umuntu eyenza. Bengicabanga ukuthi kufanele kube khona umuntu okushoyo lokho ngaphambi kokuba itshe likwenze."

Ikheji lashaya phansi komgodi ngomsindo owazwakala kuze kufike ezinzwaneni zika-Arin.

Ilambu lazinza. Ukuzamazama kwaphela. Wayesephansi komhlaba, futhi umhlaba wawungakakwi phezu kwakhe. Okungenani okwamanje.

"Isiteyishini sezinga le-11. I-Repeater isisele emuva. Sise-internal." Kwakukhona ubulukhuni ezwini lonjiniyela elalingajwayelekile, eliseduze nobumnene njengoba u-Atlas engakaze abe njalo. "Kusukela lapha, amamephu amadala futhi itshe lithukuthele. Ngine-seismic. Ngine-survey yokuqala kanye namarekhodi e-rebolting avela kuma-eighties kanye nento oyithole ngo-3 ekuseni okungekho muntu omunye owayeyitholile. Anginayo impela inqubekela phambili. Yazi lokho ngaphambi kokuthi siqale. Ngizokutshela iqiniso ngisho noma iqiniso lithi *angazi.*"

"Lokho kuphela engake ngakucela kuwe," kusho uArin.

Wamisa isiketi, kancane, egumbini eliphansi. Isiteshi sasiyinhlekelele—imirayili igobile, izinkuni ziphuke, ukuwa kwamatshe kuvimbe ingxenye yomgwaqo oya entshonalanga lapho kwawa khona. Uthuli lwalumile emgodini welambu, luncane njengofulawa, lungafuni ukuhlala emoyeni ongenandawo yokuya. Kwakushisa. Kwakushisa impela manje, ukufudumala kwamakhilomitha amabili nesigamu etsheni kugcine ukushisa komhlaba kumphikisa. Umjuluko waqala emhlane wakhe ngaphakathi kwe-Assist, wezwa amafeni okupholisa esiketi eqala ukusebenza, umsindo omnene okhipha ukushisa ku-chip kuqala. Okubalulekile kuMama. Njalo.

"Abasindile badlule lapho kwawa khona," kusho yena. "Badlule lapho kwawa khona bese behla ngomthambeka bengena egumbini elidala lokusabela. Uma igumbi lisamile."

"Uma igumbi lisamile," kusho u-Atlas. Ngokulinganayo. Eqotho. "Okungesikaziwa."

"Izimpawu zokushisa eziyisikhombisa emahoreni amane adlule," kusho uLibrarian. "Ngirekhode i-surface feed ngaphambi kokuba siyilahle. Eziyisikhombisa, kwabayi-11 ababephambi lapho kwawa khona. Babebuthene ndawonye. Ukuhlanganisana kuhle—kuchaza ukuthi babehamba ngezinyawo zabo, bekhetha ukuba ndawonye. Izidumbu azihlangani. Abaphilayo bayahlangana."

"Abantu abayisithupha bangaphefumula isikhathi esingakanani phansi lapho?" kubuza uArin.

Kwaba nokuthula. Wayesefunde ukufunda ukuthula kweNkantolo. Lokhu kwakunguMama no-Atlas bexoxisana, ngokushesha, ngaphansi kwamazwi.

"Uma isitoreji segumbi lokusabela lisekhona?" kusho uMama. "Amahora ayi-18. Uma lingekho—uma igumbi lonakele futhi basemoyeni ofile?" Akazange akuthobe; ukuthoba bekuzobe kusho amanga, futhi iNkantolo ayisayimanga kuye. "Amahora amathathu. Mhlawumbe amane. Asazi kuze singene ngaphakathi."

"Ngakho asimi lapha," kusho uWolf.

"Cha," kusho uArin. "Asimi."

Wasondela lapho kwawa khona.

---

*Ukudilika kwakuyisithiyo sodonga lwamatshe aphukile, amamitha amathathu awo, kusukela osayizi wesibhakela kuya kowesifriji, kufakwe futhi kuhlanganiswe ngaphansi kwesisindo sawo. Ku-Arin, elambini, kwakuyisithiyo. KuNkantolo, esikhathini eseluliwe ngaphansi kokuqonda kwakhe, kwakuyinkinga enomumo, futhi bayiphendulela ndawonye ngendlela iNkantolo ephendulela ngayo konke: ngengxabano.*

ATLAS: *Ungadonsi phansi. Phansi kuphethe umthwalo. Uma udonsa phansi, uletha emuva phezu kwakhe.*

WOLF: *Ngakho sizoba sesimeni esihamba kancane. Ukuhamba kancane kubiza abathathu umoya wabo.*

ATLAS: *Ukuhamba kancane kubiza umoya wabo. Ukuhamba ngokushesha kubiza umsindisi, bese sebengenawo umoya* futhi *bengenawo umsindisi. Phinda leyo zibalo usebenzisa okokufaka okufanele.*

JUDGE: *U-Atlas ulungile ngokomthetho. Umsindisi ofile akasindi muntu. Sihlanza phezulu, isiqeshana ngesinqeshana, samukele isikhathi esithathayo.*

MERCURY: *Usezolahlekelwa isibindi futhi. Inhliziyo yakhe isishayile. Ubheka ubukhulu bayo.*

MOTHER: *Pho makube khona omunye ongene ngaphansi nayo ngaphambi kokuba enze njalo.*

FOOL: *Ngizongena. Uyangilalela uma esaba ngoba ngiyingedwa ongamesabi.*

WOLF: *Uyesaba wena.*

FOOL: *Kusobala. Kodwa ngenza kahle kakhulu ukubonakala njengomuntu ongamesabi, futhi yilokho okubalulekile. Atlas—ngiphe iziqeshana ezintathu eziphuma ngaphandle kokuletha uphahla. Ngizokwenza kubonakale njengomlingo wamakhadi.*

ATLAS: *Phezulu kwesobunxele. Bese kuba yisiwedge esingenhla maphakathi. Bese kuba yinde ohlangothini lwesokudla, futhi kube yiyo kuphela, ungadluli ngisho namamilimitha, noma wonke umkhakha wasenyakatho uyawa bese siba nengxoxo emfushane kakhulu.*

LIBRARIAN: *Ngirekhoda uchungechunge. Uma lokhu kuyasebenza, kuyindlela. Singase siyidinge kabili ngaphambi kokufika egumbini lokusabela.*

JUDGE: *Pho yenza kube yindlela. Yenza kube into ephindaphindekayo. Asizami inhlanhla yethu lapho. Sakha umgwaqo.*

[END SLOW MODE]

---

"Phezulu kwesobunxele kuqala," kusho uFool, ngokukhanya nokudlala, sengathi basemsebenzini futhi okubi kakhulu kungaba ukuhluleka kokulingisa. "I-block ephezulu kwesobunxele, ibukeka ingenacala. Khipha yona. U-Atlas uthi ayibambi lutho. U-Atlas akakaze akukhulumele amanga mayelana nokuphathelene nomthwalo ngoba u-Atlas akakwazi ngisho ukukwazi lokho. Kuyiphutha lobuntu. Siyamthanda ngalokho."

U-Arin welula isandla. Isandla sikaGuardian sabamba i-block. Wazilungiselela wadonsa, ama-actuator esiketi ekhishwa umsindo omncane ophakeme ngaphansi komthwalo wangempela, i-block yaphuma ngesililo esinzima nentuthu yothuli, akukho okwalandela.

"Uyabona? Umdlalo wamakhadi." Izwi likaFool lahlala liqinile. "I-wedge engenhla maphakathi ilandelayo. Umoya ofanayo. Awunyakazisi intaba, uxazulula itshe elilodwa ngesikhathi. Awukaze wehluleke ukuxazulula itshe."

Wathatha i-wedge. Wathatha le ende engakwesokudla, futhi akazange adlule ngisho nangesentimitha. INkantolo yamemezela uchungechunge, wayilandelela, kwase kuqaqeka idwala njengentambo evuleka uma usuthole intambo efanele—hhayi konke ngesikhathi esisodwa, kodwa ngokungagwemeki, isiqephu ngesinqephu, kwaze kwavela isikhala lapho i-Guardian egobile ingakwazi ukudlula khona.

Umoya wakhe waphinde waba mfushane phakathi nendawo. Akazange akubone. Umsebenzi wawudle ukwesaba; umsebenzi wawuhlala wenza njalo, selokhu engumfana ethekwini lomsebenzi. Inqobo nje uma kunedwala okufanele lixazululwe, kwakungekho ndawo esele ebumnyameni.

Kodwa ngaphesheya kwesikhala, indlela yancipha. Yancipha. Ilambu lakhanyisa umphimbo wedwala ongangaphezu kwe-suit kancane, udonga oluphakeme luguqa, izinsika zokhuni zigobile ngaphansi komthwalo ezazilahlekelwa yizo.

Wema. Umoya waba mfushane futhi awulungile.

“Mama,” kusho yena. “Angikwazi. Kukhulu kakhulu—kuthinta amapuleti—”

“Ngiyazi.” Akukho ukukhathazeka ezwini lakhe, iqiniso nje elilethwe ngokuqondile. “Zombili izingalo sezithintene. Lokho kuyiqiniso. Awukucabangi. Kuqinile.”

“Angikwazi ukungena lapho. Angikwazi ukuphenduka lapho, angikwazi—”

“Arin.” Mercury, manje, ngezwi elicacile. “Ingalo yesobunxele. Ngaphansi kwe-Assist. Ube nayo kusukela uneminyaka eyishumi nesithupha. Uyazi leyo.”

Umoya wakhe wabanjwa.

“Kusho,” kusho uMercury. “Ngokuzwakalayo. Uhlala ungitshela ukuthi uyizakele ukuze ungadingi ukuzethemba ukuthi uyayikhumbula. Ngakho ungazethemba. Themba i-inki. Kusho.”

Amagama okuqala aphuma ngokuthintana, lawadala, umkhuleko omdala esandleni sakhe—umthandazo wasehlane wokulwa nokwesaba awazakele esezineminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa futhi awuphindaphinda kuzo zonke izimayini kusukela lapho.

“Qhubeka.”

“Ukwesaba kuyabulala ingqondo.” Izwi lakhe lazinza kulowo mugqa. Amagama ayephambi kokudabuka kwakhe; wayewaphethe kuzo zonke izindawo ezimnyama zempilo yakhe, isigatshana ngesigatshana.

Futhi—

—wayizwa iphindaphindwa.

INkantolo yathatha umoya ngemuva kwakhe. Hhayi amagama, kodwa umoya—uMama, ephansi futhi eqinisekile; uMercury, egcina isilinganiso; futhi ngaphansi kwabo uAtlas, kubo bonke, uAtlas ongumqondofana, owayefunde imigqa esandleni sakhe ku-link wanquma ngokubala okuthile u-Arin angeke akubone ukuthi ukwesekwa okuhle kakhulu angakunikeza manje kwakuwukushaya umoya wokuphefumula.

Wakhuluma isigatshana esilandelayo futhi bamlandela, futhi u-Arin wezwa amafeni okupholisa e-suit elungisa—wazizwa iNkantolo ingena ohlelweni lwe-Guardian ifanisa ukuphefumula kwakhe nomoya wokuphindaphinda, ihlisa amafeni, ilungisa umsindo we-actuator ukuze uhambisane nomoya, ukuze wonke umshini uphefumule naye, ukuze indawo ayesaba kakhulu ibe yindawo ephila naye.

Wavumela ukwesaba kudlule kuye, kumphambukele, njengoba lawo magama amadala ayemfundisa, kwathi lapho sekuphelile waphenduka wabheka lapho bekukhona khona.

Indawo ayizange yande, kodwa yama ukuyizwa njengomphimbo, yaqala ukuthatha umumo.

“*Futhi lapho sekudlulile, ngizophendula iso lami langaphakathi ngibheke indlela yakhona.*”

“Nangu,” kusho uFool, cishe engazwakali. “Siyakwamukela.”

“*Lapho ukwesaba sekuphelile akusayi kubakho lutho.*” U-Arin wabeka isandla sakhe esinsikeni yokhuni egobile. Wayihlola. Wezwa uAtlas emnika inombolo yomthwalo engakabuzile. “*Ngizoba ngedwa kuphela.*”

Wangena.

---

Indawo yehla ngamamitha angamashumi amane, kwazwakala sengathi ihora lonke.

Wayehamba ngezandla nezinyawo—ukuma kwe-Guardian kwezinhlanu, amapuleti ehlikihla idwala. Amadolo akhe, lawangempela, ayesephenduke abuthuntu esikhwameni sokupholisa kudala; wakubona njengokubona ukufunda kwegeji, waqhubeka. Ijasho laqoqana esinqeni sakhe futhi kwaba nokuluma phezulu esihlathini angakwazi ukufinyelela kukho, angeke akwazi ukufinyelela amahora amaningi, futhi lobuncane balokho—ukuluma, phakathi kwazo zonke izinto, lapha phansi—kwamphatha kancane. Kabili, udonga oluphakeme lwanyakaza, kwavela umsindo ojulile nomhlabathi, kwathi kabili iNkantolo yathula, ngamunye wabo elalele ngakho konke abanakho, kwathi kabili kwaphumelela.

Kodwa okwesithathu, akuzange kuphumelele.

I-slab yehla ngemuva kwakhe, umsindo onjengomhlaba ohlanza umphimbo, ivala indlela ebuyela esiteshini sezinga le-11. Uthuli lwakhuphuka endaweni yehla phezu kwakhe, ilambu laphenduka isiphithiphithi esijikijela. Umzimba ka-Arin waqina—

*uvalelwe, wangcwatshwa, akukho ndlela yokubuya, akukho ndlela yokubuya, kwenzeka kanje kuye, kwenzeka kanje—*

—futhi ama-piezo struts e-Guardian's spine avuka, ngoba umthelela we-slab uwalayisha, ngoba idwala elivale yena lifake amandla alo esakhiweni se-suit, futhi u-Arin wabuka i-gauge yamandla ikhuphuka *phezulu* ku-visor, amaphesenti amathathu, amane, kwaphuma ukuhleka okungathi ukukhala.

“Ingcindezi iba amandla,” aphendula kancane. “Umshini omuhle.”

“Umklamo wakho,” kusho uAtlas. “Thina siyawusebenzisa.”

“Indlela ebuyayo ayisekho, akunjalo.”

“Indlela ebuyayo ayisekho,” kusho uAtlas, eqondile. “Uvumelwe ukusaba. Awuvunyelwe ukumangala.”

Kwaba nokuthula, u-Arin waphefumula phakathi kwako.

“Leyo yinto yesibili enomusa oyishilo kimi,” kusho yena.

“Yini yokuqala?”

“Wangitshela ukuthi ukuwa kukababa akuzange kube yinto eyayingagwemeki. Kwakuwukukhetha okwenziwa ngomnotho. Idwala alizange limbulele. Umuntu wambulala.”

“Lowo kwakunguJudge,” kusho uAtlas.

“Kwakuwena,” kusho u-Arin. “UJudge wavuma. Kodwa kwakuwena owabala imithwalo wayifakazela.” Waqhubeka phambili, eya ebumnyameni, ebheke kubantu abaphelelwa umoya. “Angikaze ngibonge ngalokho.”

“Uyabonga manje,” kusho uAtlas. “Ngokwehla lapha. Ngokwakha indlela esikhundleni somgodi. Lelo yibonga ingcweti yesakhiwo engalifunda.”

Indawo yehla yaya endaweni ebanzi. Uthuli lwaqala ukuzinza, ngokungathandi. Phambili, ekukhanyeni okuphindayo kwelambu, u-Arin wabona into engelona idwala—umnyango wensimbi, uphume kancane esikhaleni kodwa usahleli, ophuzi nomnyama okujwayelekile we-refuge chamber, futhi phezu kwawo, kukhothwe ngobudaka ngesandla, imigqa eyisikhombisa, eyodwa idwetshwe kabili.

Umoya wakhe wabanjwa. Kulokhu akubanga ukwesaba.

“Isikhombisa,” kusho uLibrarian kancane. “Imigqa eyisikhombisa. Eyodwa idwetshwe kabili. Bavuselele inani labantu. Bafuna sazi ukuthi bangaki abasele.”

“Bayaphila,” kusho uMama, okokuqala usuku lonke, kwavela okuthile ezwini leNkantolo okungeyona ukuthula, okungeyona ukulawula, kodwa iqiniso elimsulwa elalilethe bonke lapha. “Arin. Bayaphila.”

Wawela indawo yokugcina. Isibambo somnyango sasiyinsimbi egobile, eshisayo ngothuli lwamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye, kubanda ngisho nangaphansi kwepuleti. Wabeka isandla esikhulu esilimele se-Guardian ensimbini ebandayo yomnyango, kancane, sengathi uthinta okuthambile, kwathi ngaphesheya, kancane kodwa ngokucacile, kwavela izwi elikhuluma isiSesotho, elinye liphendula, kwashesha kwavela amazwi amaningi, umsindo wabantu ababezibalele ethuneni bezwa kukhothwa esivalweni.
