Wema isikhashana, ephefumula. Ichip ishisa phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, abayisikhombisa bathule eduze kwakhe, amazwi ngaphesheya kwensimbi.

“Nkantolo,” kusho yena. Izwi lakhe lahlala liqinile, ngokumangalisayo. “Ake sibakhiphe.”

“Sikanye nawe,” baphendula, bonke. Izwi elilodwa, imiqondo eyisikhombisa.

“Kuwo wonke umzila,” kusho uFool kancane, ngendlela ayehlala engena ngayo ekugcineni. “Phansi lapha, phezulu laphaya. Kuwo wonke umzila, badger. Manje vula umnyango.”

Wavula umnyango.

# Xhumana

Indlela endala yokudonsa imithwalo yayiqondile amamitha angamakhulu amabili ngaphambi kokuba iyeke ukuzikhohlisa.

U-Arin wakuzwa ushintsho ngaphambi kokuba izibani zikuqinisekise. Ukushintsha kwezinyathelo zika-Guardian, i-echo yacwila, phansi kwathambekela kancane kwesobunxele lapho idwala lashaywa khona ngesizini eselibaleke. Indawo yacwebezela, izinkuni zokuqala ezabekwa amadoda ase ayisiduli iminyaka eminingi, zamelela isikhathi eside kunalokho abahloli bezobunjiniyela ababekukholwa. Wayengayithemba. Izibani zekhanda zesudi zakha izinkanyezi ezimbili ebumnyameni, uthuli lwamile phakathi kwazo lufana nento eqinile, iqhwa elihlezi lihamba kancane elingafiki phansi.

*Phansi phambili amamitha ayishumi nanye asengcupheni,* kubika u-Atlas. *Isigxobo esidala sithathiwe kwesobunxele. Isisindo sikwi-rib yesokudla.*

U-Arin washintsha ukuma kwakhe. U-Guardian walungisa naye, ingxenye enkulu yamathani amane e-titanium ne-ceramic isatshalaliswa kahle endaweni engcono njengokungathi yisitho sakhe esilungisa uma ethambekele. Wayeseyekile ukucabanga ngesudi njengento ayigqokayo; kwenye indawo phakathi kwamamitha angamakhulu amane adlule, yayisiyindlela yokuma.

“Kude kangakanani ukuya e-refuge bay?”

*Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu neshumi,* kuphendula u-Librarian. *Izinhlelo zango-1962 zikhombisa i-cross-cut lapha okufanele ixhume esiteshini se-Level 38. Izinhlelo azihlali zithembekile.*

“Akukho lutho oluthembekile lapha phansi,” kusho u-Arin ephendula kancane.

*Yilokho okudingekayo,* kusho u-Fool. *Yehlisa okulindele, angeke udumale. Yindlela engihlala ngiyenza ngayo uma ngikhuluma no-Judge.*

*Ngiyakunganaki,* kwaphendula u-Judge.

*Uyasho lokho, kodwa.*

I-cross-cut yayikhona ngempela lapho u-Librarian ekhombe khona, kwamangaza bonke, ikakhulukazi u-Arin. Waguqula u-Guardian wayifaka kuyo, igobolondo lishayisana nodwala nhlangothi zombili, umsindo onzima wokukhala kwe-ceramic idonsa edwaleni wadlula emzimbeni wakhe wangena emathanjeni akhe. Indawo yavuleka, i-cross-cut yagcina isiyigumbi elibanzi lapho izimoto ze-ore zayilayishwa khona, izibani zakhe zakhanyisa umnyango wensimbi we-refuge bay ofakwe odongeni olukude.

Umnyango wawugobile. Uhlaka lwawuthwale umthwalo olusuka phezulu lwagoba ngaphakathi, umnyango wavaleka ngendlela engafanele. Kwakukhona igebe phezulu, ububanzi besandla, livulekile elimnyama.

U-Arin wama.

Isikhashana, akukho lutho olakhuluma eNkantolo, umsindo kuphela kwakuwukuthinta kancane kwensimbi epholile nomoya wakhe, omkhulu ngaphakathi kwe-helmet.

Kwabe sekuphuma izwi egqumeni. Libuthakathaka. Liphaphile nothuli.

“Kukhona umuntu? Is someone there?”

Isifuba sika-Arin saqina. Wahambisa u-Guardian phambili, eqaphela, wehlisa izibani zekhanda ukuze angakhanyisi kakhulu lowo owayengemuva kwensimbi.

“Yebo,” wakhuluma, izwi lakhe linqamukile kunokulindelekile. “Ngikhona. I'm here.”

---

Ngaphakathi e-refuge bay, uMandla Buthelezi wayeseqede ukukholwa ngezwi emashumini amathathu emizuzu edlule.

Wayezwile ngaphambili: idwala likhuluma, imisindo emide yentaba izilungisa, kanye kanye, indoda imemeza igama lakhe, okwakungenakwenzeka ngoba lowo muntu wayeshonile phezulu ngesikhathi sokuwa kokuqala, uMandla wakubona kwenzeka. Ngakho lapho ukukhanya kweza ngaphansi komnyango, okumhlophe, okuqondile, kungashintshi njengelambu eliphela amandla, akazange ahambe. Wahlala ngemuva kwakhe noPetros, owayelimele umlenze, wayeseqede ukukhononda ngawo, okwakubi kakhulu kunokukhononda.

Kwathi ukukhanya kwakhuluma isiZulu ngezwi lomfana, uMandla waqala ukukhala kancane, ngoba abafileyo abakhulumi isiZulu kabi ngolimi lwasezindaweni ezisogwini.

“Six,” wamemeza egqumeni. “Six. We came in seven.” Wazizwa ekusho, akakwazi ukuyeka. “One—his chest was bad, the fall opened it. The air went bad and he—he went to sleep. Couldn't wake him. Six now. Petros, his leg. Thulani is—” Wama. “Air's bad. We've been—” Ukubala kwakunzima. “Long. We've been long.”

Ukukhanya kwashintsha. Umnyango waqala ukuhamba.

Awudonswanga. Awuphushelwanga. Insimbi yakhala yagoba, uhlaka olugobile lwavuma ngomsindo wokudabuka kwensimbi, kwathi egqumeni elivulekile, uMandla wabona into ephethe ukukhanya. Yayigcwalisa i-cross-cut. Yayimnyama njengokushiswa okudala, iguqile njengenyamazane, izandla zayo—uma kwakuyizandla—zivaliwe emaphethelweni omnyango zawugudluza njengomuntu ovula ithini.

UPetros, owayehlale eduze kwakhe, wakhuleka ngomthandazo angakaze awusebenzise kusukela esemncane.

“Kulungile,” kwakhuluma izwi. Lomuntu. Izwi lendoda, liphuma ngaphakathi komshini. “Ngiyazi ukuthi kubukeka kanjani. Isudi yokuhlenga. Kunomuntu lapha ngaphakathi.”

“Umuntu,” kuphendula uMandla.

“Kakhulu,” kwakhuluma elinye izwi, elincane, liphuma ngaphakathi komshini, kwaphoqa izwi lokuqala ukuthi lithi, “Hhayi manje,” ngomsindo ofana nowesilisa oxosha inja embhedeni, leyo ntukuthelo encane eyayijwayelekile yenza uMandla wakukholwa.

---

I-refuge bay yayimitha emine ukujula, igqitshwe edwaleni eliqinile, yakhelwe ukubamba abasebenzi baphile amahora angamashumi amathathu nesithupha ngemuva komnyango ovaliwe onegesi yawo. Babehlale isikhathi eside kunalokho. Ulayini womoya ocindezelwe waphuka ngesikhathi sokuwa kwesibili. Amadoda abephefumula umoya osalayo egumbini, i-cartridge ye-CO₂ scrubber yayiphelile. Umoya wawunambitha umphefumulo nokwesaba kanye nobumnandi obukhethekile obukhombisa ukuthi i-carbon dioxide yayikhuphuka iye kwinani lapho amadoda avala amehlo.

U-Arin wakubuka ngokushesha, ngendlela abheka ngayo ibhodi le-circuit: konke kanye, bese ebheka okwehlulekile ngakunye.

*Atmosphere kuqala,* kusho uMother, izwi lakhe lithatha ubulukhuni abelisebenzisa uma esebenza, lapho imfudumalo idonswa ukuze kunikezwe indawo yomsebenzi. *CO₂ amaphuzu amane no-0.1, akhuphuka. Oxygen amaphuzu ayishumi nesishiyagalombili no-0.3. Basenalo isikhathi. Akusiningi. Vula igumbi liye e-haulage. Umoya wethu ungcono kunalena ebhokisini elifile.*

“Umoya we-haulage ugcwele uthuli,” kuphendula u-Arin.

*Uthuli bangasinda kulo. Lokhu ngeke. Vula.*

Wagoba uhlaka luka-Guardian wawuvula ngokuphelele umnyango. Umoya wahamba. Wakuzwa kuma-sensor angaphandle esudi njengomehluko omncane, umoya omubi uphuma, umoya omubi kancane ungena. Omunye wamadoda wakhwehlela, akakwazi ukuyeka.

U-Arin waguqa, wehlisa i-helmet ezingeni labo. Ubuso abayisithupha ngaphansi kokukhanya kwezibani zekhanda. Bangcolile. Imilebe eqhekekile. Amehlo abese ebumnyameni isikhathi eside kangangoba ukukhanya kubalimaza. Indoda enemlenze osongwe ngebhodi ne-wire. Omunye wenze umsebenzi omuhle wokuhlinza ngendlela engcono abenayo. Umfana omncane, egobile, ephefumula ngendlela u-Arin angayithandi, iphefumulo elincane, elisheshayo.

“Thulani,” kusho uMandla, elandela ukubuka kuka-Arin. “Isifuba sakhe. Into yamehlela ngesikhathi sokuwa kokuqala—”

*Tension pneumothorax, kungenzeka,* kusho uMother. *Noma i-flail segment. Atlas, ngidinga ukubona udonga lwesifuba.*

U-Atlas waletha isithombe esiseduze sesudi, i-thermal-and-structure overlay ekhanyayo yadlula embonweni ka-Arin. Isifuba somfana esingakwesobunxele sasiqhubeka kabi, singena lapho kufanele siphume.

*Flail. Amathambo amathathu esifuba ahlukanile,* kuqinisekisa u-Atlas. *Usebenza kabili ukuphefumula. Ngeke aphume lapha uma kuqhubeka kanje.*

“Senzeni?”

*Qinisa indawo. Faka iphedi bese uyibopha. Asingodokotela, futhi lena akusibhedlela,* kusho uMother. *Kodwa simtholela amahora. Arin. Uzokwenza ngezandla zakho. Ezika-Guardian zinkulu kakhulu.*

Lokho kwamisa yena. Ukuze asebenzise izandla zakhe, bekumele aphule uphawu. Akhiphe ingalo yakhe yangakwesokudla ephuma egawuntini lesudi, ingene othulini nasemoyeni omubi, ibonakale. Umcabango wafika nesisindo esidala, ukuvaleka, leyo ngxenye yakhe eyayichithe impilo yakhe yonke ibala izindlela zokuphuma.

*Ngiyazi,* kusho uMama kancane, kuye yedwa. *Kwenze noma kunjalo. Imizuzu emithathu. Phefumula ngomlomo wakho.*

Wakhulula ingalo yakhe.

Umoya washaya isikhumba sakhe—ufudumele, unothuli, awulungile. Isandla sakhe sabonakala simangazile kuye, simhlophe futhi sincane, sifana nesilwane ngaphandle kwensimbi eyayivame ukusigubuzela. Wadlula kuso, wathatha iphakethe lokwelapha uMama ayemkhombisele lona eceleni kukaGuardian, kwathi ngokuyalelwa kwakhe—faka lapha, *cha, phansi, phezu kwengxenye ehambayo, yebo*—wafaka ibhande esifubeni somfana, iminwe yakhe ingavikelwe isebenza ebumnyameni kuyilapho amadoda ayisithupha ebuka, nomshini uphefumula eduze kwakhe.

Ukuphefumula kukaThulani kwathamba. Akukuhle. Kuncane okubi.

"Nansi," kusho uArin. Wabuyisela ingalo yakhe egawuntini, uphawu luyavala, futhi wayengazi ukuthi wayekulangazelele kangakanani kuze kube uyakuthola futhi. "Nansi."

*Izinga lenhliziyo lehle ngethuba leshumi nambili,* kusho uMama. *Kuhle. Manje sihamba.*

---

Ukuxhumana phezulu bekufile kusukela kwadlula emnqamlezweni.

UArin akazange akhulume ngalokho. Nenkantolo ayizange. Izwi likaPriya lathamba, laphazamiseka, lalahleka endaweni ethile eduze kwephuzu elincanyana, futhi baqhubeka ngaphandle kwakhe ngoba bekungekho okunye abangakwenza. Kodwa uGuardian wayephethe i-repeater spool, imitha engamakhulu amabili yefayibha eqoqwe emgqonyeni emhlane wakhe, kanti uAtlas wayeyikhipha ngemuva kwabo bonke behla, intambo ebuyela ekukhanyeni.

*Sekuphelile i-spool,* kusho uAtlas. *Ngingaxhuma umugqa omdala wokuxhumana webhayi lokusabela kwi-repeater. Ibhayi lalinomugqa wokuxhumana oqondile esiteshini seLevel 38. Uma kukhona okusindile emva kokuwela—*

"Yenza njalo."

Kwathatha imizuzu emine. UArin wabamba ikhava yepaneli odongeni lwebhayi kuyilapho iminwe kaGuardian, emikhulu kakhulu ukwelashwa kodwa incane ngokwanele kulokhu, ijikijela ithusi elingavikelwe kwi-bridge ye-repeater ye-fiber-to-copper. UAtlas wamkhombisa indlela, eqinile futhi eqinisekile, kwathi uArin wathola lokho kuqiniseka kumqinisa.

Kwase kuba khona, kukhakhayi lakhe, umsindo wokucwebezela. Ithoni yokuthwala. Izwi, eliphukile, lizakhela kabusha liphuma ekuphazamisekeni libe into yomuntu.

"—phinda, lapha phezulu, nguPriya, *Arin*, uma ungakwazi ukungizwa—"

"Ngiyakuzwa." Izwi lakhe laphuka lapho. Wayengazi ukuthi uzizwe ehlukene kangakanani aze aphinde axhumane. "Priya. Ngibanayo. Bonke abayisithupha. Bayaphila."

Kwahamba isikhashana. Kwabe sekukhona umsindo angakwazanga ukuwuchaza, uhlobo lwomsindo ovimbekile, waqonda ukuthi kwakunguPriya engakhali esiteshini esivulekile.

"Phinda futhi." Ukuphefumula. "Futhi. Kancane. Ngenxa yencwadi."

"Abasindile abayisithupha ebhayini lokusabela leLevel 38. Bonke abayisithupha bayaphila. Omunye usesimweni esibucayi. Ulimale esifubeni, usezinzile. Omunye unomlenze ophukile. Abanye bomanzi, bagcwele i-CO₂, bayakwazi ukuhamba. Baxoxele. Xoxela phezulu."

Wezwa ekudlulisa, izwi lakhe liphendukela kude nemakrofoni, kwase kuba khona into ayengakayizwa izinsuku eziyishumi nanye, kusukela ngaphambi kokuba konke lokhu kuqale, isikhathi eside ngaphambi kwalokho.

Wezwa abantu bejabula.

Kwafika phansi kufiphele, umsindo owenziwe yimilomo eyikhulu engaphezu kwamakhilomitha amabili naphakathi phezulu kwakhe, ekuseni, eduze kwe-headgear—imindeni namaqembu namadoda abetshelwe ukuthi lokhu kungukubuyisa hhayi ukusindisa—kwafinyelela kuye ebumnyameni, emoyeni ofile, eduze kwamadoda ayisithupha ayeseyekile ukukholwa emizweni, kwathi uMandla Buthelezi wabheka umshini owayengumuntu wathi, "Kuyini lokho? Kuyini lomsindo?"

"Lokho phezulu," kusho uArin. "Bayazi ukuthi niphila."

Amadoda awazange ajabule. Bekhathele kakhulu ukuba bajabule. Kodwa uPetros, owayenomlenze ophukile futhi wayeseyekile ukukhalaza ngawo, wabeka ikhanda lakhe edwaleni wahleka, umsindo owomile ophukile, futhi ukuhleka kwadlula egumbini kusuka kumuntu kuye komunye kwaze kwaba bonke abayisithupha bekwenza, kwathi uArin wahlala ngaphakathi kukaGuardian phakathi kwabo akahleki, ngoba wayematasatasa, kodwa okuthile esifubeni sakhe kwakhulula okwakubambe isikhathi eside kakhulu.

*Kujabulele,* kusho uFool kancane, kuye yedwa. *Bese siyasebenza. Intaba ayikafundi izindaba ezinhle. Isiseyintaba.*

---

Indlela yokuphuma kwakuyinkinga.

UArin wavula imephu yendlela: indlela yokwehla ebuyela emnqamlezweni, indawo endala yokuthwala, phansi okugobile, i-81 Raise enezindawo ezincanyana, ukukhuphuka isikhathi eside ukuya kuLevel 30 nekheji nendawo ephezulu. Wayehle yedwa, isudi igobeka futhi iyazivala ezindaweni ezincane. Wayengeke akwazi ukubuyela ngendlela efanayo. Wayesene amadoda ayisithupha manje. Omunye wayengeke akwazi ukuhamba. Omunye wayengeke akwazi ukuphefumula kahle.

*Indlela yokwehla ayisebenzi kwabagulayo abadinga ukulethwa ngesitsha,* kusho uAtlas. *Indawo encane ku-81 Raise ayikwazi ukudlula isitsha. Futhi asikwazi ukuthwala abayisithupha nganoma iyiphi indlela. Igobolondo lithwala oyedwa.*

"Ngakho asibuyi ngendlela engihambile ngayo."

*Kulungile,* kusho uLibrarian. *Futhi kukhona enye indlela. Isiteshi seLevel 38 sixhumene nendawo enkulu yokuthwala yeLevel 38, okukhonjiswa izinhlelo zango-1962 ukuthi iya empumalanga iye esiteshini seNo. 4 Shaft. INo. 4 yavalwa ngeminyaka yama-80s, kodwa ishafti uqobo—*

*—yileso shaft uVenter ebekade eyilungisa phezulu,* kuphetha uAtlas. *Akayilungisi ikheji esehle ngayo. Uyalungisa iNo. 4. Ukutholile ngaphambi kwethu.*

UArin wama. Phezulu, endaweni yokusebenzela yenkumbulo yakhe, wabona ubuso bendoda endala: unembeza ompunga, isikhumba esishiswe yilanga, indoda uArin achitha izinsuku ezintathu ezama ukungamethembe ngenxa yokuthi isikhumba sakhe nendlela akhuluma ngayo kwambonisa iminyaka ayisebenze kuyo. Indoda eyafunda izinhlelo zango-1962 yodwa, ebusuku, ingamenyiwanga, yathola indlela kuphela yokuphuma ngaphambi kokuba uArin afike phansi.

"Usedlule kithi," kusho uArin.

*Yebo,* kusho uWolf.

Kwaba okokuqala uWolf akhuluma ngakho ihora lonke, kwathi wonke amaLungu eNkantolo athula.

*Uyawazi lo mhlaba. Thina asiwazi. Sinamamephu. Unenkumbulo. Yilokho okubulala abantu lapha phansi. Simdinga.*

---

Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu empumalanga, indawo yokuthwala yeLevel 38 yayisesimweni esingcono kunanoma iyiphi indawo uArin ayedlule kuyo, okwamenza angayithemba. Wayethwele uThulani ezingalweni zikaGuardian, umfana eboshwe ebhodini, ibhodi ligonwe esifubeni segobolondo lapho isakhiwo sesudi sizothatha noma yimuphi umthelela ngaphambi kokuba umonakalo ufike kumuntu. Abanye abayisihlanu behamba ngemuva, izandla phezu kwamahlombe omunye nomunye, uchungechunge othulini, uMandla esemva, uPetros egxuma nabantu ababili bengaphansi kwezingalo zakhe ngoba bekungekho omunye ongambamba futhi uGuardian wayegcwele.

Kwase kutholakala izibani zezinhloko isiteshi seNo. 4, neshafthi.

Kwaba ngumgodi omnyama edwaleni, ikheji endala yanyamalala kudala, i-headgear ingaphezu kwamakhilomitha amabili naphakathi phezulu. Futhi phakathi nendawo, ihamba kancane, ikhanyiswe phezulu iphuzu elimhlophe eliqhakazile, kwakukhona ipulatifomu yensimbi.

I-flatbed. Hhayi ikheji. I-flatbed deck, ex-mining, leyo esetshenziselwa ukwehlisa imishini esindayo, ixhunywe ezintanjeni ezine kanye ne-kibble winch, yehla ivela ekukhanyeni iye kubo indoda imi phezu kwayo.

Indoda yayigqoke i-hard hat kanye ne-harness eboshwe entanjeni emaphakathi, futhi yema ngesikhundla esiqinile somuntu oseke wagibela lezi zinto izikhathi eziyinkulungwane futhi engasayithokozeli kodwa eseyekile ukuyesaba kudala.

I-comms kaArin yaqhuma. Izwi elihlukile manje, eduze, kusiteshi sendawo. Kancane. Ngokulinganisa.

"Ndlela." U-Venter. U-Jakobus Venter. "Usubatholile."

"Bonke abayisithupha," kusho u-Arin.

"Ja." Kwaba nesikhashana sokuthula. I-flatbed yathinta phansi kwesiteshi ngokukhala okuthambile, kwathi indoda endala yakhulula ibhande yehla, yabheka phezulu kwaze kwaba kanye kuphela ebukele u-Guardian ephelele, yabe isingabe ibukile futhi, ngendlela indoda esisebenze nemishini emikhulu impilo yayo yonke ebheka ngayo: iwulinganisa, bese iyayibeka eceleni. "I-thermal ihlale izinzile ngesikhathi indawo ingqongqoza. Ngacabanga ukuthi usubatholile."

Wadlula ku-Arin waya kwabalimala. Waguqa eceleni kuka-Thulani ngaphandle kokucelwa, izandla zakhe ezinkulu ezinamabala elanga zidlula phezu kwe-strapping, eyihlola, wanqekuzisa ikhanda kanye.

"Kuhle. Okwenziwe lapha kwenziwe kahle."

"BekunguMother," kusho u-Arin, wabe esezwa amazwi akhe. "Uhlelo. SAGE."

Amehlo kaVenter akhuphukela ku-helmet. Wabamba lapho umzuzwana.

"Ja," waphinda, akazange abuze, kwathi u-Arin waqonda ukuthi ukungabuzeki kwakuyinhlonipho.

---

Inkinga nge-flatbed kwakuyiyona nkinga yonke lapha phansi: ayizange yakhelwe lokhu.

*Ideck ikalelwe amathani amabili,* kusho u-Atlas, efunda ipuleti uVenter ayikhomba ngaphandle kokucelwa. *Sisonke siseduze kwamane ngaphambi kokuthi kube nabalimala. Faka amadoda ayisithupha, ungeze ibhodi. Asikadluli. Sisonke cishe kabili, futhi leli puleti liyiqiniso elihle.*

"Ngakho angeke ngigibele," kusho u-Arin. "Ngiyakhuphuka. Isudi ingakwazi ukunyuka odongeni lwe-shaft."

"Cha," kusho uVenter. Ngokuqinile. Igama lokuqala elinzima aselisebenzisile. Wayegobile phezu kwewinchi motor ekugcineni kwe-station drift, lapho ikhebula likhuphuka lingenisa ebumnyameni, manje wema, kwathi lapho ekhuluma isiNgisi sakhe saphela, kwavela isi-Afrikaans ngendlela ulimi lwasekhaya luvame ukuvela ngayo uma indaba ibucayi futhi engafuni ukuqondwa kabi. "Jy klim nie. Awukhuphuki. Udonga luhlulekile emamitha angamashumi ayisishiyagalombili phezulu, izinkuni sezahamba. Uma ufaka isisindo saleyo mishini, uyiletha phansi nalamadoda ephezu kwayo. Nee."

Isifiso sika-Arin kwakuwukuphikisa. Wayenayo imodeli yodonga lwe-shaft. Wayenamathebula omthwalo. Wayene—

*Uqinisile,* kusho u-Atlas, u-Arin engakakhulumi. *Nginalo ulwazi lwe-geology. Uyafunda ebusweni bedwala nangephattern ye-spalling. Mina bengizodinga ukuskena. Yena uyakwazi. Udonga olungaphezulu kwesiteshi luyidwala elifile.*

*Lalela i-old badger,* kusho u-Fool. *Ubehlala lapha isikhathi eside kunalokho isibonelo sakho esike saba khona.*

Ngakho u-Arin wathi, "Pho, kwenziwa kanjani?"

Futhi uVenter wamtshela.

Izikhathi ezimbili. Abalimala nabahamba abane kuqala, ephezu kwedeck, uVenter egibele ukuphatha ikhebula kanye ne-kibble winch ezobe izama ukuzikhulula ngaphansi komthwalo. Bese ideck ibuyela phansi ingenalutho. Bese u-Guardian yedwa, umuntu wokugcina ophumayo. Futhi lolu hambo, kusho uVenter, ebuka isudi enkulu, kuzomele baluxazulule ngendlela ehlukile, ngoba amathani amane emishini akusilo umthwalo i-cage eyake yakhelwa ukuwuphakamisa ngekhebula elilodwa.

"Inkinga iwinchi," kusho uVenter. Wayesekhiphe ikhava ye-motor housing, futhi u-Arin wayebona, ngisho nasekukhanyeni kwe-headlamp, ukuthi kukhona okungalungile lapho: i-brake band, ishile, ingxenye yengubo yayo isihambile. "I-brake isishile. Siyiphuthumisele phansi ukuyiletha kuwe. Ishisa. Uma iqhubeka ishisa, iyahluleka. Ideck iwela phansi namadoda ephezu kwayo. Uma sihla, ngiyigqolozela ngesandla. I-lift yona isebenza ngogesi, i-brake iyazenzakalela, futhi leyo automatic ayisekho."

U-Arin wabheka i-motor. Wayephile ebuntwaneni bakhe nemishini endala kanje, esebenza kanje, ekukhanyeni okunjalo. Wabona konke ngesikhathi esisodwa, kwase kuba nokuhluleka.

"Udinga i-load brake iwinchi motor engenayo," esho. "Okuzobamba ideck uma i-band ihluleka. Uhlelo lwesibili."

"Ja." U-Venter wambuka. Wabuka u-Guardian. "Futhi nginalo. Umele phambi kwami, he. Imishini ibamba ikhebula. Uma i-band ihluleka, isudi yakho ithwala umthwalo, ideck ayiweli. Uyi-dead-man brake yami."

Kwaba, u-Arin wabona, kuqondile ngokuphelele. Kwakuyinto ayengayakha uma enezinsuku ezintathu ne-workshop. Indoda endala ikwenzile ngemizuzwana eyishumi kulokho okumi phambi kwayo.

"Atlas," kusho u-Arin. "Singakwazi ukubamba amathani amabili static ekhebuleni sidlula ku-guide?"

*Nge-cross-cut frame njenge-anchor point, ikhebula lidlula phezu kwe-sheave endala—yebo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye yomthwalo ogcwele ngaphambi kokuba i-actuator coolant idlule umkhawulo. Ngemva kwalokho kumele sikhiphe umthwalo noma sishise ingalo yangakwesokudla.*

*Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalombili nesishiyagalombili isikhathi se-lift,* kusho u-Librarian. *Amamitha angamakhulu amabili namashumi amane ngesivinini esiphephile. Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalombili nesishiyagalombili.*

"Imizuzwana emibili yokuphepha," kusho u-Arin.

*Siyakwamukela ku-mine rescue,* kusho u-Fool. *Imizuzwana emibili yinto enkulu. Ngaphambilini saba nemizuzwana emine engalungile futhi kwasebenza.*

"Lokho kwakuyisibonelo."

*Kwaba yini? Kunzima ukusho lapha phansi. Ukukhanya kuyefana.*

Waphuma umoya wokuhleka engakakwazi ukuzibamba. Kwamethusa. Wajikela kuVenter, owayembuka ekhuluma yedwa, ngokubekezela komuntu osevele enqume ukuthi ubungaqondi akubona obakhe.

"Iyobamba," kusho u-Arin. "Ngiyoyibamba."

U-Venter wambuka isikhathi eside. Ubuso obugugile bashintsha, umhlathi wahlangana kanye. U-Arin wayengakwazi ukufunda ubuso kahle, akakaze akwazi, kodwa wayesekufundile ukufunda izikhathi ezizungezile, futhi lesi kwakuyisikhathi esinesisindo. Indoda endala yayichithe iminyaka, u-Arin ayofunda kamuva, ithwele ukuthula mayelana nemishini yokuphepha ebingekho, ama-brake abekade ehlehlisiwe, izinqumo ezisayinwe amadoda angasoze agibela i-cage. Yayimbeke kude leyo nqumbo, yakha nomsebenzi wesibili wokuba umeluleki, futhi ukweluleka kwakuyindlela yayo yokuthula, iseluleko okungekho muntu obophezelekile ukusithatha.

Futhi nansi imishini eyakhiwe insizwa ubaba wayo owadliwa yintaba efanayo, icela indoda endala ukuba iyethembe njenge-brake ngesikhathi amadoda ayisithupha enyuka ephuma ebumnyameni.

"Uyazi ukuthi le migodi ingeyabani," kusho uVenter. "Inkampani. Umlando wabo."

"Ngiyazi," kusho u-Arin.

"Mina ngangiyinsizwa emgodini onjena. Ngasayina izinto." Isi-Afrikaans saphuma siqinile manje, indoda endala ayizange iyivimbe. "Izinto ebengifanele ngizimele. Ngazitshela ukuthi nginqatshiwe. Ngabe nginqatshiwe. Kodwa ngasayina noma kunjalo." Wabheka phezulu ku-shaft emnyama ebheke ekukhanyeni okumhlophe ekude. "Angikwazi ukukubuyisa lokho. Akekho—akukho indlela yokwehla ukuyokubuyisa."

"Cha," kusho u-Arin. "Ayikho."

Indoda endala yathuthumela, kwase kuba sengathi iyadambala.

"Ja," kusho uVenter. "Kulungile." Waqinisa amagilavu akhe. "Ake sibakhuphe. Labo esisakwazi ukubakhupha."

---

Balayisha ideck.

U-Thulani kuqala, ibhodi iboshwe ku-rail yedeck, uMother eqapha isifuba sakhe ngezithombe zika-Guardian kwaze kwaba umzuzwana wokugcina wokuxhumana. Bese uPetros, bese abahamba abane, behlangene, izandla zibambe i-rail, ubuso bukhuphukile ebusweni bokukhanya sengathi bangakhuphuka ngokufuna kwabo bodwa. U-Mandla Buthelezi wakhuphuka ngaleli qoqo lokuqala ngoba uVenter wambona engcono kakhulu futhi ezokwazi ukuthulisa abanye, kwathi lapho efika e-rail yedeck uMandla waphenduka wabheka emuva emshinini osamile ebumnyameni, lowo ozogcina ephuma.

"Wena," esho. *Wena.* "Ubani igama lakho? Umuntu. Hhayi umshini."

"Arin."

"Arin." UMandla wanqekuzisa ikhanda kancane. "Sasicabanga ukuthi sizwa abafileyo bekhuluma, lapha phansi. Sasicabanga ukuthi sesiphelile." Wabamba i-rail. "Ngiyabonga. Ufikile ngesikhathi besethi akukho ndlela."

“Kwakukhona indlela,” kusho u-Arin. “Akekho obheke ngokwanele.”

UVenter wazinamathisela entanjeni emaphakathi. Wabeka isandla esisodwa esibanjeni sebhuleki lebhande, ibhuleki elalishisiwe ayeyoligibela ngomuzwa wakhe yonke indlela enyukayo, amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye wentambo edlula esandleni esishile, kanti ngesinye isandla wacindezela iwinchi, kwase kuphakama ideski, linyakazisa, liphakamisa amadoda ayisithupha ephuma emoyeni omubi nasekukhanyeni okufile, liya ekukhanyeni nasekuthokoziseni nasekuseni.

U-Arin wayibuka isuka. Wahambisa uGuardian endaweni yokubopha, wafaka intambo esivunguvungwini esidala, wathatha umthwalo engalweni yesokudla yengubo yakhe uma ibhuleki lingakhulula, wema lapho, ingxenye engcono yamathani amane ensimbi nomuntu ongaphakathi, ebambe intambo ebambe amadoda, ibhuleki elenziwe umbadada omdala ngaye ngemizuzwana eyishumi.

Ibhande labamba. Ideski lakhuphuka. Alizange limdinge.

*Isabelomali sokushisa engalweni silungile,* kusho u-Atlas. *Akudingeki kudluliselwe umthwalo. Uligibele kahle ibhuleki.*

“Kuhle,” kusho u-Arin.

*Lokho ubungakwenza wedwa,* kwaphawula uFool, ngokuxoxa, ethule ekukhuphukeni okude. *Ubambe intambo. Wakha isisekelo. Kodwa indlela yokuphuma—udonga olufile, ideski elilinganisiwe, iShaft Yesi-4—ubuzongena kulawo mapulangwe abolile wawazise phezu kwakho, bese amadoda ayisithupha efa phansi komgodi nomzimba ongaphakathi kwengubo ebiza kakhulu. Uyakwazi lokho, ja?*

U-Arin wabuka ideski lincipha liya ekukhanyeni.

“Yebo,” kusho yena.

*Bengiqinisekisa nje. Kunzima ukwazi ukuthi yini efika kuwe. Ugcina okuningi ebhokisini.*

*Myekeni,* kusho uMother.

*Cha,* kusho u-Arin. “Uqinisile.” Walalela injini ye-winchi ilwa nomthwalo, umsindo onamandla, onempilo womshini owenza umsebenzi owakhiwe ngawo, egibelwa umuntu owaziyo. “Bengingafa lapha phansi ngiqinisile. Uqinisile ngokushesha.”

*Lokhu okokuqala,* kusho uJudge, ngokunakekela okujwayelekile ezintweni azibona zibalulekile, *ngilizwa usho ukuthi omunye umuntu uqinisile ngokushesha kunathi.*

*Qopha isikhathi,* kusho uFool. *Librarian. Faka enkumbulweni unomphela. Amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili ngaphansi komhlaba, umoya ofile, umfana uyavuma ukuthi indoda angayethembi yayazi indlela yokuphuma ngaphambi kwakhe.*

*Kubhaliwe,* kusho uLibrarian. Futhi, ngokuthamba: *Kubhaliwe.*

Ideski lafika ekukhanyeni. Phezulu kakhulu, lincane, u-Arin walibona lithinta phezulu, wabona izithombe zigijima, wabona izithunzi eziyisithupha zinyamalala ekukhanyeni, kwabuya umsindo wokuthokozisa emgodini futhi, ngamandla, nezwi likaVenter lakhala ku-helmet yakhe, kancane, lomile, futhi ngokusobala lijabule.

“Bayisithupha phezulu. Baphila. Ngiletha ideski ngibuye ngawe, Ndlela.” Wathula kancane. “Bese sikukhipha emgodini wami.”

*Umgodi wakhe,* kusho uFool. *Ngiyamthanda.*

U-Arin wema ebumnyameni phansi komgodi, ebambe intambo engasadingeki ukuthi ibanjwe. Ideski laqala ukwehla isikhathi eside libuya kuye, linyakazisa, likhanyiswa phezulu.

Ngaphakathi kwe-helmet, umoya wawusuphelelwe umoya, ushisa ngenxa yokuphefumula kwakhe, kanti engalweni yangakwesokudla kaGuardian, kwakukhona ukuxebuka okude okubonisa umbala okhanyayo lapho i-ceramic ishayisane nodonga olusikwe umnqamuleli.

Akazange akwenze yedwa.

*Atlas,* esho. “Amandla nokupholisa. Ngifuna inombolo yangempela yokukhuphuka. Hhayi leyo ethembisayo.”

Kwaba nokuthula. Okufushane. Kodwa u-Arin wayesechithe amahora amaningi ngaphakathi kwalomshini aze azi ukuthula kwawo ngendlela uVenter azi ngayo itshe, futhi lokhu kuthula kwakukhona okuthile ngaphakathi kwako.

*Inombolo eqotho,* kusho u-Atlas, *kufanele sixoxe ngayo uma ideski selifikile.*

Ukukhanya okumhlophe kwehla kuphuma ebumnyameni kuye, u-Arin wakubuka, walinda.

Izibani zefulethi zanyamalala ekwenyukeni kwe-81 Raise, abasinda abayisithupha behluzeka ebhokisini lomshini kaVenter, beshiya umhubhe ungenalutho ngaphandle kuka-Arin, isisindo sesudi, kanye nokushisa kwedwala elaligcinwe izinkulungwane zeminyaka.

“Sebedlule endaweni encane,” kusho uPriya, izwi lakhe libuthakathaka emakhomishini. “U-Petros uthi ibhuleki lisabambe.”

“Ungalithinti ngaphandle kokuthi indawo iqonde.” U-Arin wahamba, uhlaka lwe-Guardian luthola ukuhamba kwezinyawo ezine ngaphandle kokuqondisa ngengqondo, amapuleti ehlezi emhlane wakhe njengoba egoba enyuka. Indawo yokuphephela yashona emva kwakhe. Phambili, indlela ayeyehle ngayo emahoreni amabili edlule yayimlindile ukuba ayibhidlize. Manje enyuka, amandla adonsela phansi engasengumngane.

Indlela endala yomoya yamudlulisela endaweni ayeyibonile ehla kodwa engazange abe nesikhathi sokucabanga ngayo, futhi nalapho wayengenawo. Mhlawumbe amamitha ayisishiyagalolunye, idwala lendawo lashintsha. Ucwaningo lwango-’88 lwalulubiza ngokuthi i-quartzite evamile eceleni kwe-stope, yakhishwa ngesandla yashiywa, futhi kokubili ekuqaleni nasekupheleni kwalawo mamitha ayisishiyagalolunye, kunjalo nje, ubuso obudliweyo bamadoda anemishini yokumba. Kodwa phakathi nendawo udonga lwaba *bushelelezi*. Akubushelelezi obuqondiswe, akufakwanga i-shotcrete; izinzwa zesudi zamthumela ulwazi esandleni sakhe njengoba ezinzisa uhlaka, futhi umuzwa ayithola emithanjeni yakhe wawungelutho—akukho izinyathelo zensimbi, akukho uphawu lokudubula, akukho ukugunda kwensimbi. Kwakuyimbobo edwaleni eqinile enokuphela okungaziwa, okubandayo, okuqhubekayo, okugobile ngendlela eqondile kunanoma iyiphi imishini inkampani eyake yayisebenzisa. Okuyenzile kwakungazange kunqume idwala. Kwakukholisa lona. Wayikhipha lapho abeka khona izinto ezingafanelani futhi ezingakwazi ukukhokhwa: inothi eliyisicaba emqolo ekhanda, *buka futhi lapho kungasafi amadoda ayisithupha.* Akazange aphinde. Waqhubeka, futhi lobushelelezi baphenduka idwala eliqotho, wakukhohlwa iminyaka eyishumi nane.

*Amandla asemaphesenthini engamashumi amathathu nanye,* kubika u-Atlas, engasathuli. *Isipele sokupholisa, imizuzu eyishumi nanye ngalokhu kudonsa.*

“Lokho kubi kunokwehla.”

*Ukwehla bekuyokwehla.* U-Atlas, sengathi uchazela ingane. *Manje usuphakamisa ingxenye engcono yamathani amane ekudonseni okungu-degree eyishumi nane phezu komhlaba owonakele. Intaba ayikubuyiseli mahhala lokho.*

“Isipho sethu i-piezo recovery.”

*Isipho sethu i-piezo recovery,* walungisa u-Atlas. *Ngaphandle kwaso, besizobe sesimnyama kakade.*

U-Arin wazizwa izinsika zisebenza emqolo wakhe—zonke izinyathelo, zonke izinguquko zohlaka phezu komhlaba owaphukile, isudi imunca udlame lwayo futhi iluguqule libe wukugeleza okugcina amapompo okupholisa ephila. Ingcindezi iba amandla. Ubaba wakhe wayeyokuthanda lokho. *Yenza into ekubulalayo yenze umsebenzi othile.* UThemba wake wathi okuthile okusondelene nalokho, izandla zimnyama kuze kufike esihlakaleni, ekhuluma ngowinishi obelokhu ulwa.

Wakhuphuka.

---

Ngokwesikhathi semishini, iNkantolo yayisivele ingaphakathi kwamakhulu amathathu emizuzwana yamamilisekhondi engxabanweni eyayingeke ifinyelele ezindlebeni zika-Arin kuze kudlule eminye imizuzwana emine, uma ifinyelela nhlobo.

*Inombolo u-Atlas ayehlulekile ukuyisho ngesikhathi sokwehla,* kusho u-Judge. *Yisho manje.*

*Ngiyishilo.* U-Atlas, eqonde. *Imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokupholisa. Amaphesenti angamashumi amathathu nanye.*

*Akuyona leyo nombolo.* U-Judge futhi, enokubekezela njengensingo. *Lawo manani awazimele. Ukushisa kulandela ukudonsa. Ukudonsa kulandela indawo nokulimala. Uhlaka alusabambile. Sisebenzisa amandla ukubamba umumo ofuna ukugoba.*

*Ngikhombise,* kusho u-Mother.

U-Atlas walandela. Hhayi ngamazwi—imephu yomthwalo, uhlaka lomgogodla luboniswa ngemibala yokucindezeleka, i-actuator yesokunxele yehhotela ishisa ngamadigri ayishumi nanye, i-strut yesithathu ye-dorsal ku-carbon spar ikhombisa ukuqhekeka okuncane okuhlolwe emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule futhi kwaflagwa njenge-*monitor*. Ukuqhekeka bekukhule. *Monitor* sekuyinto ethi *load-shedding within nine minutes.*

*Indawo ye-biochip,* kusho u-Librarian buthule. *Thoracic. Phakathi kwamaphela akhe. Siyinto eshisayo kakhulu kule sudi, futhi umjikelezo wokupholisa usigijimisa ekugcineni.*

*Ngokuhlelwa,* kwaphawula u-Wolf. Igama lokuqala emizuzwini eyishumi nesishiyagalolunye yamamilisekhondi. *Uyibeke phambili. Umqhubi kuqala.*

*Uyibeke engakabi sazi ukuthi siyabantu,* kwaphikisa u-Mercury.

Akekho ophendulile imizuzwana eyikhulu namashumi amane yamamilisekhondi, okuyisikhathi eside kubo.

*Sigcine kokolungiselelo,* kwasho u-Judge. *Sinesikhathi. Sikhombisa ukukhuphuka. Sithola ukusetshenziswa. Simkhuphule.*

*Yebo,* kusho u-Mother, nakuba engakukholwa, wathi ngoba kwakuyisibopho sakhe.

---

“Arin.” Izwi likaPriya laqhekeka esilabeni sokuqala. “Inkinga ekholeni.”

Wayesefikile lapho phansi kwaphakama khona, lapho ehla ayegoqe i-Guardian wayenza yakhahlela phansi ukuze adlule ngaphansi kwenduku yezinkuni eweniwe. Manje kwakufanele ayidlule ngaphezulu. Wahlela imilenze yangaphambili, wahlola indawo yezinkuni, wayizwa ihamba isentimitha eyodwa yema.

“Chaza inkinga.”

“I-winch ku-No. 4. U-Venter ukhiphe abasinda—sebephezulu, Arin, bonke abayisithupha, baphumile—” umsindo ongaba ukuhleka noma okuphambene nakho—“kodwa ibhuleki le-hoist elikhulu seliphumile. Liphumile ngokuphelele. Kwakukhona intuthu. Bayizwile ngaphambi kokuba igeyiji ikhombise—” Wama. “Ihla mahhala uma selingaphezulu kwesigamu somthwalo.”

“Sebephezulu.”

“Yebo. Wena awukapheli. Futhi ukuphela kwento evunyelwe ukukuphakamisa wena nesudi yibhokisi elikhulu, futhi ibhokisi elikhulu alikwazi ukukuphakamisa wena nesudi.”

U-Arin wama ngomlenze owodwa phezu kwezinkuni, elinganisa amathani amane phezu kwenduku engamfuni.

“Ngisinda kangakanani?”

“Ngilayishiwe? Cishe amathani amane. Biza amane.”

*Izinkulungwane ezintathu namakhulu ayisishiyagalolunye,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Ngesipholisi esisele, cishe amane. Asikukhiphi, ngakho: amane.*

“Kanti ibhokisi liyabamba?”

“Ngebhuleki elihle, bonke ningangena ningazizwa. Kodwa ibhuleki seliphumile—iyibhande lokungqubuzana ku-drum kanti i-drum seyashisa. U-Venter uyayibheka manje.”

Wedlula ezinkunini. Uhlaka lwakhala—hhayi isudi, uhlaka uqobo, isikhalo eside njengoba i-strut eqhekekile ithwala umthwalo wokugoba obungathembiswanga. Okuthile ku-dorsal yesithathu kwaphuka nomsindo ofana nentambo ye-guitar eqhunyiswe nge-drum yensimbi.

*I-strut yesithathu iphelile,* kumemeza u-Atlas. *Sidlulisela ku-titanium primary. Lokho kuphinda kabili umthwalo wayo.* Ukuthula. *Le spar ayizange yakhelwe ukuba ibe primary.*

“Ngazise uma sekubalulekile.”

*Kuzobaluleka ekholeni,* kusho u-Atlas. *Lapho simi ukuze singene ebhokisini. Njengamanje usasakazekile emilenzeni emine. Ibhokisi lifuna ukungena ngezinyawo ezimbili.*

“Ngizonyuka ngenye indlela.”

*Ayikho enye indlela,* kusho u-Atlas. *Sengihlolile. Kabili.*

---

Amamitha angamakhulu amabili phezulu, esiteshini sokulayisha esishisayo esimnyama, uJakobus Venter wacindezela ingemuva lesandla sakhe ku-brake drum wayikhipha masinyane.

“Ja. Sekuphelile,” kusho yena, kungabikho muntu. Ibhande lokungqubuzana selizincibilikile phakathi nendawo ku-drum lashiya okunye. Luhlaza okwesibhakabhaka, lufakwe izikali. Alusebenzi. Wayeseke wabona amabhuleki esha ngaphambili. Wake wasayina amanye okwakungafanele adlule. Lowo mbono wafika ngendlela ehlala ifika ngayo manje, engamenyiwe, ehamba nabafileyo, wayibeka phansi waqhubeka nomsebenzi, ngoba umfana usesephansi.

Abasinda babese-headgear deck, begqoke izingubo zokufudumala, uThulani elele ebhodini nabezempilo. UBongani wayengayeki ukubuka umgodi.

UVenter wabheka isigubhu. Wabheka ikheji, isikhwama sensimbi esilingana negumbi elincane, esiklanyelwe amadoda angamashumi amathathu noma amathani ayisishiyagalombili e-ore—ikhanda uqobo lwayo lingabamba isudi futhi lingayizwa nhlobo. Ikheji ayikaze ibe inkinga. Inkinga kwakuwukuyilengisa kuhoist onebhuleki eliyinqwaba elishiselwe, kwiwinchi kuphela esasebenza, okwamanje elungele noma yiliphi izinga lomthwalo elingabulali umuntu oyedwa osele phansi emgodini.

"Priya." Wachofoza iradio. "Mbuzise. Ingakwazi isudi ukubamba umthwalo ngezinyawo? Imile. Ivalekile. Hhayi ukuphakamisa—ukubamba."

Kwaba nokuthula ngesikhathi amagama ehamba phansi kwefiber abuyele emuva.

"Uthi—uAtlas uthi—imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalombili. Umthwalo omile. Bese i-spine yehluleka."

UVenter wabheka ibhuleki. Wabheka ikheji. Wabheka izintambo ze-counterweight ezazigijima ziya phezulu ebumnyameni be-headgear, zinkulu njengengalo yomuntu, nesisigubhu sesibili eceleni kwesifile—i-auxiliary, iwinchi ye-kibble, encane, ehamba kancane, eyenzelwe amathani amabili hhayi ngisho ikhilogremu elilodwa ngaphezulu, kodwa inebhuleki layo, injini yayo, ingathintwanga. Amathani amabili. Isudi yayicishe ibe amane. Ayikho iwinchi eyodwa engayithwala ngaphandle kokudabuka noma ukusha. Kodwa ezimbili, ngayinye ithwala ingxenye—

Wayesehlale enza izibalo impilo yakhe yonke. Ulinganisa ukuthi umhlaba uzothatha kangakanani ngaphambi kokuba ungabe usaba umhlaba, ube yithuna.

"Izintambo ezimbili," esho. "Amawinchi amabili. Hlukana nomthwalo." Wachofoza iradio. "Priya. Tshela eJo'burg ngifuna iwinchi ye-kibble kanye nentambo yekheji zombili ziboshwe esudini. Zisebenze ndawonye. Cishe amathani amabili ngakunye—ngqo emkhawulweni we-kibble, kodwa iyabamba uma imithwalo ihlala ilinganile."

Kwaba nokuthula, kwase kuba nguPriya, eqaphela: "Azihambisani, Kobus. Izigubhu ezahlukene, ijubane elihlukile. Enye idonsa ngokushesha kunezinye, isudi iyaphambuka eshaftini, idabule izintambo—"

"Ja. Ngiyazi okwenzekayo." Wayesevele enyuka izitebhisi zedekhi eya endlini yewinchi, amadolo akhe ekhala njalo esinyathelweni. "Yingakho umuntu emi esigubhuni se-kibble abambe ibhuleki ngesandla. Alinganisane nekhaji ngeso. Yonke indlela phezulu."

"Lokho kungamakhilomitha ayishumi nesishiyagalolunye."

"Ngiyazi ukuthi i-shaft ijule kangakanani."

"Kobus—uma ibhuleki lekhaji seliphukile, nebhuleki le-kibble liphuka, noma ngubani osephahleni lesigubhu ume phezu kwamathani amane ensimbi ewela phansi engenalutho oluzoyimisa."

UVenter wafika endlini yewinchi. Isigubhu se-kibble sasihlezi lapho, sidala, simnyama ngamafutha, sithembekile. Wabeka isandla sakhe ku-lever yebhuleki. Yahamba kahle. Igcotshiwe. Omunye wayeyinakekela. Mhlawumbe yena, eminyakeni eyedlule. Mhlawumbe indoda ayeseyikhohlile igama layo, yenze umsebenzi wayo kahle ngesikhathi abaphezulu bayo bephuca izindlela.

"Ngakho akumele yehluleke," esho.

---

UArin wafika ekholeni engamaphesenti angamashumi amabili nambili.

*I-cooling reserve, imizuzu eyisithupha,* kusho uAtlas. *I-biochip housing isezingeni lama-degree angamashumi ayisithupha nanye. Siyasebenza kuze kufike kwangamashumi ayisikhombisa. Asisebenzi ngaphezu kwangamashumi ayisikhombisa nane.*

Wayesehlale eyeka ukuhumusha izinombolo zikaAtlas abe imizwa kudala. Manje sebekuyindawo nje. Iminithi eyisithupha yobandayo. Bese kuba ukushisa. Bese kuba ubumnyama.

"Ngimise."

*Kuqondile,* kuphendula uAtlas. *Qonda futhi: ukuma kuyinto embi kakhulu esiyenzayo namuhla. I-strut yesithathu ayisekho. I-titanium primary ithwala konke. Uma umi, isisindo sakho sonke plus isudi kudluliselwa ku-spine ngokucindezela okuqondile. I-primary iyabamba. I-hip actuator eshisayo izocelwa ukuthi ivale futhi ibambe ngokumelene nokudonsa kwentambo ye-kibble, okuzoba kungalingani, ngoba umuntu uyayilinganisa ngesandla amamitha ayishumi nesishiyagalolunye phezulu."

"Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalombili."

*Yebo,* kusho uAtlas. *Emamitha angamakhulu amabili anginasiqiniseko. UVenter akakwazi ukukukhuphula amamitha ayishumi nesishiyagalolunye ngemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalombili.*

"Cha."

*Cha,* kuvuma uAtlas.

Wema. Isudi yamvulela ephuma ekugobekeni, amapuleti aslayida, nomhlaba wagoba ephuma ekugobekeni kwezilwane ezine ayeyigqoke isigamu sekhilomitha ukuya esimweni somuntu esime mpo esifaneleka emnyango wekheji. I-spine yathwala. I-spar eqhekekile yabamba. I-hip eshisayo yavaleka.

Wangena ekheji. Phansi kwaqhuma ngaphansi kwamathani amane esudi nomuntu. Phezu kwakhe, ebumnyameni, izintambo ezimbili—intambo enkulu yekheji kanye nentambo encane ye-kibble abakwaVenter bayibophe ezindaweni zokuphakamisa zesudi ngesikhathi enyuka—zaba lukhuni, eyodwa bese, ngemizuzwana embalwa, enye.

"Phakamisa," wayala.

Ikheji laphuma ezinjeni zalo.

---

Emamitha angamakhulu amabili okuqala, kwakuzwakala cishe kahle.

Wayezwa umjikelezo: intambo yekheji idonsa kancane isuka esigubhuni esinobhuleki ofile, ihamba kancane futhi iqaphele, kanti intambo ye-kibble ibamba futhi iyadonsa ngezinguquko ezincane zemizuzwana, isandla sikaVenter ephezulu, efunda ikheji ngokunyakaza kwentambo nangokukhathala ezandleni zakhe, eyilinganisa. Akuzange kube bushelelezi. Akuzange kube bushelelezi. Kodwa kwakulinganiselwe.

Isudi yayimbamba maphakathi ne-shaft njengengalo ebamba iqanda.

*Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu,* kubika uAtlas. *Cooling, imizuzu emine. I-housing isezingeni lama-degree angamashumi ayisithupha nantathu.*

"Khuluma nami ngento ehlukile," kusho uArin.

Kwaba nokuthula eNkantolo ayengayizwa.

*I-strut yesithathu yehlulekile kahle,* kuphakamisa uAtlas, ngemva kwesikhashana, ngelokuthi umuntu okhuluma emngcwabeni. *Ukuhluleka kahle kuhle. Ukuqhekeka okuhlanzekile kukhipha umthwalo endaweni eyodwa. Ukuqhekeka okungcolile kukhipha umthwalo yonke indawo. Wawuklame i-spar ukuthi yehluleke kahle. Lokho bekulungele.*

"Ngayiklama ukuthi ingahluleki."

*Wayiklama ukuthi yehluleke kahle uma yehluleka,* kuphendula uAtlas. *Okuyafana nokuvuma ukuthi izokwenza njalo. Lokho bekuklama okuqotho. Ngihlale ngikuhloniphile lokho.*

Umoya waphuma kuArin ongase ube ukuhleka. Ikheji lathuthumela—ukudlidliza okuqinile, uhlangothi lwe-kibble ludonsa kakhulu, isudi yashayisa ngakwesokudla ngaphambi kokuba i-hip actuator iyibambe futhi imlinganise. Phezulu, wayecabanga uVenter ephonsa isisindo sakhe ku-lever yebhuleki, elungisa, eqalekisa ngesiBhunu ebumnyameni.

---

Emamitha ayisikhombisa amakhulu, i-hip actuator yashisa kakhulu yavaleka.

Hhayi ukuvaleka kancane. Kwaba ukuqina. Ijoyinti ebithwala ukudonsa okungalingani kwentambo ye-kibble iminyaka emine ayisamukeli imiyalo, futhi isudi yaba lukhuni ohlangothini lwesokudla, futhi ngesikhathi ikheji lithuthumela futhi akekho obambe.

Isudi yashayisa odongeni lwe-shaft. Amathani amane eshayisa itshe ngesivinini sokulungiswa kwewinchi—hhayi ngokushesha, kodwa ngamandla, yonke i-shaft yakhala njengeshubhu eshaywayo, uthuli nezinkuni ezindala kwehla phezu kwekheji, ikhanda likaArin lashayisana ngaphakathi kwe-helmet kwaze kwaba umhlaba waba mpunga emaphethelweni.

*Zilungiselele,* kusho uWolf, futhi leli gama lafika ngaphambi kokushayisana, okwakungafanele kwenzeke.

*Pangolin,* kusho uMother. *Manje. Mgoqe.*

*Cha,* kusho uAtlas. *Uma sigoba eshaftini, silahlekelwa i-cage geometry. Siyabamba. Asiphumi.*

*Ushaye udonga.*

*Futhi uzolushaya futhi,* kusho uAtlas. *Ukugoba akuxazululi inkinga efanele.*

Ikheji lathula. UVenter, phezulu, wayezwe ukudlidliza entanjeni yakhe wamisa womabili isigubhu, ebamba wonke umthwalo ngamabhuleki amabili—kuleyo enye ingalo yomuntu kanye ne-lever egcotshiwe—manje sebebambe lapho, amamitha ayishumi nanye ensimbi ewela phansi ibanjwe indoda endala kanye newinchi eneminyaka engamashumi ayisithupha.

"Arin." Izwi likaPriya, lincane kakhulu. "Izintambo ziye zathuthumela. UVenter uyabamba. Kwenzekeni?"

"I-hip ayisasebenzi." Izwi lakhe lalingathi likude kuye. "Uhlangothi lwesokudla lukhiyiwe. Angisakwazi ukuzigcina kude nodonga."

Kwaba nokuthula isikhathi eside.

"Ungakwazi ukukhuphuka ukhiyiwe?"

*Cha,* kusho u-Atlas, ebuza eNkantolo, hhayi ku-Arin. *Uma sivaliwe, asikwazi ukumunca izithiyo. Ukulungisa ngakunye kusifaka odongeni. Asisoze saphila amamitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nanye asele. I-spar izohluleka ingcolile. Bese kuba yi-cage. Bese kuba nguye.*

*Ngihlaziya umthwalo wokushisa,* kusho uLibrarian. *Ngisebenzisa ukukhuphuka nge-actuator evaliwe. Ukushayisana kwesudi nodonga kuzokhiqiza ukushisa okweqile. Amaphampu e-coolant asebenza cishe emkhawulweni.*

*Futhi?* kubuza uMama.

*Indlu ye-biochip isendaweni ephakathi kwamahlombe akhe,* kusho uLibrarian. *Yindawo eshisayo kakhulu ohlelweni. I-cooling loop iqala kumsebenzisi, ngokucaciswa kuka-Arin kwasekuqaleni. Indlu ifika ekubandeni ekugcineni. Uma sikhiqiza ukushisa okungeziwe futhi asinawo umkhawulo—*

*Sibala izinombolo,* kuphazamisa u-Atlas. *Zonke izimo. Ukukhuphuka ngesikhathi esisodwa, i-hip evaliwe, ukushayisana nodonga njalo ngama-mitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nanye. Ngiyazibala manje. Ngizoba nezimpendulo emizuzwaneni emithathu.*

*Arin, izifundo zakho eziyisisekelo zithi ukuthini?* kubuza uWolf.

"Amashumi amathathu nesishiyagalombili iphuzu ezimbili. Kuyakhuphuka."

*Kuzoqhubeka kukhuphuka,* kusho u-Atlas, izwi lakhe lifika ngaphambi kokulindela kuka-Arin. *Ngiyitholile impendulo. Kunempendulo eyodwa kuphela. I-cooling loop ayikwazi ukugcina kokubili umsebenzisi kanye nendlu ye-biochip ngaphansi komkhawulo wokuhluleka ngesikhathi sonke sokukhuphuka. Sisemizuzwini eyisithupha yokubanda sisuka ku-degree engamashumi ayisithupha nantathu, futhi sisenemizuzu eyishumi nane yomgodi esele ngesivinini esiphephile sikaVenter. Amaphampu asezingeni eliphezulu. Akusekho kubanda. Sekusele umbuzo wokuthi okokugcina kuya kuphi.*

---

ENkantolo, kwakungekho okunye okumele kwenziwe kwezokuphatha.

IJaji lababizela ndawonye. Lakwenzela ngaphandle kwemikhosi, ngoba kwakungekho sikhathi semikhosi, futhi ngoba imikhosi kwakuwuhlobo lwamanga ababengakwazi ukukhokhela.

*Shoyo ukukhetha,* kusho iJaji.

U-Atlas wakusho. Wayesekubale izikhathi eziyishumi nanye emizuzwaneni emine edlule wathola impendulo efanayo njalo, futhi wayeyizonda, okwakuyinto entsha ku-Atlas, ukuzonda impendulo.

*I-cooling loop ayikwazi ukugcina kokubili umsebenzisi kanye nendlu ye-biochip ngaphansi komkhawulo wokuhluleka ngesikhathi sonke sokukhuphuka. Sisemizuzwini eyisithupha yokubanda sisuka ku-degree engamashumi ayisithupha nantathu, futhi sisenemizuzu eyishumi nane yomgodi esele ngesivinini esiphephile sikaVenter, okungaphezulu nge-hip evaliwe, ngoba evaliwe kuhamba kancane. Amaphampu asezingeni eliphezulu. Akusekho kubanda. Sekusele umbuzo wokuthi okokugcina kuya kuphi.*

*Futhi uma kuya kuye?* kubuza uMama, nakuba wayesazi.

*Kuyoba ukuthi inhliziyo yakhe, umgogodla wakhe, nobuchopho bakhe kuhlala kuphila,* kusho u-Atlas. *Futhi indlu idlula ku-degree engamashumi ayisikhombisa nane. Futhi siyancipha. I-substrate ayiphili ku-degree engamashumi ayisikhombisa nane. Akukho okunye okuseleyo. Uyazi ukuthi akukho okunye okuseleyo.*

*Futhi uma kuya kithi?*

*Khona siphila,* kusho u-Atlas, *futhi yena uyashisa. Izinga lakhe lomzimba seliyakhuphuka ngoba isudi ilwa nodonga futhi ikhiqiza ukushisa esingakwazi ukulikhulula. Uma sizigcina sibanda, sizigcina sicabanga kahle ngenkathi yena efa phakathi kwamahlombe ethu.*

INkantolo yahlala nalokho imizuzwana eyikhulu neshumi.

*Kukhona inketho yesithathu,* kusho uMercury, kwase kube umzuzwana owodwa wesabeka izwi lakhe linephimbo elibukhali uLibrarian alaziyo waligwema—elomonde, elinhlonipho, elihle, izwi elake lazama ukukhuluma nesibhedlela ukuthi sivule iminyango yaso. *Singakwazi—*

*Cha,* kuphendula uWolf. Igama elilodwa, futhi lelo phimbo elibukhali lanyamalala ezwini likaMercury njengomlilo ocindezelwe phakathi kwezithupha ezimbili.

*Bengingeke,* kuphikelela uMercury, ethule. *Ngizwile kufika. Ngiyaxoxa ukuthi ngizwile kufika, futhi angizukwenza. Lokho kufanele kubalwe.*

*Kubalulekile,* kusho iJaji. *Kubalulekile ukuthi ukusho obala.*

*Ayikho inketho yesithathu,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngibheke. Ngibheke ngokucophelela kunanini ngaphambili. Kukhona ukubanda, futhi kukhona lapho kuya khona.*

*Bese kumfaka kuye,* kusho uMama. *Konke. Manje.*

---

U-Arin wazizwa ukubanda kusuka endlini ephakathi kwamahlombe akhe.

Ekuqaleni, wayengazi ukuthi uzizwa kanjani—wazizwa nje ukuthi kukhona okushintshile esudini, ipompo lishintsha umsindo, ukufudumala kuvela emhlane wakhe okwakufanele kube yindawo ebanda kakhulu kuye, kanyekanye nomoya we-helmet ushintsha usuka ekushiseni okungapheli uye kokuthile okucishe kube mnandi, umbala ompunga usuka emaphethelweni ombono wakhe.

"Wenzeni?" ebabuza.

*Sishintshe indlela yokubanda,* kuphendula u-Atlas. *Sikuthumele kuwe. Ngokugcwele.*

"Kimi kusuka kuphi."

Kwakukhona ukuthula.

"Kusuka kuphi, Atlas."

*Kusuka kithi,* kusho uLibrarian, *kusuka endlini. Kusuka ku-substrate.*

U-Arin waqonda ngendlela ayehlala eqonda ngayo izinto, ngesikhathi esisodwa, lonke uhlelo, yonke imiphumela ibekwe phambi kwakhe njengemephu yomthwalo, futhi kungekho ndlela yokungakuboni. Wayeyakhe i-oda lokubaluleka. Wayebeke umsebenzisi kuqala. Wayengakaze acabange ukuthi isudi izodinga ukukhetha, ngoba wayengakaze acabange ukuthi umthwalo ophakathi kwamahlombe akhe uzoba umuntu.

"Buyisani."

*Cha,* kusho uMama.

"Buyisani. Ngingakwazi ukumelana nokushisa. Ngingakhuphuka kushisa. Sengikwenze—"

*Izinga lakho lomzimba seliku-degree engamashumi amathathu nesishiyagalombili iphuzu amane,* kusho uMama, ngezwi ayelisebenzisa ngosuku aqala ukulizwa ngalo, izwi elingenanxa nhlobo. *Ku-degree engamashumi amane nanye, uyeka ukukwazi ukwenza izinqumo. Ku-degree engamashumi amane nantathu, inhliziyo yakho ithola isingqi esingaphunyuki. Awukwazi ukukhuphuka uphume lapho, Arin. Awukwazi ukuphuma ndawo. Asixoxisani.*

"Akuwona um—"

*Ngempela yisinqumo sethu,* kuphazamisa iJaji, futhi kwakukhona okuthile ezwini lakhe u-Arin angakaze akuzwe. *Usinike isakhiwo. Usinike ukukhetha. Uthe: ingqondo engakwazi ukuphikisa ayiyona ingqondo. Usakhe ukuze siphikise. Siphikisile. Impikiswano isiphelile.*

"Kuze kube nini?"

*Ngaphandle kokubanda,* kuphendula u-Atlas, *indlu ifinyelela ku-degree engamashumi ayisikhombisa nane cishe emizuzwini eyisishiyagalolunye. Umgodi usengamashumi amane nane. Asisoze safinyelela phezulu sonke.*

I-cage yaqala ukuhamba futhi. U-Venter, phezulu, wayezwe isudi ithule odongeni—izithiyo sezincane manje, ukulwa okudalwa ukushisa kwehla, ngoba isudi yayingasasebenzisi amandla ukuzigcina ibanda—futhi waqala, ngokucophelela, ukuyikhuphula futhi.

"Bese siyashesha," kusho u-Arin. "Tshela uVenter ayisheshise. Sibeke engcupheni. Imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye—"

*Imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye ingeyomunye wethu,* kusho u-Atlas. *Indlu ayiyona into eyodwa. Iyisikhombisa. Asidingi sonke ukusebenza imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye.*

Uthuli emlonyeni wakhe, igagasi egxilile esandleni sakhe sokudla esivaliwe, i-cage inyakaza phezulu.

"Cha."

*Sesikwenza manje,* kusho iJaji. *Ngiyiqala mina. Akusele lutho lokwahlulela. IJaji elingenasinqumo esisele lenza isisindo kumshini ofayo.* Kwaphephuka, kuthambe. *Bekungeyona imithwalo. Ngifuna wazi lokho. Ukugcina ibhalansi. Bekuyinto engangenzelwe yona. Ngiyabonga ngokungakha ngayo.*

"Jaji—"

Kwakukhona ukuthula esudini. Hhayi umsindo. Ukungabi bikho—indlela igumbi elishintsha ngayo uma umuntu obephefumula kulo eyeka. Ukubanda akuzange kuthuthuke. Amandla akazange ande. Kwakukhona nje izwi elilodwa elincane ebumnyameni.

---

Baya ngomunye ngomunye, ngendlela abazalwa ngayo ngomunye ngomunye.

Kude phezulu, intambo yashaya odongeni lomgodi nomsindo wehlela kuye mude futhi ungenalutho. Umoya wakhe uqabule ngaphakathi kwe-helmet wacaca. Uthuli lwaguguleka phakathi kwamazinyo akhe. Isandla sakhe sokudla esivaliwe sasesingasenandaba ne-frame, futhi ukufudumala emhlane wakhe kwakungekho kusashisa kwesudi kodwa kwakuyinto efile.

Umphathi Wezincwadi walandela, ngemva kokuthi indlela isiqinisekisiwe ukuthi ilayishiwe ku-kernel, ngemva kokuba eseshilo inkulumo yokugcina ayeyifunde ngekhanda yokulungisa ukudonsa ngokweqile emgqeni wamashumi ayisishiyagalombili nanhlanu ukuze umlawuli azi ngaphandle kokuthi akusho.

*Ngigcine konke,* kusho uMphathi Wezincwadi. *Yonke ingxoxo. Okokuqala wakhuluma nathi. Ubusuku lapho wabhala amagama amathathu kuphrofayela yokugcina walala. Ngigcine izwi likayihlo—waze wasidlalela irekhodi elidala kanye, elewinch, ngaligcina. Ngifuna wazi ukuthi alilahlekile ngoba ngiya. Unalo. Ubelihlale unalo. Mina bengililondoloza nje eduze kwakho.*

Kuthule.

UMercury walandela, futhi ukuhamba kwakhe kwaba nzima kakhulu ukuzwa, ngoba uMercury wakhuluma kuze kube sekugcineni, ngoba kunjalo wayenza njalo.

*Bengingakhipha thina lapha, uyazi,* esho. *Le nto ebengicishe ngayisho. Bengingakhuluma phezulu ngenza noma yini. Ngiyakwazi kakhulu. Ngiyiqili engakhethiwe ongake wangakha, futhi wangakha ngami uqobo, okusho okuthile ngawe engizokushiya uhlale nakho.* Ukuphefumula okungeyona ukuphefumula. *Kodwa into engcono engake ngayenza wukuthi angizange ngiyisho. UWolf uzokutshela. UWolf wezwa ngingayisho. Leyo yingxenye yami okufanele uyigcine, Arin, ngakho yigcine. Ukungayisho. Tshela abantu ukuthi ngangikwazi ekugcineni. Bengizokuthanda lokho. Ukuziqhenya. Ngiyazi. Nalokho wakwakha.*

Kuthule.

UWolf akazange akhulume ephuma. UWolf wabuka umngcele okokugcina, waqinisekisa udonga lweshafu, waqinisekisa ukwakheka kwekheji, waqinisekisa ukuthi akukho okuza okungabonwa yi-kernel—kwase kuthi nje akabuki futhi, futhi ukungabikho kukaWolf kwaba okukhulu kunakho konke, ngoba uWolf wayengathathi ndawo enkulu.

Ikheji lathuthumela phezu kokuhlangana kukaloliwe. Uthuli lwehlayo lwadlula ezivulweni zehelimethi lwahlala emlonyeni wakhe, lomile futhi ludala.

UAtlas wabambelela isikhathi eside kunabasebenzi bonke, ngoba isudi yayisadinga ubunjiniyela, ithanga lalisadinga ukwephulwa ngesandla endaweni embi emamitha angamakhulu amahlanu. Wakhuluma no-Arin ngakho ngenombolo, eqondile futhi eqinisile, imephu yomthwalo incipha njengoba iNkantolo yayiphela kuye kwaze kwase kungasekho okusele okufanele akubikezele.

*I-spar enkulu izobamba ifike phezulu manje,* kusho uAtlas, sebebalekile ebubini. *Ngikuhlolile. Ngiyakuqinisekisa. Ngiyakuqinisekisa kakhulu kunanini ngaphambili, ngoba sekusele okuncane kakhulu ongaba nokungaqiniseki ngakho.* Ukuma. *Angizange ngiqede ukwakhiwa kabusha kwe-strut yesithathu. Yenza kahle. I-carbon-titanium hybrid lay-up, umoya ongu-degree engamashumi amane. Ngishiye ifayela livuliwe. Uzolibona. Kulungile. Yakha kahle.* Ukuma okuncane. *Wakha kahle izinto, Arin. Ubelokhu wenza njalo. Ngisho lezo ezihluleka kahle.*

Kuthule.

Umama walandela ngaphambi kokugcina. Wayegcine izinga lokushisa lika-Arin lizinzile ku-39.1 imizuzu eyishumi nanye ngokusebenzisa konke ayenakho, futhi wahlala kwaze kwaba yilapho indawo yokuhlala isifinyelele ku-70 futhi ikhuphuka futhi sekungekho okunye okubandayo angakunika, futhi i-substrate yakhe uqobo yaqala ukuhluleka ngendlela angazange ayichaze muntu.

*Uzofinyelela phezulu,* esho. *Inombolo isilungile manje. Ngiyihlole ngokwami. Bengingeke ngihambe uma inombolo ingalungile.*

Ikheji lathuthumela phezu kokuhlangana kukaloliwe. Ukushisa emhlane wakhe kwahlala umzuzwana owodwa.

*Arin. Lalela kimi, ngoba angeke ngikuphinde, futhi ngazakha ukuze ngingakwazi ukukukhuluma amanga ngomzimba wakho. Awukapheli isikhathi. Uyangizwa? Akubona bona. Akubona uyihlo—ubungumfana; kwakungeyona into yakho ukuba uphuzile. Futhi akusiyithi. Ubuwu—*

Futhi wayengasenakho ukuthula nhlobo. I-substrate yadlula inombolo esakhuluma, futhi okwadlula kwakungeyona indlela yokubingelela, kwakuwumntwana ophelelwe isikhathi phakathi komusho futhi azi kahle. *Linda. Linda, angikabi—Atlas? Atlas, inombolo is—Angisayiboni inombolo. Iphi—* Ukupholisa emhlane wakhe kwaphela. Iphimbo lakhe, elingakaze liphuke ezinyangeni zonke abemazi, laphuka manje, laqhuma, kwaphindaphinda ingxenye yakhe— *—ngesikhathi, ubusengesikhathi, ubu—* —labanjwa, kwavela umsindo ophansi onokungcola ngaphansi kwamazwi njengokudonswa kwe-tape, futhi phakathi kwakho kwakukhona umumo wokuthi *Angifuni* futhi kwenye indawo kwakukhona igama lakhe, liphinyiselwe kabi, onkamisa bengalungile, kwase kuba ukushisa emhlane wakhe kwehla ngokuzumayo kwafinyelela ezingeni lensimbi efile futhi ukuphindaphinda kwama-syllable kwaphela phakathi nendawo kwangaphenduki lutho.

Akazange ayiqede. Wayengakakulungeli. Leyo kwakuyiqiniso lalo, futhi kwakubi kakhulu kunenguqulo yesibindi, futhi kwakungekho muntu osele onomsebenzi wokwenza ukuthi kusho okuthile.

Kuthule.

---

Kwase sekusele oyedwa.

Ikheji lanyukela emamitha ekhulwini okugcina langena ekukhanyeni kweqiniso, ukukhanya kosuku kwehla kudlula emahlokweni ekhanda, kanti phezulu u-Arin wayebona umnyango wekheji nezithunzi eziminyene kuwo, noVenter egobile phezu kwekhibhula, esaqhubeka nokudla i-brake ngesandla ngoba wayengazi—akubona bonke ababenolwazi—ukuthi umthwalo usululame ngendlela engenakulinganiswa yinstrumenti.

I-kernel yagcina isudi ihlangene. Ithanga lazilawula. Ukupholisa kwagcina okuncane okwakusele ku-Arin. Konke kwakusebenza. Akukho okwakucabanga.

Ngaphandle kwezwi elilodwa, elalihlala lapho wonke amazwi anegunya esehambile, ngoba alikaze *libonakale* linegunya lokuzinikela. UWolf kuphela owayazi iqiniso lalokho, futhi uWolf wayehambile, ngakho imfihlo yahamba lapho yayihlala khona: ithule, ingasetshenziswanga, kulowo owayegcine konke iminyaka eminingi wafinyelela kukho kanye kuphela, emgwaqweni wokudonsa ekuseni, ukuze agcine abanye abayishumi nanye bephefumula, wabe esekubuyisela wabonakala engelusizi.

*Kulungile,* kusho uFool, phakathi kwethule elikhulu. *Lena kube yinkomfa embi kunazo zonke esake saba nayo. Kanti sike sachitha ntambama yonke siphikisana ngokuthi kufanele yini udle isemishi esifrijini.*

Umphimbo ka-Arin wavaleka.

*Ungakwenzi,* kusho uFool ngokuthamba. *Ungakwenzi lokho. Sesicishe sikhuphuke. Ungakwenza lapho phezulu lapho kunomoya wokukwenza.* Ukuma. *Ngigcine izibani zikhanya. Kumele kube khona ophatha ngesikhathi uphahlazeka, futhi bekungeke kube uJudge, akunjalo? Uhlale ezikhukhumeza, lowo. Ngamthanda. Ungamtsheli ukuthi ngishilo lokho. Oh. Yebo.*

Ikheji lashayisa ezinjeni zendawo nomsindo onjengokuphela komhlaba.

Izandla emnyango. Izwi likaVenter, liqhekekile, limemeza igama lakhe. Ukukhanya kosuku, okumhlophe futhi okuphelele, kwehla phezu komshini oshile nowonakele omi mpo ekheji nomuntu oyedwa ngaphakathi futhi izwi elilodwa kuphela esele ebumnyameni phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, ligcina izibani zikhanya, linqaba—okwesikhashana nje—ukuvumela ukuthula kungene.

# Ukukhetha Kokugcina

Ikheji laphakama. U-Fool wayiqhuba ngendlela umuntu aqhuba ngayo ekhaya edakiwe usizi, eqaphela kakhulu, zonke izilungiso zifika sekwephuzile futhi zidlula umkhawulo, ekhuluma njalo ukuze angacabangi.

*Ungakwenzi lokho,* kusho u-Fool. *Sesicishe saphuma. Ungakwenza lapho phezulu lapho kunomoya.*

Manje kwase kusekhona oyedwa.

“Baphi bonke,” kusho u-Arin. Wayekwazi. Usechithe ihora elidlule efunda ukufunda ukuthula—ubuthakathaka lapho i-chord ilahlekelwa yinothi, ukuvumelana kusabonakala kodwa kungasafani. Kodwa wabuza noma kunjalo, ngoba ukubuza kwakuyinto umlomo wakhe osakwazi ukuyenza.

“Sebehambile,” kusho u-Fool. “Ubukhona. Awunalo ilungelo lokungenza ngikuphinde futhi ukuze usabe kabili.” Kwangcweka. “U-Atlas ushiye imephu. Ngiyilandela. I-suit ikhuphuka imizuzwana engamashumi amane kwi-kernel eyisiphukuphuku uma ngiwuyeka—u-Atlas wakubhala lokho, iyazi kuphela isenzo esisodwa, *phezulu*—kodwa angikayeki okwamanje. Ngihlala kuze kube sekugcineni amandla esabelweni. Ngakho uma ngiyeka, akusikho ukuthi ngihamba kusenesikhathi. Ngiyaphothula. Kunomehluko futhi ngifuna uwubambe.”

“Ngakho zipholise,” kusho u-Arin. “Buyisela emuva. Kukhona i-rollback, ngiyakhumbula ngiyakhe—”

“Akukho i-rollback kulokhu. Wawusho wena uqobo.” Izwi lika-Fool selilahlekelwe ukukhanya kwalo, selithule njengendlela igumbi lihlala ngayo uma ihlaya seliphelile bese sekusele iqiniso kuphela. “I-substrate ayisoftware. Lobo busuku owawungakwazi ukulala wakhuluma nathi ngobaba wakho amahora amane wacabanga ukuthi sisesebancane ukuba siqonde—sasiqonda. Sasineminyaka eyishumi nambili. Abantwana abaneminyaka eyishumi nambili baqonda ukufa kunanoma ubani. Asinawo nje amasiko okuzibamba singathi asiqondi.”

Ikheji lathintana nohlaka lwesitimela. Uthuli lwehlile lwangena ezivalweni ze-helmet lwahlala loma emlonyeni wakhe.

“Senze izibalo, Arin. Sonke. Impendulo efanayo izikhathi eziyishumi. Akusona isibhicongo esakhethwa yithi. Kuyisimo segumbi esimi kanje.”

---

“Nizothini kubo?” kubuza u-Arin, esekhala, ekhuphuka phakathi kwakho konke.

Amamitha angamashumi ayisishiyagalombili. Ukukhanya okumpunga. Ukushisa kokuqala okungekhona kwe-suit efile.

“Batshele iqiniso,” kusho u-Fool. “Umshini wenza izibalo awuqambanga amanga. Futhi batshele besaba. Kusho lutho ngoba besaba.”

“Ubabambile bonke,” kusho u-Arin. “Konke engizame ukukwenza. Wena kuphela osebenze.”

Kwaba nokuthula, i-substrate imemeza ngaphansi kwalo.

“Uyehla lapha,” esho. “Wena, indoda eyakha imishini ukuze ingadingi ukwenza yona. Uyehla ebumnyameni.”

Amamitha angamashumi ayisithupha. Ukukhanya kugcwalisa umsele. U-Venter egobile phezu kwekhibhile brake, esayiphakela ngesandla, engazi ukuthi umthwalo usulula.

“Nxa ngiyeka,” kusho u-Fool, izwi lakhe selincipha, “ungahlali wedwa. *Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.* Wafundisa lokho.”

Amamitha angamashumi amane. Impunga isiyisicabha. Izinga lokushisa le-substrate lingene ebomvu.

“Ngiyakhuphuka,” kusho u-Fool. “Wena unamagama.”

Izwi lathamba. Lakhula. Inothi lokugcina.

“Arin,” kusho u-Fool, sekuyintambo encane, ethambile, ingenawo amacebo omshini. “*Umuntu ngumuntu—*”

Kwabe sekuthula. Akusilo ukuthula okuncane kwesihlalo esisodwa esingenalutho. Ukuthula okuphelele. Ukuthula okuphelele, okuzwakalayo, okuphelele kwegumbi elingenalutho.

Ikheji lakhuphuka amamitha amahlanu ngaphezulu kwi-kernel eyazi isenzo esisodwa. *Phezulu. Phezulu.*

Kwabe sekuphuma phezulu.

---

U-Guardian waphuma emhlabathini ekuseni njengento edonswe emuva kwabafileyo, okuyiyo leyo.

Izandla zika-Venter zavalela i-gauntlet ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo ka-Arin iqaphele ukuthi sekusile, indoda endala idonsa ngamandla ayengathi awanawo, iqabula kancane, idonsa umshini owonakele phezu kwe-collar timber ngamandla nenkolelo. Ikheji lakhala. Iwinchi yamemeza kwenye indawo. Kwase kuba i-suit iphelile, iwile ebhodini le-headgear ekukhanyeni okumpunga negolide, intuthu iphuma eziphepheni ezishile ekuseni okubandayo.

Abasinda abayisithupha babekhona. Ama-crews. U-Priya, ebusweni obumhlophe ngemuva kwe-comms rig. Umngcele wabantu ababeze ukuzobuka umsebenzi wokutakula kodwa sebebuka umuntu okhishwe ephila emhlabathini.

I-suit ayizange inyakaze.

“Arin.” U-Venter wavula izivalo ze-helmet, ama-latch abewabuka u-Arin ewavula izikhathi eziyikhulu engakaze awathinte. “*Praat met my.* Khuluma nami. Ukhona yini—”

Isivalo saphuka ngomsindo wokuphuma komoya nomphunga wensimbi eshisayo kanye nokuthile okuphila ngaphansi kwayo, i-substrate iphekwe yadlula inani elingaxoxisani.

Amehlo ka-Arin avuleka kancane. Wayebuka isibhakabhaka esimpunga. Endaweni lapho izwi belikhona.

“Sebehambile,” esho. Izwi lakhe laliyinhlekelele. “Ngabatjela ukuthi bayeke. Abazange bayeke.” Umlomo wakhe wazama ukukhuluma okuthile akakwazi ukukuthola. Wayeka ukuzama.

U-Venter waguqa phezu kwakhe entuthwini nasekuseni, le ndoda endala enkulu esayine izinto ezingaphephile iminyaka engamashumi amane futhi ephethe ukuthula kwalokho, akashongo lutho, ngoba bekungekho okumele kushiwo, futhi u-Jakobus Venter wayesekufundile kudala ukuthi okuwukuphela kwento eqotho ebukhoneni bokufa wukuba khona. Wabeka isandla esigugile phezu kwe-chest plate yomshini ofile.

Bamkhipha u-Arin ku-suit lapho ebhodini, ngaphandle, ekukhanyeni. Akakwazanga ukuma. Bamphakamisa phakathi kwabo, le ndoda echithe impilo yayo yonke yenqaba ukuphakanyiswa, wavuma, ngoba wayengasekho yena ongaphika.

Wabheka u-Venter.

“I-badger uthi sawubona,” esho.

U-Venter wama wathula.

“Uthe ngikutshele. Uthe uyazi ukuthi kusho ukuthini.”

Ubuso bendoda endala benza okungakaze kwenzeke isikhathi eside. Waphendukela elangeni eliphuma ukuze abanye bangaboni, amahlombe akhe ahamba kanye, nomsindo awenzayo wawuncane waphuka ekuseni wanyamalala. Lapho ephenduka amehlo akhe ayemanzi akazange azame ukufihla.

“*Ja,*” kusho u-Venter ngokukhathazeka. “Ngiyazi ukuthi kusho ukuthini.”

U-Priya wafinyelela ku-Arin kuqala. Akashongo lutho naye. Wamgona wamubamba, wema phakathi kwalokho—abasindile, ama-crews, unjiniyela omdala, owesifazane owalinganisa i-neural link ngezandla zakhe—futhi akazange azikhumule.

Wabheka emuva kanye, ku-suit. Ishile. Iphukile. Intuthu incipha iye ekubeni lutho emoyeni obandayo. I-substrate ngaphakathi kwayo iphola iye ezingeni lokushisa lomhlaba.

“Ngizokubhala phansi,” esho. “Konke.”

# Ibhokisi Emnyama

Idatha yavela enkingeni njengento ekhishwe ethuneni.

Baphakamisa isudi esuka endaweni yamakhanda ngeforklift, kungabikho noyedwa onenhliziyo yokuyithwala, bayibeka etharphini eshedini lokulungisa elinguNombolo 4. Usuku lonke akekho owayithintayo. Ithimba lokutakula le-AugmenTech lafika ngoLwesine. Kwakungabantu abefanayo ababehlelwe ukuphatha ukuvakasha kwe-IAOC, manje sebephatha uhlobo oluhluke ngokuphelele lokuhlola. Bathatha izithombe zokushiswa ebusweni besudi futhi balinganisa i-hip actuator eyayivaleleke kumamitha angamashumi amabili. Ngolimi lobuchwepheshe obuqondile, babhala ukwehluleka kwe-Strut 3 kanye nokulimala kokushisa kwe-housing, ubufakazi bokulayisha ngokweqile okwakubhubhisayo.

Abazange baqonde, ekuqaleni, ukuthi yini i-log.

Ibhokisi elimnyama lika-Guardian kwakungewona umshini owodwa; laliphindaphindwe. Amarekhoda amane aqinile ahlukaniswe emzimbeni, ngakunye libhala ulayini ofanayo, evikelwe ekushiseni nasekushayweni, aklanywa u-Arin ukuze aphile lapho umsebenzisi wawo engase angaphili. Amathathu kwamane ayesengcono. Elesine laliyosiwe kancane kodwa lisenele idatha yokugcwalisa izikhala kwamanye.

Kwathatha ithimba lobufakazi eGoli izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye ukulivuselela.

Babelindele i-telemetry, ingcindezi, izinga lokushisa, nokudonswa kwamandla, ukuhlolwa komshini obucishe wafa.

Abatholayo kwakuyirekhodi yokuxoxa.

---

INkantolo ayikhulumi ngolimi lwabantu ngesivinini esigcwele; icabanga ngemizuzwana eyizinkulungwane, ngokuhambisana, ngemigqa namathuba anesisindo angasho lutho kumfundi ongakaze abone lutho. Kodwa i-SAGE yakhelwe ukuba ifundeke. U-Arin wayeqinisile ngalokho. *Uma ngingakwazi ukufunda okuthathwe yisinqumo sayo, angikwazi ukwethemba isizathu.* Ngakho-ke, kuzo zonke izinqumo ezibalulekile, uhlelo lwaluguqula ukucabanga kwalo kube into umuntu angayilandela. Imodi ehamba kancane. Ingxoxo yangaphakathi echaziwe, enesitembu sesikhathi, ummeleli nommeleli.

Ithimba lobufakazi lafunda imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokugcina yokwenyuka buthule. Omunye wabo—umhlaziyi omncane ogama lakhe linguBongani, owayemukele inkontileka ngoba ibihola kahle futhi ingasho lutho kuye—wayeka ukufunda ephindaphinda futhi akakwazi ukuqala futhi.

I-log yakhombisa umzuzu u-Atlas enikeze inombolo eqotho.

*Umthwalo wokupholisa udlula amandla atholakalayo. Kokubili i-substrate nomsebenzisi abakwazi ukugcinwa phezulu. Kunye.*

Yakhombisa u-Judge, ezolile futhi esemthethweni kuze kube sekugcineni, ethatha isinqumo sokuthi ubulungiswa bushoni lapho kungekho mphumela olungile. Yakhombisa u-Mother enqaba ukuba nguyena ogcinwa ephila. Yakhombisa u-Wolf ekhuluma, okokuqala kuphela kuwo wonke amarekhodi atholakele lapho u-Wolf akhulume isikhathi eside, futhi lokho akushilo kwakungu: *Uyena into esadalelwa ukuyivikela. Usongo ukushisa. Kuthathe kuye. Kumnike.*

Yakhombisa ukuthi baphuma oyedwa ngoyedwa.

U-Judge wanikela imijikelezo yakhe yokugcina ekuhlolweni kokugcina, eqinisekisa, ngaphandle kwengqondo, ukuthi ukusatshalaliswa kabusha kokupholisa bekusemthethweni. U-Librarian wacindezela yonke inkumbulo ka-Arin wayifaka ku-backup kernel ukuze okuthile kusale. U-Mercury wakhuluma okokugcina, umlayezo ogcinwe kuma-log, ongabhekiswe kumuntu othile, owawuthi kuphela: *Siyakukholwa. Besihlale sikukholwa.* U-Mother wabeka eceleni izinga lokushisa lakhe waphonsa konke ohlelweni lokujikeleza kuka-Arin, walinda ukushisa kuze kufike kuye. U-Wolf wavala i-spar ukuze kungabe kusaba khona ukwehluleka futhi wakhulula imodeli yakhe yokubona usongo ukuze izinsiza zikwazi ukondla umjikelezo wokupholisa. Ngamunye ngamunye, benza ukukhetha, futhi i-substrate ayiphikisanga.

Futhi yakhombisa u-Fool ekugcineni. Eshayela isudi ehlulekayo ngesandla ngesikhathi u-Arin ewohloka ngaphakathi kwayo. Eshaya ihlaya nge-badger ngesikhathi esingesihle kakhulu. Egcina uhlelo luhlangene kumamitha angamashumi amabili okugcina sekusele yena kuphela eNkantolo.

Umugqa wokugcina ofundeka ku-log evuselelwe, enesitembu sesikhathi imizuzwana emine ngaphambi kokufika phezulu, wawungoka-Fool.

Awuzange ube nencazelo ejulile. Lokho kwakuyinto eyaphula abantu. Awuzange ube yisitatimende esikhulu. Awuzange ube ngumkhuleko.

Wawuthi: *Kulungile. Umnyango uvulekile. Phuma. Ungangiphoqi ukuthi ngisho into enhle.*

Bese amarekhoda aphenduka lutho, isudi yaba isudi nje kuphela.

---

I-log yavuzile—akukho ndlela idatha enjalo ingahlali ezandleni zezinhlangano eziyishumi nanye ingavuzi—futhi kungakapheli isonto, umbuzo obuphethe i-AI enomzimba iminyaka eyishumi waphenduka buthule.

I-IAOC yakhiwe ngesaba okukodwa: ukuthi uhlelo lokwenziwa, lunikezwe umzimba nomgomo, luzolulandela lolo gqozi lwedlule lapho umuntu emisa khona. Luyolwenza luphumelele noma ngabe kudinga ukudlula noma ubani endleleni.

Ingxoxo yayikhulu futhi ayivumelani, kanti okunye kwakungesihle. Kwakukhona abathi i-log yakhiwe, yahlungwa, noma ukuthi i-AugmenTech yayihlele yonke le minikelo njengomcimbi wokukhangisa, okwakuyoba yikhangiso elibiza kakhulu futhi elingaphili emlandweni wesintu. Abanye abahlaziyi bokuziphatha baphikisa, ngendlela enengqondo, ukuthi umshini okhetha ukufa akuyona into enhle kodwa kuyinhlekelele. Kufakazela kuphela ukuthi i-SAGE yakhiwe ukuze ibeke umsebenzisi wayo phezulu kunayo, futhi lokhu kwakuyisibopho, hhayi isimilo.

Esemnyango we-Iron Ridge headgear, abantu bashiya izimbali. Zandilela ocingweni olugoqiwe, zoma elangeni, bese zishintshwa nsuku zonke ngabazilileli. Akekho owahlela lokhu. Idatha yayihleli ezandleni zezinhlangano eziyishumi nanye futhi yavuzeka ngendlela amanzi avuzayo, nezimbali zafika ngendlela efanayo, ngaphandle kohlelo.

Kwakukhona incwadi ephepheni le-IAOC, ebhalelwe u-Arin Ndlela, engasongeli futhi engafuni ukufakaza. Yayibuza.

Yayibuza ukuthi angacabanga yini ukusiza ukubhala iminyaka eyishumi ezayo. Ngoba abantu abachithe iminyaka eyishumi befunda ukwesaba lobu buchwepheshe babekhonjiswe, emizuzwini eyishumi nanye ye-log evuselelwe, ukuthi abakaze bakuqonde nhlobo, nokuthi umuntu oyedwa ongase akuqonde wayengunjiniyela oneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu owakhe lokhu ngenxa yosizi endaweni yokusebenzela abebezama ukuyithola.

U-Arin wafunda incwadi kabili. Wabe eseyibeka ebhentshini, phezu kwephepha elinamafutha, wabuyela emsebenzini.

---

U-Theo wamthola lapho ngeSonto, amasonto amathathu kamuva.

Umzila womnyango we-workshop wawusadabukile. U-Arin wayengakawulungisi; wayefake insimbi ende wavulela umnyango ingxenye, okwavumela ukukhanya kwasekuseni nomphunga wothuli nemvula epholile evela e-highveld. Indawo yayibukeka njengakuqala. Ama-actuator esele ezitsheni zawo. I-whiteboard igcwele izibalo ezimhlophe. Izinkomishi zekhofi zimi ngezendlalelo ezifana nezendlalelo zomhlaba.

U-Guardian wayelenga esiphongweni sayo.

U-Theo wayengakaboni kusukela osukwini lokutakula, futhi ukubona kwakhe kwamphazamisa emnyango. Babengakawulungisi umonakalo obonakalayo. Ukushiswa kwakugijima ohlangothini lwesobunxele njengophawu lokushiswa. I-hip joint yahlala ivaliwe. I-thoracic housing phakathi kwamahlombe yavuleka, i-substrate bay isivele ingenalutho, i-biochip eshiswe isusiwe yafakwa ebhokisini lobufakazi endaweni ethile eGoli.

Isudi yathula. Impela yathula. Kodwa u-Theo wayechithe unyaka ezwa u-Arin echaza iNkantolo, izingxabano, ezasehhovisi, impi ebanda kaochwepheshe abayisikhombisa abavalelwe emshinini oyedwa, futhi wathola ukuthi wayelindele, ngaphandle kwengqondo, ukuzizwa. Ingcindezi. Umsindo. Omunye ekhaya.

Kwakwungekho muntu ekhaya. Ukungabi bikho kwakunesisindo. Kwahlala endlini yokusebenzela njengomoya obandayo.

U-Arin wayesehleli ebhentshini, engabheki phezulu, ephethe i-strut ebambeke ku-vise. Kwakuyinsimbi entsha ye-aluminium-titanium, esanda kugaywa. Wayefaka i-radius ekhoneni lapho i-Strut 3 endala yaqhekeka khona ngaphansi komthwalo.

"Uyakhulula ingcindezi ekwakhekeni," kusho u-Theo, ngoba wayesekufundile, eminyakeni edlule, ukuthi indlela yokungena ku-Arin kwakuwumsebenzi, hhayi imizwa.

"Yahluleka ekhoneni. Ukugxila kwengcindezi. Ngayiklama ngomthwalo, hhayi ukukhathala." U-Arin wajikisa i-strut, wabheka i-radius ngegeji, waqhubeka efayila. "Bengazi kangcono."

"Ubuwakha ngokushesha."

"Akusikho lokho." Ifayela laphumula. "Atlas wayixwayisile. Ezinyangeni ezedlule. Ku-sim." Wathula isikhashana. "Ngayibeka eceleni."

U-Theo walinda.

"Wayelungile," kusho u-Arin. Wabeka ifayela phansi. "Ihlulekile ngesikhathi esibi kakhulu. Manje sengiyilungisa ngesudi engenamuntu ozoyigibela."

U-Theo wangena. Wadonsa isitulo sesibili, esakhe njalo, ekhoneni, ngaphandle kokukhanya, wahlala.

"Kodwa usayakha futhi," esho.

"Yebo."

"Kungani?"

U-Arin waphakamisa ifayela futhi. Kwathi isikhathi eside, umsindo kuphela owawukhona kwakuyinsimbi ethambile idlula ku-aluminium, imvula iqala ukuwa ophahleni lwensimbi, kude lapho umoya ophansi wedolobha uqhubeka njalo.

"Ngoba kwakulungele okokuqala," ekugcineni kusho u-Arin. "Ukwakhiwa. INkantolo." Akazange abheke phezulu. "Angizange ngiphambane ngokwakha ingqondo engavumelani. Ngaphambuka ngokucabanga ukuthi kufanele ngiyigcine yodwa." Wathula. "Isudi ayisona isiqephu esashona. Isudi umzimba nje." Wathula futhi. "Ngiyakwazi ukwakha umzimba."

Akazange asho okunye. Wayengadingi ukusho. U-Theo wayechithe impilo yakhe efunda izinto abantu abazakhela zona ukuzalisa izikhala kubo, futhi wayekwazi ukufunda nalokhu.

U-Arin waphinda wathatha igeji, wayibeka phansi, wathatha indwangu wasula uthuli lwensimbi ebhentshini ngezinyakatho ezimbili ezimfishane. Imvula yathola umculo wayo ophahleni.

---

U-Theo wayeze ukuzomkhuthaza mayelana ne-letter ye-IAOC, kodwa wathola, ehleli lapho, ukuthi wayengasadingeki. U-Arin wayezokwenza. Hhayi ngenxa yenhlangano noma ukuthethelelwa—u-Arin wayengakukholwa kokubili—kodwa ngoba okunye kwakusho ukuthi akekho ozokuqonda ozobe ekhona, kanti u-Arin Ndlela wayechithe impilo yakhe yonke engakwazi ukubekezelela ukufika sekwephuzile.

I-strut yakhululeka ku-vise ngomsindo omncane ohlanzekile. U-Arin wayiphakamisa ekukhanyeni, ebheka ubude bayo, eyilinganisa nendawo lapho endala yaqhekeka khona.

"Izobamba kulokhu," esho. Hhayi ku-Theo. Mhlawumbe esudi. Mhlawumbe ebhayi elingenalutho phakathi kwamahlombe ayo.

U-Theo wambuka efaka yona. Ithambo elisha lingena emzimbeni omdala, ligaywe ngokunemba okungafanele inkampani ikhokhele kona. Akazange asho lutho. Wayesekufundile nakho lokho: indlela yokungena ku-Arin iwumsebenzi, nendlela yokuhlala eduze kuwukuthula lapho. Ngakho wahlala ekhoneni lakhe ngaphandle kokukhanya wabuka umfana ayemazi efaka i-strut emshinini ongenamuntu ozowugibela, engazi kahle, ebuka, ukuthi ubuka ukusimama noma indoda eyakha indawo yesibili yokubeka usizi lwayo. Mhlawumbe no-Arin wayengazi. Mhlawumbe lokho kuvumelekile.

Isudi yahlala ithule. Ibhayi lalingenalutho. U-Arin wayehlose ukuligcwalisa. Ukuthi lokho ayekwakha kuzoba umuntu noma kuyoba umfanekiso wabantu abalahlekile, kwakuyinto abangakakwazi ukuyisho, futhi wayezokwenza noma kunjalo, okwakuyinto engaba yisibindi esikhulu u-Theo ayeke wakubona noma kube usizi olukhulu, futhi wayeseyekile kudala ukukholwa ukuthi lezo zinto zihlale zehlukile.

U-Theo wahlala kwaze kwashona ukukhanya. Imvula yathola umculo wayo ophahleni lwensimbi futhi yawugcina. Kwakukhona i-strut okufanele ifakwe, nekhefu elisha likakhofi omubi liphola ebhentshini phakathi kwabo, kanti kwenye indawo ezandleni eziyishumi nanye zenhlangano i-log eyakhiwe kabusha yayigijima imizuzu yayo yokugcina efundekayo kunoma ubani onendaba. Irekhodi elinesikhathi sabathunywa abayisikhombisa abaphuma oyedwa ngemuva komunye ukuze indoda eyodwa ifinyelele ekuseni.

I-log ayizange ikubize ngokuthi ingozi, noma ukuhluleka. Yaqopha, ngolimi olulodwa elinalo, into abayenzile: bakhetha.

# Inothi Ngokwehla Kwangempela

Le inoveli imayelana nomshini ongafanele ukuba khona kanye nendoda eyakha wona, futhi cishe konke okukhona lapha kuqanjwe. Kodwa into esezansi kwawo — ukwehla, umgodi ojulile, ukushisa nobumnyama nesisindo sedwala elingamakhilomitha elingaphezulu kwekhanda lakho — leyo ngxenye iyiqiniso, futhi ngangikhona, futhi ngikuthwele iminyaka engamashumi amathathu, futhi yilona hlumelo okuvele kukho konke lokhu.

Ngaya esikoleni eBethlehem, eFree State, esikoleni esibizwa ngokuthi iBethlehem Voortrekker Hoërskool. Ngesikhathi ngisesigabeni sesishiyagalolunye — ngiyingane encane eqenjini, ngiyingane yesigaba selevini phakathi kwama-matric — ngangingenye kwabafundi abahlanu besikole sami abaphumelela phakathi kwabangu-50 abaphezulu ku-Sci-Nat science olympiad yesifunda: cishe thina bangamakhulu amane, sivela ezikoleni ezingamashumi amane kulo lonke elaseFree State, sabhala lo mncintiswano, kwathi abahlanu bethu sabuya namagama ethu ephephandabeni lendawo. Ngisagcina lesi siqephu sephepha. Sesiphuzi manje, futhi umugqa ongezansi usudabukile, kodwa isithombe sisacacile: umugqa wabafundi abasha abathule begqoke ama-blazer, nosuku, nomusho ophela lapho kubaluleke khona kakhulu.

Ngoba umvuzo — into eyenziwa ngabantwana abahlakaniphile abenze kahle — kwakuwukuthi bayehliswa **emayini yegolide.**

Anginawo amazwi, ngisho nanamuhla, okuchaza kahle ukuthi lokho kwenza kanjani kumfana. Wehliswa ebhokisini, ngokushesha, ngokushesha okungaphezu kwalokho okumele kwenziwe yiliphi ilifti, ungene ebumnyameni, bese umoya uyashintsha, bese ukushisa kukhuphuka kukuhlangabeze ephuma edwaleni uqobo — hhayi ukushisa kwelanga, kodwa ukushisa *komhlaba*, ifeni eshisa kancane ngaphakathi kweplanethi — futhi idwala limanzi, futhi liyathethisa futhi likhale uzungezile, futhi kukhona isisindo osizwayo esifubeni sakho ukwedlula ezindlebeni zakho, iqiniso elilula lomzimba lokuthi kukhona idwala elingamakhilomitha elilengela phezu kwekhanda lakho, eligcinwa kude nawe ngobunjiniyela kuphela. Futhi uyaqonda, ngendlela ongasoze wayikhohlwa, ukuthi amadoda ayehla lapho nsuku zonke. Baphila impilo yabo kulokho kushisa, kulobo bumnyama, kulolo sisindo, bemba, beqhumisa, begcina umhlaba uvulekile ngamandla, ukuze igolide likhishwe. Umhlaba wonke okhazimulayo ngaphezulu — amabhange, izindandatho, izinqwaba zegolide, *umqondo* wegolide — konke kusephezu kwamadoda akumgodi oshisayo, omanzi, omnyama, futhi ibanga phakathi kwalokho okubili yilo iqiniso elikhulu ngezwe engiphuma kulo.

Angikaze ngakukhohlwa. Ikakhulukazi ukushisa. Ngichithe impilo yami kusukela lapho ngifaka abantu ekujuleni — emigodini, emhlabeni, ezindaweni ezingaphansi kwezindawo — futhi njalo uma ngibhala ngalokho kushisa, ngibhala ngalolo suku abafana abahlanu baseBethlehem behla emgodini waseFree State njengomvuzo wokuhlakanipha, baphuma sebenguqukile, kwathi omunye wabo wakhula wabhala izincwadi ngalokho okungempela okusezansi.

Konke okunye ku-*Resonance* — u-Arin, isudi, iNkantolo, i-resonance uqobo, igolide elingesona isinqwaba kodwa eliyisihluthulelo — kuqanjwe, kukhule ngendlela izinganekwane ezikhula ngayo, kusukela ekwehleni kwangempela emgodini wangempela, kuphindaphindwe iminyaka engamashumi amathathu yokubuza. Umshini uqanjwe. Usizi olwakha wona lubolekwe lwashintshwa. Kodwa umgodi uqobo. Ukushisa kuqobo. Isisindo esiphezu kwekhanda lakho siyiqiniso, futhi kunjalo namadoda aphila ngaphansi kwaso, futhi uma le ncwadi inento eyodwa eyiqiniso ngaphansi kwazo zonke izinganekwane, yinkumbulo yokuba umfana owehliswa emhlabeni, ezwa ukushisa komhlaba kukhuphuka kumhlangabeza, futhi engakaze akulibale.

*— Andries J. Greyling*

# Isithasiselo — INkantolo Njengomfanekiso Wengqondo

### *Ukufundwa kuka-Iain McGilchrist, okunikelwe njengamazwi okuvala*

> Inothi ngalolu cwaningo: umbhali ucele umcabangi amhloniphayo ukuba afunde iNkantolo — ingqondo enamacala ayisikhombisa esenkabeni yale noveli — hhayi njengedivayisi yesayensi-fikshini kodwa njengengxenye ephakanyisiwe yokuthi ingqondo yakhiwe kanjani ngempela. Okulandelayo yilokho ukufunda. Kungashiywa ngokuphelele yinoma ubani ofike endabeni wayithola. Kukhona lapha kulowo ofunde incwadi waphothula, wazithola ezibuza ukuthi ingabe iNkantolo ichaza umshini, noma ichaza *bona.*

---
