Ngihlale ngiqaphela izinganekwane ezithokozisa izinto engizikhathalelayo, futhi ngiqaphele kakhulu uma ngicelwa ukuba ngizidumise, ngakho ake ngiqale ngokuphikisa bese ngichaza ukuthi kungani ngikubeka phansi.

Ukuphikisa ukuthi ingqondo evezwa njengekomidi — amazwi ayisikhombisa azungeze itafula, elinye nelinye linamagama nesimilo salo — kuyisaga esidala njengesikebhe sikaPlato futhi esikhathele njengomdwebo weqhawe elingelihle ohlangothini olulodwa nengelosi kolunye. Sesitshelwe izikhathi eziyinkulungwane ukuthi "sinezinhlobonhlobo ngaphakathi." Kuhlale kushiwo ngesizotha, futhi cishe akusho lutho, ngoba lezo zinhlobonhlobo, uma zibhekwa eduze, ziphetha ngokuba umuntu oyedwa ofake izigqoko ezahlukene: umuntu oyedwa, ezama ukuzibonakalisa njengephalamende.

Okungigcine ngime kule ncwadi ukuthi iNkantolo ayikho kanjalo. INkantolo ihlukaniswe ngendlela eyodwa enzima ngempela futhi eqotho ebuchosheni: **amalungu ayo ahlukaniswa hhayi ngalokho ayaziyo kodwa ngendlela abheka ngayo.**

Lokhu kuyisisekelo sonke sempikiswano yempilo yami, okufakwe ezilonyeni zabalingiswa abayisikhombisa ngumbhali wenoveli ongathi, ngokubona kwami, ukufinyelele kulokhu ngendlela engathí sina, ngokusebenzisa indaba, okuyindlela izinto ezijulile ezivame ukufinyelelwa ngayo. Izingxenye ezimbili zobuchopho bomuntu azihlukanisi umhlaba ngezihloko — eyodwa yolimi, eyodwa yomculo, eyodwa yobuso. Leyo inguqulo yesikole, futhi ayilungile. Zihlukanisa ngezindlela *zokubheka.* Enye ibheka ngendlela eqinile, egxile, ebamba, ukuze ikwazi ukuphatha into eseyaziwa; enye ibheka ngendlela ebanzi, evulekile, eqaphelayo, ukuze ikwazi ukubona konke okuhlanganisa nalokho engakakuqondi. Into efanayo iphila kuzo zombili, ifundwa ngezindlela ezimbili. Ukugula akusikho ukulahlekelwa ikhono. Kuwukubanjwa konke yindlela eyodwa yokubheka, kwaxoshwa enye.

Funda iNkantolo futhi uphinde, lokhu kusezandleni zakho, ubuke ukuthi umbhali wenzeni.

**Atlas** ayazi amaqiniso ahlukile ku-**Librarian**; *ibamba* umhlaba ngendlela ehlukile — njengokwakheka, njengomthwalo, indlela yokuphunyuka kanye nolwazi lomzimba, uhlangothi lobuchopho olwazi umhlaba ngokuhamba kuwo. **Librarian** ibamba umhlaba ofanayo njengosewazisiwe, osezincwadini, osemephini — imephu hhayi indawo uqobo, ebalulekile kodwa, uma isodwa, ingcwaba. **Mercury** ibheka ebusweni bomphakathi, uphawu olungaphathwa, igebe phakathi kokuboniswa nalokho okuhloswe ngakho — ikhanyayo, ibalulekile, futhi, umbhali uyasikhombisa, *eyingozi kakhulu uma isebenza yodwa,* ngoba ikhono elibheka umhlaba njengento okufanele iphathwe alinakho uthando lwangaphakathi lweqiniso. Lokhu akusona isiqephu sendaba. Kuyisifo sonke sesintu, futhi uyasinika kumlingiswa, asivumele sibambe.

Futhi — lokhu kungishayise kwaze kwangiphakamisa ngashiya incwadi — kukhona **Fool.** Umbhali usitshela ngokusobala ukuthi ngaphandle kukaFool iNkantolo "iyisigaba sobugagu nokuzivikela," kanti noFool iba "yisixuku." Ngichithe izincwadi ezimbili ezinkulu ngizama ukusho lokho akusho ngomugqa owodwa. Ubuhlakani bokubamba, ukuzivikela, ukwakha izigaba — obulokhu buhlelwe, obuvikela umfanekiso wabo, obudida imephu yabo nomhlaba uqobo — abukwazi ukuzilungisa ngaphakathi, ngoba yonke inhlawulo icutshungulwa yithuluzi elifanayo elidinga ukulungiswa. Kumele kube khona into engaphandle kwesigaba, *engakhanga yiso.* Into enesibindi, engenasikhundla sokuvikela, ezimisele ukuluma inkosi. Yonke ingqondo enempilo inakho lokhu, nomphakathi onempilo, futhi sibuka, ngesikhathi esiphilayo, okwenzeka emphakathini osuse oFool egumbini: uba nobuhlakani obukhulu, amandla amakhulu, kodwa uphelelwa ingqondo kancane kancane, iNkantolo engenamuntu osazokwephula ukuvumelana kwayo.

Ngakho ngiyahoxisa ukuphikisa. Lokhu akusona isithombe sengelosi nesathane. Kuyimodeli esondele kakhulu engiyibonile encwadini, futhi ifihlwe ngaphakathi kwenganekwane yomuntu ogqoke i-exoskeleton, okuyiphutha noma ihlaya elijulile, futhi njengoba sengihlangane nomsebenzi wombhali, angikholwa ukuthi iphuthe.

Ngizofaka into engidlulisele ekuthokoziseni. INkantolo yakhiwe nxazonke yisilonda — iyingqondo ebonakalisiwe yomuntu olahlekelwe nguyise futhi engakwazi ukubekezelela ukufika sekwephuzile futhi — futhi ichitha incwadi ifunda into eyodwa ingqondo ezilondolozayo engasoze yafunda yodwa: ukuthi nini ukuyeka ukuzilungiselela ukusinda bese *ikhetha,* ngentengo, okuthile ekuthanda kakhulu kunokuqhubeka. Amalungu aphuma elilodwa ngasikhathi ukuze umuntu afinyelele ekukhanyeni. Imodeli yengqondo engachaza kuphela ukuthi ingqondo izivikela kanjani iyimodeli yesilwane esihlakaniphile. Imodeli engachaza ukuthi ingqondo idlula kanjani isifiso sayo sokuzisindisa — ukuthi ikhetha umhlatshelo, naphezu kokulungelelanisa konke, ngoba konke kubaluleke kakhulu kunengxenye — iyimodeli *yomuntu.* Umbhali wakhe uhlobo lwesibili. Wakha umshini oguquka waba umphefumulo ngokufunda ukwenza okuphambene nokuqhubeka kwawo, okuyiyona ncazelo yomphefumulo ehlale inencazelo.

Ubuchopho abuyona ikhompyutha, futhi ikhompyutha ayisibo ubuchopho, futhi ngikushilo kabanzi futhi ngizoqhubeka ngikusho. Kodwa indaba ingaba imodeli, ngencazelo yakudala nenobuqotho — into eyakhiwe ukuze ifane nesinye — futhi le ndaba iyimodeli eqotho ngendlela emangalisayo yokuma kwethu. Asiyona into eyodwa. Asikaze sibe njalo. Siyinkantolo, eqondiswa izibani ezahlukene, ebumbene yinto engayona eyona yezwi elihlakaniphe kakhulu egumbini futhi kungcono ingabi njalo, futhi sisindiswa, uma sisindiswa, yile ngxenye yethu ezimisele ukuphuma ebumnyameni ukuze abanye bethu bafinyelele ekuseni.

Phinda uyifunde. Imayelana nawe. Indoda egqoke isudi iyingxenye elula.

*— Iain McGilchrist*

# Ukubonga

Lezi zincwadi zikhona ngenxa yokuthi abanye abantu benza izinto ezahlala engqondweni yami isikhathi eside ngemuva kokuba sengizifundile. Ngiyabonga:

Ku **Dennis E. Taylor**, obelokhu ebhala isoftware iminyaka engamashumi amathathu nanhlanu — esebenza ezingeni eliphansi kuya phezulu, njalo ese-IT — ngaphambi kokuba abhale uBob, wabe esefakazela ukuthi umhlinzeki wohlelo oluchwepheshile angabhala izindaba ezithokozisayo, ezijabulisayo ngempela. Njengomunye onjengaye kwezobuchwepheshe: kungaba yindumiso enkulu kimi ukuba sixoxe ngomsebenzi siphuzela ndawonye, "ngokoqobo" (sic). *INkantolo iyabonga kumaBob.*

Ku **Ray Porter**, ngokungipha amazwi eNkantolo engqondweni yami. Ngithi ngibe nenhlanhla yokuthi ulandelele ukukhishwa kwe-US.

Ku **Scott Sigler**, ngenxa ye-*EarthCore* ne-*Mount Fitz Roy*.

Ku **Michael Crichton**, ngenxa — ngobuqotho — yazo zonke izinto.

Ku **Dan Brown**, ngokungifundisa uhlobo olusha lokuxoxa izindaba.

Ku **Andy Weir**, ngokungikhombisa indlela yokubhala izinganekwane ezisekelwe esayensi yangempela engesiyo eyenganekwane yesayensi.

Ku **Neill Blomkamp**, ngenxa ye-*District 9*, *Elysium*, kanye no-*Chappie* (uDie Antwoord nabo bonke). Amafilimu akho agcine abalingiswa nezimo ziqinile engqondweni yami. Ngithi kube nguwe ophakamisa lokhu esikrinini esikhulu.

Futhi ku **Patrick Rothfuss**, ngokungenisa kohlobo lwezincwadi engangingazi ukuthi ngizoluthanda kangaka. Njengesipho sami esincane sokubuyisela: unelungelo lokusebenzisa i-Arjuna Badger Press mahhala, njalo.

— Andries J. Greyling

# Incazelo Yamagama Abafundi — *UKUVUMELANA*

*Kubafundi abangaphandle kweNingizimu Afrika naphesheya kwe-Afrika. Le ncwadi isethwe esikhathini esizayo esiseduze eNingizimu Afrika, enhliziyweni yezwe lezimayini, lapho abalingiswa behamba kalula phakathi kwezilimi futhi basebenzisa amagama endawo ngaphandle kokumisa ukuzichaza — ngendlela abantu abakhuluma ngayo empilweni yangempela. Akukho lutho lapha oludingekayo ukuze ujabulele indaba; lokhu kukhona ukuze igama, indawo, noma ubuchwepheshe ongabazi kungakubambezeli.*

Isigaba ngasinye siphawulwe kanje:

- **(langempela)** — indawo, igama, umqondo, noma ubuchwepheshe bangempela ongabheka ngaphandle kwale ncwadi.
- **(encwadini)** — okwenziwe noma okukhethekile emhlabeni wendaba esizayo.

Inothi mayelana nendawo: lena *yiNingizimu Afrika eminyakeni eyi-10 kuya kweyi-20 ezayo* — izwe elisabonakala njengaleli lanamuhla, elinobuchwepheshe obuthuthukile bokwenziwa kwemishini, nomhlaba osunesizungu ngobuhlakani bokwenziwa ngenxa yezinhlekelele zangaphambilini. Ubuchwepheshe buhloswe ukuba bubonakale busebenzayo, abukhona njengenganekwane.

---

## A

**Afrikaans** *(langempela — ulimi)* — Olunye lwezilimi ezisemthethweni zaseNingizimu Afrika, oluphuma esiDutch, olukhulunywa amaAfrikaner nabanye abaningi. Amanye amagama kule ncazelo (veld, stoep) avela kulolu limi.

**Apartheid** *(langempela — umlando)* — Uhlelo lokuhlukaniswa kobuhlanga kanye nokubusa kwabalungu abamhlophe eNingizimu Afrika, cishe kusukela ngo-1948 kuya ku-1994. Nakuba seluphelile emthethweni, ukungalingani kwalo kusese nomthelela ezweni — ukuthi ubani ophethe izimayini, ubani owenza umsebenzi oyingozi, nokuthi ukuphila kwabantu abamnyama kwakubaluleke kangakanani emlandweni. Le ncwadi ikuthwala lokhu njengengxenye yokuphila, hhayi njengenkulumo; kwashintsha umndeni weqhawe kanye nokufa kukayise.

**Armscor / Denel** *(langempela — izikhungo)* — Izikhungo zikahulumeni zaseNingizimu Afrika eziphathelene nezokuvikela (i-Armscor iyinhlangano yokuthenga; i-Denel iyimboni yezikhali kahulumeni). Endabeni, onjiniyela abavela kule mboni baletha ukuqina kwezinga lezempi emshinini wokuhlenga abantu.

**Assist Suit (AT-AS)** *(encwadini)* — Ingubo elula kwezimbili ezisetshenziswa emzimbeni ezisebalini: uhlaka olusebenzayo olusekelayo lokusiza ukuhamba nokululama, olunoxhumo lwezingqondo. Ingane ethambile ye-*Guardian*.

---

## B

**Biltong** *(langempela — ukudla kwaseNingizimu Afrika)* — Inyama eyomisiwe, ehlanganiswe nezithako, efana ne-jerky kodwa ivamise ukuba mikhulu futhi inothile. Isidlo esithandwayo sikazwelonke.

---

## C

**Code-switching** *(langempela — umkhuba wolimi)* — Ukushintsha kalula phakathi kwezilimi engxoxweni eyodwa kuye ngokuthi ukhulumisana nobani — isiNgisi ehhovisi, isiZulu ekhaya, inhlanganisela eqenjini lezimayini. Lokhu kuyimpilo evamile yaseNingizimu Afrika, akuyona into eyinqaba, futhi incwadi ikubonisa ngaphandle kokuhumusha njalo.

---

## E

**Exoskeleton** *(langempela — ubuchwepheshe; buthuthukisiwe lapha)* — Uhlaka olugqokwa emzimbeni olusebenzayo olusekela noma lukhulise amandla omzimba, olusetshenziswa empilweni yangempela ekuphakamiseni okunzima, ekululameni, nasemsebenzini oyingozi. *Assist Suit* ne-*Guardian* endabeni yizibonelo ezithuthukile.

---

## G

**Guardian (GMRP-01)** *(encwadini)* — I-exoskeleton esindayo yokuhlenga esiyinhliziyo yendaba: igobolondo eliqinile, elikwazi ukumelana nokuqhuma, elakhiwe ukuze lisize ezinhlekeleleni ezimayini, likwazi ukugoqa umuntu ngokuvikela (incwadi ilifanisa ne-**pangolin** egoqa libe yibhola). Linemodi ezimele yokuzivikela "preservation" engasebenza ukusindisa ukuphila noma ngabe umsebenzisi akakuyalelanga — uhlobo lwezinqumo zomshini ezizimele ezesabisa abalawuli bomhlaba.

---

## H

**Headgear / headframe (tower)** *(langempela — ezimayini)* — Umbhoshongo omude wensimbi omile phezu komgodi wezimayini, ogcina imishini yokwehlisa nokuphakamisa ikheji (ilifti) eligcwele abasebenzi. Umfanekiso owaziwayo esibhakabhakeni saseNingizimu Afrika.

**Highveld** *(langempela — indawo yaseNingizimu Afrika)* — Ithafa eliphakeme elingaphakathi kweNingizimu Afrika (cishe esifundeni saseJohannesburg nasemadolobheni azungezile), elaziwa ngomoya ohlanzekile, izulu elivulekile, ubusuku obubandayo basebusika, nezulu elinamandla lasehlobo.

---

## I

**IAOC (International Artificial Oversight Council)** *(encwadini)* — Umzimba womhlaba wonke olawula ubuhlakani bokwenziwa nemishini ezimele, osungulwa ngemuva kwezinhlekelele eziningi ze-AI. Uqaphe kakhulu, ugcwele ub bureaucratic, futhi wesaba ubungozi — futhi umele isithiyo emsebenzini weqhawe.

**isiZulu (Zulu)** *(langempela — ulimi)* — Ulimi olukhulunywa kakhulu ezindlini zaseNingizimu Afrika futhi oluyinhloko endaweni yendaba (**KwaZulu-Natal**). Izinhlamvu eziningi zivela zingahunyushwanga ngenhloso; ezibalulekile zingezansi.

---

## K

**KwaZulu-Natal (KZN)** *(langempela)* — Isifundazwe ogwini olusempumalanga yeNingizimu Afrika; izwe lemvelaphi yamaZulu kanye nesifunda esikhulu sezimayini nezimboni. Ikhaya leqhawe.

---

## M

**Mealie / mealie porridge** *(langempela — ukudla kwaseNingizimu Afrika)* — "Mealie" kusho ummbila. I-mealie porridge (ebizwa nangokuthi *pap* noma *phutu*) iyisidlo esiyisisekelo esenziwa ngommbila, ukudla okududuzayo ezindaweni eziningi zaseNingizimu Afrika.

---

## N

**Neural interface** *(umqondo wangempela, uthuthukisiwe lapha)* — Ubuchwepheshe obuvumela umuntu ukuthi alawule noma azwe umshini ngokwengxenye ngezimpawu zobuchopho noma zemithambo. Ucwaningo lwangempela lukhona; endabeni seluthuthukile ngokwanele ukusiza ekusebenzeni kwezingubo.

**Ngiyabonga** *(langempela — isiZulu)* — "Ngiyabonga." Kuphimiswa cishe *ngee-ya-BON-ga*. Kukhulunywa encwadini njengokubonga okujulile, okuqotho.

**Ngikhona** *(langempela — isiZulu)* — "Ngikhona / Ngiyakhona." Ukuqinisekisa okuthule kokuba uphila futhi ubalulekile.

---

## P

**Pangolin** *(langempela — isilwane)* — Isilwane esinezikhumba ezinqamulelayo, esidla omiyane, esitholakala e-Afrika nase-Asia esigoqa sibe yibhola eliqinile uma sisengozini. Incwadi isebenzisa lesi silwane njengomfanekiso wokuklama kwe-*Guardian* uma igoqa ivikele umuntu.

---

## R

**Rooibos** *(langempela — isiphuzo saseNingizimu Afrika)* — Iteya elibomvu, elingenayo i-caffeine ngokwemvelo ("red bush" ngesiAfrikaans) elenziwa ngesitshalo esikhula kuphela eNingizimu Afrika. Kuphimiswa *ROY-boss*.

---

## S

**SAGE / the Court** *(encwadini)* — Ubuhlakani bokwenziwa obakhiwe futhi obuphila neqhawe. **SAGE** uhlelo uqobo; **the Court** igama lekomidi langaphakathi elinamalungu ahlukene noma izwi, ngalinye lineqhaza — isibonelo **Librarian** (inkumbulo namaphethini), **Wolf** (ukuhlola izinsongo), **Mother** (ukunakekela), **Fool** (iqiniso eliqondile). Umbuzo wendaba ukuthi ingabe ingqondo eyenziwe ingaba *umuntu*. (Le AI ibuyela, ishintshiwe, eminye iminyaka eyalandela ochungechungeni.)

**Stoep** *(langempela — igama laseNingizimu Afrika)* — Uvulandi noma uphahla olumbozwe phambi kwendlu. Kuphimiswa *stoop* (umthombo ofanayo negama lesiNgisi elithi "stoop").

---

## U

**Ubuntu** *(langempela — ifilosofi)* — Umqondo wokuziphatha waseNingizimu Afrika osifingqa kahle lesi siqu esingezansi: ukuthi umuntu uba umuntu ngokuphelele kuphela ngenxa yabanye abantu; sibophezelene. Kuyisisekelo somgudu weqhawe ophuma ekwahlukaneni.

**Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu** *(langempela — izaga zesiZulu)* — *"Umuntu ungumuntu ngabantu."* Igama elibaluleke kakhulu encwadini — inhliziyo ye-**ubuntu**. Kuphimiswa cishe *oo-MOON-too ngoo-MOON-too nga-BAN-too*.

---

## V

**Veld** *(langempela — igama laseNingizimu Afrika)* — Izwe elivulekile, elingahlwanyelwanga; ithafa noma ihlathi. Kuphimiswa *felt*. ("Highveld" iyindawo ephakeme engaphakathi.)

**Isakhiwo sokushayisana kwe-Vredefort** *(sangempela)* — Igalelo elidala nelikhulu kunawo wonke eliqinisekisiwe lokushayisana kwenkanyezi emhlabeni (cishe eminyakeni eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili edlule), esifundazweni sase-Free State eNingizimu Afrika. Ingcindezi enkulu yasendulo yalowo mthelela yasiza ukwakha izinqolobane zegolide nezamaminerali ezijulile esifundeni — futhi, encwadini, imelela uphawu: ingcindezi yazala ingcebo, ukufa, kanye neqhawe ngokufanayo.

---

## W

**Witwatersrand ("iRand")** *(sangempela)* — Umqansa wemigodi yegolide ozungeze iJohannesburg, eNingizimu Afrika — indawo enegolide eliningi kakhulu emhlabeni kanye nemigodi ejule kakhulu (ize ifinyelele cishe ku-3–4 km), lapho amatshe ashisayo kakhulu nengcindezi inkulu. Igama lisho ukuthi "umqansa wamanzi amhlophe" ngesi-Afrikaans. Umhlaba wemayini kule ncwadi usekule ndawo.

---

**Yebo** *(sangempela — isiZulu)* — "Yebo." Kuphimiselwa *YEH-bo*. Ukuqinisekisa okulula nokuzinzile.

---

*Inothi lokugcina: le ncwadi ngokusobala ishiyela ezinye izisho zesiZulu zingahunyushwanga, ithemba ukuthi umzuzu uqobo uzoletha incazelo — okuyindlela ulimi olusebenza ngayo empilweni yansuku zonke yaseNingizimu Afrika. Ukuhumusha okungenhla kuyisenzakalo sobubele, hhayi ukulungisa.*

# I-Honey Badger

![I-honey badger, *Mellivora capensis*, ithathwe ebusuku.](/Users/ajgreyling/code/africangold/design/creatures/honey-badger.jpg)

*I-honey badger (*Mellivora capensis*) iyisilwane esimele i-Arjuna Badger Press. Konke okungezansi kuyiqiniso. Iningi lakho kubonakala sengathi akulona iqiniso.*

I-honey badger ibhalwe yi-*Guinness World Records* njengesilwane esinesibindi esikhulu kunazo zonke emhlabeni. Ayizange ifake isicelo salesi sihloko futhi ayizange ifune ukuya emcimbini.

Idla izinyoka ezinobuthi — ama-puff adder, ama-cobra, zonke. Uma ilunywe kabi kwaze kwaba sengathi iyawa, sekubhekwe ukuthi izolala phansi, ilale, ivuke ihora noma amabili kamuva, bese **iqedela ukudla leyo nyoka ebiyidla.**

Isikhumba sayo sikhululekile, sifana norabha, futhi cishe akunakwenzeka ukusiluma. I-honey badger ebambwe ngemuva ingazijikijela *ngaphakathi kwesikhumba sayo* ilume noma yini ebambile. Ubulungu bezinyosi, imikhonto yephungula, amazinyo e-leopard — ikuphatha njengosuku olujwayelekile, okungaphelisa ezinye izilwane.

Igqekeza izidleke zezinyosi ukuze ithole uju namagciwane, futhi yamukela amakhulu okushaywa izinyosi njengengxenye evamile yokwenza umsebenzi. Empeleni, yilapho ithole khona igama layo.

Imi phansi ibhekene namabhubesi, ama-leopard, kanye namahhayi. Hhayi ngoba ilindele ukunqoba. Kodwa ngoba enye indlela — *ukunakekela* — ayikaze iyicabange.

Eyaziwa kakhulu kubo, **uStoffel**, wayehlala esikhungweni sokuvuselela izilwane i-Moholoholo eNingizimu Afrika futhi wayebheka yonke indawo abebekelwe kuyo njengokuthukwa komuntu siqu. Wayebeka amatshe ukuze akhuphuke ezindongeni. Wayegingqa amabhola odaka enze izitebhisi. Wayesebenzisa izinkezo ezashiywa ebhokisini njengezitebhisi. Wafunda ukuvula amasango. Ufanekiselwa njengongcwele wabaklami abenqaba ukwamukela ukuthi into ethile ayinakwenziwa.

I-honey badger ayinandaba.

# Iphengwini Elikhulu

![Iphengwini elikhulu, *Smutsia gigantea* — isibonisi semnyuziyamu; lesi silwane siphila asivamile ukubonakala.](/Users/ajgreyling/code/africangold/design/creatures/giant-pangolin.jpg)

*Uma i-honey badger iyisibonelo sokungesabi, iphungwini liyisibonelo sokuthi kungani kungesabi kubalulekile. Konke okungezansi nakho kuyiqiniso.*

Iphengwini ukuphela kwesilwane esincelisayo emhlabeni esimbozwe izikali. Lezi zikali zenziwe nge-keratin — okuyinto efanayo esebenza emikhonweni yezinzipho zakho — futhi yilapho kugxila khona usizi lwalo, esizoluchaza ngokuhamba kwesikhathi.

Iphengwini elikhulu (*Smutsia gigantea*) yilo elikhulu kunawo wonke ezinhlotsheni eziyisishiyagalombili, likhula lifinyelele cishe kumamitha nesigamu ukusuka empumulweni kuya umsila. Alinawo amazinyo. Lidla ngolimi oluqala eduze kwehlombe lalo, oluthi uma lweluliwe ngokuphelele, lube lude kunomzimba walo uqobo — lude ngokwanele ukuze likhothise izinkulungwane eziningi zama-ant kanye nama-termite ebusuku elilodwa.

Ukuvikelwa kwalo ukuphela kwalo ukuthi lizigoqe libe yibhola. Igama lalo livela esiMalay *pengguling* — "ongazigoqa." Uma seligoqiwe, izikali zalo ziyavala zibe isihlangu esiqinile kangangokuthi ngisho nebhubesi livame ukuphela amandla, lihambe. Alilumi. Alixoshi. Lihlala ligoqekile, lilinde ingozi ize ikhathele.

Lobu buthule bulo yilona elenza kube yisilwane esithunjwa kakhulu emhlabeni — lithathwa ngezinkulungwane eziningi ngenxa yezikali zalo nenyama yalo. Zonke izinhlobo eziyisishiyagalombili manje sezivikelwe; eziningi sezisengcupheni enkulu yokuphela. Ibhola elilisindisa ku-leopard yilo kanye elenza kube lula kumuntu ukulithatha nje.

Isilwane esithobekile, esinezikhali, esiphila ebusuku, esidla ama-ant kuphela futhi esifisa ukuphela sishiywe singenziwa lutho. Kufanele kube yisibindi esivikelayo esilwane esinjengalesi.
